Abstract: Crowdsourced imagery provides timely, fine-grained, street-level observations for disaster mapping, complementing conventional remote sensing imagery (RSI) during emergency response. However, such imagery is often unstructured, spatially ambiguous, and lacks reliable geographic metadata, making manual geolocalization and interpretation labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This work proposes a multi-task Geospatial Reasoning Disaster mapping framework, namely GRDisaster, to examine the potential of vision-language models (VLMs) in understanding, geolocalizing, and reasoning over crowdsourced disaster imagery. GRDisaster is built on a newly curated benchmark dataset derived from PhotoMappers, comprising 26,340 images organized into human-validated volunteered geographic information (VGI), street-view imagery (SVI), RSI cross-view triplets covering multiple disaster events from 2018 to 2024. The framework combines deterministic and probabilistic cross-view geolocalization with multi-view fusion to associate VGI images with georeferenced SVI and RSI. It introduces two sets of spatial reasoning indicators for cross-view geolocalization validation and disaster damage assessment. These indicators use structural, environmental, and global-scene cues to validate cross-view correspondences and visually observable damage evidence with expert-verified annotations to assess disaster severity, improving the interpretability of VLM outputs. To our knowledge, this study provides the first systematic investigation and unified evaluation framework for examining how VLM-based spatial reasoning can transform crowdsourced disaster imagery into actionable geospatial artificial intelligence (GeoAI) through cross-view geolocalization validation, interpretable spatial reasoning, and damage-aware severity assessment.

</details>


### [10] [Vmem-$\varphi$: Low-Compute Out-of-Distribution Detection in Spiking Neural Networks from Membrane-Potential Statistics](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00350v1)
*Arul Rana,Agrim Tripathi,Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Md. Shaown Miah,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SNN的膜电位动力学进行分布外检测，通过简单统计量结合多描述符偏差（MDD）方法，在事件相机腐蚀基准Gen1-C上实现高效低成本的OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法依赖模型输出或计算组件，在低功耗SNN目标检测中不可用或不适用；事件相机的OOD检测面临特殊挑战。

Method: 利用亚阈值膜电位Vmemo(t)作为内部信号，提取per-channel统计量；提出Multi-Descriptor Deviation (MDD)腐败盲方法；构建Gen1-C基准（含6种传感器级压力测试，5个严重等级）。

Result: 在最高腐蚀等级下，MDD在6种腐蚀中的5种达到AUROC > 0.88，仅用64帧观察窗口；剩余一种腐蚀也是对检测器影响最小的。

Conclusion: 时间膜电位动力学可作为SNN事件感知中有效且低成本的OOD检测信号。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer an energy-efficient approach to processing event-camera data, yet out-of-distribution (OOD) detection remains challenging in this setting. Existing OOD detection methods often depend on model outputs or computational components that are unavailable in object detection SNNs or are poorly suited to low-compute deployment. To that effect, we show that the subthreshold membrane potential \(V_{\mathrm{mem}}(t)\) provides a useful internal signal for detecting distribution shifts. Simple per-channel statistics derived from these membrane dynamics enable OOD detection. To evaluate this approach, we introduce Gen1-C, an event-camera corruption benchmark developed upon the Prophesee Gen1 automotive detection dataset, containing six sensor-motivated histogram-level stress tests at five severity levels. We further propose the Multi-Descriptor Deviation (MDD), a corruption-blind method that operates on membrane-potential statistics. At the highest corruption severity, MDD achieves an AUROC of more than 0.88 on five of the six corruptions using only a bounded 64-frame observation window. Notably, the remaining corruption is also the one that has the smallest effect on the underlying detector. These results show that the temporal membrane-potential dynamics can provide an effective and low-cost signal for OOD detection in SNN-based event perception.

</details>


### [11] [From Image Latent Space to Fuzzy Rules: Interpretable Analysis of Gastrointestinal Foundation Model](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00414v1)
*Michael D. Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原型模糊规则的框架，无需微调即可解释预训练基础模型（如ViT）在医学影像上的特征表示。通过聚类学习类特定原型，将patch特征表达为原型相似度，并用可读的模糊IF-THEN规则进行分类。在胶囊内镜、GI内镜和结肠息肉分割任务上验证，性能可比黑盒分类器，且领域预训练特征兼具判别性和符号可压缩性。规则还可用于分析合成医学图像，定位合成图像与真实组织的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医学影像任务上表现出色，但其潜在表征如何编码临床相关信息仍不清晰，尤其在安全关键领域需要可解释性。现有方法多为黑盒分类器，缺乏人类可读的决策依据。

Method: 1) 无需微调，直接利用冻结的ViT-S/16 backbone特征；2) 通过聚类在学习特征空间中构建类特定原型；3) 将patch特征表示为与原型的相似度；4) 使用模糊IF-THEN规则进行分类，输出人类可读的决策逻辑；5) 在ImageNet-1K和GastroNet-5M预训练的ViT上测试，提取最后两个Transformer块的特征。

Result: 无需微调，性能与k-NN、kernel SVM、linear probing等黑盒分类器相当；领域特定预训练（GastroNet-5M）产生的特征兼具判别性和符号可压缩性；提取的规则可定位合成医学图像与真实组织的偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了透明、深度可解析的视角来理解基础模型如何组织临床相关结构，同时为下游任务（如合成图像分析）提供了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Foundation models pretrained on large-scale datasets demonstrate strong transferability to medical imaging tasks. However, understanding how their latent representations encode clinically relevant information remains an open challenge in safety-critical domains. This study proposes a prototype-based fuzzy-rule framework that interprets the patch-level features produced by the inner layers of pretrained foundation models, without any fine-tuning. Class-specific prototypes are learned by clustering in the feature space, yielding compact visual patterns. Patch features are then expressed as prototype similarities and classified by fuzzy rules with linguistic IF-THEN conditions that are human readable. The framework is applied across the final two blocks of ViT-S/16 backbones pretrained on ImageNet-1K and GastroNet-5M, and benchmarked against k-nearest neighbours, kernel SVM, and linear probing under identical frozen features, on wireless capsule endoscopy classification, gastrointestinal endoscopy classification, and colonic polyp segmentation. The experimental analysis shows that the proposed method, without backbone fine-tuning, reaches accuracy comparable to these black-box classifiers, and that domain-specific pretraining yields features that are both discriminative and symbolically compressible. Because the resulting rules are extracted from real data and expressed in interpretable terms, they are further used as an instrument to investigate synthetic medical images, providing a human-readable account of which real prototypes and rules a generator reproduces or fails to reproduce, localising where a synthetic image departs from real tissue rather than summarising it with a single score. The framework thus offers a transparent, depth-resolved view of how foundation models organise clinically relevant structure, together with a practical downstream use of the extracted rules.

</details>


### [12] [Scores That Hold, Benchmarks That Leak: Measuring Dataset Contamination in Public Brain-Tumor MRI Classification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00421v1)
*Bhanu Prakash Vangala,Sowmya Guda,Latha Peddi,Navya Vangala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了脑肿瘤MRI分类领域深度学习基准数据集存在严重的数据泄露问题，包括图像重复、患者信息泄露和文件头特征泄露三个层次。研究发现即使移除所有识别出的泄露测试图像，准确率基本保持不变，说明仅凭稳定的排行榜无法保证基准数据集的完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医学影像领域报告的准确率常超过98%，但这些高准确率是否真正反映了模型的泛化能力存疑。论文认为准确率指标无法捕捉基准数据集质量的关键维度——数据集完整性（dataset integrity），即测试集与训练集在图像、患者和采集来源三个层面的独立性。

Method: 研究提出了一个三层污染框架：①重复泄露（duplicate leakage）②患者泄露（patient leakage）③来源标签泄露（source-label leakage）。作者对三个最广泛使用的脑肿瘤数据集进行审计，并与胸部X光片负对照组进行比较，在9种架构和3种评估条件下量化各层泄露对性能的影响。

Result: 污染问题在每个层面都严重：主数据集28.8%的官方测试集图像在训练集中存在近似副本；第二个数据集22.3%的测试图像与训练图像字节相同；95.5%的可追溯测试图像与训练集共享患者；完全不包含解剖信息的文件头特征能达到0.959的平衡准确率，与微调ResNet相当。关键发现：移除所有泄露测试图像后平衡准确率基本不变。

Conclusion: 数据集完整性是一个独立、可测量的基准质量维度，稳定的表现无法证明其完整性。现有基准数据集上的高准确率不能证明模型学会了识别肿瘤而非利用数据集特定的线索。研究发布了污染文件列表、恢复的患者标识符和去重后的数据集。

Abstract: Automated classification of brain tumors from MRI is a heavily published application of deep learning in medical imaging, with reported accuracies on public benchmarks routinely exceeding 98%. However, accuracy does not capture a critical dimension of benchmark quality: dataset integrity, defined as the independence of test from training data at the image, patient, and acquisition-source levels. We introduce a three-layer contamination framework comprising duplicate, patient, and source-label leakage to assess the public corpora on which this literature rests. We audit the three most widely used corpora against a chest-radiograph negative control and quantify each layer's effect on measured performance across nine architectures and three evaluation conditions. Contamination is severe at every layer: 28.8% of the dominant corpus's official test split has a near-twin in its own training split, a second corpus leaks 22.3% of its test images byte-identically, 95.5% of traceable test images share a patient with training, and file-header features containing no anatomy separate tumor from no-tumor at 0.959 balanced accuracy, at parity with fine-tuned ResNet backbones. The unexpected result is that removing every identified leaked test image leaves balanced accuracy essentially unchanged: stable performance after deduplication does not establish benchmark integrity. Our findings establish dataset integrity as a distinct, measurable axis of benchmark quality that a stable leaderboard cannot certify. For biomedical research, reported accuracy on these corpora alone does not establish that a model has learned to recognize tumors rather than exploit dataset-specific cues. We release the contaminated-file lists, recovered patient identifiers, and deduplicated splits.

</details>


### [13] [PACT: End-to-End Learning of Human Pose, Contacts, and Forces from Video](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00451v1)
*Rikhat Akizhanov,Yangsong Zhang,Nikolai Kaliazin,Peter Wolf,Yoshihiko Nakamura,Pascal Fua,Fabio Pizzati,Ivan Laptev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACT是一个端到端模型，通过单目视频联合估计人体姿态、接触状态和接触力。该方法在人体重建基础模型上添加可学习的接触力token和时间transformer，利用物理约束进行监督，并使用合成和真实视频数据训练。实验表明该方法在接触力和姿态估计任务上达到最优性能，且能泛化到训练分布外的交互场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉姿态重建与接触力估计分离处理，这种分离限制了联合推理能力并可能造成错误传播。分离式流程难以建模人体运动、环境接触与交互力之间的物理一致性约束。

Method: 1) 在人体重建基础模型上引入可学习的接触力token；2) 使用时间transformer整合视觉特征与世界坐标系运动；3) 联合预测头同时细化姿态、估计接触状态和力；4) 基于物理的监督损失确保重建运动与交互力的一致性；5) 开发数据标注流水线，结合接触标注与基于物理的运动和力优化，生成训练标签。

Result: 在接触和力估计任务上达到最优性能，优于分离式重建方法，并能泛化到训练分布外的交互场景。引入ForceWall基准数据集，包含攀岩视频及对应的传感器真实接触力数据。

Conclusion: 端到端联合学习是实现从视频中恢复人体运动和物理交互的有效方法。联合建模姿态、接触与力的方法显著优于分离式处理流程。

Abstract: Human motion, environmental contacts, and interaction forces are governed by common physical laws, yet existing approaches typically separate visual pose reconstruction from contact and force estimation. This separation limits joint reasoning and can propagate errors between stages. We introduce PACT, an end-to-end model that jointly learns to estimate human pose, contacts and contact forces from monocular video. Our approach augments a human reconstruction foundation model with learnable contact-force tokens and a temporal transformer that integrates visual features with world-space motion. Joint prediction heads refine human poses and estimate contacts and forces, while physics-based supervision encourages consistency between the reconstructed motion and interaction forces. To address the scarcity of force annotations, we develop a data annotation pipeline that combines contact labeling with physics-based motion and force optimization, producing training supervision from synthetic and real-world videos. We also introduce a real-world climbing benchmark ForceWall with climbing videos and corresponding ground-truth contact forces obtained from the force sensors. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art contact and force estimation, outperforming staged reconstruction approaches and generalizing to interactions beyond the training distribution. These results support end-to-end joint learning as an effective approach to recovering human motion and physical interactions from video.

</details>


### [14] [PhysVista: Benchmarking Physical Intelligence in VLMs via a Perception-Reasoning-Assessment Loop](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00559v1)
*Xinge Peng,Yiting Lu,Tianwu Zhi,Wen Wen,Jianzhao Liu,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysVista是一个评估视觉语言模型(VLM)物理智能的新基准，通过"闭合认知环路"框架从物理状态感知、物理动力学推理和物理合理性评估三个维度联合评估模型的物理理解能力，同时区分事件级和尺度级推理，并包含真实世界和AI生成视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估范式存在碎片化问题，聚焦于孤立认知阶段而忽视感知、推理与物理判断之间的内在协同。此外，缺乏整体视角导致难以诊断VLM是否能可靠评估新兴生成模型的物理真实性。

Method: 提出PhysVista基准，采用闭合认知环路框架，重构人类"观察-推理-评估"的认知过程。在评估维度上，联合评估物理状态感知、物理动力学推理和物理合理性评估；在粒度上，区分事件级推理和尺度级推理；在数据上，同时纳入真实世界视频和AI生成视频。

Result: 跨多个VLM的广泛实验揭示了模型在物理推理和合理性评估方面的显著局限性，表明视觉识别与真正的物理理解之间存在持续差距。

Conclusion: VLM在物理理解方面仍有重大不足，强调需要更原则性的设计来实现物理具身的多模态智能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, yet whether they truly capture the physical consistency underlying real-world dynamics remains unclear. Existing benchmark paradigms often suffer from fragmented evaluation, focusing on isolated cognitive stages while overlooking the inherent synergy between perception, reasoning, and physical judgment. The lack of a holistic perspective limits the ability to diagnose whether VLMs can reliably evaluate the physical authenticity of emerging generative models. To address these issues, we introduce PhysVista, a benchmark designed to evaluate physical intelligence in VLMs through a closed cognitive loop framework inspired by the human seeing-reasoning-assessment process. PhysVista restores this loop by jointly evaluating physical state perception, physical dynamics reasoning, and physical plausibility assessment. It further distinguishes event-level reasoning and scale-level reasoning to enable fine-grained analysis of physical understanding. In addition, PhysVista incorporates both real-world and AI-generated videos, allowing evaluation across diverse domains and emerging generative scenarios. Extensive experiments across a diverse set of VLMs reveal substantial limitations in physical reasoning and plausibility assessment, highlighting a persistent gap between visual recognition and genuine physical understanding, and pointing toward more principled designs for physically grounded multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [15] [FORTE: Adaptive Scoring and Exact Keyframe Selection for Long-Video Question Answering](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00573v1)
*Haifeng Huang,Biyin Xu,Chunsheng Xin,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FORTE框架，通过自适应相关度评分和全局关键帧优化，实现长视频中问题相关帧的高效选择，解决传统方法固定候选池导致的证据遗漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分的关键帧选择方法在固定、均匀采样的候选池内搜索，无法选择池外证据。在有限评分预算下，如何将评估自适应地分配给有前景的帧，同时继续探索代表性不足的时间区域是关键挑战。

Method: FORTE采用两阶段框架：①自适应相关度评分阶段：使用高斯过程预测器估计未评分帧的相关度，利用时间局部性和近似带状核结构，将计算复杂度从O(n³)降至O(n)；评分选择策略平衡预测相关度与时间覆盖率。②全局关键帧优化阶段：最大化同时捕获实测相关度和时间覆盖率的优化目标，利用对数覆盖结构在候选池大小线性时间内识别最优子集。

Result: 在四个长视频问答基准上的实验表明，FORTE在所有测试的评分预算下均达到最高的平均准确率。跨不同相关度评分器和下游MLLMs的进一步评估证明了其泛化能力。

Conclusion: FORTE通过训练无关的自适应采样与优化策略，有效解决了长视频关键帧选择中覆盖性与效率的权衡问题，在多个基准上展现出最优性能。

Abstract: Query-aware keyframe selection enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to process long videos using only a small set of question-relevant frames. Existing score-based methods, however, typically search within a fixed, uniformly sampled candidate pool, preventing evidence outside this pool from ever being selected. Given a limited relevance-scoring budget, the key challenge is to allocate evaluations adaptively to promising frames while continuing to explore underrepresented temporal regions. We introduce FORTE, a training-free framework that addresses this challenge through two stages: adaptive relevance scoring and global keyframe optimization. Starting from sparse, uniformly distributed observations, our efficient Gaussian-process relevance predictor estimates relevance for unscored frames, exploiting temporal locality and the approximately banded kernel structure to reduce the core computation from cubic to linear time in the number of frames for fixed bandwidth. The scoring stage then selects which frames to score next by balancing predicted relevance with temporal coverage, prioritizing promising regions while also exploring less-represented parts of the video. The optimization stage selects the final keyframes by maximizing an objective that jointly captures measured relevance and temporal coverage. We derive an exact algorithm that leverages the logarithmic coverage structure to identify the optimal subset of the scored candidate pool in time linear in the pool size, for a fixed final-frame budget. Experiments on four long-video question-answering benchmarks show that FORTE achieves the highest observed mean accuracy among the compared selectors under every tested scoring budget. Further evaluations demonstrate its consistent effectiveness across different relevance scorers and downstream MLLMs.

</details>


### [16] [Discrete Annotation, Continuous Preference: Rethinking Supervision for Accurate and Generalizable Aesthetic Image Cropping](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00582v1)
*Ziqing Zhang,Xiao Liu,Kai Liu,Jianze Li,Weihang Zhang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出连续偏好场（CPF）来解决美学图像裁剪中标注数据存在的主观性和离散性缺陷，并基于此训练了VLM-based裁剪模型CPIC，同时重新校准了现有基准数据集CPICD，显著提升了模型性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前美学图像裁剪数据集存在两个根本性问题：(1)人类标注的主观性；(2)标注被限制在固定采样网格上的刚性离散性。这些缺陷不仅限制了模型的准确性和泛化能力，还严重扭曲了公平评估。

Method: 提出连续偏好场（CPF）方法，通过四个步骤从离散标注中恢复密集偏好分布：(1)峰值聚类；(2)格点外精细化；(3)负形塑造；(4)场组装。基于CPF奖励信号，使用GRPO算法训练VLM-based裁剪模型CPIC以克服模板坍塌问题。同时引入CPICD对主流基准数据集进行重新校准。

Result: CPIC实现了最先进的性能，并在分布外数据上展现出优异的泛化能力。CPICD有效修正了网格边界伪影，为未来裁剪研究建立了可靠评估基础。

Conclusion: CPF成功将人类裁剪偏好建模为多峰值、连续且尖锐的场，解决了传统标注的离散性问题。CPIC和CPICD的组合为美学图像裁剪领域提供了更精确的模型和更可靠的评估标准。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping aims to identify the optimal crop of an image in terms of aesthetics and composition. While supervision based on annotated data is fundamental, the field has been hindered by a long-standing problem: existing datasets suffer from (1) human subjectivity and (2) rigid discreteness confined to fixed sampling grids. These flawed annotations not only limit the accuracy and generalization of trained models but also severely distort fair evaluation. To overcome this, we propose to model human cropping preference as a multi-peaked, continuous, and sharp field over the crop space. We introduce the Continuous Preference Field (CPF), which recovers a dense preference landscape from discrete annotations through (1) peak clustering, (2) off-lattice refinement, (3) negative shaping, and (4) field assembly. Based on this, we train CPIC, a VLM-based cropping model optimized via GRPO with the CPF reward, which overcomes template collapse, achieving state-of-the-art performance and exceptional out-of-domain generalization. Finally, to resolve the long-standing benchmark evaluation crisis, we introduce CPICD, a comprehensive recalibration of existing ground-truth boxes. By leveraging the CPF to correct grid-bound artifacts across mainstream benchmarks, CPICD establishes a rigorous and reliable foundation for future cropping research. Extensive experiments and user studies demonstrate the superiority of our CPF, CPIC, and CPICD. Code, model, and data are available at https://github.com/zzqingz/CPIC.

</details>


### [17] [HAWK: Rethinking Multimodal Drafting for Speculative Decoding](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00623v1)
*Wenhan Yang,Anirudh Rao,Ashwin Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAWK是一种针对大视觉-语言模型(LVLMs)的推测解码改进方法，通过层选择与状态融合、压缩视觉隐藏状态提供以及预测偏移建模三大创新，解决了现有方法中轻量级草稿模型难以利用多模态信息以及多步推测时与目标模型一致性下降的问题，在SmolVLM-256M上实现最高2.60x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在LLM上已实现显著的无损加速，但在LVLMs上效果不佳。原因有两点：1) 轻量级草稿模型难以有效利用丰富的多模态信息；2) 标准蒸馏仅沿原始训练轨迹监督草稿模型，未建模草稿自身提议后目标预测如何变化，导致多步推测时两者一致性降低，接受率下降。

Method: HAWK采用三重创新策略：①使用表示相似性选择信息量最大的目标层，并学习如何融合其隐藏状态；②直接向草稿模型提供目标模型的压缩视觉隐藏状态而非原始视觉token，降低浅层草稿的利用难度；③训练草稿模型捕捉其自身提议后目标预测的变化模式，提升多步推测时的一致性。

Result: 在SmolVLM-256M上十项多模态基准测试中：贪婪解码下，平均接受长度从3.32提升至4.08，加速比从2.19x提升至2.60x；采样解码下，从2.89提升至3.41和1.92x提升至2.19x。

Conclusion: HAWK通过解决视觉信息传递效率和多步预测偏移问题，显著提升了LVLMs推测解码的效率，为多模态模型的实时推理提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has achieved substantial lossless speedups for LLMs, but remains less effective for large vision-language models (LVLMs), where lightweight drafters struggle to use rich multimodal information. A second limitation is that standard distillation supervises the drafter only along the original training trajectory, without modeling how target predictions shift after the drafter's own proposals. As drafting moves away from this trajectory, the drafter can increasingly disagree with the target, reducing acceptance in later steps. We propose HAWK to address both limitations. HAWK uses representation similarity to select informative target layers and learns how to combine their hidden states. For visual information, it directly provides the drafter with compressed visual hidden states from the target model instead of raw visual tokens, making the visual information easier for a shallow drafter to use. HAWK also trains the drafter to capture how target predictions change after its own proposals, improving its agreement with the target during multi-step drafting. On SmolVLM-256M across ten multimodal benchmarks, HAWK raises average acceptance length from 3.32 to 4.08 and speedup from 2.19x to 2.60x over EAGLE-3 under greedy decoding, and from 2.89 to 3.41 and 1.92x to 2.19x under sampling.

</details>


### [18] [VisionQ: VLM-as-a-Judge Taxonomy, Dataset and Benchmark for Qualitative Analysis in Computer Vision](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00666v1)
*Vu Dinh Xuan,Duc-Hai Nguyen,Minh-Dung Dao,Vu Quynh Giao,Quang Hong Nguyen,Binh-Son Hua,Barry O'Sullivan,David Murphy,Hoang D. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisionQ是一个用于评估视觉-语言模型(VLM)在定性比较任务中表现的新型基准测试。它基于同行评审的CV论文中的比较图像，每个判断都关联到特定的命名视觉标准，而不仅仅是给出标量质量分数或整体偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估基准仅提供标量质量分数或整体偏好判断，导致评判者可能因错误的视觉原因选择偏好图像。缺乏对具体视觉标准的条件化评估，无法验证模型是否基于正确的视觉特征做出判断。

Method: 构建了包含1,409篇CVPR/ICCV论文、1,800+验证比较图和3,911个人工标注数据点的语料库；设计了6轴51叶的视觉标准分类法；采用隐藏方法名称、标题和论文身份的评估协议；训练了VisionQ-Judge(DPO-tuned Gemma-4-E4B)，使用对称证据对减少最后一选项预测。

Result: 最强VLM评判模型仅达到63.1%准确率(随机基线32.2%)，且不同标准间可靠性差异显著。VisionQ-Judge相比基线减少7.0pp的最后选项预测，提升2.5pp准确率。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在细粒度视觉标准判断上仍有较大提升空间，VisionQ提供了一个严格且可解释的评估框架，能够诊断模型在不同视觉标准上的具体能力短板。

Abstract: Qualitative comparison figures are central evidence in computer vision papers, and vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to judge them. Yet existing benchmarks score only scalar quality or overall preference, so a judge can be rewarded for picking the preferred image for the wrong visual reason. We introduce VisionQ, the first benchmark built from peer-reviewed CV comparison figures that grounds every judgment in a named visual criterion: each question states the criterion, and a judge is credited only when it selects the output the authors identify as best on that criterion. We call this task criterion-conditioned visual discrimination. VisionQ comprises (1) a corpus of 1,409 CVPR and ICCV papers with 1,800+ validated comparison figures and 3,911 hand-annotated data points linking method crops to author-stated visual claims; (2) a six-axis, 51-leaf taxonomy of the visual criteria behind qualitative judgment; (3) a criterion-conditioned evaluation protocol that hides method names, captions, and paper identity and reports accuracy per criterion; and (4) VisionQ-Judge, a DPO-tuned Gemma-4-E4B judge trained on symmetric evidence pairs, which reduces last-option predictions by 7.0pp and improves accuracy by 2.5pp on a held-out test set. Evaluating 20 open- and closed-source VLM judges, we find that the strongest reach only 63.1% accuracy (chance 32.2%) and that reliability varies sharply across criteria. Code: https://github.com/ReML-AI/visionq. Data: https://huggingface.co/datasets/visionq-anon-2026/VisionQ-1k.

</details>


### [19] [Harnessing Vision-Language Models for Perceptual Quality Assessment and Autonomous Content Adjustment in Augmented Reality](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00677v1)
*Elias Rotondo,Lin Duan,Yanming Xiu,Sangjun Eom,Conrad Li,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于视觉-语言模型(VLM)的自动化框架，用于评估和预测AR场景的视觉保真度，包含RateAR基准数据集和上下文提示策略，可替代成本高昂的用户研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强现实头戴式显示器面临场景几何约束、空间抖动和时间不稳定性的挑战。传统的用户研究评估方法成本高、可扩展性差且缺乏灵活性，在迭代应用设计中成为瓶颈。

Method: (1) 构建RateAR基准数据集，包含跨多样场景和环境条件的AR图像与视频，信度系数ICC(2,5)≥0.90；(2) 在该基准上评估11个商业VLM；(3) 设计上下文提示策略，通过消融研究验证其优越性；(4) 基于研究结果构建自动化AR内容调整系统。

Result: VLM质量预测与人类主观判断高度相关，Spearman等级相关系数高达0.8695；上下文提示策略相比其他提示方法能更好对齐人类评分；21名参与者的用户研究中，超过90%认为系统改善了虚拟内容的放置和尺寸一致性。

Conclusion: VLM可有效用于AR内容质量评估，提出的自动化框架为迭代AR设计提供了可扩展的解决方案，有望替代或补充传统用户研究方法。

Abstract: Advancements in augmented reality (AR) continue to foster innovative solutions, facilitating novel methodologies within educational systems, healthcare delivery, and risk-mitigation protocols. However, optimizing for end-user immersion and comfort remains challenging, as AR head-mounted displays contend with constrained scene geometry, spatial jitter, and temporal instability. User studies are the standard AR evaluation method for visual quality, but their cost, diminishing scalability, and inflexibility pose bottlenecks during iterative application design. To address this problem, we present an automated framework for AR content evaluation and refinement, built on vision-language models (VLMs), to evaluate and predict the visual fidelity of AR scenes as perceived by users. First, we introduce RateAR, a benchmark of AR images and videos collected across diverse scenes and environmental conditions, with good-to-excellent reliability (ICC(2,5) >= .90) across perceptual factors, including object placement, scale, and shadow consistency. Subsequently, we evaluate eleven commercial VLMs on the crafted benchmark. Results support that VLM-based quality predictions strongly correlate with human subjective judgments, achieving Spearman's rank-order correlations of up to 0.8695. An ablation study further suggests that, compared to other prompting strategies, our contextual prompting yields better alignment with human ratings while balancing introduced complexity cues. Building on these findings, we construct an automated AR content adjustment system and conduct a 21-participant user study. More than 90% of participants found that the system improved placement and size coherence of virtual content.

</details>


### [20] [FedMAD: Modulation-Aware Directional Aggregation for Federated Learning in Remote Sensing Image Classification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00693v1)
*Barış Büyüktaş,Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedMAD框架，通过解耦全局共享表征参数与客户端特定适配参数，并引入调制感知方向性聚合策略，有效解决联邦学习中因地理差异、季节变化和成像条件不同导致的数据异构性问题，在BigEarthNet-S2和EuroSAT数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习(FL)在遥感(RS)领域面临关键挑战：当客户端间数据分布异构时（如地理差异、季节变化、成像和大气条件差异），模型性能显著下降。现有FL方法难以有效处理这种非独立同分布(Non-IID)数据问题。

Method: 提出FedMAD个性化联邦学习框架：(1) 将全局共享表征参数与客户端特定适配参数分离，通过轻量级调制模块和局部批归一化层实现；(2) 引入调制感知方向性聚合策略，根据局部调制更新的对齐程度动态调整各客户端聚合权重，抑制冲突更新，增强一致性适应行为。

Result: 在BigEarthNet-S2和EuroSAT数据集上的实验结果表明，FedMAD在异构遥感数据分布下相比现有FL算法具有更好的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过参数分离机制和智能聚合策略，FedMAD能够在保持全局可迁移表征的同时有效保留客户端特定特征，为遥感领域的个性化联邦学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has recently attracted increasing attention in remote sensing (RS) since it enables collaborative model training across decentralized RS image archives without requiring direct access to local data. However, FL performance significantly degrades when the data distributions between clients are heterogeneous, which often occurs due to geographical differences, seasonal changes, and varying image acquisition and atmospheric conditions. To address this challenge, in this letter, we propose a novel personalized FL framework (denoted as FedMAD) for RS image classification problems. The proposed framework separates globally shared representation parameters from client-specific adaptation parameters to preserve client-specific features while maintaining globally transferable representations. This is achieved by integrating lightweight modulation modules and local batch normalization layers into the backbone network. Although globally shared parameters are collaboratively optimized between clients, client-specific parameters remain local to preserve domain-specific feature characteristics. In addition, FedMAD introduces a modulation-aware directional aggregation strategy that dynamically adjusts the importance of aggregation for each client according to the alignment of local modulation updates. This allows the global optimization process to suppress conflicting client updates originating from heterogeneous data distributions while enhancing the contribution of clients with consistent adaptation behaviors. The experimental results obtained on the BigEarthNet-S2 and EuroSAT datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of FedMAD compared to state-of-the-art FL algorithms under heterogeneous RS data distributions. The code of the proposed framework will be publicly available at https://git.tu-berlin.de/rsim/fedmad.

</details>


### [21] [Personalized Image Generation with Reasoning and Reflection](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00737v1)
*Bo Ni,Ngoc N. Tran,Qinwen Ge,Franck Dernoncourt,Seunghyun Yoon,Samyadeep Basu,Sungchul Kim,Puneet Mathur,Nedim Lipka,Tong Yu,Yu Wang,Ryan A. Rossi,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于用户历史记录的个性化图像生成统一基准，包含两个任务（个性化场景生成和个性化创意生成）及多维度评估协议，并提出PEARL方法，在多个个性化指标上实现15%的平均提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化图像生成方法仅依赖精选视觉样本，未能充分捕获用户全貌。实际用户上下文更丰富，包括评论、帖子、图片、标题和元数据等历史信息。真正个性化的生成器应利用这些历史数据产生与用户生活方式和审美偏好一致的图像。

Method: 构建统一基准，包含两个互补任务：个性化场景生成（将对象置于反映用户偏好和生活方式的场景中）和个性化创意生成（生成忠实于用户审美和视觉身份的指定主题图像）。提出PEARL方法，将多模态推理器与冻结图像生成器耦合，采用交错推理-反思循环，通过差分数据奖励优化。

Result: PEARL在两个任务上均优于强基线，在个性化指标上平均提升15%。

Conclusion: 该基准和评估协议为个性化图像生成研究提供了标准化测试平台，PEARL方法验证了利用用户历史信息提升生成图像个性化程度的可行性，为电子商务个性化产品展示和社交媒体内容创作等应用场景奠定基础。

Abstract: Personalized image generation has remained narrowly focused on conditional synthesis from curated visual exemplars, rather than capturing who a user is. In practice, however, a user's personal context is much richer, comprising reviews, posts, images, captions, and metadata accumulated over time. A truly personalized generator should leverage this history to produce images aligned with the user's lifestyle and aesthetic preferences. To this end, we introduce the first unified benchmark for personalized image generation from user histories. The benchmark comprises two complementary tasks and a multi-axis evaluation protocol that assesses target fidelity, visual quality, user distinguishability, semantic alignment with the user's history, and task-specific utility. Grounded in real-world e-commerce and social media settings, the benchmark includes: (1) Personalized Scene Generation, which places a given object in a scene that reflects a user's preferences and lifestyle, motivated by personalized product presentation; and (2) Personalized Creative Generation, which generates a novel image on a specified topic that is faithful to a user's aesthetic and visual identity, motivated by social media content creation. We further propose PEARL, which couples a multimodal reasoner with a frozen image generator in an interleaved reason-reflect loop optimized with differential data reward. Across both tasks, PEARL outperforms strong baselines, achieving an average improvement of 15% across personalization metrics.

</details>


### [22] [What Builds the Scene? Luminance Dominates Geometry Formation in 3D Gaussian Splatting](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00749v1)
*Rezvan Joshaghani,Steven Cutchin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了标准3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)中亮度(luminance)和色度(chroma)对学习表征的独立贡献。通过在不同通道监督下训练模型、冻结非外观参数后重新估计外观，发现仅从亮度学习的几何与RGB训练的几何相当（仅差0.085 dB），而色度可在几何冻结后完全恢复，表明3DGS中的几何形成主要受亮度驱动但非完全排他性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS从RGB监督中联合学习几何和外观，难以隔离亮度和色度对表征的独立贡献。这种模糊性阻碍了对学习过程的深入理解和优化。

Method: 在11个基准场景上进行4组独立实验，对模型施加不同的通道监督（仅亮度、仅色度、RGB），冻结非外观参数（位置、尺度、旋转、不透明度），使用相同求解器重新估计外观，比较留出样本的重构质量。

Result: ①仅亮度学习的几何在重构质量上仅比RGB训练几何低0.085 dB；②从训练模型中删除色度后，在冻结几何上重新拟合可恢复原质量或略优；③高阶球谐函数对亮度贡献（+1.44 dB）远大于对色度贡献（+0.19 dB）；④镜面反射表面色相仍随视角变化；⑤几何形成阶段的亮度优势更大；⑥相同训练后，仅色度监督产生的几何比仅亮度监督差3.9-5.5 dB，致密化过程部分解释了该差距。

Conclusion: 3DGS中的几何形成强烈受亮度主导但非完全排他，色度外观可在空间支撑形成后大量恢复。这一发现为分离几何与外观学习、优化渲染效率提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Standard 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) learns geometry and appearance jointly from RGB supervision, making it difficult to isolate how luminance and chroma contribute to the learned representation. We study this by training models under different channel supervision, freezing their non-appearance parameters (position, scale, rotation, and opacity), and re-estimating appearance with the same solver before comparing held-out reconstruction. Across eleven benchmark scenes with four independent runs each, geometry learned from luminance alone supports held-out reconstruction 0.085 dB below RGB-trained geometry on average. If chroma is deleted from a trained model, a sufficiently expressive solver can re-fit it on the frozen geometry to the original quality or slightly better. Higher-order spherical harmonics contribute much more reconstruction quality to luminance than to chroma, improving PSNR by 1.44 dB versus 0.19 dB on average, although on mirror-like surfaces hue does still change with viewpoint. The luminance advantage is even larger when geometry is being formed. Chroma-only supervision produces geometry 3.9-5.5 dB worse than luminance-only supervision after the same appearance solve; densification explains part of this gap. Overall, geometry formation in standard 3DGS is strongly luminance-dominated but not luminance-exclusive, and much of the chromatic appearance can be recovered after spatial support has formed.

</details>


### [23] [VTV-FM: Flow Matching through Variational Terminal-Velocity Closure](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00785v1)
*Haoyang Jiang,Yuheng Li,Di Yang,Yanhai Xiong,Haipeng Chen,Yi He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTV-FM是一种二阶流匹配框架，通过最小化加速度能量来推导缺失的终端速度，从而实现更灵活的传输路径建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法使用一阶（线性）桥接路径，无法建模弯曲运动、加速度和方向变化；二阶相空间动力学虽然更灵活，但静态数据集缺乏终端速度信息。

Method: 提出VTV-FM，通过最小化加速度能量推导出闭式解来补全缺失速度；同一最小加速度变分构造同时定义最优传输配对代价和训练用加速度目标。

Result: 在低维数据集、PDE物理场和CIFAR-10上，VTV-FM在传输几何和生成质量上均优于一阶和高阶流匹配基线。

Conclusion: 通过加速度能量最小化变分框架，成功将二阶动力学引入静态数据的流匹配训练，显著提升生成效果。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) learns generative transport by fitting continuous-time motion from a simple source distribution to the data distribution. Most existing methods use first-order bridges: once a source and a target sample are paired, the path is a straight motion with constant velocity. FM with optimal transport (OT) improves the pairing, but the bridge itself remains linear, limiting its ability to model curved motion, acceleration, and changing directions. A natural remedy is to use second-order phase-space dynamics; however, learning the bridge requires target-side terminal-velocity information that static datasets do not provide. We propose Variational Terminal-Velocity Flow Matching (VTV-FM), a second-order FM framework that derives the missing velocity by minimizing acceleration energy, yielding a closed-form closure for static data. The same minimum-acceleration variational construction also defines the OT pairing cost and the acceleration targets used for training. Experiments on low-dimensional datasets, PDE-governed physical fields, and CIFAR-10 show that VTV-FM improves transport geometry and generation quality over first-order and high-order FM baselines.

</details>


### [24] [Video Generation Models: A Survey of Post-Training and Alignment](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00812v1)
*Chaoyu Li,Xiaoyi Gu,Yogesh Kulkarni,Eun Woo Im,Mohammadmahdi Honarmand,Zeyu Wang,Juntong Song,Fei Du,Xilin Jiang,Kexin Zheng,Tianzhi Li,Fei Tao,Pooyan Fazli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是第一篇全面综述视频生成模型后训练（post-training）与对齐（alignment）的论文，将现有方法分为四类：监督微调、自训练与蒸馏、偏好/奖励驱动、以及推理时方法，并讨论了数据集、基准测试及开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频生成模型虽具备强大的生成先验，但在可靠遵循人类意图、保持时间一致性、满足物理和安全约束等方面仍存在不足。与图像和文本生成相比，视频生成的对齐面临独特挑战，包括误差累积、运动-外观耦合、多目标权衡以及时间特性监督信号有限等。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的后训练框架，根据对齐信号的执行方式区分隐式对齐（implicit alignment）和显式对齐（explicit alignment），并在此视角下将现有方法组织为四类：监督微调方法、自训练和蒸馏方法、偏好和奖励驱动方法、以及推理时方法。

Result: 本文提供了视频生成后训练与对齐方法的系统性分类框架（taxonomy），梳理了数据集、基准测试和评估实践，并指出了可扩展奖励设计、长时序一致性、稳定性-表达性权衡、以及安全感知生成等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为推进可控、可靠视频生成模型的研究提供了结构化的概念基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Video generation has rapidly progressed from short, low-quality clips to high-resolution, long-duration sequences with complex spatiotemporal dynamics. Despite strong generative priors learned through large-scale pretraining, pretrained video models often fail to reliably follow human intent, maintain temporal coherence, or satisfy physical and safety constraints. Compared with image and text generation, alignment in video generation presents unique challenges, including error accumulation over time, motion-appearance coupling, multi-objective trade-offs, and limited supervision for temporal properties. These challenges motivate systematic post-training strategies that adapt pretrained models without retraining them from scratch. In this survey, we present the first comprehensive review of post-training and alignment in video generation models. We frame post-training as a unifying framework and distinguish between implicit alignment and explicit alignment based on how alignment signals are enforced. From this perspective, we organize existing approaches into four broad categories: supervised fine-tuning methods, self-training and distillation methods, preference- and reward-based methods, and inference-time methods. This taxonomy provides a coherent view of how alignment signals shape model behavior across both training and deployment. Beyond methodological advances, we review commonly used datasets, benchmarks, and evaluation practices, and discuss open challenges such as scalable reward design, long-horizon temporal consistency, stability-expressiveness trade-offs, and safety-aware generation. This survey aims to provide a structured conceptual foundation and practical guidance for advancing controllable and reliable video generation models.

</details>


### [25] [Align Then Reason: A Multimodal Lip-Sync Judge for Dubbing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00825v1)
*Rui Liu,Bhavin Jawade,Haoqi Li,Shivam Mehta,Karan Saxena,Yinghong Lan,Cameron R. Wolfe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATR是一种用于配音质量控制的无参考唇音同步评判系统，通过先建立唇部表示与音素单元的单调对齐，再利用LLM进行推理判断，在7种语言上相比基线模型平均AUC提升45-59%，并可泛化到未见过的数据集和下游任务（如配音行重排序、脚本到片段分配）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前配音质量控制缺乏有效的无参考评判方法。现有视觉语音识别器和视频-语言模型即使经过微调恢复唇动内容，对时间误差仍然不敏感，无法准确判断候选文本行是否与说话人的视觉发音在内容和时间上匹配。

Method: 提出Align Then Reason (ATR)方法，包含两个阶段：(1) 对齐阶段：建立帧级唇部表示与候选文本音素单元之间的单调对齐；(2) 推理阶段：利用LLM基于对齐得分器提供的局部证据（每个音素单元的对齐证据）和校准的全局对齐分数，联合推理内容和时间一致性。

Result: 在7种语言基准上，ATR相比Qwen3.5 SFT基线，2B/4B/9B推理器分别提升59.4%/50.2%/50.8%的平均AUC；跨LLM家族泛化，LLaMA-3.1-8B和Mistral-7B分别提升45.9%和46.6%。在下游任务中，配音行重排序比最佳唇读基线提升52.0%，脚本到片段分配提升17.7%。

Conclusion: ATR通过将单调对齐与LLM推理相结合，能够有效解决多语言唇音同步评判问题，其方法具有良好的跨数据集和跨LLM家族的泛化能力，可应用于实际配音工作流程中的质量控制。

Abstract: Dubbing quality control requires a reference-free judge that can determine whether a candidate text line matches a speaker's visible articulation in both content and timing, using only silent video and text because dubbed audio may not yet exist. Existing visual speech recognizers and video-language models are poorly suited to this setting: even when fine-tuned to recover spoken content from lip motion, they remain largely insensitive to temporal errors. We introduce $\textit{Align Then Reason}$ (ATR), a multilingual lip-sync judge that first establishes a monotonic alignment between frame-level lip representations and the phonetic units of the candidate line, then reasons over this alignment to make the final judgment. An alignment scorer provides the LLM with both local evidence for each phonetic unit and a calibrated global alignment score, enabling it to reason jointly about content and timing. On a seven-language benchmark, our method improves mean AUC over the corresponding Qwen3.5 SFT baselines by 59.4%, 50.2%, and 50.8% with 2B, 4B, and 9B reasoners, respectively. The gains generalize across LLM families, reaching mean AUC improvements of 45.9% and 46.6% over the best baseline for LLaMA-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, respectively. They also transfer across datasets to three unseen MuAViC languages. Furthermore, we evaluate on two downstream tasks built from real dubbing lines. On dub-line reranking, ATR-9B outperforms the best lip-reading baseline by 52.0%, while on script-to-clip assignment, ATR-9B improves over the best lip-reading baseline by 17.7%.

</details>


### [26] [SmoothOperator: Enhancing Representations for Fine-grained Open-set Recognition via Modulated Label Smoothing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00851v1)
*Thiru Thillai Nadarasar Bahavan,Yu Xia,Sachith Seneviratne,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmoothOperator方法，通过基于样本"显著性"动态调整标签平滑系数来改进开放集识别任务。该方法作为现有球面表示学习方法的即插即用插件，在语义偏移基准测试中实现了最多4.7%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集识别面临的核心挑战是无法在训练时建模未知类别的无限分布，导致未知样本被误分类。现有方法通过将特征空间塑造成已知类紧致且分离的结构来应对这一问题，但标签平滑对所有样本应用相同的平滑系数，忽视了不同样本在嵌入空间中的分布差异。

Method: 本文发现球面表示学习目标共享"对齐-均匀性"结构，标签仅通过对齐项进入。标签平滑因此充当对齐调节器。基于此，本文提出SmoothOperator (SmoothOP)，根据样本的"显著性"——即样本自身类别相对于最强竞争类别的突出程度——动态设置每个样本的平滑系数。显著性高的样本获得强平滑，减少对齐并放松其吸引力。

Result: SmoothOP可集成到四种现有球面表示学习方法中，训练开销极小。在语义偏移基准测试中，增强变体在数据集、语义偏移程度和开放集识别后处理器上普遍优于基线方法，AUROC、OSCR和闭集准确率提升高达4.7%。

Conclusion: 通过动态标签平滑而非固定系数，SmoothOP能更精细地平衡表示学习的对齐与均匀性，为开放集识别提供了一种简单有效的即插即用改进方案。

Abstract: Open Set Recognition (OSR) aims to enable models to accurately classify known classes while rejecting samples from unseen classes. A key challenge in OSR lies in the inability to model the unbounded distribution of unknown classes during training, often leading to the misclassification of samples from these classes. Rather than modeling unknowns, recent work shapes the feature space so that known classes are compact and well separated, and spherical representation learning methods have achieved strong results this way. Label smoothing has been identified as one of the key drivers of this success, yet it applies the same coefficient to every training sample, regardless of how well each sample is already embedded. We show that the spherical representation learning objectives used in OSR share a single alignment--uniformity structure in which labels enter only through the alignment term. Label smoothing therefore acts as an alignment dial, and a fixed coefficient sets this dial to the same value for every sample. We propose a plug-in, SmoothOperator (SmoothOP), which sets the smoothing coefficient of each sample from its \textbf{prominence}, an embedding-space signal measuring how clearly the sample's own class stands out against its strongest competing class. Our method integrates into four existing spherical representation learning methods at minimal training overhead. SmoothOP assigns strong smoothing to samples with high prominence, which reduces their alignment and relaxes their pull. On the Semantic Shift Benchmark, SmoothOP-augmented variants generally outperform their base objectives across datasets, degrees of semantic shift, and OSR post-processors, with gains of up to 4.7\% in AUROC, OSCR, and closed-set accuracy.

</details>


### [27] [Lang3DSeg: Annotation-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Segmentation with Point Transformers](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00855v1)
*Cigdem Kokenoz,Amir Salarpour,Alkim Domeke,Christopher Salas,Pedram MohajerAnsari,Long Cheng,Mert D. Pesé,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lang3DSeg提出一种基于点变换器的开放词汇3D LiDAR语义分割方法，无需人工标注即可实现室外大规模点云分割。通过类优先级合成规则和深度间隙截断策略解决2D-3D投影中的深度歧义问题。在nuScenes和SemanticKITTI上分别达到52.8%和41.4% mIoU，为无需标注的开放词汇方法中的最高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督学习LiDAR分割依赖昂贵的人工逐点标注；当前开放词汇方法主要采用体素稀疏卷积架构；点变换器仅在室内稠密有界场景中应用，室外大规模LiDAR点云分割缺乏有效解决方案。

Method: 提出Lang3DSeg框架：(1)以点变换器为骨干网络，从头训练无需几何预训练；(2)针对2D-3D投影中的深度歧误问题，采用显式类优先级规则合成掩码；(3)对每个投影实例在其深度分布的首个间断处截断，直接修正投影误差。

Result: 在nuScenes验证集达52.8% mIoU，SemanticKITTI达41.4% mIoU，为已发表的无标注方法中最高；在单帧LiDAR扫描上运行，推理实时且无需运行视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: 首次验证点变换器可作为室外LiDAR开放词汇分割的有效骨干网络，通过深度间隙截断策略有效解决了2D-3D投影的深度歧义问题，实现了无需人工标注的高性能3D语义感知。

Abstract: Accurate 3D semantic perception is critical for safe autonomous navigation. However, supervised LiDAR segmentation remains tied to closed taxonomies and to the cost of point-wise manual annotation. Open-vocabulary methods avoid that cost by projecting the output of 2D vision-language models onto LiDAR and distilling it into a 3D network. These methods rely almost exclusively on voxel-based sparse convolutions, and point transformers have so far been limited to indoor environments, where 3D data is dense and bounded. We present Lang3DSeg, which establishes a point transformer as the backbone for annotation-free open-vocabulary segmentation of outdoor 3D LiDAR, and is trained from scratch without geometric pre-training. This training paradigm necessitates addressing the inherent noise in 2D-to-3D label projections; specifically, naive projection often suffers from depth ambiguity, where points behind an object are erroneously assigned its semantic label. We therefore composite masks using an explicit class-priority rule and truncate each projected instance at the first gap in its depth distribution, correcting the projection error directly rather than averaging it over registered sequences. Lang3DSeg achieves 52.8% mIoU on nuScenes validation and 41.4% on SemanticKITTI, the highest among published annotation-free methods on both benchmarks. Every 3D semantic segmentation is on a single LiDAR sweep, and inference operates in real-time without running vision-language models.

</details>


### [28] [Machine Translation for Sign Languages](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00881v1)
*Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Anton Pelykh,Edward Fish,Harry Walsh,JianHe Low,Karahan Sahin,Oline Ranum,Sobhan Asasi,Steven Emery,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文是对手语机器翻译领域的综合性综述，涵盖了过去十年的技术演进、当前挑战及伦理考量，强调计算机视觉、手语语言学与聋人研究之间的跨学科合作的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管手语机器翻译取得了显著进展，但仍面临数据集稀缺、评估指标不足、模型难以捕捉手语的并行性、多层次性和三维结构等核心挑战。这些挑战阻碍了该领域的进一步发展，需要系统性的梳理与反思。

Method: 采用跨学科视角，整合计算机视觉、Transformer架构、大规模数据集采集等技术支持，同时融入手语语言学和聋人研究的理论框架，对识别、翻译和生成系统进行系统性分析。

Result: 梳理了从孤立手语识别到端到端翻译系统的技术演进脉络；识别出数据集局限、评估指标不完善及手语语言学特性建模困难等核心问题；提出了伦理治理、社区参与和合理使用等关键议题。

Conclusion: 手语机器翻译的持续发展依赖于计算机视觉、手语语言学与聋人研究领域的持续协作，以及与聋人社区的深度合作。技术进步必须与伦理考量并重，才能实现真正有意义的应用。

Abstract: Sign language machine translation has progressed substantially over the past decade, evolving from isolated sign recognition to end-to-end translation systems. Advances in pose estimation, transformer architectures, and large-scale dataset collection have driven progress, yet challenges remain. Datasets are limited compared to spoken-language resources; evaluation metrics inadequately capture the linguistic quality of output; and models must capture the simultaneous, multi-layered, and three-dimensional structure of sign languages. This manuscript provides a comprehensive review that seeks to balance technical challenges with stakeholder considerations. We examine the linguistic properties that make sign languages computationally unique, trace the evolution of recognition, translation, and production systems, and analyze ongoing technical challenges. Crucially, we address ethical considerations around data governance, community involvement, and appropriate use. Drawing on interdisciplinary perspectives spanning computer vision, sign language linguistics, and deaf studies, our analysis emphasizes that continued progress requires sustained collaboration across these fields and with deaf communities.

</details>


### [29] [A Matched-Budget Audit Framework for Recaptioned Image-Text Supervision Distributions](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00952v1)
*Giyeong Oh,Junghun Park,Yuhan Bae,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复用的匹配预算审计框架，用于评估重标注（recaptioned）监督分布的质量，通过五个维度的画像来审计约490M规模的多源重标注语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前重标注语料库的监督分布难以审计：长度相关代理指标无法捕捉标题风格伪影（caption-register artifacts），下游T2I基准测试又将语料库与训练选择纠缠在一起，无法实现大规模语料库级别的审计。

Method: 提出一个固定文本预算B=64的可控基本单元（CBU）审计框架，从五个轴进行画像：提示侧覆盖率、图像条件保真度和标题表面健康度。在五个公共源语料库上进行了七组配对比较实验，使用Qwen和Gemma作为评判模型。

Result: 在四个跨语料库比较中，发布的表面将每张标题的支持CBU提高了+3.39至+6.36；在CC12M上暴露了"长vs密集"的前沿边界，且在两位评判模型和四个预算下表现一致。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效审计重标注语料库的监督分布质量，发布的审计多源重标注语料库和审计产物包可用于未来研究。

Abstract: Recaptioned image-text corpora are now standard for text-to-image (T2I) training, with vision--language model (VLM) captioners replacing sparse alt-text by dense descriptions. A recaptioned corpus is a supervision distribution induced by a documented captioning policy ($π$), captioner ($V_c$), and source corpus ($C$). Length-correlated proxies miss caption-register artifacts and downstream T2I benchmarks entangle the corpus with training choices, so this distribution is hard to audit at corpus scale. We introduce a reusable matched-budget audit framework for recaptioned supervision distributions $D_{π,V_c,C}$: at a fixed text budget of $B = 64$ it reports a five-axis profile spanning prompt-side coverage, image-conditioned faithfulness, and caption-surface health, with claimed controllable basic units (CBU) as the common claim unit. We instantiate the framework on seven paired comparisons over five public source corpora. Across the four cross-corpus pairs, the released surface raises supported CBU per caption by $+3.39$ to $+6.36$ under both Qwen and Gemma Judges, and on CC12M the same framework exposes a long-vs-dense frontier that is consistent across both judges and four budgets. We release the audited multi-source recap corpus ($\approx$ 490M) together with the audit-artifact bundle.

</details>


### [30] [Two Clocks in Diffusion MLLMs: When Answers Stabilize Before Rationales Unfold](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00953v1)
*Keuntae Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中答案稳定化与理由生成的时间动态关系，发现答案稳定时理由侧仍有大量内容未生成，且提示策略的影响主要归因于覆盖率而非条件准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在掩码扩散MLLM中，答案候选可能在理由完全展开前就已稳定，但这种"回顾性稳定化"与"token承诺"之间的区别尚未被清晰定义和分析。作者希望通过区分这两个时间节点，深入理解模型生成过程中的时间动态特性。

Method: 研究者在三个视觉问答基准上进行分析，区分回顾性稳定化与token承诺，比较它们相对于理由生成的时间关系；测试不同块长度（128 vs 8）对稳定化时机的影响；在EOS启用条件下评估直接指令对不同模型（Nemotron、LaViDa）准确率的影响；进行匹配画布图像消融实验以分离视觉敏感性与答案稳定化两个时间读出。

Result: 89.4-98.1%的理由侧画布在单块、EOS抑制的LaViDa运行中稳定时仍未书写；将块长度从128降至8，该比例从89.4%降至1.7%；EOS启用下，直接指令使Nemotron在M3CoT和ScienceQA上分别提升15.0和19.5个百分点，但使LaViDa/V*Bench准确率下降11.0点；对称分解表明每次变化的较大绝对分量与覆盖率相关而非条件准确率。

Conclusion: 答案稳定化、理由展开和视觉敏感性是可区分的时间过程；提示策略差异的影响主要归因于覆盖率而非条件准确率；块长度和EOS设置显著影响答案稳定化时机和有效观察窗口。

Abstract: An answer candidate in a masked diffusion MLLM can stabilize while its rationale is still unfolding. We distinguish retrospective stabilization of the logged candidate from token commitment, and examine these two clocks relative to rationale generation. Analyzing our results across three visual question-answering benchmarks, we find that 89.4-98.1% of the rationale-side canvas remains unwritten at stabilization in single-block, EOS-suppressed LaViDa runs. On V*Bench, reducing block length from 128 to 8 changes this fraction from 89.4% to 1.7%, together with answer coverage and the eligible observation window. Under EOS-enabled prompting, direct instructions improve Nemotron's overall accuracy by 15.0 and 19.5 percentage points on M3CoT and ScienceQA, but reduce LaViDa/V*Bench accuracy by 11.0 points. A symmetric decomposition associates the larger absolute component of each change with coverage rather than conditional accuracy. Matched-canvas image ablations measure visual sensitivity alongside answer stabilization, separating the two temporal readouts. Together, these measurements distinguish answer stabilization, rationale unfolding, and visual sensitivity, and identify coverage as the larger component of the prompting differences.

</details>


### [31] [Video-Index: A Curated Meta-Benchmark for Video Understanding](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00960v1)
*Enxin Song,Yinuo Xu,Shusheng Yang,Wenhao Chai,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"攻击金字塔"框架，从5个层级对视频基准测试进行系统性审计，发现115个视频基准中存在严重的快捷方式泄露问题：35个基准在不看视频的情况下准确率接近100%，51个基准打乱帧顺序仍保持96%的准确率，63个基准至少一半问题存在近似重复。发布Video-Index数据集包含840个经过攻击验证的困难样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基准测试声称测量模型能力，但模型可能通过利用答案选项、问题文本或部分视觉线索作弊，而非真正理解视频内容。需要系统性地识别和防范这些"快捷方式"攻击。

Method: 提出攻击金字塔框架，包含5个层级的快捷方式攻击（逐步增加对题目各部分的访问权限），对115个视频基准进行系统性审计；使用代理(Agent)将评估请求转化为规格说明，通过确定性选择器和红队门控构建可复现的基准测试。

Result: 115个基准中：35个基准的"盲攻击"（不看视频）接近全视频准确率；51个含时序探针的基准打乱帧仍保持中位数96%准确率；63个基准至少50%题目存在近似重复问题。筛选出505,518个QA对进入审计池。最终发布Video-Index：210个最难验证项目×4个能力组=840项，来自76个来源。

Conclusion: Claude Opus 5比所有开源模型高37个百分点以上，Agent工具可额外提升约20个百分点，但所有系统在效率和准确性上仍有提升空间。Video-Index为视频理解能力评估提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: A video benchmark should reward the capability it claims to measure, yet models can exploit answer options, question text, or partial visual evidence. We introduce the attack pyramid, five levels of shortcut attacks with increasing access to each item, and audit 115 video benchmarks with it. On 35 benchmarks, attackers that never see a frame approach full-video accuracy. On 51 benchmarks with temporal probes, shuffled frames keep a median 96% of full-video accuracy. Near-duplicate questions make up at least half the items in 63 benchmarks. We screen 505,518 question-answer pairs from 112 of them into an audited pool. Agents turn evaluation requests into specifications, and a deterministic selector with a red-team gate composes reproducible benchmarks. We release Video-Index, the 210 hardest verified items under these attacks in each of four capability groups, 840 items from 76 sources. With the same fixed input, Claude Opus 5 outscores every open-source model by over 37 percentage points, and agent tools add about 20 more, yet all systems leave room to improve efficiency and accuracy. Blog: https://www.enxinsong.com/blog/video-index/ GitHub: https://github.com/Espere-1119-Song/Video-Index Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Video-Index/Video-Index

</details>


### [32] [Concept Driven Domain Adaptation: Finding an Abstract Needle in a Haystack](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00973v1)
*Haiming Zhao,Tai Wang,Kun Zhang,Xicheng Peng,Zhiyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出概念驱动领域适应（CDDA）框架，解决科学教育中"概念到示例视频检索"问题。该方法通过三阶段对齐策略，将抽象科学概念与纪录片视觉证据连接，在保留图像-文本匹配能力的同时，实现更强的概念级检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语言的视频时刻检索方法假设查询描述可观察事件，但科学教师需要检索的是体现科学原理的具体视觉现象。这种抽象-具体差距导致现有方法无法有效支持教学检索需求。

Method: CDDA采用三阶段框架：(1)使用教科书和教师手册的示例-概念对结构化文本嵌入空间；(2)在冻结视觉编码器下将概念感知几何迁移到纪录片视觉；(3)使用稀疏视觉概念监督联合适应双塔编码器。从几何视角，阶段化对齐减少文本-概念和视觉-概念的角度差距。

Result: 在精选的初中物理检索基准上，CDDA在概念检索和图像-文本匹配两个任务上均优于Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B等竞争性多模态基线方法。

Conclusion: CDDA通过概念作为中间语义锚点，有效弥合了抽象科学概念与具体视觉证据之间的抽象差距，为教育领域的智能视频检索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Science teachers frequently search for documentary excerpts not by describing what appears on screen, but by querying the abstract concepts they intend to teach. This use case exposes a limitation of existing language-based video moment retrieval methods, which typically assume that queries describe observable events, whereas instructional search requires retrieving concrete visual phenomena that instantiate an underlying scientific principle. We study this setting as concept-to-example video retrieval, an abstract-needle-in-a-haystack problem where compact curriculum concepts must be grounded in temporally sparse documentary evidence. To bridge this abstraction gap, we propose Concept-Driven Domain Adaptation (CDDA), a three-stage framework for adapting two-tower vision-language models to concept-level retrieval. CDDA treats concepts as intermediate semantic anchors: it first structures the textual embedding space with textbook and teacher-handbook example-concept pairs, then transfers this concept-aware geometry to documentary visuals under a frozen visual encoder, and finally jointly adapts both encoders with sparse visual concept supervision. From a geometric perspective, this staged alignment reduces text-concept and vision-concept angular gaps, thereby encouraging concept-level adaptation while preserving the pretrained model's concrete image description alignment. On a curated middle-school physics retrieval benchmark, CDDA achieves stronger pedagogically oriented concept retrieval than several competitive multimodal baselines, including Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, while maintaining concrete image-text matching after adaptation.

</details>


### [33] [VIEScore2: Unified Image Evaluation with Spatially Grounded Explanations](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00994v1)
*Xianda Du,Max Ku,Weiming Ren,Zhi Rui Tam,Chunlin Ren,Ping Nie,Min-Hung Chen,Wenhu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIEScore2是一个统一的图像生成与编辑质量评估框架，通过将图像表示为N×N网格，同时预测质量分数和缺陷位置。它支持带条件图像的任务，通过GRPO强化学习进一步优化缺陷定位，并在多个基准上超越了通用VLM和专用空间评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像评估器仅提供标量质量分数，无法定位支撑该评分的图像区域。同时，现有方法在处理生成与编辑任务时缺乏统一接口，空间监督来源异构，缺乏可验证的训练目标。

Method: 1) 将图像表示为N×N网格，文本原生格式支持异构空间监督；2) 单次前向传播同时预测质量分数和缺陷定位；3) 基于38K样本混合三种监督模式（仅分数、仅定位、联合监督）进行监督微调；4) 使用GRPO强化学习优化，结合cell级Dice重叠、分数准确性和输出格式有效性作为奖励；5) 参数无关解析器将结构化预测转为可读解释。

Result: 主基准上，总体分数SRCC达0.601，超越最强零样本通用VLM基线Gemini-3-Flash（0.491）。缺陷定位方面，六个基准中三个取得最高per-image网格IoU，五个进入前三，包括训练数据外的数据集。

Conclusion: VIEScore2通过统一架构和混合监督训练，在质量和定位双任务上均展现了有效性，文本原生网格表示为异构空间监督提供了可验证的训练接口，GRPO强化学习进一步释放了定位潜力。

Abstract: Existing synthetic image evaluators typically provide only a scalar quality score and do not identify the image regions that support it. We introduce VIEScore2, a unified evaluator for image generation and editing tasks with optional conditioning images. VIEScore2 represents an image as an N x N grid and jointly predicts quality scores and defect locations in a single model pass. Its text-native grid representation provides a common interface for heterogeneous spatial supervision and enables directly verifiable post-training objectives. We train on 38K examples spanning score-only, localization-only, and joint supervision across generation and editing tasks. Starting from supervised fine-tuning, we further apply GRPO to improve defect localization using rewards that combine cell-level Dice overlap, score accuracy, and output-format validity. A parameter-free parser converts the structured predictions into readable explanations. On the primary suite, VIEScore2 achieves an overall-score SRCC of 0.601, compared with 0.491 for Gemini-3-Flash, the strongest zero-shot general-purpose VLM baseline under matched inputs. For defect localization, VIEScore2 outperforms both general-purpose VLMs and specialized spatial evaluators on three of six benchmarks in per-image grid IoU and ranks among the top three on five, including datasets beyond its training sources.

</details>


### [34] [Watch Your Speech: Text-aware Video-to-Speech Synthesis with Textual Conditioning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01012v1)
*Gunwoo Lee,Yoori Oh,Yoseob Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WYS框架通过引入文本条件作为显式语言线索，解决视频到语音合成中的视觉歧义问题，实现高保真语音生成，在LRS2/LRS3数据集上达到音频-视觉同步的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频到语音合成任务面临"一对多"映射问题，即视觉动态信息不足以唯一确定对应的语音内容，导致生成的语音存在歧义和不确定性。

Method: 1) 提出基于注意力机制的嵌入融合模块，有效整合文本上下文与视频序列；2) 采用条件流匹配(Conditional Flow Matching)目标函数进行高保真语音生成；3) 以文本作为显式语言线索指导语音合成过程。

Result: 在LRS2和LRS3数据集上：1) 音频-视觉同步指标(LSE-C/LSE-D)达到SOTA；2) 文本准确率(WER)保持较强竞争力；3) 主观评价验证生成的语音具有接近人类自然度的音质。

Conclusion: 文本条件化方法能有效解决内容可控视频到语音合成中的视觉歧义问题，融合文本线索与视觉信息是提升合成质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Video-to-speech synthesis aims to generate natural-sounding speech from silent talking-face videos while ensuring phonetic accuracy. A fundamental challenge in this task is the inherent one-to-many mapping problem, where visual dynamics often lack sufficient information to uniquely determine the corresponding utterance. To address this, we propose Watch Your Speech (WYS), a video-to-speech synthesis framework that incorporates textual conditioning as an explicit linguistic cue to mitigate visual ambiguity. Our framework features an attention-based embedding fusion module that synergistically integrates textual context with video sequences, coupled with a conditional flow matching objective for high-fidelity speech generation. Extensive experiments on the LRS2 and LRS3 datasets demonstrate that WYS achieves superior performance, establishing new state-of-the-art results in audio-visual synchronization (LSE-C/D) while maintaining highly competitive textual accuracy (WER). Subjective evaluations further confirm that our model generates speech with near-human naturalness, validating the effectiveness of textual conditioning in content-controlled video-to-speech synthesis. Project page: https://github.com/gunwoo5034/Watch-your-Speech

</details>


### [35] [FutureWorlds: Learning Robotic World Models from Alternative Futures](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01019v1)
*Hao Wu,Shengju Qian,Weiyan Wang,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Yuanpeng He,Qingsong Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FutureWorlds框架通过多样束搜索构建候选未来，结合候选特定边界记忆和MemSPO算法将视频轨迹奖励转化为组相对优势来优化世界模型，在三个机器人数据集上显著提升长期预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型预测动作条件的未来场景存在两个核心挑战：相似候选难以提供有意义的质量比较，而差异化的轨迹需要维护各自的历史状态。

Method: 1) 基于多模态离散自回归模型，使用多样束搜索平衡置信度与多样性；2) 候选特定边界记忆机制保留场景状态，确保生成与策略评分使用匹配历史；3) MemSPO算法将视频轨迹奖励转换为组相对优势来优化世界模型。

Result: 在RT-1、BridgeV2和RoboCasa上，32帧预测的LPIPS分别降低14.78%、20.84%和9.12%；仅200次MemSPO更新即可提升生成质量并支持超越训练视野的持续预测。

Conclusion: 记忆消融、解码敏感性分析和光流评估表明，这些改进不仅体现在视觉质量上，还延伸至更准确的运动预测和更一致的对象状态预测。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict action-conditioned future scenes, providing a foundation for understanding action outcomes. However, turning alternative predictions into useful learning signals remains challenging: similar candidates limit informative quality comparisons, while diverging trajectories require persistent maintenance of their individual histories. We introduce FutureWorlds, a framework that unifies candidate construction, history maintenance, and learning from relative quality. Built on a multimodal discrete autoregressive model, FutureWorlds uses diverse beam search during reinforcement learning to construct candidate futures that balance confidence and diversity. Candidate-specific bounded memory preserves scene states and ensures that generation and policy scoring use matching histories. We further propose MemSPO (Memory-Conditioned Search-Guided Policy Optimization), which converts video trajectory rewards into group-relative advantages to optimize the world model. On RT-1, BridgeV2, and RoboCasa, FutureWorlds reduces LPIPS for 32-frame predictions by 14.78%, 20.84%, and 9.12%, respectively, relative to the strongest baseline on each dataset. Under fixed evaluation configurations, only 200 MemSPO updates further improve generation quality and support continued prediction beyond the training horizon. Memory ablations, decoding sensitivity analysis, and optical-flow evaluation show that these gains extend beyond visual quality to more accurate motion prediction and more consistent object states. Project page and code: https://github.com/Alexander-wu/FutureWorlds.

</details>


### [36] [Towards Automatic Video Annotation with ASH: Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Multi-Object Tracking and Segmentation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01022v1)
*Arash Rocky,Q. M. Jonathan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有基于记忆注意力的视频实例分割(VIS)方法内存消耗大、无法处理长视频、多类别开放词汇跟踪计算成本高的问题，提出两个创新：GPT（通用存在标记）通过虚拟提示批处理将图像编码成本从O(N)降至O(1)，ASH（标注与分割处理器）通过重叠时序分块和IoU跨块身份匹配实现任意长度视频处理。基于SAM3构建的SAM3-ASH在零样本条件下达到MOTS20最优HOTA，峰值GPU内存低于25GB，为可扩展免训练视频标注奠定实用基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆注意力VIS方法存在两个核心瓶颈：(1) 内存需求大，只能处理短视频；(2) 单提示推理设计使多类别开放词汇跟踪计算成本极高。这限制了其在任意视频全自动跟踪标注中的应用。

Method: (1) GPT：重构SAM3推理管道，通过虚拟提示批处理同时处理N个文本提示，将图像编码成本从O(N)降至O(1)，无需修改任何已学习组件；(2) ASH：扩展任意记忆注意力VIS追踪器，通过重叠时序分块和IoU跨块身份匹配处理任意长度序列，无需数据集特定训练。将ASH应用于SAM3形成SAM3-ASH pipeline。

Result: SAM3-ASH在完全零样本条件下于MOTS20达到SOTA HOTA，在7个额外基准数据集上与训练有素的专用方法竞争，峰值GPU内存保持在25GB以下。

Conclusion: 本文为可扩展、免训练的自动化视频标注建立了实用基线，证明了通过算法优化（而非增加训练数据或模型规模）提升零样本跟踪性能的可行性。

Abstract: Memory-attention-based Video Instance Segmentation (VIS) methods have demonstrated strong zero-shot tracking capability, yet their substantial memory requirements confine them to short video clips and their single-prompt inference design makes multi-category open-vocabulary tracking computationally prohibitive. This work introduces two contributions toward fully automated tracking annotation of arbitrary video. The Generalized Presence Token (GPT) reformulates SAM3's inference pipeline to process N text prompts simultaneously via virtual prompt batching, reducing image encoding cost from O(N) to O(1) with no modifications to any learned component. The Annotation and Segmentation Handler (ASH) extends any memory-attention VIS tracker to sequences of arbitrary length through overlapping temporal chunks with IoU-based inter-chunk identity matching, requiring no dataset-specific training. Instantiated on SAM3, the resulting pipeline -- SAM3-ASH -- achieves state-of-the-art HOTA on MOTS20 under fully zero-shot conditions and remains competitive with trained specialists across seven additional benchmarks, while peak GPU memory consumption stays below 25 GB, establishing a practical baseline for scalable, training-free automated video annotation.

</details>


### [37] [Bootstrapping Video Interaction Generation with Synthetic State Transitions](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01039v1)
*Jiho Jang,Jinyoung Kim,Nojun Kwak,Kyungjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个生成可控交互合成数据集的框架，通过结构化分类法和图像编辑模型创建"起始"和"结束"状态图像作为视觉锚点，并提出状态引导采样（SGS）技术来生成流畅视频，最终通过微调显著提升视频生成模型对物理交互的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型虽然能合成高保真视频，但在表现物理交互及状态转换方面存在不足，这是机器人和VR/AR应用的关键瓶颈。

Method: 1）利用结构化分类法和图像编辑模型创建明确的"起始"和"结束"状态图像作为视觉锚点；2）提出状态引导采样（SGS）技术，缓解条件生成中的伪影问题；3）开发自动化评估系统以确保数据质量。

Result: 在数据集上微调基础模型后，显著提升了生成可信交互视频的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了视频生成中物理交互可信度不足的问题，为机器人和VR/AR应用提供了高质量的训练数据。

Abstract: While recent video generative models can synthesize high-fidelity videos, they struggle to portray plausible physical interactions and the resulting state transitions, a critical bottleneck for applications in robotics and VR/AR. To address this, we introduce a framework to generate a scalable synthetic dataset of controllable interactions. Our pipeline leverages a structured taxonomy and state-of-the-art image editing models to create explicit `start' and `end' state images, which serve as visual anchors for the interaction. To generate a seamless video utilizing these anchors, we propose State-Guided Sampling (SGS), a novel sampling technique that mitigates artifacts common in naive conditional generation. Furthermore, we develop and validate a new automated evaluation system that aligns with human judgments to ensure data quality. Experiments show that fine-tuning a base model on our dataset significantly enhances its ability to generate plausible interactions.

</details>


### [38] [Ego2Act: Evaluating Goal-Directed Manipulation in Egocentric Video Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01092v1)
*Patrick Amadeus Irawan,Iskandar Muda Rizky Parlambang,Rava Maulana,Qinrong Cui,Erland Hilman Fuadi,Zayd M. K. Zuhri,Nanda Ryaas Absar,Ahmed Elshabrawy,Wilfried Ariel Mulyawan,Shoubin Yu,Yue Zhang,Mohit Bansal,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego2Act是一个用于评估视频生成模型作为物理世界模拟器的目标导向基准测试，包含来自110个真实世界任务的2,640个视频，并提出Ego2ActJudge评估pipeline。研究发现当前模型在多步骤任务执行和细粒度物理动力学方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评估基准主要关注单步动作或逐步指令，缺乏对多步骤物理推理能力的系统评估，特别是在需要规划能力的第一人称视频生成方面。这一空白阻碍了视频模型作为具身智能世界模拟器的发展。

Method: 构建Ego2Act基准：110个真实世界任务→2,640个egocentric视频，涵盖不同物体杂乱度和多步骤复杂度；提出Ego2ActJudge：无参考评估pipeline，融合任务完成度和物理合理性评估。

Result: Ego2ActJudge在任务完成度和物理合理性评估上与人类共识的一致性优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型在模拟任务执行时常跳过或部分执行步骤，导致后续依赖状态缺失而无法完成目标；在复杂物体操作和持续世界建模的细粒度物理动力学方面持续失败。Ego2Act为推动视频模型实现物理可信的目标导向模拟提供了严格的测试平台。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly being explored as world simulators for embodied planning and learning. To do so effectively, these models must not only generate visually appealing frames, but also predict how environments dynamically evolve when executing goal-directed actions. While evaluating these capabilities is crucial, existing benchmarks focus mainly on single short actions or step-by-step instructions. This leaves multi-step physical reasoning underexplored, especially in egocentric video generation that requires planning to simulate proper execution to accomplish high-level goals by carrying out multiple real-world manipulations. We introduce Ego2Act, a goal-directed benchmark featuring 2,640 videos from 110 real-world tasks across day-to-day settings, varying object clutter and multi-step complexity. Given an initial scene image and a high-level goal, Ego2Act evaluates whether video generation models can produce realistic egocentric videos of a hand manipulating objects to carry out the task. To support scalable evaluation, we also introduce Ego2ActJudge, a reference-free evaluation pipeline that achieves better task completion and physics plausibility evaluation alignment with human consensus compared to relevant baselines. Our findings reveal that models' generated simulations often skip or partially execute steps, leaving later steps missing dependent states, which leads to unfulfilled goal. Furthermore, models consistently fail at fine-grained physical dynamics, particularly during complex object manipulation and persistent world modeling. We hope Ego2Act provides a rigorous testbed for advancing video models toward physically plausible, goal-directed simulation.

</details>


### [39] [MVDG: Efficient Multi-view 3D Disambiguation on Unconstrained Real-World Images](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01098v1)
*Hanyuan Xiao,Gonglin Chen,Haolin Xiong,Wenbin Teng,Haiwei Chen,Yajie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MVDG框架，通过基于3D基础模型VGGT的多视图推理解决"幽灵匹配"(doppelgangers)问题，实现高效的多视图消歧，减少了对成对比较的依赖，在保持准确率的同时提升了SfM精度和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D重建和视觉定位中，视觉相似但实际不同的3D表面（称为"幽灵匹配/doppelgangers"）是主要障碍。现有方法依赖成对分类器，存在多视图上下文推理受限和推理复杂度O(n²)的根本性限制。

Method: 1) 基于3D基础模型VGGT构建多视图消歧框架MVDG，可对任意数量的多视图图像进行联合推理；2) 引入3D感知多视图特征，通过单次编码-解码过程处理视图，减少成对比较依赖；3) 针对直接多视图微调在噪声监督下不稳定的问题，从AerialMegaDepth构建伪成对训练集，使用采样子集微调实现稳定优化；4) 建立伪成对分类准确率与SfM指标的预测关系用于高效验证。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在保持相当成对准确率的同时，在SfM精度和推理速度上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MVDG成功实现了可扩展的多视图消歧，通过结合3D基础模型和伪成对训练策略，有效解决了幽灵匹配问题，为大规模野外3D重建和视觉定位提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Illusory matches between distinct yet visually similar 3D surfaces--doppelgangers--remain a fundamental obstacle for large-scale, in-the-wild 3D reconstruction and visual localization. Prior work mitigates this issue with pairwise classifiers, but this design limits multi-view contextual reasoning and incurs O(n^2) inference complexity for downstream structure-from-motion (SfM). We present MVDG, a scalable multi-view disambiguation framework built on the 3D foundation model VGGT, which jointly reasons over an arbitrary number of multiview images. By incorporating 3D-aware multi-view features, our method reduces dependence on pairwise comparisons by encoding and decoding views in a single pass. We further observe that direct multi-view fine-tuning of VGGT can be unstable under noisy supervision; motivated by label ambiguity in Doppelgangers, we construct a pseudo-pairwise training set from AerialMegaDepth and show that fine-tuning on sampled subsets yields stable optimization and strong generalization to held-out scenes. Finally, because full SfM evaluation (even with faster pipelines such as GLOMAP) remains expensive, we process a pseudo-pairwise dataset for efficient validation; we derive a predictive relationship between regular SfM metrics and the classification accuracy on this pseudo-pairwise test. Experiments show that our method achieves comparable pairwise accuracy while improving both SfM accuracy and inference speed over baselines.

</details>


### [40] [Open Vocabulary Word Recognition From Transcribed Bangla Texts](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01134v1)
*Faias Satter,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对手写孟加拉语文本识别问题，采用三种目标检测模型（SSD+MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN+InceptionResNetV2及两者的集成模型）进行训练测试，并引入改进的非极大值抑制算法提升性能。在9841张自定义数据集上，集成模型表现最优，F1-score达92.61%，词汇识别准确率达96.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件市场中OCR系统虽多，但针对孟加拉语的可靠解决方案严重匮乏，尤其是手写文本识别更具挑战性。词识别是OCR流程的关键阶段（前处理之后的第二阶段），其失败会导致整个系统性能下降，因此亟需研究高效的孟加拉语手写词识别方法。

Method: 研究采用三种目标检测模型进行对比：SSD结合MobileNetV2、Faster R-CNN结合InceptionResNetV2，以及两者的集成模型。为提升识别效果，引入改进的非极大值抑制（NMS）算法。实验使用包含9841张手写孟加拉语词图像的自定义数据集，涵盖不同个体的多样书写风格。

Result: 集成模型表现最优，F1-score达到92.61%，在词级别上能正确识别96.12%的词汇。三种模型中，集成模型的综合性能最佳，验证了模型融合策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究成功实现了高准确率的手写孟加拉语词识别系统，验证了深度学习方法在该任务上的可行性。未来可通过引入后处理阶段来纠正系统产生的错误，进一步提升系统性能。

Abstract: An optical character recognition (OCR) can scan a paper and extract text using technology, making people's jobs easier. While various OCR systems are available in the software industry, finding a reliable equivalent solution for Bangla takes much work. When it comes to handwritten texts, the situation is much more unusual. Recognizing words from word images is the most critical stage in any OCR process. It is the second stage after segmenting words from text pictures. If this stage fails, the overall performance of the OCR will be poor, regardless of how well the other phases perform. This study aims to recognize words using deep learning in a handwritten Bangla word image. Three object detection models, SSD with MobileNetV2, Faster R-CNN with InceptionResNetV2, and an ensemble model of these two, have been used to train and test handwritten word images. A modified Non-Maximum Suppression has been introduced to enhance the effectiveness of the models' results. A customized dataset of 9841 handwritten Bangla word images has been compiled, featuring diverse handwriting styles from various individuals. All three models' performances have been checked against the test dataset, and the ensemble model has been the most impressive, with an F1-score of 92.61%. Also, at the word level, the ensemble model correctly recognizes 96.12% of the words to some extent. The system can be further improved by introducing a post-processing phase to correct errors generated by the system.

</details>

*Maria Correia de Verdier,Rachit Saluja,Jason Sho,Maryam Vabarizad,Rennie Yung-Chieh Chen,Uyen N. T. Nguyen,Mona Alrehaili,Layal Aweidah,Deniz Bulja,Wesley C. Chan,Hernan Chaves,Madhavi Duvvuri,Huseyin Ekin Ergin,Undrakh-Erdene Erdenebold,Ekim Gumeler,Mohamed Sobhi Jabal,Chin-Chi Kuo,Fatima Mubarak,Sevde Nur Emir,Scott Riley K. Ong,Johanna Ortiz,Almudena Pérez-Lara,Andreas M. Rauschecker,Shayan Sirat Maheen Anwar,Charit Tippareddy,Tam Tran,Sorawis Visrutaratna,John Mongan,Adam E. Flanders,Robyn Ball,Greg Zaharchuk,Peter D.
