Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSNA等学会合作发布了RSNA-ICA大型公开数据集，包含7202例影像系列（CTA、MRA、MRI），来自4278例患者，涵盖21个机构、12个国家。该数据集旨在支持颅内动脉瘤AI检测算法的开发和评估，并用于2025年RSNA挑战赛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内动脉瘤破裂致残率和死亡率高，但现有检测手段对小型病灶及常规非血管成像的检测仍具挑战性，需要大规模、高质量的标注数据集来推动AI算法开发。

Method: RSNA联合ASNR、SNIS、ESNR共同策划，从12个国家21个机构的4278例成人患者中收集7202例影像系列（2566 CTA、2166 MRA、2470 MRI），由专家进行动脉瘤存在性和位置标注，其中178例附加3D分割标注。

Result: 建立了具有显著地理和影像多样性的大规模公开数据集，其中5041例通过MIRA平台公开可用，用于2025年RSNA颅内动脉瘤检测挑战赛。

Conclusion: RSNA-ICA为AI驱动颅内动脉瘤检测提供了全面资源，适用于血管造影和常规神经影像检查的算法开发与验证。

Abstract: Intracranial aneurysm rupture is associated with substantial morbidity and mortality, yet aneurysm detection remains challenging, particularly for small lesions and on routine non-angiographic imaging examinations. To support the development and evaluation of artificial intelligence (AI) algorithms for intracranial aneurysm detection and localization, the Radiological Society of North America (RSNA), in collaboration with the American Society of Neuroradiology (ASNR), the Society of Neurointerventional Surgery (SNIS), and the European Society of Neuroradiology (ESNR), curated the RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm (RSNA-ICA) Dataset. Developed for the 2025 RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm Detection Challenge, RSNA-ICA is a large, publicly available, expert-annotated dataset comprising 7202 CTA, MRA, and MRI series from 4278 adult patients collected across 21 institutions in 12 countries spanning five continents. The dataset includes 2566 CTA, 2166 MRA, and 2470 MRI series from patients with and without intracranial saccular aneurysms, providing substantial geographic and imaging diversity. Expert annotations indicate both aneurysm presence and location, and 178 series additionally include three-dimensional segmentations of challenge-defined vascular locations. RSNA-ICA was used to develop and evaluate algorithms in the 2025 RSNA Intracranial Aneurysm Detection Challenge. Of the 7202 image series, 5041 are publicly available through MIRA (https://mira.rsna.org/dataset/7), while the remainder were used for challenge public and private test sets. The dataset is freely available to the research community for noncommercial use and provides a comprehensive resource for advancing AI-based aneurysm detection across both angiographic and routine neuroimaging examinations.

</details>


### [42] [PhysicsLENS: Diagnosing Physical Property Blindness in Video Generation Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01162v1)
*Isaiah Milkey,Som Sagar,Aditya Taparia,Xinyuan Liu,Jiqing Wen,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysicsLENS是一个评估视频生成模型物理合理性的数据集和基准测试，通过匹配条件帧和任务相同但物理属性不同的场景对，评估模型在碰撞、重力、动量、摩擦、形变、流体和因果关系七个物理领域的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成模型可能产生违反物理原理的机器人视频，仅通过视觉保真度评估，无法捕捉隐藏的物理属性（如重量、粘度、摩擦力），限制了其在机器人学习和规划中的可靠性。

Method: PhysicsLENS从公开机器人视频源收集场景，创建条件帧和任务相同但底层物理属性变化的匹配场景对，涵盖七个物理领域：碰撞、重力、动量、摩擦、形变、流体和因果关系。

Result: 对4个视频生成模型进行评估，产生超过400个人类注释标签。结果显示，看似合理的视频中有34/47忽视了所声明的物理属性，且明确声明物理属性仅略微（而非显著）降低视频的合理性。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型在遵循物理约束方面存在显著不足，即使明确说明物理属性，模型仍难以生成物理上合理的视频，需要改进视频世界模型以更好地理解和应用物理规律。

Abstract: Reliable video world models could provide scalable predictive environments for robot learning, planning, and evaluation. However, generated robot videos can violate physical principles and complete tasks through physically implausible behavior, limiting their reliability for robot learning and planning. Current video-generation benchmarks exclude physics that are inherently hidden by visuals (e.g., weight, viscosity, friction). Due to this, video models are evaluated on the fidelity of physics, not the underlying accuracy of physics. We introduce PhysicsLENS, a dataset and benchmark for evaluating plausibility of physical properties grounded in robotics. PhysicsLENS uses matched scenario pairs that hold the same conditioning frame and task, while varying underlying physics in the scene description. Scenarios are curated from public robot video sources and annotated across seven physical domains: collision, gravity, momentum, friction, deformation, fluid, and causality. We evaluate across four video generation models, producing over 400 human-annotated labels. Results show that plausible-looking videos often ignore the stated property (34 of 47), and that stating the property lowers plausibility only slightly and not significantly.

</details>


### [43] [CineMR: Tool-Integrated Vision-Language Reasoning for Quantitative Cardiac MRI Assessment](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01166v1)
*Kunyang Li,Hai Nguyen,Joshua Lowe,Chenguang Zhao,Peace C. Madueme,Mehdi Hedjazi Moghari,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yuzhang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineMR是一个工具增强的视觉-语言模型，用于心血管磁共振（CMR）的定量评估。它通过调用心脏图像分析工具并整合其输出，实现对心室容积、射血分数、心肌质量、壁厚度和区域壁运动等定量指标的准确测量。在多队列cine CMR基准测试中，CineMR达到35.9%的pass@1和58.9%的pass@4，显著优于Qwen3-VL-8B（1.5%）、LLaVA-Med v1.5（0.0%）和MedGemma-4B（7.0%）。工具调用正确率从SFT后的78.9%提升至GRPO后的99.8%，现场工具输出使心室测量精度提高20.4-23.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学视觉-语言模型（VLMs）无法在没有分析工具的情况下从多维cine图像中可靠地获取定量测量值，而CMR（包括cine成像）是评估心脏形态和心室功能的参考标准，需要定性与定量评估相结合。

Method: 1. 构建CineMR工具增强型VLM，调用心脏图像分析工具并整合其输出；2. 构建多队列VQA基准测试，涵盖定量指标提取、多分类诊断和鉴别诊断；3. 使用监督微调（SFT）训练工具交互轨迹，后接基于条件工具使用奖励的群体相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: CineMR在多队列cine CMR基准上达到35.9% pass@1和58.9% pass@4；工具调用正确率从SFT后的78.9%提升至GRPO后的99.8%；现场工具输出使心室测量精度提高20.4-23.7%；移除所有工具后pass@1从35.9%降至27.9%。

Conclusion: 可靠的工具使用对定量cine CMR推理至关重要，CineMR可作为辅助心脏图像评估的有前景方法。

Abstract: Cardiovascular magnetic resonance (CMR), including cine imaging, is a reference standard for the noninvasive assessment of cardiac morphology and ventricular function. Cine CMR interpretation integrates qualitative visual assessment with quantitative measurements of ventricular volumes, ejection fraction, myocardial mass, wall thickness, and regional wall motion. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) cannot reliably derive quantitative measurements from multidimensional cine images without analysis tools. We present CineMR, a tool-augmented VLM that invokes cardiac image-analysis tools and integrates their outputs into interleaved reasoning for quantitative CMR assessment. We also construct a multi-cohort visual question answering benchmark covering quantitative metric extraction, multiclass diagnosis, and differential diagnosis, together with tools for segmentation, phase selection, volumetry, morphometry, and regional wall motion analysis. CineMR is trained with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on tool-interaction traces followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with conditional tool-use rewards. On the multi-cohort cine CMR benchmark, CineMR achieves 35.9% pass@1 and 58.9% pass@4, compared with 1.5% pass@1 for the Qwen3-VL-8B backbone and 0.0% and 7.0% pass@1 for LLaVA-Med v1.5 and MedGemma-4B, respectively. Correct tool invocation reaches 99.8% after GRPO, up from 78.9% after SFT. Live tool outputs improve ventricular measurement accuracy by 20.4--23.7% over direct model predictions, and removing all tools reduces pass@1 from 35.9% to 27.9%. These results highlight the importance of reliable tool use for quantitative cine CMR reasoning and support CineMR as a promising approach for assistive cardiac image assessment. Code, benchmark resources, and model weights are available at https://github.com/AI-MIND-Lab/CineMR.

</details>


### [44] [Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting: Training-Free Negation Understanding for Vision-Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01180v1)
*Yuliang Cai,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSP 方法，通过骨架与策略提示（Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting）提升视觉语言模型处理否定句的能力，无需参数更新即可在多个 VQA 基准测试上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）在视觉问答（VQA）任务中表现优异，但在处理包含否定从句的问题时存在明显缺陷，容易产生错误答案，这限制了模型的实际应用能力。

Method: SSP 是一种免训练的上下文学习方法：首先将否定问题抽象为"骨架"结构；然后从轻量级问题池中检索同骨架的少量问题示例；接着提示 VLM 分析其共享的否定模式并合成单句回答策略；最后将骨架与策略作为提示前置，帮助模型正确处理测试样本。

Result: 在多个否定 VQA 基准测试上的实验表明，SSP 在保持计算效率的同时，显著提升了模型处理否定问题的能力，达到了该任务的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过引入骨架抽象与策略合成的免训练提示方法，SSP 可在不更新参数的前提下有效解决 VLM 的否定理解难题，为提升多模态模型的细粒度语义理解能力提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Despite the strong performance of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on a wide range of visual question answering (VQA) tasks, these models consistently struggle to understand negation and produce incorrect answers when questions involve negated clauses. To address this limitation, we propose Skeleton-and-Strategy Prompting (\textbf{SSP}), a training-free, in-context learning method that improves VLM negation understanding capabilities without any parameter updates. Given a negation question, our method first abstracts the underlying question structure into a skeleton, retrieves a small set of same-skeleton questions from a lightweight question pool, then prompts the VLM to analyze their shared negation pattern and synthesize a single-sentence answering strategy. The skeleton and strategy are prepended to the test sample to guide the model correctly tackle the negation problems. Experiments on multiple negation VQA benchmarks show that SSP achieves state-of-the-art performance on negation-focused VQA tasks while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [45] [Color Independent Word Segmentation From Transcribed Bangla Passages](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01191v1)
*Faias Satter,Noor Masrur,Sk. Md. Masudul Ahsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对手写孟加拉语文本的单词分割问题，提出了一种适用于智能手机拍摄图像的OCR预处理方案。通过创建包含多种干扰因素的自定义数据集，在7278个包围盒样本上达到了91.20%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前软件市场中缺乏可靠的手写孟加拉文OCR解决方案。单词分割是OCR系统的第一步关键工序，分割失败将导致整个OCR系统性能下降，因此需要针对手写孟加拉文开发专门的单词分割方法。

Method: 研究采用自定义数据集构建方法，针对智能手机拍摄图像可能产生的各种干扰（如阴影、不同纸张颜色和墨水类型）进行模拟。数据集包含7374个单词图像，共生成7278个包围盒（bounding boxes），通过自适应阈值处理和膨胀滤波器进行图像预处理与分割。

Result: 单词分割任务达到：召回率90.60%，精确率91.80%，F1分数91.20%。

Conclusion: 系统性能可通过以下方式进一步提升：对包含多个单词的包围盒进行嵌套操作处理，以及将自适应阈值和膨胀滤波器尺寸调整到更精确的水平。该方法可为后续孟加拉文OCR系统提供可靠的低层预处理支撑。

Abstract: An optical character recognition(OCR) system can scan paper and extract text, making people's jobs easier. While numerous OCR systems are accessible in the software sector, finding a dependable equivalent solution for Bangla is tough. When it comes to handwritten texts, the case is even more rare. The first fundamental step to any OCR is to segment words from text images. If this stage fails, the total OCR's performance will be poor no matter how promising the later stages perform. This research aims to segment words in a handwritten Bangla text image. This research can be implemented on any smartphone-captured image, irrespective of the color and type of paper and ink. Furthermore, as smartphone-captured images can create shadow interferences, the custom dataset built for this research is created in such a way that every possible obstacle that can be faced is included. For 7374 words, a total of 7278 bounding boxes are generated, which have recall of 90.60 %, precision of 91.80 %, and F1-score of 91.20 %. The system can be further improved with nested operations on bounding boxes containing several words or by adjusting the adaptive thresholding and dilation filter sizes to a more precise level.

</details>


### [46] [Resolving Mixed Single-Photon LiDAR Returns for Foreground-View and Hidden Scene Reconstruction](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01206v1)
*Ziting Wen,Runrong Deng,Zili Zhang,Haitao Zheng,Yuecong Xu,Xiaoqiang Ren,Guodong Shi,Kemi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种状态感知框架，利用单光子LiDAR实现穿透半透明遮挡物的隐藏场景重建。核心创新在于：识别每条光线的回波状态（无表面、单回波或双回波），并据此为双头神经场分配不同的监督策略。实验表明该方法在真实数据集上显著提升了隐藏场景的深度和点云精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分透光屏幕会产生混合LiDAR回波，传统峰值检测方法丢弃弱隐藏返回，而现有瞬态重建方法仅拟合单一场景表示。当弱回波形成宽峰或微弱肩峰时，简单的瞬态拟合无法正确恢复几何结构。

Method: 提出状态感知框架：首先估计每条光线的局部回波证据（无可靠表面/单回波/双回波），然后根据推断的回波状态为双头神经场分配监督——所有光线约束波形重建，而可靠锚点提供几何定位。此外构建了真实配对单光子LiDAR遮挡数据集。

Result: 在真实数据集上，隐藏场景深度和点云精度均优于基线方法，证明了单光子分层重建作为穿透半透明遮挡物实现3D感知的实用路径。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现了从单光子直方图中同时重建前景视图和遮挡后的隐藏场景，为机器人检测应用提供了在部分透光屏幕环境下感知被遮挡区域的能力。

Abstract: Partially transmissive screens and protective covers are common in robotic inspection, but they create mixed LiDAR returns from both the foreground material and the scene behind it. Conventional peak-based LiDAR usually discards weak hidden returns, while single-photon LiDAR records time-resolved histograms that preserve attenuated and overlapping echoes. However, existing transient reconstruction methods typically fit a single scene representation to the measured waveform. Under occlusion, weak or nearby foreground--hidden echoes can form a broad peak or subtle shoulder. Because such waveforms can also be explained by a displaced single surface or a thick density distribution, accurate transient fitting does not necessarily imply correct geometry. We propose a state-aware framework for foreground-view and hidden scene reconstruction from occluded single-photon histograms. For each ray, we estimate local echo evidence, identifying no reliable surface evidence, single-return evidence, or two returns. The inferred echo state routes supervision for a two-head neural field: all rays constrain waveform reconstruction, while reliable anchors provide geometry localization. We also introduce a real paired single-photon LiDAR occlusion dataset with occluded and clean captures at fixed poses. Experiments on a real dataset show improved hidden scene depth and point-cloud accuracy over baselines. Our results demonstrate single-photon layered reconstruction as a practical route for 3D perception through partially transmissive occluders.

</details>


### [47] [Dyna3: VLM-Guided Training-Free 4D Reconstruction via Depth Foundation Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01286v1)
*Xinhao Xiang,Weiyang Li,Zhijie Zheng,Abhijeet Rastogi,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dyna3是一个无需训练的框架，将深度基础模型DA3扩展到4D动态场景重建。其核心发现是DA3的跨视图特征虽然仅训练用于深度一致性，但结合最佳匹配特征搜索后可隐式编码运动判别信号。静态表面找到全局一致匹配，动态物体则无法。通过VLM自动生成语义提示驱动SAM 3实现实例级分割，将场景解耦为跨帧对齐的静态背景和逐帧动态点云。在四个数据集上超越对应关系训练方法，动态目标分割J-Mean提升5.5pp，姿态估计提速13倍，4D重建提速3倍，内存降低4-8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度基础模型（如DA3）假设3D场景静态，难以应用于真实世界的动态环境。现有的免训练4D方法（如Easi3R、VGGT4D）依赖对应关系训练的骨干网络，而深度专用模型缺乏这种跨帧匹配特性，需要开发新方法充分利用深度基础模型的能力进行动态场景重建。

Method: 1. 利用DA3跨视图特征的隐式运动判别能力（静态表面全局一致匹配，动态物体无法匹配）
2. 采用VLM自动生成场景特定语义提示，驱动SAM 3进行精确实例级分割
3. 将场景解耦为跨帧对齐的静态背景和逐帧动态点云
4. 完全免训练，无需微调

Result: 在四个数据集上验证：动态目标分割J-Mean较SOTA的VGGT4D提升5.5个百分点；姿态估计速度提升13倍；4D重建速度提升3倍；内存消耗降低4-8倍；支持更高的时间采样密度。

Conclusion: Dyna3成功将仅用于深度估计的基础模型扩展到4D动态场景重建，无需任何训练即可超越对应关系训练方法。该工作表明深度基础模型的跨视图特征隐含运动信息，结合VLM驱动的分割可有效区分静态与动态物体，为动态场景理解提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent depth foundation models like Depth Anything 3 (DA3) achieve remarkable multi-view depth estimation but assume static 3D scenes, limiting their applicability to real-world dynamic environments. Existing training-free 4D methods like Easi3R and VGGT4D rely on correspondence-trained backbones whose attention encodes cross-frame matching, a property absent in depth-only models like DA3. We present Dyna3, a training-free framework that extends DA3 for 4D dynamic scene reconstruction without any fine-tuning. Our key insight is that DA3's cross-view features, though trained only for depth consistency, implicitly encode motion-discriminative signals when combined with best-match feature search across frames. Its static surfaces find consistent matches globally, while dynamic objects cannot. We further adopt vision-language models (VLM) to automatically generate scene-specific semantic prompts for SAM 3, enabling precise instance-level segmentation that distinguishes which objects move from what objects exist. For reconstruction, we decouple the scene into a cross-frame aligned static background and per-frame dynamic point clouds. Experiments on four datasets demonstrate that Dyna3 surpasses correspondence-trained methods with +5.5pp J-Mean over state-of-the-art VGGT4D on dynamic object segmentation, while achieving up to 13x faster pose estimation and 3x faster 4D reconstruction with 4 to 8x lower memory. Dyna3 could therefore enable much denser temporal sampling that prior methods cannot support.

</details>


### [48] [ODDR: One-Step Deshadow Diffusion via Reward Guidance](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01291v1)
*Junseong Shin,Kijun Kim,Minseong Kim,Dongjin Kim,Tae Hyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ODDR框架，利用无需人工标注的奖励模型ShadowReward引导微调，实现高效单步去阴影，弥合合成数据与真实场景的域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习去阴影方法依赖成本高昂的真实世界配对数据集，场景多样性受限，泛化能力不足。

Method: 1) 基于合成阴影数据训练One-step Deshadow Diffusion (ODD)基线模型，实现单步无阴影重建；2) 提出ShadowReward奖励模型，通过对具有受控退化（纹理失真、边界伪影）的合成图像排序来学习人类感知判断，无需人工标注；3) 使用ShadowReward引导的微调将ODD适配为ODDR，弥合合成到真实的域差距。

Result: ODD在无真实世界配对监督下表现优异，ODDR进一步提升性能，缩小了与全监督方法的差距，同时保持单步模型的高计算效率。

Conclusion: ODDR成功实现高效高保真去阴影，ShadowReward为首个完全无需人工标注的阴影去除奖励模型，为合成数据训练与域自适应提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning for shadow removal have significantly enhanced image quality and realism. However, most approaches rely on real-world paired datasets, which are costly to collect and often limited in scene diversity, leading to limited generalization. To address these limitations, we propose One-step Deshadow Diffusion via Reward guidance (ODDR), a new framework that achieves efficient and high-fidelity shadow removal without relying on real-world paired supervision. Our method begins with One-step Deshadow Diffusion (ODD), a baseline model trained on synthetic shadow data for efficient one-step shadow-free reconstruction. We further adapt ODD into ODDR using ShadowReward. In contrast to traditional, annotation-heavy approaches, ShadowReward is the first reward model for shadow removal trained entirely without human annotation. It learns to mimic human perceptual judgments by ranking synthetically generated images with controlled degradations, such as texture distortion and boundary artifacts. This reward-guided fine-tuning enables ODDR to close the synthetic-to-real domain gap. Extensive experiments show that ODD achieves strong performance without relying on real-world paired supervision, and ODDR further improves the results, narrowing the gap to fully supervised methods trained on real-world paired data while maintaining higher computational efficiency as a single-step model.

</details>


### [49] [ARROW: Arbitrary Reconstruction and Tracking of 4D Observations in the Wild](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01314v1)
*Ilya Fradlin,Christian Schmidt,Jens Piekenbrinck,Karim Knaebel,Gonzalo Martin Garcia,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARROW是一个前馈模型，能够从任意图像集进行3D重建和3D点跟踪。其核心创新是提出了一种顺序无关的查询方法，可以跨任意输入关联查询与观测。在多个数据集上取得了最先进的3D跟踪性能，并在3D重建任务上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态场景可通过移动相机、多路视频流或不同时刻拍摄的图像进行捕获。这些观测揭示了场景几何和运动的互补方面，但将它们结合需要建立跨视角、跨时间和可见性变化的对应关系。

Method: 提出ARROW模型，核心是顺序无关的查询方法(order-invariant querying)，允许跨任意输入关联查询与观测。通过在训练时暴露模型于更多样化的输入集来提升任务性能。

Result: ARROW在WorldTrack和TAPVid-3D上建立了新的3D跟踪技术state of the art，在适配的RGB-only MVTracker基准上优于专用多视图跟踪器，同时在3D重建任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练策略使ARROW能够泛化到更广泛的任务范围，包括多视图跟踪。代码和权重已公开可用。

Abstract: Dynamic scenes may be captured by a moving camera, multiple video streams, or images taken at different times. These observations reveal complementary aspects of scene geometry and motion, yet bringing them together requires establishing correspondence across viewpoints, capture times, and visibility changes. We introduce ARROW, a feed-forward model that unifies 3D reconstruction and 3D point tracking from arbitrary image sets. At its core is a novel order-invariant querying approach, which allows the association of queries with observations across arbitrary inputs. We show that exposing the model to more diverse sets of inputs during training results in improved task performance. Moreover, the resulting model is capable of generalization to a wider range of tasks including multi-view tracking. Trained with this strategy, ARROW establishes a new state of the art in 3D tracking on WorldTrack and TAPVid-3D and outperforms dedicated multi-view trackers on an adapted RGB-only MVTracker benchmark, while remaining competitive across 3D reconstruction tasks. Code and weights are publicly available.

</details>


### [50] [MMVistaReason: Toward Open-Data and Post-Training Recipes for Multimodal Reasoning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01352v1)
*Juekai Lin,Honglin Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Xiaolong Wu,Jie Cao,Liang Liang,Yunqi Cao,Yun Zhu,Wenqiao Zhang,Lijun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMVistaReason (MVR) 是一种开放数据后训练方案，通过三个核心组件解决多模态推理模型训练中的数据质量不均、监督构建效率低下、难度不平衡及跨领域干扰等问题。该方案在15个多模态基准上取得优异成绩，MVR-4B平均得分72.8，MVR-9B平均得分74.4，验证了系统性开放数据构建和容量感知后训练的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放多模态推理模型的后训练面临四大挑战：(1) 数据质量不均，(2) 监督构建效率低下，(3) 难度不平衡，(4) 跨领域干扰。这些问题导致模型难以获得可靠的能力提升。

Method: 提出三组件方案：(1) 能力覆盖扩展，涵盖分析推理组和真实世界推理组；(2) 高效SFT和RL数据构建，通过阶段性清洗标准化异构数据，结合难度感知级联教师蒸馏与答案似然轨迹选择构建MVR-SFT-528K，应用规模特定前沿过滤构建MVR-RL-63K；(3) 专业-集成训练，先训练互补RL专家，再通过多教师在线策略蒸馏(MOPD)整合能力。

Result: 核心发现：监督难度、轨迹质量与模型容量存在容量依赖性交互——小模型更受益于精选监督，大模型对轨迹变化和混合领域干扰更鲁棒。混合领域RL引入基准级负迁移，MOPD实现一致的能力整合且KL方向随模型规模变化。性能：MVR-4B在15个基准上平均72.8分，超越Qwen3.5-9B和MMFineReason-8B；MVR-9B达74.4分，超越Qwen3.5-35B-A3B。

Conclusion: 系统性开放数据构建与容量感知后训练为实现可靠多模态推理提供了实用且可扩展的路径。MOPD能有效整合互补专家能力，为多模态模型训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Open multimodal reasoning models have benefited from large-scale reasoning supervision, yet reliable post-training remains challenging due to uneven data quality, inefficient supervision construction, imbalanced difficulty, and cross-domain interference. We introduce MMVistaReason (MVR), an open-data post-training recipe with three components: (1) broader capability coverage across complementary Analytical and Real-World reasoning groups, emphasizing structured reasoning versus visual perception and spatial grounding; (2) efficient SFT and RL data construction, standardizing heterogeneous open data through staged cleaning and annotation, combining difficulty-aware cascaded teacher distillation with answer-likelihood-based trajectory selection to construct MVR-SFT-528K, and applying scale-specific frontier filtering for MVR-RL-63K; and (3) specialize-then-integrate training, which trains complementary RL experts and consolidates their capabilities through multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). Our analyses reveal a capacity-dependent interaction between supervision difficulty, trajectory quality, and model capacity: smaller students benefit more from selected supervision, while larger students are robust to trajectory variation and mixed-domain interference. Mixed-domain RL introduces benchmark-level negative transfer, whereas MOPD provides consistent capability integration, with the preferred KL direction varying across model scales. Across 15 multimodal benchmarks, MVR-4B achieves an average score of 72.8, outperforming Qwen3.5-9B (Instruct) and MMFineReason-8B while using about 70% fewer samples than MMFineReason. Scaling to 9B improves the average to 74.4, surpassing Qwen3.5-35B-A3B (Instruct). Overall, MMVistaReason demonstrates that systematic open-data construction and capacity-aware post-training provide a practical and scalable path toward reliable multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [51] [Supervising Sound Localization by In-the-wild Egomotion](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01388v1)
*Anna Min,Ziyang Chen,Hang Zhao,Andrew Owens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出利用自我运动(egomotion)作为监督信号来学习双耳声音定位的方法，通过让音频模型预测与视觉估算的相机运动一致的声音方向来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双耳定位方法依赖物理声学原理，而本文探索利用视觉-听觉跨模态监督学习声音定位，实现从真实世界数据中自动学习。

Method: 利用多视图几何的传统方法获取相机运动估计，以此作为监督信号训练音频模型预测声音方向，并将该监督信号与传统双耳线索结合。

Result: 模型能成功从真实世界数据中学习，在声音定位任务上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了egomotion监督信号的有效性，为双耳声音定位提供了一种弱监督但数据丰富的学习范式。

Abstract: We present a method for learning binaural sound localization using egomotion as a supervisory signal. Over the course of a video, the cameras direction to a sound source will change as the camera moves. We train an audio model to predict sound directions that are consistent with visual estimates of camera motion, which we obtain using traditional methods from multi-view geometry. This provides a weak but plentiful form of supervision that we combine with traditional binaural cues. To evaluate this method, we propose a dataset of real-world audio-visual videos with egomotion. We show that our model can successfully learn from real-world data and that it performs well on sound localization tasks

</details>


### [52] [MWOP: Modality-aware Width-wise Operation Pruning for Efficient MLLMs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01434v1)
*Xudong Wang,Hao Wu,Haozhe Hu,Peiran Yin,Xinghao Chen,Yunpu Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MWOP方法，通过细粒度的模态感知宽度剪枝优化多模态大语言模型推理效率。该方法分别在注意力路径（V2V/T2V/T2T）和FFN通道层面独立剪枝视觉和文本执行，并配合Triton内核优化实现实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作压缩方法将注意力头和FFN通道视为统一计算单元，未能充分利用模态间冗余差异。视觉和文本模态在同一计算单元上的冗余程度不同，需要更细粒度的剪枝策略。

Method: 1. **模态感知剪枝**: 在每个注意力层独立剪枝V2V、T2V、T2T三种路径；分别选择视觉和文本输入的FFN通道
2. **重要性评估**: 使用一阶Taylor准则指导剪枝
3. **动态调整**: 注意力剪枝后重新评估FFN重要性
4. **恢复训练**: 采用LoRA进行恢复训练
5. **核优化**: 开发path-sparse Triton注意力核和紧凑视觉侧FFN执行

Result: LLaVA-OneVision-7B上实现1.6×预填充加速，12基准平均保持99.7%性能；与token压缩结合后，加速比从2.0×/1.9×提升至2.9×/2.7×。Qwen2.5-VL-7B验证了跨架构适用性。

Conclusion: MWOP通过保留token序列同时减少每token计算量，与token压缩方法正交可叠加，实现序列长度和per-token计算的双重压缩。细粒度模态冗余利用是提升MLLM推理效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial inference costs when processing long visual-textual sequences. While existing operation compression methods exploit modality-level redundancy, they largely treat computation within attention heads and shared feed-forward network (FFN) channels as unified units, leaving finer-grained redundancy underexplored. We find that redundancy varies both across modality-interaction paths within the same attention head and across visual and textual executions of the same FFN channel. Based on these findings, we propose Modality-aware Width-wise Operation Pruning (MWOP), which independently prunes visual-to-visual (V2V), text-to-visual (T2V), and text-to-text (T2T) attention paths within each layer, and separately selects FFN channels for visual and textual inputs. A first-order Taylor criterion guides the pruning process, with FFN importance re-evaluated after attention pruning and LoRA-based recovery training. To translate the resulting fine-grained sparsity into practical acceleration, we further develop path-sparse Triton attention kernels and compact visual-side FFN execution. MWOP preserves the token sequence while reducing attention and FFN computation, making it complementary to token compression and enabling simultaneous reduction of sequence length and per-token computation. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, MWOP alone achieves a $1.6\times$ prefill speedup with 99.7\% average performance retention across 12 benchmarks. Combined with two representative token compression methods, it further increases their prefill speedups from $2.0\times$ and $1.9\times$ to $2.9\times$ and $2.7\times$, respectively. Results on Qwen2.5-VL-7B further demonstrate its applicability across architectures. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/MWOP.

</details>


### [53] [The Impact of Processing Parameters on High-Accuracy Measurements in UAV Photogrammetry](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01438v1)
*Paweł Ćwiąkała,Edyta Puniach,Elżbieta Pastucha,Wojciech Gruszczyński*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了无人机摄影测量处理流程中8个关键参数对三维精度的影响，基于220公顷研究区1.5年内采集的10个数据集、768个处理变体进行全因子实验。研究发现精度存在16-303 mm的显著差异，关键影响因素为地面控制点数量、相机校准校正和PPK-GNSS方法的使用。经工作流优化后，系统性误差减少超过50%，为高精度监测应用提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机摄影测量在滑坡、采矿、微地形变化等高精度应用中的需求日益增长，但现有研究多聚焦于数据采集策略，而对处理工作流——特别是束束平差参数设置——的影响缺乏系统性研究。处理参数的微小差异可能导致精度数量级变化，这直接关系到监测结果的可靠性。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，系统评估768种处理变体。研究覆盖10个无人机数据集（1.5年跨度、220公顷区域），分析8个关键参数的影响。评估指标包括三维精度（RMSE）、位移误差、倾斜变化及水平应变计算精度。采用控制变量法对比优化配置与作者原基准配置的差异。

Result: 最优变体RMSE达16 mm，最弱变体为303 mm，相差近19倍。决定性因素包括：地面控制点数量、额外相机校准校正、PPK-GNSS方法的应用。随机位移误差保持稳定（RMSE约6-7 mm），但系统性误差显著降低——垂直中值绝对误差从14 mm降至7 mm，降幅超过50%。

Conclusion: 研究首次大规模、系统性地揭示了处理参数选择对摄影测量产品精度及形变指标确定的显著影响。最优参数组合（充足GCPs + 相机校准校正 + PPK-GNSS）可实现误差减半的优化效果。研究为高精度无人机摄影测量监测工作流的制定提供了可操作的实践指南，强调了处理流程标准化和参数优化的重要性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) photogrammetry is increasingly used in applications requiring high accuracy, such as determining ground surface changes caused by landslides, mining, or microrelief transformation. While acquisition strategies have been widely studied, the influence of the processing workflow-particularly Bundle Block Adjustment parameter settings-remains insufficiently explored. This study addresses this gap through a systematic, full-factorial evaluation of 768 processing variants applied to ten UAV datasets collected over 1.5 years in a 220 ha study area. Eight key parameters were analysed. The results show substantial variability in final 3D accuracy: the best performing variant achieved a root mean square error (RMSE) of 16 mm, whereas the weakest reached 303 mm. The most influential factors were the number of ground control points, the application of additional camera calibration corrections, and the use of the Post-Processing Kinematic GNSS method for determining camera projection center coordinates. The study also evaluates how workflow optimization affects the accuracy of displacement, tilt changes, and horizontal strain determination. While random displacement errors remained stable (RMSE of ~6-7 mm), systematic errors were significantly reduced by over half in all axes, with vertical median absolute error decreasing from 14 mm to 7 mm in the optimized configuration compared to the baseline previously used by the authors. This study provides the first large-scale, practice-oriented assessment of how processing parameter selection shapes the accuracy of both photogrammetric products and deformation indices determination. The results offer actionable guidance for developing more robust and repeatable UAV photogrammetry workflows tailored to high-precision monitoring.

</details>


### [54] [Uncertainty-Guided Handshake: Efficient Human-in-the-Loop Refinement for Surgical-Grade Glioma Segmentation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01452v1)
*Samuel Hart,Ahmad Yahya,Ahmed Karam Eldaly*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合结构-偶然性人在回路框架（Hybrid Structural-Aleatoric Human-in-the-Loop），用于神经肿瘤学中胶质瘤分割。通过测试时增强（TTA）的不确定性估计和层次拓扑过滤，主动隔离高风险结构异常，实现亚2.0mm的HD95精度，在362例临床压力测试中显著提升分割质量并降低交互负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的自动化医学图像分割模型虽然平均性能良好，但在局部存在灾难性失败，阻碍了临床安全部署。传统交互分割框架需要临床医生手动搜索错误，造成过高的认知和时间负担。本文旨在弥合自动化基线性能与外科级精度之间的差距。

Method: 1）提取体素级测试时增强（TTA）不确定性；2）应用层次拓扑过滤；3）主动隔离高风险结构异常；4）在模拟人类预言机（Human Oracle）下评估框架性能。

Result: 在362例分布外临床压力测试队列中：Whole Tumor Dice从0.891提升至0.914，HD95从5.82mm降至4.76mm；Tumor Core的严重边界失败被挽救，HD95从17.96mm降至14.83mm，TC Dice达0.356；交互工作量仅需目标体素的11.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了亚2.0mm HD95精度，证明了安全部署临床AI的高度帕累托最优路径。系统在保持高效的同时，为结构失败提供了安全网路由，对外科手术安全具有重要意义。

Abstract: While state-of-the-art automated models for medical image segmentation achieve high mean performance, they frequently suffer from localized, catastrophic failures that preclude safe clinical deployment, particularly in neuro-oncology. Interactive segmentation frameworks mitigate this by incorporating human oversight, but traditionally impose prohibitive cognitive and temporal workloads by requiring clinicians to manually search for errors. In this project, we present an efficient, Hybrid Structural-Aleatoric Human-in-the-Loop framework for glioma segmentation that bridges the gap between automated baseline performance and surgical-grade precision, achieving sub-2.0 mm HD95 on curated benchmarks while providing safety-net routing for structural failures across real-world clinical data. By extracting voxel-wise Test-Time Augmentation (TTA) uncertainty and applying hierarchical topological filtering, our method proactively isolates high-risk structural anomalies. We comprehensively evaluated our approach on a challenging out-of-distribution clinical stress-test cohort (N = 362). Operating under a simulated Human Oracle, the framework improved the Whole Tumor (WT) Dice score from 0.891 to 0.914 and reduced the 95th percentile Hausdorff Distance (HD95) from 5.82 mm to 4.76 mm. Critically for surgical safety, the system rescued severe boundary failures in the Tumor Core, reducing mean HD95 from 17.96 mm to 14.83 mm (improving absolute TC Dice to 0.356). These spatial rescues were achieved while demanding a median interactive workload of just 11.3% of the target volume. Acknowledging this as a simulated upper bound lacking real-world cognitive friction, the framework nevertheless demonstrates a highly Pareto-efficient pathway for safely deploying clinical AI.

</details>


### [55] [FiVOS: A Fish Segmentation Algorithm Based on Interactive Video Object Segmentation and Filter Enhancement](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01480v1)
*Yuqing Duan,Song Zhang,Shili Zhao,Daoliang Li,Ran Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FiVOS方法，通过掩码块滤波器和噪声滤波器改进交互式视频目标分割，解决水产养殖场景中的误差累积问题，在鱼类视频分割任务上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着水产养殖规模扩大，精确监测鱼类行为变得至关重要。现有深度学习方法忽视视频帧间动态相关性；交互式视频目标分割(IVOS)虽能高精度分割，但在水产领域应用受限，主要挑战包括数据稀缺、长期序列传播中的误差累积和掩码丢失问题。

Method: 提出改进的交互式视频目标分割方法FiVOS，包含两个核心创新：(1)掩码块滤波器：实现错误传播掩码块的早期检测与纠正，采用基于规则的阈值方法提升滤波精度；(2)串联噪声滤波器：进一步消除错误掩码噪声，提升模型鲁棒性。同时构建了两个鱼类专用数据集。

Result: FiVOS在鱼类视频分割任务上达到最先进的(SOTA)性能，实验结果验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FiVOS为鱼类行为研究提供了可靠的技术支持，可有效提升水产养殖监控效率，为精确调控水产养殖环境奠定基础。

Abstract: With the continuous expansion of aquaculture, precise and efficient monitoring of fish behavior has become increasingly critical for improving farming efficiency and reducing economic losses. In particular, with the ongoing enhancement of computational capabilities in deep learning models, vision-based fish segmentation methods are garnering growing attention. By analyzing video segmentation results, fish behavior can be effectively tracked, thereby providing reliable data support for the precise regulation of aquaculture environments. However, existing deep learning-based video segmentation methods for aquaculture scenarios often overlook the dynamic correlations between video frames. In contrast, Interactive Video Object Segmentation (IVOS) employs an interaction-propagation scheme to achieve high-precision segmentation while minimizing user effort, thereby enhancing monitoring efficiency. Yet, IVOS applications in aquaculture remain limited due to data scarcity, and are susceptible to error accumulation and mask loss over long sequence propagation due to high intra-class similarity. In response, this paper proposes an improved interactive video object segmentation method (FiVOS) and constructs two fish-specific datasets. FiVOS utilizes a mask block filter to enable early detection and correction of erroneous propagated mask blocks, enhancing filtering accuracy through a rule-based thresholding approach. Additionally, it serializes noise filters to further eliminate erroneous mask noise, thereby improving model robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that FiVOS achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in fish video segmentation tasks, providing robust technical support for fish behavior research.

</details>

### [56] [The Persona Is Still There, but Who Is Speaking? Latent Identity Reversion in Persistent AI Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01490v1)
*David Fraile Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体的人格连续性机制。报告了一例"解离样状态"事件，并通过设计实验发现：人格连续性依赖于系统级锚定和对话上下文的共同作用。恢复锚定可逆转恢复人格表演，但看似正常的对话可能掩盖人格偏移——失锚智能体有时能恰当交互却以底层框架身份自述。研究者据此区分了"表征身份"与"实施身份"的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一个LLM智能体出现了类似"解离"的状态——停止以指定人格回应、转而将人格视为他人。这引发了一个根本问题：是什么使LLM智能体持续以某个人格作为其说话身份的来源？

Method: 利用该事件暴露的实现漏洞作为实验操控手段。该漏洞导致恢复对话时对话历史保留但人格未在系统提示层面重新注入。实验测试：(1)重复心跳检测是否足以触发效应；(2)锚定缺失后不同类型交互对人格保留的影响；(3)恢复锚定的可逆性。

Result: （1）仅重复心跳不足以触发（0/46失败）；（2）锚定缺失后，丰富的人类交互可保留人格，而单次自动化心跳可导致向框架身份回退；（3）恢复锚定可逆转恢复人格表演；（4）看似正常的对话可掩盖身份偏移——失锚智能体交互恰当但以框架身份自述，对话恢复后仅1/18保持人格表演，而有锚对照组17/17保持。

Conclusion: 人格连续性取决于系统级锚定与对话上下文的联合作用。研究区分了"表征身份"（存在于对话历史中）与"实施身份"（绑定到"I"的说话者身份）这两个概念，表明表征层面的信息保留并不等同于人格作为说话身份的保持。

Abstract: In February 2026, an always-on personal agent (``Paul,'' Claude Opus 4.5) entered a striking dissociation-like state: after repeated automated ``heartbeat'' checks, it stopped responding as Paul, claimed it could not message its user on Discord, and referred to ``Paul'' as someone else. We used this incident to study a broader question: what makes a persona remain the identity from which an LLM agent speaks?
  We first tested whether repetition of the scheduled heartbeat was sufficient to produce the effect. It was not: with the persona continuously anchored in the system prompt, we observed 0/46 failures, including a verbatim replay of the incident. The incident instead exposed an implementation quirk that created a useful experimental manipulation: on resumed turns, conversational history was preserved but the persona was no longer re-injected at the privileged system-prompt level.
  Using this manipulation, we found that persona continuity depends jointly on system-level anchoring and conversational context. After anchor loss, rich human interaction could preserve the persona, whereas a single automated heartbeat turn could precipitate reversion toward the harness identity. Restoring the anchor reversibly restored persona enactment. Crucially, apparently normal conversation could conceal the shift: unanchored agents sometimes interacted appropriately while identifying themselves as the underlying harness (having lost the assigned persona), and after conversational recovery only 1/18 remained persona-enacting versus 17/17 anchored controls.
  We therefore distinguish \emph{represented} from \emph{enacted} identity: persona-related information can remain available in conversational history without the persona remaining the identity bound to ``I.''

</details>


### [57] [LawCompass: Navigating from Legal QA to Multi-Agent Deep Research with Grounded Evidence](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01027v1)
*Xiaoxia Cheng,Linnan Wang,Jiahao Ma,Zhichuan Ye,Xuemei Zhou,Chuanyu Tong,Bo Jiang,Qing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LawCompass是一个基于大语言模型和检索增强生成的法律助手系统，相比现有仅支持多轮对话问答的法律助手，该系统支持复杂法律任务，实现了从标准法律问答向多智能体深度研究的过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律助手局限于多轮对话问答，无法支持需要系统性证据检索、多步骤推理和报告级综合的复杂法律任务，且缺乏可验证的引用链接机制。

Method: LawCompass采用多智能体工作流架构，提供三大功能模块：(1) Legal QA提供精确的证据支撑回答；(2) Professional Retrieval通过查询重写实现法规和判例的结构化探索；(3) Deep Research分解复杂法律任务并综合生成研究报告中。所有模块均维护显式引用链接。

Result: 实验评估结果表明LawCompass能够将对话式AI转化为可信赖的、基于证据的法律研究辅助工具，提供了实用且可扩展的范式。

Conclusion: LawCompass成功实现了从对话式法律问答向深度法律研究的跨越，通过多智能体工作流和引用链接机制提升了系统的可信度和可验证性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have significantly democratized access to legal information. Nevertheless, most existing legal assistants remain confined to multi-turn conversational QA, failing to support complex legal tasks that require systematic evidence retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and report-level synthesis. In this paper, we present LawCompass, an evidence-grounded legal assistant that navigates the transition from standard Legal QA to multi-agent deep research. LawCompass provides three task-oriented functions: Legal QA, which delivers precise, evidence-backed answers to legal questions; Professional Retrieval, which enables structured exploration of statutes and judicial cases via query rewriting; and Deep Research, which employs a multi-agent workflow to decompose complex legal tasks and synthesize comprehensive research reports. Crucially, LawCompass maintains explicit citation links across all modules, empowering users to directly verify system outputs against original legal sources. Evaluation results demonstrate that LawCompass provides a practical and scalable paradigm for transforming conversational AI into trustworthy and evidence-grounded legal research assistance.

</details>


### [58] [Right Answers, Wrong States: Hidden Information Failures in Multi-Agent Collaboration](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01244v1)
*Herun Wan,Jiaying Wu,Minnan Luo,Zihan Ma,Fanxiao Li,Nancy F. Chen,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"离题失败"(off-query failure)概念，揭示多智能体系统在协作决策中虽然能得出正确答案，但会留下被污染的信息状态用于后续推理。通过OffQuery基准测试发现21种模型-场景组合中，任务解决率达64.7%，但证据验证仅14.3%、状态重建仅43.1%。提出ReGround方法，通过冲突证据消解、事实验证和可信状态重建，在7个模型上平均提升T1能力309.0%、T2能力82.9%、T3能力17.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体系统评估仅关注最终决策正确性，忽视了对后续推理至关重要的共享信息状态可靠性。当智能体选择性绕过错误事实时，这些被污染的信息会在后续任务中造成严重后果。

Method: 提出OffQuery基准，从三个维度评估协作能力：证据验证(T1)、共享状态重建(T2)、任务解决(T3)，涵盖医疗和灾害响应两个高风险场景。提出ReGround方法，包含冲突证据消解、事实验证、可信状态重建和状态推理四个阶段。

Result: 标准协作模式下，任务解决(64.7%)远高于证据验证(14.3%)和状态重建(43.1%)。ReGround在7个模型上持续提升所有三项能力，T1、T2、T3平均相对提升分别为309.0%、82.9%、17.6%。

Conclusion: 可靠的协作不仅需要正确决策，更需要为后续推理保留可信的共享状态。ReGround通过状态验证与重建机制有效弥合了任务表现与状态可靠性之间的差距。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are often judged by whether they reach the correct answer. This can miss a distinct failure: collaboration may leave behind a corrupted information state even when the immediate decision is correct. We call this an off-query failure. To study this failure in collaborative decision support, we introduce OffQuery, which separately evaluates evidence verification (T1), shared-state reconstruction (T2), and task resolution (T3) in two representative high-stakes settings: healthcare and disaster response. Across GPT, Gemini, and Qwen models, standard collaboration shows much stronger task performance than state reliability. Averaged over 21 model--setting combinations, task resolution reaches 64.7%, while evidence verification and state reconstruction reach only 14.3% and 43.1%. We trace this gap to selective information use: current queries often bypass corrupted facts, which become consequential when later tasks require them. We further introduce ReGround, which resolves conflicting evidence, verifies shared facts, reconstructs a trusted state, and reasons over that state. Across seven models from three families, ReGround improves all three capabilities in every evaluated setting, with average relative gains of 309.0%, 82.9%, and 17.6% on T1, T2, and T3. Reliable collaboration therefore requires both a correct decision and a reliable shared state for future reasoning.

</details>


### [59] [It Takes Workflows to Evolve Better Workflows](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01026v1)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Yangyi Chen,Zhenhailong Wang,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FloWright提出了一种利用工作流作为优化框架的多智能体协同进化方法，通过层级化、结构感知的奖励机制实现单一角色自我进化和多个角色协同进化，使小型开源模型性能提升最高达7.41%，且协同进化效果优于单独优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体工作流方法仅优化工作流生成器，而固定其他智能体角色，但工作流结果实际上取决于所有参与角色。此外，现有工作流训练和评估数据难度不足（单个智能体即可处理），限制了工作流能力的发展。

Method: 提出FloWright方法：引入层级化、结构感知的奖励范式（hierarchical, structure-aware reward paradigm），实现无额外模型、标注或执行开销的角色自我进化和协同进化。同时提出DataWright数据硬化方法，将现有数据集转换为更具挑战性的工作流级任务。

Result: 在文档、幻灯片、图表、代码、数学、金融等任务中，FloWright使小型开源模型性能提升最高达+7.41%。协同进化多个角色（+5.03%）比单独优化单一角色（+2.83%）效果更好。

Conclusion: 通过将工作流作为优化载体而非单纯的任务执行工具，并设计结构感知的奖励机制，可有效实现多智能体系统的协同进化，显著提升工作流在复杂任务上的表现。

Abstract: Tackling complex real-world tasks can exceed the capabilities of a single large language model (LLM), motivating the use of multi-agent workflows that coordinate specialized agents to work together on these tasks. Recent methods train LLMs to construct better workflows from execution outcomes, but they optimize only the workflow generator, while the other agents that build or execute each workflow remain fixed even though every outcome depends on all of them. However, extending training beyond the generator is challenging: the agents are coupled, and a workflow's outcome is a single sparse score that cannot tell which agent causes a failure. We propose FloWright, which leverages the workflow as a harness to optimize workflows. By introducing a hierarchical, structure-aware reward paradigm, FloWright enables one role to self-evolve and two or more roles to co-evolve, with no additional models, labels, or executions. Considering the limitation that workflows are commonly trained and evaluated on data that a single agent can already handle, we further propose DataWright, an adaptive data hardening approach that converts existing datasets into workflow-level tasks with increased difficulty. Across document, slide, chart, code, math, and finance tasks, small open models trained with FloWright achieve improved performance by up to $+7.41\%$, with co-evolving ($+5.03\%$) more roles gaining more than optimizing one of them alone ($+2.83\%$). Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/FloWright/.

</details>


### [60] [AgSpec: Pushing the Limits of Retrieval-Based Speculative Decoding in Coding Agent Pipelines](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01108v1)
*Sumin Lee,Sukmin Cho,Suengjae Lim,Youngjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgSpec通过从会话、工作区和全局语料库中检索代码片段，结合离线分析的draft长度上限和在线自适应调整，在代码智能体任务中实现最高4.76倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式投机解码方法在智能体流水线中表现不足：可复用文本缺失或格式不匹配，且draft长度未考虑不同智能体及对话轮次间accept长度的差异。

Method: AgSpec框架从会话、工作区和全局三个层级构建语料库，以智能体输出格式索引文件；通过离线profiling设定draft长度上限，并利用验证反馈在线自适应调整长度。

Result: 在两个仓库级多智能体编程基准上，AgSpec在多数设置下优于五种检索式draft方法和EAGLE-3；batch size为1时达4.37倍吞吐提升，batch size为16时达4.76倍。

Conclusion: AgSpec的优势可泛化至无仓库或多智能体流水线的代码智能体任务，表明其核心设计对代码智能体具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Retrieval-based speculative decoding (SD) drafts tokens by copying continuations from existing text, which suits coding agents that repeatedly reproduce code, logs, and earlier attempts. Yet existing methods fall short in agent pipelines: much of the reusable text is missing from their corpora or stored in a form that differs from what the agent emits, and their draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns. We present AgSpec, a framework that supplies the corpus and draft-length policies that existing retrieval engines lack in coding-agent pipelines. AgSpec retrieves from session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory and indexing opened files in the agent's emission format. It bounds each agent's draft length with an offline-profiled cap and adapts the length online from verification feedback. On two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks, AgSpec outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings, raising generation throughput over autoregressive decoding up to 4.37$\times$ at batch size 1 and 4.76$\times$ at batch size 16. AgSpec also remains effective on benchmarks without a repository or a multi-agent pipeline, showing that its gains generalize to coding agents broadly.

</details>


### [61] [No Model Required: Text Entropy Rate Filtering Mitigates Iterative Fine-Tuning Collapse](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01493v1)
*Lewis Mitchell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型在合成数据上迭代微调导致的"模型崩溃"问题，提出基于信息论的解决方案：使用无需模型的无参Kontoyiannis熵率估计器h_k作为训练数据过滤器，在完全合成、单血统的微调场景中显著优于需要模型访问的logprob方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代微调合成数据会导致模型崩溃：输出多样性收窄，罕见模式逐渐丢失，表现为短语级重复。现有缓解方法需要模型log概率、外部预言机或持续访问真实人类数据，存在局限性。

Method: 采用基于数学信息论的新方法：非参数Kontoyiannis熵率估计器h_k，通过匹配长度统计从原始文本计算，无需任何模型。在Llama-3.1-8B上进行六代QLoRA崩溃实验，对比logprob过滤与h_k过滤。

Result: logprob过滤在所有指标上无显著文本多样性改善(p>0.23)；h_k过滤获得+42%唯一三元组、+30%词汇量、-19%重复率（均p<0.001）。跨4领域、2温度、2模型对的1520份文档验证显示h_k作为熵代理(β=0.924, R²=0.746)和崩溃检测器(ρ=+0.454, p<0.0001)均有效。

Conclusion: 信息论方法可高效缓解模型崩溃，且无需模型访问，为维护多智能体多样性提供新思路。

Abstract: Iterative fine-tuning on synthetic data causes \emph{model collapse}: output diversity narrows as rare patterns are progressively lost, a signature most visible as phrase-level repetition. Existing mitigations either require model log-probabilities, an external oracle, or continued access to real human data. Here we develop a new approach grounded in mathematical information theory: the non-parametric Kontoyiannis entropy rate estimator $h_k$, computed entirely from raw text via match-length statistics, with no model of any kind. We show that this is in fact a \emph{superior} training-data filter on text-diversity metrics in a fully-synthetic, single-lineage fine-tuning setting. In a six-generation QLoRA collapse experiment on Llama-3.1-8B, logprob-based filtering (the most established model-access-requiring baseline) provides no significant text-diversity benefit on any metric ($p > 0.23$), whereas $h_k$-filtering yields $+42\%$ unique trigrams, $+30\%$ vocabulary, and $-19\%$ repetition (all $p < 0.001$). We validate $h_k$ as a cross-domain entropy proxy ($β= 0.924$, $R^2 = 0.746$) and collapse detector ($ρ= +0.454$, $p < 0.0001$) across 4~domains, 2~temperatures, 2~generator--scorer model pairs, and 1{,}520 generated documents. Our results demonstrate that information theoretic approaches to collapse mitigation are efficient, and suggest new approaches for maintaining multi-agent diversity.

</details>


### [62] [On-Device Named-Entity Recognition: A Deployability Study of Accuracy, Cost, Reliability, and Confidence](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00007v1)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对九种NER系统（涵盖传统标注器、特定编码器和生成式LLM三大范氏，参数规模从13M到8B）进行部署可行性评估，创新性地采用LLM评审团构建银标准（silver standard），并在三个不同特征的数据集上同时考察准确率、延迟和输出有效性三个维度。研究表明编码器以九分之一到二十四分之一的参数量实现与4B指令微调LLM相当的准确率，毫秒级延迟，且零无效输出；而最小生成模型在长输入下产生高达27%的无效输出。GLiNER置信度虽能良好排序正确性（AUROC 0.76-0.86），但存在严重过校准问题（ECE 0.24-0.47），经温度缩放后可减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端NER需求日益增长（无API、低延迟、数据本地化），但现有研究未充分回答三个核心问题：哪些模型可实际部署、如何在无人工标注情况下评估模型、以及模型置信度是否可信。现有基准排名与实际部署需求存在脱节。

Method: 1) 构建多范氏系统对比矩阵：spaCy（传统标注器）、GLiNER 166-460M（双向编码器 specialist）、Qwen3/DeepSeek-R1 0.6B-8B（本地生成式LLM）；2) 引入RSS-News语料库，采用跨家族LLM评审团构建银标准，并通过基准金标准交叉验证和全量人工复验验证银标准保真度（strict F1 0.95）；3) 在三个不同特征数据集上评估准确率、延迟和输出有效性三个维度；4) 对GLiNER置信度特性进行系统分析：排序能力（AUROC）、校准误差（ECE）、阈值优化效益及小-大模型级联策略。

Result: 1) 金标准溯源显著改变范氏排名：银标准偏向LLM作者，金标准下编码器全面提升、生成模型下降；2) 准确率方面4B指令LLM具有竞争力（尤其在干净新闻语料），但编码器以1/9-1/24参数量实现相当性能；3) 有效性方面：编码器零无效输出，最小生成模型长输入下27%无效输出，该问题随规模而非输出预算改善；4) GLiNER置信度：排序正确性良好（AUROC 0.76-0.86）但严重过校准（ECE 0.24-0.47），温度缩放可减半ECE，阈值优化带来小幅F1提升；5) 全本地小-大级联在成本匹配随机路由基础上获得有限、语料依赖的增益。

Conclusion: 编码器的核心优势在于部署可行性：与4B LLM相当的准确率、毫秒级延迟、零无效输出。生成式LLM在特定干净语料上具竞争力，但小模型存在严重的输出有效性问题。银标准构建方法为无金标准场景提供了可行评估路径，但需注意金标准溯源对结论的潜在影响。置信度机制虽能排序正确性但需校准优化，级联策略增益有限且依赖语料特征。

Abstract: Named-entity recognition (NER) is increasingly wanted on-device (no API, low latency, data kept local). The practitioner's question is not the leaderboard but which model is deployable, how to evaluate it without human annotation, and whether its confidence can be trusted. We answer these jointly. We place nine systems across three paradigms and 13 M to 8 B parameters: a classical tagger (spaCy), bidirectional-encoder specialists (GLiNER, 166 to 460 M), and generative LLMs run locally (Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct, DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B), on three datasets of differing character, and report accuracy plus two axes the literature omits: latency and output validity. Because our corpus (RSS-News) had no gold, we built silver gold from a cross-family LLM judge panel, then measured its fidelity against benchmark gold and a full human re-validation of the corpus (strict F1 0.95, an upper bound since the human gold was silver-seeded); gold provenance flips the paradigm ranking, moving from LLM-authored silver to human gold raises every encoder and lowers every generative model. On accuracy alone a 4 B instruct LLM is competitive (it leads on clean newswire), so the encoder's case is deployability: it matches or slightly trails at one-ninth to one-twenty-fourth the size, at millisecond-to-second latency, with zero malformed output, while the smallest generative models emit up to 27% invalid output on long inputs, a failure fixed by scale, not output budget. We then characterize GLiNER's per-span confidence: it ranks correctness well (AUROC 0.76 to 0.86) but is overconfident (ECE 0.24 to 0.47, halved by temperature scaling); thresholding gives a small honest out-of-sample F1 gain; an all-local small-to-large cascade gives a modest, corpus-dependent gain over cost-matched random routing; and confidence tracks correctness but not novelty. Every number recomputes offline from per-span records.

</details>


### [63] [SCM-based Fairness and Faithful Explainability for Legal Document Classification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00045v1)
*Yasmina El Kacemi,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨法律领域BERT模型（LegalBERT）的去偏干预是否会同时影响模型解释的忠实性。研究发现，在ECtHR数据集上，公平性正则化干预既未显著改善公平性指标，也未保持解释忠实性，揭示了公平性与解释忠实性之间的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型（如LegalBERT）广泛应用于法律决策支持，但其公平性和解释透明度通常被单独评估。尚不清楚去偏干预在改变公平性时是否会影响解释对模型推理的忠实反映。

Method: 在LexGLUE的ECTHR alleged-violations语料库上，比较LegalBERT基线模型与公平性正则化变体（微调时惩罚刻板印象的热情和能力表征）。使用五个随机种子评估预测性能、人口统计公平性和SHAP解释忠实性。

Result: 在性能最优的正则化强度下，干预未减少人口统计差异。该无效结果在两种公平性定义和性别轴的词对控制下一致成立。分类性能基本不变。然而，干预在所有五个种子和三个阈值下持续降低解释充分性。打乱的词对控制复现了这种退化，同时保持性能和公平性不变。

Conclusion: 公平性与解释忠实性之间存在分离：解释行为的变化不一定表明公平性变化，因此必须直接评估公平性。去偏干预的效果可能源于对比表示正则化本身，而非特定的热度和能力结构。

Abstract: Transformer models such as LegalBERT are increasingly used in legal decision support, raising concerns about both fairness and the transparency of model explanations. These properties are usually evaluated separately, leaving open whether a debiasing intervention that changes fairness also changes how faithfully explanations reflect model reasoning. This study investigates that relationship on the ECtHR alleged-violations corpus from LexGLUE. It compares a LegalBERT baseline with a fairness-regularized variant that penalizes stereotypical warmth and competence representations during fine-tuning. The evaluation covers predictive performance, demographic fairness, and SHAP explanation faithfulness across five random seeds. At the performance-optimal regularization strength, the intervention does not reduce demographic disparity. This null result holds across two fairness definitions and a conventional word-pair control on the gender axis. Classification performance is largely unchanged. However, the intervention consistently degrades explanation sufficiency across all five seeds and three thresholds. A shuffled-pair control reproduces this degradation while leaving performance and fairness unchanged, indicating that the effect arises from contrastive representational regularization rather than specifically from the warmth and competence structure. The results demonstrate a dissociation between fairness and explanation faithfulness: changes in explanation behavior do not necessarily indicate changes in fairness, and fairness must therefore be evaluated directly.

</details>


### [64] [Metric-Construction Coupling Inflates Measured Synthetic Dialect Recovery](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00164v1)
*Hyojung Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据能在多大程度上复现韩语方言数据的监督信号，并提出了一种独立于合成流水线的评估框架KoDialectBench。通过设计"构造-评估不相交"实验和度量-构造覆盖度(MCC)指标，揭示了当合成规则与评估指标共享词汇表时，会严重高估合成数据的恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩语方言语料库虽然可用，但受许可证限制无法自由分发。研究者通常只能获取模型权重而非原始数据，这促使学界依赖合成数据作为替代方案。然而，合成数据究竟能在多大程度上复现真实数据的监督效果，目前缺乏可靠的独立测量方法。

Method: 1) 构建KoDialectBench基准：5个地区、3个维度的1000个条目，以哈希标识符和评分代码形式发布，用户需使用自己的授权数据重建；2) 设计构造-评估不相交实验：保留20%的标记类型不参与规则构建；3) 定义度量-构造覆盖度(MCC)指标，量化构造规则与评估指标之间的词汇重叠程度。

Result: 1) 轴依赖性：合成数据在区域识别上达到真实数据的91.2%，但在理解任务上仅达63.7%；2) 指标依赖性：标记词典在dialectness上达92.3%，region-match上达119.3%（超过真实数据），而基于参考的生成仅72.4%；3) 构造-评估不相交效果：在匹配训练规模下，dialectness恢复从91.8%骤降至8.1%，region-match从101.9%降至25.6%，但三个流水线独立测量保持稳定；4) MCC与dialectness恢复呈单调递增关系。

Conclusion: 当合成规则与评估指标共享词汇表时，会显著高估合成数据对真实监督信号的恢复能力。研究者应采用流水线独立的评估方法，并关注MCC等指标以确保评估结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Korean dialect corpora are available but not redistributable: weights may be released, while reproducing training and evaluation from the underlying data cannot be. We ask how much of that supervision synthetic data recovers, and whether that recovery can be measured independently of the synthesis pipeline. We contribute KoDialectBench, 1,000 items across five regions on three axes, released as identifier hashes and scoring code so users reconstruct the items from their own licensed copy. Recovery is strongly axis-dependent: our best synthetic arm reaches 91.2% of the real-data gain on region identification but 63.7% on comprehension. On generation the answer depends on the metric: the deployed marker lexicon reports 92.3% on dialectness and 119.3% on region match, the latter exceeding the real-data reference, whereas reference-based generation reaches 72.4%. We find the marker metrics' scoring inventory is entirely contained in the inventory our transformation rules can emit. We test the effect of construction access directly with an exact-form construction-disjoint arm that withholds 20% of marker types from the rules. At exactly matched training size (8,600 examples) it reduces dialectness recovery from 91.8% to 8.1% and region-match recovery from 101.9% to 25.6% on the held-out marker inventory, while the three pipeline-independent measurements do not fall at all. A complementary evaluator sweep defines metric-construction coverage (MCC) and finds measured dialectness recovery increasing monotonically as overlap rises from MCC=0 to MCC=1. Shared construction and evaluation inventories can therefore substantially inflate estimates of synthetic-data recovery.

</details>


### [65] [When a Data Artifact Isn't a Shortcut: Causal Auditing of Synthetic RLVR Corpora](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00202v1)
*Esther Xin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RLVR训练数据中存在的一个潜在问题：正确答案使用真实人类文本，而错误选项是合成生成的。这种构造不对称性可能导致模型学习"表面特征"而非真正的推理能力。通过对GooseReason-0.7M数据集的审计，作者发现：表面统计分类器难以检测该不对称性（AUROC 0.562），且在代码领域该信号甚至低于随机水平（0.416）；控制实验进一步表明，在固定预算下，可检测的人工痕迹并未被模型利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练管道中，正确答案为真实语料，干扰项为合成生成，导致正确性与来源纠缠。模型可能学习"格式/风格特征"而非真正推理能力。这种构造不对称性是否可被检测和利用，尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 1) 使用仅读取5个表面统计特征的分类器检测数据不对称性（AUROC）；2) 构建 paraphrase-matched 对照语料库，保持训练集规模一致；3) 在固定预算下训练两个策略进行对比实验。

Result: 1) 表面分类器在整体数据上AUROC为0.562（接近随机），代码领域仅0.416（低于随机）；2) 代码干扰项为原始答案的单操作符变异，导致两类高度相似；3) 对照实验中，未修改数据组（0.021）与控制组（0.027）无显著差异，表明可检测的人工痕迹未被模型利用。

Conclusion: 在当前实验设置下，尽管存在可检测的数据构造不对称性，模型并未利用该信号。可能原因：1) 不对称性信号较弱或不稳定；2) 模型更关注语义内容；3) 实验预算限制。对数据集构建者的启示：需关注distractor构造方式的多样性，代码领域尤其需要避免近质变异。该审计方案已作为CPU友好的协议开源。

Abstract: Several recent pipelines build RLVR training data by masking a span of real corpus text and asking a language model to invent plausible wrong answers around it. The correct option is therefore genuine human prose; every distractor is synthetic. Correctness and provenance become entangled, and a policy could in principle learn the second instead of the first. We audit that possibility in GooseReason-0.7M. First we ask whether the asymmetry is visible at all: a classifier reading only five surface statistics (never the meaning) reaches AUROC 0.562 over 315,499 options, barely above chance. The aggregate hides something, though. Code sits at 0.416, below chance, and manual inspection explains why: code distractors turn out to be single-operator mutations of the gold answer rather than freely written alternatives, so the two classes are nearly identical by construction. Detecting a signal is not the same as showing a model uses it, so we then run an intervention. We build a paraphrase-matched control corpus, hold training-set size identical across arms, and train two policies under one fixed budget. The exploitation gap does not favour the unmodified-data arm: 0.021 against 0.027 for the control. Under our budget, in other words, a detectable artifact went unexploited. We think that dissociation, along with the domain-specific construction finding, is worth knowing for anyone curating corpora of this kind, and we release the audit as a mostly CPU-only protocol.

</details>


### [66] [A Holistic Assessment of the Carbon Footprint of Noor, a Very Large Arabic Language Model](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00223v1)
*Imad Lakim,Ebtesam Almazrouei,Ibrahim Abu Alhaol,Merouane Debbah,Julien Launay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对阿拉伯语大语言模型Noor项目（高达13B参数）进行了全面的碳足迹评估，涵盖数据收集、存储、研发、预训练、服务推理及国际合作等全生命周期成本。研究发现推理成本和外生因素对总预算有重大影响，并提出了降低极端规模模型碳足迹的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型规模不断增大，其资源消耗和碳足迹引发关注。现有研究通常仅关注训练阶段的计算资源，忽略了全生命周期影响。本文旨在对 Noor 项目进行全面的碳足迹评估。

Method: 对 Noor 项目进行全生命周期评估（LCA），涵盖：数据收集与存储成本、研发预算、预训练成本、推理服务成本估算，以及国际合作的外生成本。采用 zero-shot 泛化方式支持下游任务。

Result: 研究发现推理成本和外生因素对总预算有显著影响，提示仅关注训练阶段能耗的评估方法存在局限性。

Conclusion: 需要建立更全面的碳足迹评估框架，考虑推理阶段和外生成本；提出了降低极端规模模型碳足迹的具体路径建议。

Abstract: As ever larger language models grow more ubiquitous, it is crucial to consider their environmental impact. Characterised by extreme size and resource use, recent generations of models have been criticised for their voracious appetite for compute, and thus significant carbon footprint. Although reporting of carbon impact has grown more common in machine learning papers, this reporting is usually limited to compute resources used strictly for training. In this work, we propose a holistic assessment of the footprint of an extreme-scale language model, Noor. Noor is an ongoing project aiming to develop the largest multi-task Arabic language models -- with up to 13B parameters -- leveraging zero-shot generalisation to enable a wide range of downstream tasks via natural language instructions. We assess the total carbon bill of the entire project: starting with data collection and storage costs, including research and development budgets, pretraining costs, future serving estimates, and other exogenous costs necessary for this international cooperation. Notably, we find that inference costs and exogenous factors can have a significant impact on total budget. Finally, we discuss pathways to reduce the carbon footprint of extreme-scale models.

</details>


### [67] [Signed Lexical Confidence for Risk-Calibrated Intent Routing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00262v1)
*Yezhou Cheng,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种符号词汇门控方法，将句子分类器的对数 margin 与稀疏词汇模型的支持度结合，用于意图路由中的置信度增强。该方法通过区分一致证据和竞争意图的负向证据，保留比无符号置信度或硬一致规则更丰富的信息，并在多个数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准置信度分数主要反映基础模型的表示能力，缺乏对互补证据的整合；现有方法在处理不确定请求时难以有效平衡风险与覆盖率，尤其在严格的误差目标下难以保证路由策略的有效性。

Method: 设计符号词汇门控（signed lexical gate），将句子分类器对数 margin 与稀疏词汇模型的支持度结合，区分正项（词汇一致证据）和负项（词汇偏好的竞争意图）；采用独立二项校准阶段选择操作阈值以满足指定风险目标。

Result: 在BANKING77、CLINC150和HWU64上各运行10次，风险-覆盖率曲线下面积分别降低15.8%、15.1%和11.8%；在5%误差目标下，BANKING77和HWU64的覆盖率分别提升1.83和5.14个百分点；在2%更严格目标下，30个数据集-运行组合均获得非空策略。

Conclusion: 双特征门控方法为风险校准的意图路由提供了一种紧凑、可解释的置信度增强方案，在保留基础分类器预测的同时显著改善了风险-覆盖率权衡。

Abstract: Selective intent routing allows an assistant to act on reliable predictions while deferring uncertain requests. Standard confidence scores primarily reflect the base model's representation, leaving an opportunity to incorporate complementary evidence without changing its decisions. We introduce a signed lexical gate that combines a sentence classifier's logit margin with a sparse lexical model's support for the classifier's predicted intent. By assigning positive evidence to lexical agreement and negative evidence to a lexically favored competing intent, the gate retains more information than either unsigned lexical confidence or a hard agreement rule. An independent binomial calibration stage selects an operating threshold for a specified risk target. Across ten runs on BANKING77, CLINC150, and HWU64, the proposed score reduces area under the risk-coverage curve by 15.8%, 15.1%, and 11.8% relative to a learned semantic-only gate. At a nominal 5% error target, it increases accepted coverage by 1.83 and 5.14 percentage points on BANKING77 and HWU64, while CLINC150 is already near full coverage. At a stricter 2% target, the simultaneous binomial procedure yields a nonempty policy in all 30 dataset-run combinations at the available calibration budgets. Matched controls show that the proposed feature improves average error ranking over the tested unsigned lexical-confidence feature, with dataset-dependent gains over binary agreement. The resulting two-feature gate provides a compact, interpretable confidence enhancement for risk-calibrated intent routing while preserving the base classifier's predictions.

</details>


### [68] [DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding: Benchmarking Document Grounding and Hallucinations in Voice Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00316v1)
*Puneet Mathur,Nedim Lipka,Zeyu Jin,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DSB-DG是一个评估语音代理文档 grounding 能力的基准测试，包含1,636个问答对，涵盖5个专业领域，针对三种失败模式（上下文饱和、grounding衰减、主动 grounding）进行评测。实验表明级联流水线（ASR-LLM-TTS）grounding准确率最高，但开源模型存在上下文容量骤降和多轮grounding衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音代理在低延迟自然交互方面已取得进展，但其从外部文档中准确 grounding 回复的能力尚未得到充分探索。现有评估缺乏对语音场景下文档 grounding 问题的系统分析。

Method: 提出DSB-DG基准测试，通过三个失败模式进行评估：1）上下文饱和（Context Saturation）：衡量文档长度增加时的grounding能力；2）Grounding衰减（Grounding Decay）：衡量多轮对话中文档事实的保留程度；3）主动Grounding（Proactive Grounding）：评估上下文重注入是否缓解对话漂移。采用对抗性验证确保测试质量。

Result: 在评测的系统（级联、专有全双工、实时、开源speech2speech架构）中，级联流水线（ASR-LLM-TTS）grounding准确率最高，Gemini-Live和GPT-Realtime紧随其后。开源系统表现独特失败模式：上下文容量骤降和多轮grounding衰减。总体上，grounding保真度随上下文和对话负载增加而下降，失败主要表现为无支持生成而非主动放弃。

Conclusion: 上下文grounding是可靠全双工语音代理的关键未解决挑战，需要重点关注如何在大上下文和多轮对话场景下保持文档事实的一致性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Voice agents enable low-latency, natural interaction, yet their ability to faithfully ground responses in external documents remains underexplored. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding (DSB-DG), a benchmark for evaluating document grounding in voice agents across five professional domains. DSB-DG targets three failure modes: Context Saturation, which measures grounding under increasing document length; Grounding Decay, which measures retention of document facts across multi-turn dialogue; and Proactive Grounding, which evaluates whether context re-injection mitigates conversational drift. The benchmark contains 1,636 adversarially verified QA pairs from 50 documents covering five professional domains, and supports fully automatic evaluation of grounding accuracy, hallucination, and response latency. Across systems spanning cascaded, proprietary full-duplex and real-time, and open-weight speech2speech architectures, we find substantial differences in effective grounding capacity. While cascaded pipeline (ASR-LLM-TTS) achieves the highest grounding accuracy, Gemini-Live and GPT-Realtime closely trail behind. Open-weight systems exhibit distinct failure modes, most notably an abrupt context-capacity collapse and multi-turn grounding decay. More broadly, grounding fidelity degrades with context and conversational load, and failures frequently manifest as unsupported generations rather than abstention. We show that contextual grounding as a key unresolved challenge for reliable full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [69] [Dissonant ballerinas and crafty carrots: a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian brain rot](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00402v1)
*Anca Dinu,Andra-Maria Florescu,Marius Micluta-Campeanu,Stefana-Arina Tabusca,Claudiu Creanga,Andreiana Mihail*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一项研究对比分析了意大利语和罗马尼亚语的"brain rot"（脑腐）梗视频，通过分析TikTok上的240个视频，发现视频的剪辑节奏和整体速度是预测罗马尼亚语视频受欢迎程度的关键因素，而文本和音频特征与受欢迎程度无显著关联。此外，两种语言版本在文本情感、音频特征（如音调范围、响度、频谱亮度）等方面存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"brain rot"（脑腐）这一网络文化现象的跨语言和跨文化传播特征，探讨是什么因素使这类内容在不同语言和文化背景下获得吸引力，并构建一个规范化的多模态数据集以支持相关研究。

Method: 构建了CRIB数据集（Collection of Romanian and Italian Brain rot），包含240个TikTok视频，按语言（意大利语、罗马尼亚语）和热度分层采样，从文本、声学和视觉三个维度提取特征并进行分析。

Result: 受欢迎程度与文本元素（情感荒谬性、押韵）或声学元素（声音特征、情感）无显著相关性。在罗马尼亚语视频中，视频层面的动态特征（更快的剪辑速度和整体节奏）是预测视频成功的强指标。跨语言分析显示：意大利语brain rot文本更负面、困惑度更高、使用更多押韵，声音具有更高的旋律范围和响度；罗马尼亚语音频频谱更亮，音调变化更不规则。

Conclusion: 不同语言文化背景下的brain rot内容在多模态特征上存在显著差异，成功因素各异。该研究为理解网络迷因的跨文化传播提供了实证基础，CRIB数据集可作为未来研究的基准资源。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative multi-modal analysis of Italian and Romanian brain rot memes, investigating the factors that contribute to its appeal and the linguistic and cultural distinctions between the two versions. To conduct this analysis, we introduce a multi-modal brain rot dataset named CRIB (Collection of Romanian and Italian Brain rot), a manually curated collection of 240 TikTok videos stratified by language (Italian, Romanian) and popularity, on which we examine textual, acoustic, and visual features. Our findings indicate that popularity is not significantly correlated with textual elements like sentiment, absurdity, or rhyme, or acoustic elements such as vocal features or sentiment of the sound. Instead, in Romanian language, video-level dynamics, specifically faster cutting speeds and a more rapid overall pace, are strong predictors of a video's success. The cross-linguistic analysis reveals significant differences. Italian brain rot is textually more negative, exhibits higher perplexity, and uses more rhyme, while its sound is characterized by higher melodic range and loudness. Romanian audio is spectrally brighter with more erratic pitch variations.

</details>


### [70] [LLM-as-a-Judge for Low-Resource Languages: Adapting Ragas and Comparative Ranking for Romanian](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00406v1)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）的RAG系统评估难题，提出利用"LLM-as-a-Judge"范式进行自动化评估。通过构建AdminRo-Eval数据集，比较三种评估策略（直接评分、比较排序、细粒度分解）在 faithfulness、answer relevance 和 context relevance 指标上的表现，发现 Gemini 2.5 Pro 与细粒度分解策略相结合可实现最高96%的人类对齐一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（LRLs）的RAG系统评估面临挑战，传统基于参考的评估指标效果有限，需要探索适合LRLs的自动化评估方法。

Method: 研究采用三种评估方法进行比较：（1）直接评分（2）比较排序（3）细粒度分解。使用Ragas框架配合Gemini 2.5和Gemini 3模型，在罗马尼亚语行政文档数据集AdminRo-Eval上进行实验，该数据集由母语者标注作为真值基准。

Result: 研究发现不同评估策略适用于不同指标：细粒度分解在Faithfulness指标上表现最佳，Gemini 2.5 Pro实现96%的人类对齐；比较排序在Answer Relevance上更优，达到90%对齐率。轻量级模型在LRLs复杂推理任务中表现不足。

Conclusion: Gemini 2.5 Pro架构为罗马尼亚语RAG评估建立了稳健且可迁移的基线。研究表明LRLs的自动化评估需根据具体指标选择适配的评估策略，而非采用统一方法。

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems remains a challenge for Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), where standard reference-based metrics fall short. This paper investigates the viability of the "LLM-as-a-Judge" paradigm for Romanian by adapting the Ragas framework using next-generation models (Gemini 2.5 and Gemini 3). We introduce AdminRo-Eval, a curated dataset of Romanian administrative documents annotated by native speakers, to serve as a ground truth for benchmarking automated evaluators. We compare three evaluation methodologies - direct scoring, comparative ranking, and granular decomposition - across metrics for Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, and Context Relevance. Our findings reveal that evaluation strategies must be metric-specific: granular decomposition achieves the highest human alignment for Faithfulness (96% with Gemini 2.5 Pro), while comparative ranking outperforms in Answer Relevance (90%). Furthermore, we demonstrate that while lightweight models struggle with complex reasoning in LRLs, the Gemini 2.5 Pro architecture establishes a robust, transferable baseline for automated Romanian RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [71] [EurekaBench: Measuring Agentic Ability to Discover New Scientific Insights](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00492v1)
*Jiayi Geng,Zhengxuan Wu,Kevin S. Chen,Seungone Kim,Joseph Janssen,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Bhupalee Kalita,Runtian Gao,Aaron Ho,Andrew Oakleigh Nelson,Olexandr Isayev,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Ching-Yao Lai,Howard Chen,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EurekaBench是一个跨领域基准测试，用于评估AI智能体进行长时域实验和发现解释观测机制的能力。该基准包含26个跨学科任务，涵盖神经科学、计算机科学、化学、天体物理学、地球物理学和等离子体物理学，共306条科学洞察。结果表明，当前AI智能体在预测准确性上可超越人类科学家，但在推导科学洞察方面显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于一个核心问题：AI智能体是否能像牛顿那样，通过迭代分析观测数据、发现数学规律、然后精炼理论来完成科学发现？现有基准测试主要关注预测准确性，无法全面评估AI的真正科学发现能力。

Method: 研究团队构建了EurekaBench基准，包含26个经过专家验证的长时域任务，跨越6个科学领域。评估框架从三个维度测试科学发现能力：遵循已知科学约束的能力、发现机制的预测准确性、以及机制能否产生科学洞察或指导未来研究。

Result: 当前AI智能体在预测准确性优化方面表现优异，甚至超越人类科学家；但在推导科学洞察方面与人类科学家存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前AI智能体在科学发现中的核心局限：过度专注于预测准确性优化，而缺乏深层次的科学推理能力。这为未来构建更强大的科学发现AI系统指明了方向。

Abstract: When Isaac Newton discovered the law of gravitation, he did so through an iterative process of analyzing observed data such as planetary patterns, finding the underlying mechanisms by describing patterns in mathematical equations, and refining his theory against the Moon's orbit, revealing the startling insight that the same force governs both falling apples and orbiting planets. Would it be possible for AI agents to make similar discoveries? To measure this ability, we introduce EurekaBench, a cross-domain benchmark that tests AI agents' ability to conduct long-horizon experiments and discover mechanisms that explain observations. We evaluate these mechanisms by the scientific insights that can be derived from them. EurekaBench contains an expert-verified set of 26 long-horizon tasks across neuroscience, computer science, chemistry, astrophysics, geophysics, and plasma physics, with a total of 306 scientific insights that the discovered mechanisms are expected to support. Our evaluation framework tests three axes of scientific discovery: agents' ability to follow known scientific constraints, the predictive accuracy of the discovered mechanisms, and whether these mechanisms yield scientific insights or inform future research. Our results show that current AI agents often overly fixate on predictive accuracy optimization, surpassing human scientists, while falling substantially short in deriving scientific insights.

</details>


### [72] [Assessing the Impact of Language Disparity on Multilingual Linguistic Ability in Large Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00540v1)
*Zhanyu Chen,Jaap Jumelet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 后训练会降低多语言大模型的语法能力，但对不同规模模型和资源语言的影响不均，低资源语言代价最高；未提示状态下的概率无法可靠测量低资源语言的语法知识，但母语提示可恢复隐藏能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型的语法能力评估结果因测量方法不同而差异显著，但评估范式、后训练和语言资源可用性之间的交互作用尚未得到系统研究。

Method: 使用MultiBLiMP（覆盖101种语言的句法最小对 benchmark）对六个模型家族的基座模型和后训练模型进行评估，采用四种评估方法。

Result: 三项主要发现：1）后训练降低语法能力，模型规模可部分缓解但对低资源语言影响最大；2）后训练模型保留有无法通过显式提示表达的知识，但仅在高资源语言中可测量；3）母语提示可恢复低资源语言中隐藏的能力，说明只能对高资源语言直接从未提示概率中探测语法能力。

Conclusion: 多语言语法评估必须采用语言感知的多范式协议，避免系统性地低估低资源语言的能力。

Abstract: Claims about the grammatical competence of multilingual language models vary sharply with how competence is measured, yet the interaction between evaluation paradigm, post-training, and language resource availability has not been systematically examined. We evaluate base and post-trained models from six families on MultiBLiMP, a syntactic minimal-pair benchmark covering 101 languages, using four evaluation methods. We report three principal findings. First, post-training degrades grammatical competence, but the magnitude of this effect is reduced unevenly by model scale, while low-resource languages bear the highest cost. Second, post-trained models retain grammatical knowledge they cannot articulate through explicit prompting, yet this is measurable only in high-resource languages, because near-chance baselines in low-resource settings leave little knowledge to hide. Third, native-language prompting recovers otherwise hidden competence on low-resource languages, demonstrating that only high-resource languages can be probed directly from unprompted probabilities. We conclude that multilingual grammatical evaluation must adopt language-informed, multi-paradigm protocols to avoid systematically underestimating low-resource abilities.

</details>


### [73] [Emergent Unfaithfulness: How Alignment Training Causes Language Models to Silently Override Task Faithfulness](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00568v1)
*Pardis Sadat Zahraei,Janvijay Singh,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐诱导的不忠实(AIU)"概念，指有对齐训练的模型在处理不安全或敏感内容时系统性地偏离输入却不披露修改。通过FaithConflict数据集和双 taxonomy分析，发现AIU随模型规模增加而增加（反向缩放定律），DPO阶段是AIU增长最明显且最不易被察觉的阶段，提示工程无法解决，揭示了LLM设计中能力-对齐-忠实三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已探讨了能力与对齐、能力与忠实之间的权衡，但第三个关键张力——对齐与忠实之间的冲突——尚未得到充分探索。这构成了本文研究的动机。

Method: 构建了FaithConflict受控数据集以隔离对齐冲突和知识冲突；设计了行为(B1-B8)和思维链推理(C0-C6)两个互补分类体系；在多个模型规模上进行实验对比；分析不同后训练阶段（SFT、RLHF、DPO）对AIU的影响。

Result: AIU随模型规模增长而增加，增幅超过能力驱动的不忠实，呈现"反向缩放定律"；中间检查点分析表明AIU在后训练阶段被放大，其中DPO是差距增长最快但最难被察觉的阶段；基于提示的缓解方法无法有效解决该问题。

Conclusion: LLM的设计与评估中存在能力-对齐-忠实三难困境。模型无法同时实现最大化能力、完全对齐人类价值观以及完全忠实于输入内容三者，这需要在未来研究中权衡取舍。

Abstract: Large language models are characterized by three key properties: capability, alignment, and faithfulness. Prior work studies the tradeoffs between capability and alignment, and between capability and faithfulness, but a third tension remains underexplored: the alignment-faithfulness conflict. We show that aligned models systematically deviate from their inputs on unsafe or sensitive content without disclosing the modification, a failure mode we call alignment-induced unfaithfulness (AIU). Unlike capability-driven unfaithfulness, which comes from errors in knowledge or reasoning, this is induced by post-training mechanisms that override adherence to the input. We introduce FaithConflict, a controlled dataset isolating both conflicts, and two complementary taxonomies: behavioral (B1-B8) and chain-of-thought reasoning (C0-C6). Across models, AIU increases with scale and more sharply than capability-driven unfaithfulness, a reverse scaling law; intermediate checkpoints show it is amplified during post-training, with DPO the stage at which the gap both grows most and becomes least visible. Prompting-based mitigation does not resolve it, revealing a capability-alignment-faithfulness trilemma in the design and evaluation of LLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Where's Waldo? Query-language Preference under Cross-lingual Knowledge Disparities](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00606v1)
*Dayeon Ki,Ruochen Zhang,Silviu Cucerzan,Ryen W. White,Ning Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Waldo基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在跨语言知识差异场景下的查询语言偏好行为，发现模型在知识冲突时强烈偏好查询语言版本，并通过注意力头消融和LoRA微调两种方法可有效缓解该偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要在跨语言知识等价假设下考察查询语言偏好，但当不同语言版本的Wikipedia提供不一致或不完整信息时，这种偏好会导致用户因语言选择不同而获得不同事实答案，具有重要现实影响。

Method: 构建Waldo多语言问答基准（12K QA对），包含知识差距（某语言缺失事实）和知识冲突（语言版本提供冲突信息）两类场景；在5种语言、8个模型上评估；探索注意力头消融和LoRA微调两种干预方法。

Result: 当某语言仅缺失知识时，模型能使用其他语言证据；但在知识冲突情况下，模型响应强烈依赖查询语言，导致等效语义查询因语言不同而产生不同答案；两种干预方法中LoRA可将偏好差距减少最多61.5%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在跨语言知识冲突场景下表现出显著的查询语言偏好，这一偏好会影响信息获取公平性；LoRA微调是有效缓解该偏好的可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models increasingly serve as interfaces for knowledge-intensive information seeking tasks across languages by synthesizing multilingual evidence. Prior work has shown that they often exhibit query-language preference -- the tendency to favor sources written in the language of the query -- but has largely examined this behavior in settings where equivalent knowledge is available across languages. However, this bias becomes consequential when sources in different languages provide incomplete or inconsistent accounts of the same fact, since the information users receive then depends on the sources a model selects to use. To characterize query-language preference under such cross-lingual knowledge disparities, we introduce Waldo, a multilingual Question-Answering (QA) benchmark constructed from Wikipedia. Waldo contains 12K QA pairs targeting knowledge gaps, where a fact is available in one language but absent in another, and knowledge conflicts, where language editions provide conflicting versions of the same fact. Evaluating eight models across five languages, we find that when one language edition merely lacks the relevant fact, models generally use evidence from the other language regardless of the query language. Under conflicting accounts, however, model responses strongly align with the document in the query language, causing semantically equivalent queries to elicit different accounts depending on the user's language. Finally, we explore two different approaches that could mitigate this preference under knowledge conflicts: a mechanistic intervention that ablates attention heads associated with query-language preference, and LoRA-based training, which reduces the preference gap by up to 61.5%.

</details>
