### [75] [Mixture of Decoders for Diverse Dialog Response Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00621v1)
*Wenchao Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对对话回复生成中序列到序列模型多样性不足的问题，提出在条件变分自编码器(CVAE)框架下引入混合解码器，使每个解码器学习不同的专业主题，从而提升生成回复的多样性。在开放域聊天语料上的定量指标和人工评估均显示了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列到序列模型在对话回复生成中存在低多样性问题，作者假设这是因为模型倾向于学习退化的单峰分布，导致生成的回复缺乏变化和丰富性。

Method: 将混合解码器集成到序列到序列模型中，在条件变分自编码器(CVAE)框架下运行，让每个解码器专注于学习特定主题，从而增加生成回复的多样性。

Result: 在开放域聊天语料上的实验表明，该方法在定量指标和人工评估上均优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: 混合解码器方法能有效改善对话回复生成的多样性问题，验证了通过多解码器架构捕获回复分布多模态性的可行性。

Abstract: Mixture modeling is a long established machine learning technique for learning large sets of multi-modal data. While it is known that sequence-to-sequence models for dialog response generation suffer from the problem of low diversity, we hypothesize that it is because sequence-to-sequence models tend to learn a degenerate uni-modal distribution of responses. We then propose to incorporate a mixture of decoders into sequence-to-sequence models and try to make each decoder learn specialized topics in order to improve the diversity of generated responses. Our model is developed under the framework of conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE). We evaluate our approach on an open domain chat corpus and show improvement over strong baselines in quantitative measures and human evaluation.

</details>


### [76] [Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00650v1)
*Zhengyuan Jiang,Reachal Wang,Yuepeng Hu,Yupu Wang,Yuqi Jia,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RuleEvolve是一个用于AI编码智能体的自进化框架，通过维护候选规则池并迭代改进来解决手动设计编码规则耗时且次优的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码规则依赖人工设计，过程劳动密集且往往无法达到最优性能，限制了AI编码智能体的发展。

Method: RuleEvolve维护候选规则池，每次迭代中：(1)使用LLM驱动的变异模块从现有候选者生成变体；(2)使用评判模块评估这些变体；(3)用表现最好的变体更新规则池。

Result: 在两个编码智能体框架、四个骨干LLM和三个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，RuleEvolve在功能正确性、代码长度和/或生成成本（如使用的token数）方面均优于人工工程和现有提示优化基线方法。

Conclusion: RuleEvolve通过自动化的自进化机制有效提升了编码规则的质量和性能，为AI编码智能体的规则优化提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: The performance of AI coding agents is highly dependent on their underlying coding rules. However, existing coding rules are typically hand-crafted and fixed, making the process labor-intensive and often suboptimal. In this work, we propose RuleEvolve, a self-evolving framework for coding rules. RuleEvolve maintains a pool of candidate coding rules and iteratively improves them. In each iteration, it employs an LLM-powered mutator module to generate variants from existing candidates, and then uses a judge module to evaluate these variants and update the pool with the best-performing ones. Extensive evaluations across two coding-agent frameworks, four backbone LLMs, and three benchmarks demonstrate that RuleEvolve outperforms both manual engineering and existing prompt optimization baselines in terms of functional correctness of the generated code, code length, and/or generation cost (e.g., tokens used).

</details>


### [77] [PhysicsMate: A Curriculum-Grounded Bengali Benchmark for Secondary Physics QA with Small-Model Adaptation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00664v1)
*Rashid Azraf Jahin,Saadman Sajid,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysicsMate是一个基于孟加拉国NCTB 9-10年级物理课程的知识图谱增强基准测试（1834个问答对），通过LoRA微调小模型显著提升闭卷准确率，4B模型经量化后可在资源受限环境下离线推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国中学STEM教育缺乏课程对齐的基准测试，现有通用语言模型难以处理物理问题的精确术语、单位换算和推导过程，需要专门的解决方案来弥合通用能力与课程特定知识之间的差距。

Method: 构建包含1760个节点和2600条边的多关系知识图谱（涵盖10种本体类型），基于国家课程教科书委员会(NCTB)9-10年级物理大纲开发1834个问答对基准；采用统一配方对0.6B、1.7B、4B参数模型进行LoRA低秩适配微调，并对4B模型进行量化以支持离线部署。

Result: LoRA微调在所有参数规模下均取得显著准确率提升（+5.5%、+15.0%、+23.3个百分点）；节点类型分析表明，结构化课程知识（物理量和定律命名）受益最大，而松散指定的实体级知识受益最小；4B模型成功量化并在资源受限环境中实现本地推理。

Conclusion: LoRA微调能有效弥补通用语言模型与课程特定物理知识之间的差距，尤其是对物理量和定律等结构化知识效果显著；量化的4B模型为低连接、低硬件条件下的课程对齐物理辅导提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Bengali secondary education lacks curriculum-grounded benchmarks for STEM question-solving, and general-purpose language models struggle with the precise terminology, unit conventions, and derivations that physics problems demand. We introduce PhysicsMate, a benchmark of 1834 question-answer pairs built from the National Curriculum and Textbook Board (NCTB) Grade 9-10 physics syllabus and grounded in a multi-relational knowledge graph of 1760 nodes and 2600 edges across ten ontological types. We Low-Rank Adapt at 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B parameters, with a unified recipe and demonstrate a significant increase in closed-book accuracy in all scales (+5.5, +15.0, and +23.3 percentage points). A node-type analysis shows that the most benefited by adaptation is the structured curricular knowledge, which consists of physical quantities and named laws, while the least benefited is the loosely specified entity-level knowledge. The 4B model has been adapted and quantized to a small offline binary that can be used for local inference in resource constrained environments and offers a viable path to curriculum aligned physics support in environments with limited connectivity and hardware.

</details>


### [78] [Closing the Loop: Practical Training Recipes for Looped Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00673v1)
*Andrei Marchenko,Viacheslav Bezrukov,Oleg Kashurin,Inessa Fedorova,Dmitry Bocharov,Yuliana Shakhvalieva,Maria Tikhonova,Valerii Ternovskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出循环语言模型(Looped Language Models)的高效训练方法，将预训练计算量从7.7T tokens降至310B tokens。1.4B LoopLM在12项基准测试中超越参数量相同的密集模型，匹配推理计算下逼近3.9B密集模型性能。还提出将预训练密集模型转换为循环模型的最小化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型需要多阶段训练和数万亿token的计算成本，且循环带来的收益难以从训练数据差异中分离出来。研究旨在建立实用的循环语言模型训练方案，降低训练成本并明确循环机制本身的收益。

Method: (1) 从零训练的计算高效流程：预训练+高质量中间训练+学习率预热+更强的出口门正则化；(2) 受控对比实验：保持数据、token预算和参数匹配；(3) 转换方案：单可学习输入混合标量+平滑出口损失，无需步骤特定参数。

Result: 1.4B LoopLM在12项基准测试中超越参数匹配密集模型：GSM8K +14分、MATH +10分、DROP +22分。匹配推理计算时，数学推理和阅读理解接近3.9B密集模型，仅用36%参数。转换后的Qwen3-1.7B在所有基准测试中超越持续训练的密集基线。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型从零训练成本大幅降低，引入现有预训练检查点也变为可行方案。研究成功分离出循环机制本身带来的性能提升，为循环架构的实际应用提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Looped language models increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block of layers, but existing large-scale recipes require multi-stage training over trillions of tokens, while the benefits of recurrence remain difficult to separate from differences in data and training. In this work, we establish practical training recipes for looped language models, with three main results. (1) We develop a compute-efficient from-scratch pipeline that reduces the training budget from 7.7T tokens in Ouro to 310B tokens while retaining strong reasoning performance. Pretraining followed by high-quality mid-training, together with learning-rate warmup and stronger exit-gate regularization, enables stable recurrent training without prior multi-stage schedules. (2) Under controlled comparisons, our 1.4B LoopLM outperforms a parameter-matched dense model trained on the same data and token budget on all 12 evaluated benchmarks, including +14 points on GSM8K, +10 on MATH, and +22 on DROP. At matched inference compute, it approaches a 3.9B dense model on mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension while using only 36\% as many parameters. (3) We introduce a minimal recipe for converting pretrained dense models into looped ones: a single learned input-mixing scalar and a smoothed exit loss, with no step-specific parameters. Applied to Qwen3-1.7B-Base, Looped Qwen improves over an identically continued dense baseline on every evaluated benchmark across two data regimes, with statistically clear gains on GSM8K, MATH, and MMLU-Pro on the curated mixture. Together, these results make looped language models substantially cheaper to train from scratch and practical to introduce into existing pretrained checkpoints, while isolating the gains due to recurrence itself.

</details>


### [79] [Towards Robust Numerical Claim Verification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00689v1)
*Peter Røysland Aarnes,Vinay Setty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型（LLM）在声明验证中数值推理能力脆弱的问题，提出通过对抗性微调（adversarial fine-tuning）结合参数高效微调技术来增强模型鲁棒性。实验表明，经过对抗微调的轻量级模型（Qwen3 0.6B-8B）在标签翻转扰动测试中达到98.7%准确率，显著超越GPT-5.4 Pro（74.0%）和Gemini 2.5 Flash（73.9%）等前沿系统，且该方法具有良好的泛化能力和跨语言迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在声明验证任务中的数值推理能力存在严重脆弱性：即使数值发生微小变化也会导致准确率急剧下降。这一问题在包括前沿模型在内的各类LLM中普遍存在，限制了其在需要精确数值判断场景中的应用。

Method: 采用对抗性微调策略，在数值扰动样本上进行参数高效微调（parameter-efficient fine-tuning）。实验对比了不同规模模型（0.6B至8B参数）的性能表现。

Result: 经对抗微调的Qwen3小模型在标签翻转扰动测试中达到98.7%准确率，远超GPT-5.4 Pro（74.0%）和Gemini 2.5 Flash（73.9%）。该方法在未见过的扰动类型上同样表现出色，表明模型习得的是鲁棒的数值决策边界而非记忆特定修改。跨语言迁移实验（西班牙语）也验证了方法的有效性，且在证据端扰动的VitaminC数据集上同样有效。

Conclusion: 对抗性微调结合参数高效微调可有效解决LLM数值推理脆弱性问题。该方法具有良好泛化性、跨语言迁移能力，并能扩展至证据端扰动场景，为构建可靠的数值感知声明验证系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for claim verification, yet remain brittle for numerical reasoning: even small changes in value can sharply degrade accuracy. We show that this brittleness persists in frontier LLMs, but can be mitigated through adversarial fine-tuning on numerically perturbed examples. Using parameter-efficient fine-tuning, small Qwen3 models (0.6B$\unicode{x2013}$8B) reach 98.7% accuracy on label-flipping perturbations, outperforming larger zero-shot models and frontier systems (GPT-5.4 Pro (74.0%) and Gemini 2.5 Flash (73.9%)). The gains generalise to unseen perturbation types, indicating robust numerical decision boundaries rather than memorised edits. Robustness also transfers without target-domain data, significantly improving cross-lingual performance in Spanish. We further show that the same fine-tuning recipe confers robustness to evidence-side perturbations, using the VitaminC dataset.

</details>


### [80] [How Divergence Becomes Decision Flips in Compressed Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00694v1)
*Beatriz Almeida Felicio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文证明总变差距离(Total Variation)是评估压缩语言模型决策变化的标准，比KL散度更直接准确。在802个实验配置下，翻转率与总变差的比值中位数为1.05，而KL散度因先平均后开方导致其平方根与翻转率之间存在四倍变化的换算因子，使得在10%翻转率差异下，KL可能给出错误排序的概率为11%，而总变差仅为1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署压缩语言模型时，需要知道压缩模型与原始密集模型的决策（arg-max token）有多少发生了变化。现有方法使用KL散度来衡量这种偏离，但KL散度并非直接回答"有多少决策改变"这一问题的指标。

Method: 在802个压缩和扰动变体、19个开源模型、5个语料库和9个不相关的扰动族上进行大规模实验，系统比较总变差距离与KL散度对翻转率的预测能力。

Result: 翻转率与总变差距离的比值中位数为1.05，无需任何拟合常数。KL散度的平方根与翻转率的换算因子在不同模型和语料库间存在四倍变化。预注册测试验证了总变差在留出语料和新模型上的鲁棒性。在vLLM推测解码中，总变差在teacher forcing下可预测greedy draft acceptance，平均相对误差仅1.1%-2.4%。

Conclusion: 总变差距离是评估压缩语言模型决策稳定性的正确指标，比KL散度更直接、更可靠。Hellinger距离等token级统计量也优于KL。建议在未来压缩报告和部署场景中使用总变差或token级指标替代KL散度。

Abstract: Compression reports summarize how far a compressed language model moved from the dense one, usually by a KL divergence; a deployment that relies on the dense model's outputs needs to know how many of its decisions changed. We show that total variation, not KL, answers this directly. Across 802 compressed and perturbed copies of 19 open models on five corpora and nine mechanically unrelated perturbation families, the rate at which the arg-max token changes (the \emph{flip rate}) tracks total variation at a ratio with median $1.05$, with no fitted constant. KL converts into flips only through its square root and a factor that varies fourfold across models and corpora, because KL averages over tokens before the root is taken; first-order statistics averaged per token, such as Hellinger distance, avoid this, but reports rarely give them. As a result, of two compressors reported on different models and corpora whose flip rates differ by at least $10%$, KL assigns the smaller divergence to the one that changes more decisions in $11%$ of cases, total variation in $1%$. Two pre-registered tests mark the limits: on a held-out code corpus the ratio held for all eight models while three predictions about KL each failed for half of them or more, and on three new models with real kernels it stayed in its band for 37 of 38 checkpoints but fell below one on code for two models. In vLLM speculative decoding, total variation measured under teacher forcing predicts greedy draft acceptance with a mean relative error of $1.1$--$2.4%$, without the task-specific calibration that KL needs.

</details>


### [81] [Reason in Style: Discovering and Controlling Style in Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00724v1)
*Ioana Marinescu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型输出中风格变异的无监督发现与控制问题。通过设计分离内容与风格的表示学习算法，从9个教师模型的10万+条推理轨迹中发现了6种反复出现但分布不均衡的风格，并据此微调学生模型。实验表明，该方法在6个数学推理基准上显著提升了Pass@k性能，且风格对正确性有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型同时学习内容和风格，导致输出风格难以识别和控制。现有方法缺乏对模型生成数据中风格变异的系统性发现与显式控制能力。

Method: 设计算法从语言模型输出中无监督地分离内容和风格表示；在超过10万条来自9个教师模型的验证轨迹上发现风格模式；使用重要性加权微调学生模型，使其能根据指定风格生成答案。

Result: 发现6种反复出现但分布不均衡的风格；微调后的学生模型可在指定风格条件下生成答案，Pass@k在6个数学推理基准上优于标准微调；风格请求与实现之间具有强对应关系。

Conclusion: 语言模型生成数据中的风格变异可以无监督地被发现并显式化，这为控制模型输出风格并提升推理性能提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Language models learn content and style jointly, making stylistic variation in their outputs difficult to identify and control. We study whether recurring styles in model responses can be discovered without supervision and explicitly controlled. We design an algorithm that learns to separate representations of content and style from language models' outputs and validate its effectiveness on math questions in a controlled setting. By applying this method to over 100K verified traces from nine distinct teacher models, we discover six recurring yet imbalanced styles. We then fine-tune smaller student models to follow these styles when explicitly conditioned on them, using importance weighting to balance the contribution of the styles represented in the corpus. This approach improves Pass@$k$ over standard fine-tuning on the same data across six math reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating that we can diversify the style of answers effectively. We confirm that this also results in strong correspondence between requested and realized styles. We find that style affects correctness: the probability of solving a problem depends on the style we condition on, and different problems benefit from different styles. In summary, our results show that stylistic variation in model-generated data can be discovered in an unsupervised way, and made explicit, providing a source of both control and improved reasoning performance.

</details>


### [82] [Effective Synthetic Data Curation Requires Group-Level Signals](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00779v1)
*Cathy Jiao,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了个体级信号在合成数据筛选中的不足，指出组级信号对于有效的数据筛选至关重要，尤其在训练流程日益合成化的趋势下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在大语言模型训练中日益重要，但在大规模训练时可能导致模型生成能力下降。现有数据筛选方法依赖个体级信号，无法捕捉样本间的交互效应，因此需要更有效的数据筛选策略。

Method: 研究通过对比个体级信号与组级信号在预训练和后训练场景下的效果，分析不同合成数据集的组合如何影响训练过程，并评估现有数据筛选方法的性能差异。

Result: 1) 个体级信号无法识别样本组合效应，不同组成的合成数据集在个体级影响下可能无法区分，但组级影响差异显著，按组级信号筛选可获得更好的下游性能；2) 在合成化程度越高的训练流程中，组级信号的重要性越高，仅纳入组级信息的数据筛选方法能超越基线；3) 放大样本关系权重可进一步提升筛选效果。

Conclusion: 对于有计算预算约束的模型开发者，提供了一种廉价诊断方法，可优先识别最需要组级估计的合成数据组，以极低的计算成本恢复接近完整的组级评分收益。

Abstract: Synthetic data now is essential to LLM training, used to strengthen advanced capabilities such as autonomous and long-horizon task execution. Yet recent work shows that training on it at scale can degrade model generation, making it important to decide what synthetic data is worth training on. While current data curation practices do so with individual-level signals (i.e., estimates of each data sample's training utility in isolation), across pre-training and post-training settings we show that this is insufficient for synthetic data, and that group-level signals (i.e., estimates of utility that account for interactions among data samples) are necessary for effective data curation. First, we show that individual-level signals are blind to how samples jointly affect training: synthetic datasets with different compositions can be indistinguishable under individual-level influence yet differ sharply under group-level influence, and curating by the latter yields better downstream performance, particularly in generative capability. Second, we find that group-level signals matter more as training pipelines become increasingly synthetic: among widely used data curation methods, only those incorporating them improve over baseline, with gains increasing when weights capturing relations among samples are amplified. Finally, we translate these findings into practice -- for model developers under a compute budget, we offer a cheap diagnostic that prioritizes which groups of synthetic data most need group-level estimation, recovering much of the benefit of full group-level scoring at a fraction of the compute cost.

</details>


### [83] [Can large language models unlock discrete data in ophthalmic diagnostic reports?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00795v1)
*Umair A. Zaidi,An-Lun Wu,Wei-Chun Lin,Thomas S. Hwang,Michelle R. Hribar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GPT-4o在从眼科诊断PDF报告中提取结构化数据方面的准确性和效率，对比了两种提示策略（Schema-Constrained和Prompt-Only）。结果表明两种方法均能实现高精度提取，时间缩短约92%，证明了LLM辅助眼科数据抽象的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼科诊断报告中包含大量结构化数据，但传统人工提取方式效率低下且耗时。本研究旨在探索LLM在医疗数据提取中的应用潜力，以提升眼科研究及临床数据抽象的效率。

Method: 研究纳入20份去标识化报告（涵盖视野、OCT青光眼概述、RNFL单次检查及厚度图四种类型），分别采用两种GPT-4o辅助 pipeline：Schema-Constrained（结构化输出模式+预定义JSON Schema）和Prompt-Only（详细指令提示+Python转换）。评估指标包括数值准确率、格式准确率和提取时间。

Result: Schema-Constrained数值准确率：视野100%、青光眼概述97.45%、厚度图98%；Prompt-Only在所有报告类型中均达到100%。格式准确率方面，Schema-Constrained为100%，Prompt-Only除RNFL检查外为100%。提取时间：人工审查56.51秒/份，两种方法分别为5.04秒和4.70秒，缩短约92%。

Conclusion: LLM辅助pipeline在眼科PDF诊断报告的结构化数据提取中表现出高精度和高效能。Prompt-Only数值准确率最高，Schema-Constrained格式合规性最优，两者互补优势支持开发混合验证工作流程，具有临床和科研应用前景。

Abstract: Objective: To assess the accuracy and efficiency of a large language model (LLM) using two prompt strategies to extract structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDF reports. Methods: Twenty deidentified reports across four types (Visual Field, OCT Glaucoma Overview, OCT retinal nerve fiber layer Single Exam, and OCT Thickness Map; n = 5 each) were processed using two GPT-4o-assisted pipelines and compared with a reconciled manual ground truth. Schema-Constrained used Structured Output mode with a predefined JSON Schema; Prompt-Only used a detailed instruction prompt followed by Python conversion to JSON. Outcomes were value accuracy, formatting accuracy, and extraction time. Results: Schema-Constrained value accuracy was 100.00% for Visual Field and RNFL Single Exam, 97.45% for Glaucoma Overview, and 98.00% for Thickness Map; Prompt-Only achieved 100.00% across all four report types. Formatting accuracy was 100.00% for Schema-Constrained across all report types and 100.00% for Prompt-Only except RNFL Single Exam (90.14%). Mean extraction time was 56.51 s per report for manual review versus 5.04 s for Schema-Constrained and 4.70 s for Prompt-Only, an approximately 92% reduction. Conclusions: In this small proof-of-concept dataset, general-purpose LLM-assisted pipelines extracted structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDFs with high accuracy and substantially reduced processing time. Prompt-Only achieved the highest value accuracy, while Schema-Constrained produced schema-compliant output with 100% formatting accuracy. These complementary strengths support further evaluation of hybrid, validation-aware workflows for research and clinical data abstraction.

</details>


### [84] [VERITYGATE: A Four-Gate Schema-Level Faithfulness Framework and Paired Benchmark for Grounded LLM Narrations over Structured Evidence](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00833v1)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VERITYGATE是一个用于验证LLM生成解释是否遵循结构化证据的四门检验器。研究测试了GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.3-70B和Claude Sonnet 4.6，结果显示80.3%的mini声明和47.9%的Sonnet声明未能通过检验。经一次修复后，生存率仅略有提升。Gate 4覆盖了97-100%的失败声明，但schema级检查与实际文本正确性之间存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的流畅解释可能与结构化系统中的证据不一致，需要一个可扩展的验证框架来检测声明中的幻觉问题，同时避免逐句验证的高成本。

Method: VERITYGATE采用四门检查机制：声明的证据ID、实体、数字和声明类型的验证。在r=0和r=1两种设置下，使用三种模型测试900个实例（含450个有据/无据配对），并进行修复通道实验和人类评估。

Result: 在schema级合同下，80.3%的GPT-4o-mini声明和47.9%的Sonnet声明失败。一次修复后生存率从19.7%升至28.0%（mini）和52.1%升至54.3%（Sonnet）。Gate 4覆盖97-100%的失败声明。人类研究表明schema检查与正确文本之间存在差距，GPT-4o法官测试显示顺序效应。

Conclusion: Schema级验证器可有效捕获大部分失败声明，但修复效果有限且模型间差异显著。验证器拒绝率≠文本幻觉率，两者需分开报告。领域特定法官测试仅作为有用性检查，非真实性验证。

Abstract: Fluent LLM explanations may not follow the evidence from a structured system. We present VERITYGATE, a four-gate checker for declared evidence IDs, entities, numbers, and claim types. It checks a fixed schema; it does not verify every fact in the prose. At r=0 and r=1, we test 900 instances per setting (450 grounded-ungrounded pairs) with GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.3-70B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Under this schema-level contract and before repair, 80.3% of mini claims and 47.9% of Sonnet claims fail. These are verifier rejection rates, not prose-hallucination rates. One repair pass raises claim survival from 19.7% to 28.0% for mini and from 52.1% to 54.3% for Sonnet. Verified claims per example change by +0.14 for mini, -0.71 for Llama, and -0.47 for Sonnet, so survival and output volume must be reported together. A second Sonnet pass gives no clear gain. At r=1, Gate 4 covers 97.0%, 98.7%, and 100% of failing claims for mini, Llama, and Sonnet. Small human studies support the rules but show gaps between schema checks and correct prose. A domain-specific GPT-4o judge test shows an order effect, so it is only a usefulness check. We release the code and data.

</details>


### [85] [DeBERTa-ConPara: Attack-Aware and Deployment-Realistic Detection of AI-Generated Text](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00883v1)
*Mohamed Mady,Yupei Li,Johannes Reschke,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeBERTa-ConPara，一种面向部署的AI生成文本检测器，通过在推理阶段进行Unicode规范化预处理来防御对抗攻击，在RAID隐藏测试集上达到99.61% AUROC。研究发现预处理应用位置至关重要：规范化训练语料会导致35.4%的RAID数据被去重删除对抗监督，而仅在推理时规范化则效果最佳。此外，语义保持增强、对比学习和手工特征融合分支均未能提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本检测器在部署条件下面临严峻挑战：领域和生成器间的分布偏移、输入表面的对抗扰动、以及缺乏目标领域标签进行阈值校准，导致实验室环境下表现良好的检测器性能大幅下降。

Method: 提出DeBERTa-ConPara检测器，结合攻击感知的Unicode预处理与上下文Transformer编码器。核心发现：预处理的应用位置产生相反效果——规范化训练语料会将35.4%的RAID行合并为干净样本的副本，导致对抗监督信号丢失；而在推理阶段规范化则是有效的防御手段。采用析因实验设计独立变化两个放置位置。

Result: 原始训练+规范化推理为最佳配置：在官方RAID隐藏测试集上达到99.61% AUROC、99.01% TPR@5% FPR和96.57% TPR@1% FPR；在HC3 Plus和MAGE上使用固定阈值达到93.14%平均平衡准确率。性能提升仅集中于两种攻击类型：同形异义词和零宽空格插入攻击的检测率分别从11.05%和1.12%提升至96.98%。该效果在不同架构的零样本检测器上可复现，证明属于攻击本身的特性而非模型特性。

Conclusion: 仅在推理阶段进行Unicode规范化预处理是防御AI生成文本检测对抗攻击的有效策略，而训练阶段规范化反而有害。语义保持增强、对比学习和手工特征融合在当前设置下未能提供额外收益。

Abstract: Robust detection of AI-generated text under deployment conditions is challenging: distribution shifts across domains and generators, adversarial perturbations of the input surface, and the absence of target-domain labels for threshold calibration all degrade detectors that perform well in-domain. We present DeBERTa-ConPara, a deployment-oriented detector combining attack-aware Unicode preprocessing with a contextual transformer encoder trained over HC3 Plus, M4, MAGE and RAID. Our central finding is that preprocessing acts in opposite directions depending on where it is applied: normalising the training corpus deduplicates it, collapsing 35.4% of RAID rows into copies of their clean siblings and deleting the adversarial supervision, whereas normalising at inference is an effective defence. A factorial varying the two placements independently identifies raw training with normalised inference as the best configuration, reaching 99.61% AUROC, 99.01% TPR@5% FPR and 96.57% TPR@1% FPR on the official RAID hidden test, alongside 93.14% average balanced accuracy across HC3 Plus and MAGE under a fixed threshold. The gain is confined to two of twelve attack classes: homoglyph and zero-width-space insertion rise from 11.05% and 1.12% to 96.98%. The same signature reproduces in a zero-shot detector of different architecture, showing the effect belongs to the attacks rather than to our model. We additionally report two negative results: semantic-invariance augmentation through paraphrasing and supervised contrastive learning (ConPara) does not improve the best configuration, and the handcrafted feature-fusion branch is inert in distribution and harmful outside it.

</details>


### [86] [ReHoPER: Receding-Horizon Planning for Enhanced Reasoning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00940v1)
*Saeed Ahmadnia,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReHoPER是一种无需训练、零样本的方法，通过在给出最终答案前沿多条路径生成并回答中间问题来提升大语言模型的推理能力。该方法迭代式地规划候选中间问题、选择其一作答，并基于更新后的历史进行重新规划，具有任务无关性，可在无需标注数据或任务特定提示设计的情况下跨数据集和模型通用。在多个数据集上超越强基线，在最具组合性的设置中提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在复杂推理任务中仍面临挑战，尤其是需要多步组合推理的问题。现有方法通常需要针对特定任务的提示设计或依赖大量标注数据进行微调，缺乏通用性和可扩展性。

Method: ReHoPER采用迭代式规划框架：首先规划多个候选中间问题，从中选择一个进行回答，然后基于更新后的历史状态重新规划。该方法的核心特点包括：(1) 多路径探索：在最终答案前探索多条推理路径；(2) 零样本适应：使用通用指令，无需任务特定设计；(3) 无需训练：纯推理阶段优化。

Result: 在多个数据集上显著超越强基线方法，且在最具组合性的设置中提升幅度最大。

Conclusion: ReHoPER证明了通过迭代式中间问题生成与回答策略，配合多路径探索机制，可有效提升大语言模型的组合推理能力。该方法的任务无关性和零样本特性使其具有良好的通用性和实用价值。新提出的iLLC基准测试也为未来组合推理研究提供了标准化评估工具。

Abstract: We propose ReHoPER, an inference-only, zero-shot method that improves large language models' reasoning by generating and answering intermediate questions along multiple paths before the final answer. It iteratively plans a horizon of candidate intermediate questions, selects one to answer, and replans from the updated history. ReHoPER is task-agnostic, using the same generic instructions across datasets and models without labeled data or task-specific prompt design. Across multiple datasets, including iLLC, a new controlled benchmark for compositional reasoning, ReHoPER outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in the most compositional settings. Our implementation and the iLLC generator are publicly available to support future work.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Leaderboards: Tokenomics of Agentic Small Language Model Ensembles](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00954v1)
*Alexei N. Skurikhin,Emily M. Taylor,Nathan A. DeBardeleben*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对AI智能体系统的评估需超越传统准确率指标，转向多维度运营评估。作者通过SLM集成+SLM-judge反馈环路的案例研究，在IFEval基准上达到97.34%准确率，超越GPT-5.4基线5.81个百分点，同时保持低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM评估依赖榜单排名和准确率，但当LLM进入智能体工作流时，需考虑运营可靠性、成本、延迟和Token效率等多维因素。当前评估范式不足以支撑这类系统的全面评估需求。

Method: 构建SLM智能体集成系统，采用SLM-judge介导的反馈环路；使用IFEval基准（541个提示）进行评估，涵盖指令类别和约束数量等多维度分析。

Result: 最佳集成方案达到97.34%严格提示准确率，比最强独立LLM基线GPT-5.4高5.81个百分点；分析了成本/样本、有效输出吞吐量、反馈环路恢复、延迟分解及不同指令类别表现。

Conclusion: 智能体SLM集成可通过增加测试时Token和编排开销换取更高的指令遵循保真度；未来AI系统评估需要多维度评估协议。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) move from standalone assistants into agentic workflows, evaluation must extend beyond scalar leaderboard accuracy to account for operational reliability, cost, latency, and token efficiency. We use an agentic ensemble of small language models (SLMs) with an SLM-judge-mediated feedback loop as a case study for such beyond-leaderboard evaluation. On the 541-prompt IFEval benchmark, the best ensemble achieves 97.34% strict prompt accuracy, exceeding the strongest standalone LLM baseline, gpt-5.4, by 5.81 percentage points while operating in a lower-cost regime. We then analyze the tokenomics and operational behavior behind this gain, including cost per sample, token composition, useful-output goodput, feedback-loop recovery, latency decomposition, and performance across instruction categories and constraint counts. Our results show that agentic SLM ensembles can trade additional test-time tokens and orchestration overhead for improved instruction-following fidelity, motivating multi-dimensional evaluation protocols for future agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [88] [A Citation-Grounded Benchmark for Trustworthy Earnings Call Transcript Analysis with Large Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00969v1)
*Yingzhu Zhao,Vlad Pandelea,Han Yuan,Bo Hu,Wuqiong Luo,Li Zhang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需专家标注即可评估金融文档分析中LLM生成内容可靠性的方法，并构建了ECTs-100基准数据集。研究重点关注"意识性无能"（conscious incompetence）这一失败模式，即LLM需识别证据不足并避免产生幻觉。研究发现LLM在可靠性评估上表现良好，但在正确性上存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在金融文档分析中的应用日益广泛，用户需要带有可验证引用的 grounded analysis，但传统评估方法依赖昂贵的专家标注，且金融分析涉及长上下文问答，增加任务复杂度。现有方法难以大规模评估LLM的金融分析能力。

Method: 1) 提出numeric evidence evaluation method，实现无专家标注的groundedness评估；2) 开发自动化数据集构建pipeline；3) 构建ECTs-100数据集（来自标普500前100成分股）；4) 定义并检验conscious incompetence问题（LLM需检测证据不足并避免幻觉）。

Result: LLM在groundedness（可靠性/groundedness）方面表现良好，但在correctness（正确性）方面存在显著不足；informational insufficiency（信息不足）是额外挑战。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在金融文档分析中能够较好地提供有据可查的分析，但生成的答案准确性仍需改进，且当可用证据不足以支撑结论时，LLM识别不足的能力有待提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly used for financial document analysis, including earnings call transcripts (ECTs). Beyond generating standalone claims, users increasingly prefer grounded analyses that pair claims with verifiable citations from source documents to enable independent validation. However, evaluating such analytical claims typically requires extensive expert annotation, which is costly and difficult to scale, and real-world financial analysis commonly involves long context-question-answer triplets, further increasing task complexity. To address these challenges and benchmark the current landscape of grounded analysis by LLMs, we propose a numeric evidence evaluation method that enables groundedness assessment without reliance on expert annotation. We also introduce an automated dataset construction pipeline and construct ECTs-100 from the top 100 constituents of the S&P 500 to support benchmark of both groundedness and correctness. In addition, we examine conscious incompetence, a practical failure mode in financial analysis in which LLMs must detect when available evidence is insufficient and refrain from producing unsupported hallucinations. Empirical results show that LLMs perform well in groundedness but face notable limitations in correctness, with informational insufficiency presenting an additional challenge.

</details>


### [89] [Sentence Specificity Scores for Collaborative Technical Documentation: A Domain-Transfer Study](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01046v1)
*Rocker D'Antonio,Thomas Benton Townsend,Dimitrios Michael Manias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究审计了句子特异性评分工具在技术文档上的表现，并测试这些评分是否有助于在LLM生成的固定修订版本中进行选择。研究比较了通用领域预测器SpeciTeller与针对目标文档适配的Ko等人方法，发现不同预测器在不同语料库上产生不同排序，且严格过滤和token长度调整无法调和这些差异。在Gemma集中，SpeciTeller排序将方向有效选择率从71.7%提升至83.3%；在GPT-OSS-120B集中，提升幅度较小且置信区间包含零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术文档是团队协作中跨人员和AI队友共享上下文的重要方式，而特异性（specificity）——语言中表达细节的量和精确度——决定了文档捕获信息的质量。然而，现有的特异性评分工具在不同文档类型上的表现差异尚未被充分审计，且这些评分能否有效指导LLM生成文本的选择仍不清楚。

Method: 研究者在Wikipedia和三个技术文档语料库上，对比了通用领域特异性预测器SpeciTeller与Ko等人提出的目标适配预测器。通过严格过滤和token长度调整进行消融实验，并使用bootstrap置信区间评估SpeciTeller排序在不同LLM生成版本中的选择效果。

Result: 两个预测器在不同语料库上产生不同的排序（相关系数从-0.066到0.510），且调整策略无法协调差异。SpeciTeller排序在Gemma集中将方向有效选择率提升11.7个百分点（71.7%→83.3%），但在GPT-OSS-120B集中仅提升5.0个百分点且置信区间包含零。

Conclusion: 特异性评分的解释和决策价值高度依赖于预测器选择和候选集构成。不同评分工具可能产生不一致甚至相反的排序结果，在实际应用中需要根据具体场景谨慎选择合适的评分方法。

Abstract: Collaboration depends on shared context, and technical documentation is one way that context persists across people and AI teammates. Specificity, the amount and exactness of detail expressed in language, shapes what information documentation captures and how precisely that information is communicated. This work audits sentence-specificity scoring artifacts on technical documentation and tests whether scores applied only after generation help choose among fixed LLM-generated revisions. Across Wikipedia and three technical-documentation corpora, the fixed general-domain predictor SpeciTeller and the pinned post-publication author-repository implementation of Ko et al.'s target-adapted predictor produce different corpus orders and same-sentence rank agreement from -0.066 to 0.510. Strict filtering and token-length adjustment change these patterns without reconciling them. In the Gemma set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 71.7% to 83.3% (+11.7 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval +1.7 to +21.7); in the GPT-OSS-120B set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 51.7% to 56.7% (+5.0 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval -6.7 to +16.7), and every primary single-score GPT-OSS-120B interval includes zero. These findings tie score interpretation and decision value to the predictor and candidate set.

</details>


### [90] [JoinGR: Learning to Traverse Join Graphs for Table Retrieval](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01064v1)
*Sandipan De,Abhijit Chakraborty,Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOINGR通过将数据库join图作为检索空间，结合图结构遍历和查询条件评分器来解决Text-to-SQL中的表格检索问题，在多跳表格需求场景下显著提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集表格检索器独立对schema元素排序，忽略了一个关键证据源：某些必需的表格并未在问题中直接提及，只能通过与已相关表格的join关系来识别。

Method: 将列表示为图节点，内表关系和外键关系表示为类型化边。给定问题后，JOINGR选择语义相似的锚点表格，用查询条件评分器遍历join边，聚合边沉积为表格分数。评分器是在冻结的查询、节点和边嵌入之上的轻量级MLP，使用gold表格上的成对边界损失训练。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider数据集上与最强检索基线相当；在BEAVER（具有多跳表格需求的企业级挑战基准）上，召回率显著优于密集检索和重排序基线。跨域实验表明学习的评分器可迁移到其他基准。

Conclusion: JOINGR通过建模数据库join图结构捕获了可复用的join图遍历行为，学习的评分器具有良好的跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Retrieving the right tables is a prerequisite for Text-to-SQL over realistic databases. Dense table retrievers rank schema elements independently, but this ignores a key source of evidence: some required tables are not mentioned in the question and become identifiable only through their join relationships to already relevant tables. We introduce JOINGR, a join-aware table retrieval method that treats the database join graph as the retrieval space. Columns are represented as graph nodes, while intra-table and foreign-key relationships are represented as typed edges. Given a question, JOINGR selects semantically similar anchor tables, traverses join edges with a query-conditioned scorer, and aggregates the resulting edge deposits into table scores. The scorer is a lightweight MLP on top of frozen query, node, and edge embeddings, trained with a pairwise margin loss over gold tables. On BIRD and Spider datasets, JOINGR is competitive with the strongest retrieval baselines. On BEAVER, a challenging enterprise benchmark with multi-hop table requirements, JOINGR substantially improves recall over dense retrieval and re-ranking baselines. Cross-domain experiments show that the learned scorer transfers across benchmarks, indicating that the method captures reusable joingraph traversal behavior.

</details>


### [91] [Precision over Scale: A Polish-Silesian Benchmark and a Translation System Outperforming Open-Source and Commercial Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01082v1)
*Grzegorz Kulik,Mikołaj Pokrywka,Adam Jatowt,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对波兰语-西里西亚语方言机器翻译，对比了神经机器翻译与规则系统，并发布了新的SiLTT测试集。结果表明规则系统在方言翻译任务上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方言机器翻译面临数据稀缺和语言变异大的挑战，现有基准测试集通常基于标准化的编辑文本，无法充分评估方言翻译能力。

Method: 使用神经机器翻译系统和规则系统进行波兰语-西里西亚语翻译对比评测，评测数据集包括新发布的SiLTT测试集以及BOUQuET和FLORES基准。

Result: 规则系统在SiLTT和BOUQuET数据集上表现最佳；TranslateGemma在精选数据集微调后优于强基线神经模型，但未超越规则系统在方言翻译中的表现。

Conclusion: 规则系统在方言机器翻译任务中仍具优势，神经模型虽有进步但难以完全捕获方言的语言特征。SiLTT数据集和最佳神经模型已开源以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Dialectal machine translation remains challenging due to limited data and strong linguistic variation not captured by standard benchmarks, which often assume standardized and well-edited text. We study Polish-Silesian MT using neural and rule-based systems, evaluating on SiLTT - a new Pol-Szl testset, alongside established BOUQuET and FLORES benchmarks. Results show our rule-based system is consistently strongest on SiLTT and BOUQuET datasets and that TranslateGemma fine-tuned on a curated dataset improves over strong neural baselines but does not surpass the rule-based system in dialectal settings. We release SiLTT and our best neural model to support further research.

</details>


### [92] [Counting and Min-Cost Encoding for Tokenization in Large Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01127v1)
*Shuming Shi,Xiang Zhang,Hao Yu,Wenbo Fei,Changjian Wang,Zhan Wang,Guoqing Pang,Guangye Yu,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CNF（计数过滤）和MCE（最小成本编码）方法用于改进大语言模型的 tokenizer。与传统BPE方法相比，CNF-MCE在多种文本类别上实现了更高的压缩率和token效率（提升26%-60%+），且模型在1.8B和8B规模下性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大语言模型依赖tokenizer将文本编码为token序列。不同tokenizer对同一文本可能产生差异显著的长度序列，而固定模型架构下更短的序列意味着更低的推理成本。现有BPE等方法在词汇表利用率和压缩效率上存在不足。

Method: 1. CNF方法：通过直接统计有效子串构建原始词汇表，再基于MCE在实际语料上的token使用情况进行过滤；2. MCE算法：定义文本片段的成本函数，通过全局最小化总体分割成本来确定最佳分词方案，不依赖merge list（BPE）或token概率（UnigramLM）。

Result: 在六类文本和两种词汇规模下，CNF-MCE始终优于BPE tokenizer。250K词汇量下，英文网页文本压缩率提升26%和30%；1M词汇量下，token效率提升超60%，词汇利用率从52.9%升至96.9%。1.8B和8B模型在11个基准上与BPE模型性能相当。

Conclusion: CNF-MCE能显著提升token效率，同时保持竞争力的下游性能，且具有更广泛的适用性（可配合BPE、UnigramLM等多种词汇表使用）。

Abstract: Mainstream large language models rely on a tokenizer to encode text into a token sequence. Different tokenizers may yield token sequences of substantially different lengths for the same text. With a fixed model architecture, shorter token sequences correspond to lower inference time. We propose a tokenizer training approach named Counting and Filtering (CNF) and a text encoding algorithm called Min-Cost Encoding (MCE). MCE defines a cost function over a text segment, and determines the best segmentation by globally minimizing the overall segmentation cost. CNF builds a raw vocabulary by directly counting valid substrings, and then constructs the final vocabulary through a filtering step based on actual token usage when segmenting the training corpus with MCE. The CNF-MCE conbination offers several advantages over BPE, including higher token efficiency, greater scalability, and lower dependency. Across six text categories and two vocabulary-size groups, CNF-MCE consistently achieves better compression than the evaluated BPE tokenizers. With a 250K vocabulary, CNF-MCE increases compression rate by 26% and 30% on English web text over the o200k_base and qwen250k tokenizers. Experiments scaling the vocabulary to 1M entries on English web text demonstrate sustained improvements over BPE, with a token efficiency improvement of over 60% and vocabulary utilization rising from 52.9% to 96.9%. The MCE algorithm does not depend on a merge list (as in BPE) or token probability (as in UnigramLM), making it applicable to a wide range of vocabularies, including those built from BPE, UnigramLM, CNF, and others. Language models trained from scratch at the 1.8B and 8B scales achieve comparable average performance to models using the BPE tokenizers across 11 benchmarks. These results demonstrate that CNF-MCE can improve token efficiency significantly while maintaining competitive downstream performance.

</details>


### [93] [BanglaDial-Abuse: A Corpus-Grounded Dataset for Regional Dialect Identification in Abusive Bangla Text](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01150v1)
*Hasin Almas Sifat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BanglaDial-Abuse数据集，一个用于孟加拉语区域方言识别的平衡数据集，包含1,000个句子，覆盖标准孟加拉语及Chattagram、Sylhet、Barishal三种区域方言。该数据集通过语料库驱动的合成方法生成，保留了虐待性/敌对性语义但融入区域语言变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语区域语言变体对自然语言处理构成重要挑战，尤其在非正式和非标准文本中。现有的孟加拉语NLP资源缺乏针对区域方言识别的标注数据，现有的虐待性文本数据集也未能考虑方言差异。

Method: 采用语料库驱动的合成程序构建数据集，将区域语言变体融入标准文本，同时保留其敌对或虐待性含义。变体维度涵盖：代词、所有格形式、动词形态、否定结构、疑问结构、后置词、词汇及孟加拉文字拼写惯例。数据集按四类均匀分布，每类250个样本。

Result: 描述性分析显示四类在句子长度分布上大体相当，但词汇空间存在部分差异。词汇Jaccard相似度在0.37至0.56之间，表明方言间既有共性也有区分度。

Conclusion: BanglaDial-Abuse作为区域方言识别的研究原型语料库公开可用，主要用于四分类方言识别任务而非二元虐待文本检测。当前版本定位为研究和原型开发用途，非母语者验证的金标准语言学资源。

Abstract: Regional linguistic variation remains an important challenge for Bangla natural language processing, particularly in informal and non-standard text. This paper introduces BanglaDial-Abuse, a balanced Bengali-script dataset developed for regional dialect identification in abusive and hostile Bangla text. The dataset contains 1,000 sentences distributed equally across four linguistic varieties: Standard Bangla, Chattagram, Sylhet, and Barishal, with 250 samples per class. The resource was constructed using a corpus-grounded synthetic procedure incorporating regional variation in pronouns, possessive forms, verb morphology, negation, interrogative structures, postpositions, vocabulary, and Bengali-script spelling conventions while preserving the underlying hostile or abusive meaning. Descriptive analysis shows broadly comparable sentence-length distributions but partially distinct lexical spaces across the four classes. Pairwise Jaccard vocabulary similarity ranges from 0.37 to 0.56. The primary task is four-class regional dialect identification rather than binary abusive-text detection. The dataset is publicly available through Zenodo under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 license. The current version is intended as a research and prototyping corpus rather than a native-speaker-validated gold-standard linguistic resource. Keywords: Bangla, Bengali, dialect identification, regional dialect, abusive language, low-resource NLP, Chattagram, Sylhet, Barishal, dataset

</details>


### [94] [AGO AI Quality Gate: Evidence-First Release Decisions for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01218v1)
*Giulio Zeloni,Enrico Lo Conte,Salvatore Rionero,Giuseppe Santoro,Alessandro Rastelli,Fabio Sorrentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AGO AI Quality Gate框架通过四组件设计（决策模型、分层评分、概率门控、元评估协议）解决企业RAG系统版本发布决策问题，并在RAGBench基准上验证了LLM评判器质量差异及其对决策风险的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业RAG系统在版本迭代中面临发布决策困境：promote、revise还是block？现有依据不完整，且依赖有缺陷的LLM评判器，导致回归风险难以量化。

Method: 在工业RAG评估场景中部署AGO框架，包含四组件：①四态决策模型（将缺失数据和评判错误显式建模为明确结果）；②分层评分（确定性检查+本地护栏+结构化LLM评估）；③分层beta-binomial门控（概率化回归风险量化）；④强制元评估协议（使用RAGBench的1200个分层样本验证评判器质量）。

Result: gpt-4.1-nano检测非合规答案的AUROC仅0.603（接近随机），而gpt-4o达0.783；但即便gpt-4o的领域间性能仍波动于0.62-0.88。在回归场景下，决策级配置将不安全发布率降至22.2%-35.1%（vs naive门控的29.3%-41.8%）。

Conclusion: LLM评判器质量必须针对每个部署场景单独测量；单一点估计不足以支撑发布决策；概率化门控设计可有效平衡风险与收益。

Abstract: Enterprises adopting retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) face a recurring operational decision: promote, revise, or block a system version. The evidence is incomplete and the metrics come from fallible LLM judges. We report on AGO AI Quality Gate (AGO), an evidence-first quality-gate framework deployed in industrial RAG assessment engagements. AGO integrates four key components: a four-state decision model that treats missing data and judge errors as explicit outcomes; layered scoring combining deterministic checks, local guardrails, and structured LLM evaluation; a stratified beta-binomial gate that quantifies regression risk probabilistically; and a mandatory meta-evaluation protocol to validate the LLM judge before it influences decisions. Since engagement data is proprietary, we evaluate the judge layer on RAGBench, a public benchmark of 100k annotated RAG traces across 12 datasets. On identical stratified test samples (N=1200 per judge), a low-cost judge (gpt-4.1-nano) detects non-adherent answers barely above chance (AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]), despite producing flawless protocol output, while gpt-4o reaches 0.783 [0.756, 0.807] -- yet its per-domain performance still ranges from 0.62 to 0.88. A fixed-seed gate study spanning regression, no change, and improvement quantifies unsafe promotion, false-alarm cost, and improvement throughput. Under regression, the decision-grade profile reduces unsafe promotion to 22.2%-35.1%, against 29.3%-41.8% for a naive gate. These results support the design choices that judge quality must be measured per engagement and that point estimates alone are not a release decision.

</details>


### [95] [ASCRIBE: Atomic and Significance-Based Reasoning for Thai Clinical SOAP Note Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01234v1)
*Tarm Kalavantavanich,Teerawut Ponarchar,Pattaramanee Arsomngern,Jenta Wonglertsakul,Watcharakorn Chuthong,Chiraphat Boonnag,Knot Pipatsrisawat,Titipat Achakulvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ASCRIBE是一个受医生启发的推理框架，通过为对话中提取的每个原子事实赋予临床显著性级别，使通用大语言模型成为更可靠的医疗记录生成工具。研究还发布了ThaiClinicBench泰语临床摘要基准数据集和合成训练语料库。实验表明，ASCRIBE作为提示方法在多个评估指标上优于链式思维提示；作为GRPO奖励信号训练的小模型可匹敌大型模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗文档记录给医生带来沉重负担，现有的自动SOAP笔记生成方法存在两个关键问题：(1)推理过程中常遗漏临床重要信息；(2)生成内容缺乏依据支撑。此外，泰语医疗NLP研究因缺乏公开数据集而进展受阻。

Method: 提出ASCRIBE框架，核心思想是在摘要生成前，为对话中提取的每个原子事实赋予临床显著性级别。同时发布ThaiClinicBench（首个去标识化的泰语真实临床会诊摘要基准数据集）及基于真实临床笔记构建的合成训练语料库。

Result: 作为提示方法，ASCRIBE在GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 Pro上于医生对齐的LLM-judge评估指标上优于链式思维提示，在完整性指标上较标准提示提升10.3分。作为GRPO奖励信号，仅用合成数据训练的Gemma-4-E4B模型在事实精确度上匹配Gemini 3.1 Pro，在完整性上超越后者。

Conclusion: ASCRIBE通过临床显著性分级机制显著提升了LLM生成医疗记录的可靠性。ThaiClinicBench基准的发布填补了泰语医疗NLP的资源空白，为该领域后续研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Automatic SOAP note generation can ease the documentation burden on physicians, but existing reasoning methods often omit clinically important information and generate unsupported content. Progress in Thai is further hindered by the lack of publicly available datasets. We propose ASCRIBE, a physician-inspired reasoning framework that ascribes a clinical-significance level to each extracted atomic fact in the conversation before summarization, making a general-purpose LLM a more reliable scribe. We also release ThaiClinicBench, the first de-identified Thai clinical summarization benchmark of real encounters, together with a synthetic training corpus derived from real clinical notes. As a prompt, ASCRIBE outperforms chain-of-thought prompting on GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 Pro across the physician-aligned LLM-judge metrics and improves on standard prompting by up to 10.3 points on the completeness LLM-judge metric. As a GRPO reward, it enables a Gemma-4-E4B model trained solely on synthetic data to match Gemini 3.1 Pro in factual precision and surpass it in completeness. Code and data can be found at https://github.com/loolootech/ascribe.

</details>


### [96] [Evaluating the Robustness of Japanese LLMs to IME-Related and Typographical Errors](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01241v1)
*Ryota Mibayashi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了日语大语言模型对日语特定拼写错误的鲁棒性，发现字符转置和替换会显著降低模型准确率，而输入法转换、同音转换和全角转换的影响相对有限，表明当前日语LLM在应对实际输入环境中的打字错误时仍存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言处理任务中表现优异，但其对拼写错误的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。日语尤其值得关注，因为其文本输入涉及多种书写系统和基于输入法的转换机制，目前缺乏针对日语特定拼写错误的系统评估研究。

Method: 研究提出五种拼写错误类别：字符转置、字符替换、同音转换、日语输入法转换和全角转换。将这些扰动应用于三个日语基准数据集（JMMLU、JCommonsenseQA和JamC-QA），并对十一个日语和多语言LLM进行了评估。

Result: 字符转置和字符替换错误在所有基准测试中一致性地降低模型准确率；而输入法转换、全角转换和同音转换的影响相对有限。

Conclusion: 当前日语LLM对实际日语打字错误（尤其是显著扭曲原始输入的错误）仍然脆弱，强调了在实际输入环境中进行鲁棒性评估的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across various natural language processing tasks. However, their robustness to typographical errors remains underexplored, particularly in Japanese, where text input involves multiple writing systems and IME-based conversion. In this study, we evaluate the robustness of Japanese LLMs against realistic Japanese-specific typos. We introduce five typo categories: Character Transposition, Character Replacement, Homophone Conversion, Japanese IME Conversion, and Full-Width Conversion. These perturbations are applied to three Japanese benchmark datasets (JMMLU, JCommonsenseQA, and JamC-QA), and eleven Japanese and multilingual LLMs are evaluated. The results show that Character Transposition and Character Replacement typos consistently reduce accuracy across benchmarks, whereas IME Conversion, Full-Width Conversion, and Homophone Conversion have relatively limited impact. These findings reveal that current Japanese LLMs remain vulnerable to realistic Japanese typing errors, particularly those that substantially distort the original input, highlighting the importance of robustness evaluation in practical input environments.

</details>


### [97] [Know When to Hold 'em: Correct-Token Retention in Uniform-State Diffusion Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01275v1)
*Mojtaba Nafez,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对统一状态扩散模型(USDMs)的自纠正能力缺陷进行研究，发现现有模型无法有效保留正确标记，提出了Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg)辅助损失方法，显著提升了模型的标记保留能力和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前USDMs在自纠正过程中缺乏保留正确标记的能力。即使使用贪婪尾部解码策略，最先进的USDMs(DUO、UDLM、uniform-noise SEDD)仍在每一步对大量位置(173-270/512)进行编辑，导致样本多样性崩溃。随机标记腐败实验表明，模型对干净和腐败标记的重建精度几乎相同，尽管干净标记更容易预测。验证NELBO分解显示训练几乎没有奖励保留行为。

Method: 提出Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg)——一种简单但有效的辅助损失函数，训练模型保留前向过程中未被扰动的标记，无需改变采样器。该方法使干净标记准确率平均提升26.5个百分点，同时几乎不改变腐败标记准确率，每步修订次数收敛至仅3-11个位置。

Result: CTR-Reg使三种模型的生成困惑度降低超过一半，同时保持多样性，在不同采样预算下均有效。实验在六个基准测试上进行验证。

Conclusion: 正确标记保留是自纠正扩散语言模型的关键缺失成分，CTR-Reg是一种有效的解决方案。干净标记准确率大幅提升(26.5个百分点)，自纠正过程中的修订次数显著减少(从173-270降至3-11个位置)，生成质量与多样性得到同时改善。

Abstract: Uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) can revise any token at any denoising step, which lets them correct their own mistakes, a key advantage over masked diffusion. Self-correction, however, requires both revising incorrect tokens and retaining correct ones, and we show that current USDMs lack the latter. Even under greedy-tail decoding, state-of-the-art USDMs (DUO, UDLM, and uniform-noise SEDD) keep revising 173--270 of 512 positions at every step, and these large, uncoordinated edits collapse sample diversity. A random-token corruption experiment traces this deficit to the models themselves: they reconstruct clean and corrupted tokens with nearly identical accuracy, even though clean tokens are easier targets. A decomposition of the validation NELBO shows that training barely rewards retention: incorrect predictions are heavily penalized at corrupted positions but almost free at clean ones. We propose Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg), a simple but effective auxiliary loss that trains the model to retain tokens left unperturbed by the forward process and requires no change to the sampler. CTR-Reg improves clean-token accuracy by 26.5 percentage points on average across six benchmarks, while leaving corrupted-token accuracy virtually unchanged, and its per-step revisions converge to only 3--11 positions. With just five greedy-tail steps, generative perplexity more than halves under CTR-Reg for all three models while diversity is preserved, and these gains hold across sampling budgets. Our results identify correct-token retention as a key missing ingredient for self-correcting diffusion language models, and demonstrate an effective fix.

</details>


### [98] [What Wins a Vote? Formatting, Length, and Lexical Diversity in the French Compar:IA LLM Arena](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01316v1)
*Simonas Zilinskas,Maayeesha Farzana,Christophe Benavent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对137,293条法语LLM对战投票进行文体计量分析，发现答案呈现方式与内容同等影响人类偏好。Bold使用（每标准差+11.0%胜率）和MATTR词汇多样性指标（+16.8%）与胜率关联最稳健。全量调整使36/116个模型排名变动≥10位，但调整后排名与外部能力基准的一致性并未提升。研究建议_raw和调整后排名并行发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM竞技场的成对人类偏好既反映答案内容也反映呈现方式，但现有排名通常未区分这两类因素。研究旨在量化呈现方式（格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性、句子结构）对模型排名的影响。

Method: 基于2026年7月Compar:IA发布的137,293条法语决战投票数据（含116个模型的137,113场对战），重建用户投票时可见的响应，提取格式化特征、长度、可读性、词汇多样性和句子结构指标，通过多种规格的回归模型估计各特征的独立贡献。

Result: 呈现方式与获胜相关，但长度、粗体和列表倾向共现，难以分离其独立效应。最稳健的关联为：粗体使用（联合模型中每标准差+11.0%胜率）和MATTR（移动平均类符符比，+16.8%）。多轮对话中粗体效应显著减弱，而MATTR关联保持稳定。全量调整使36/116模型排名变动≥10位。

Conclusion: 调整后排名与外部能力基准的一致性未提升，说明当前调整方法未能更好衡量模型能力。建议并排发布_raw排名和调整后排名作为透明敏感性分析。

Abstract: LLM arenas turn pairwise human preferences into model rankings. Those preferences may reflect how an answer is presented as well as what it says. We take a stylometric approach to 137,293 decisive French-language votes from the July 2026 Compar:IA release; the primary formatting analysis includes 137,113 battles across 116 models, and the joint estimates use the 127,092 battles with all required measurements. For each battle, we reconstruct the response visible when the user voted. We then compare the raw ranking with rankings adjusted for formatting, length, readability, vocabulary variety, and sentence structure. Presentation is associated with winning, but length, bold text, and lists tend to occur together, making their individual contributions hard to separate. Across the measured features, two associations change least across specifications: bold usage (+11.0% win odds per standard deviation in the joint model) and moving-average type-token ratio (MATTR), a measure of vocabulary variety that is less sensitive to answer length (+16.8%). The bold association is substantially smaller in observed multi-turn conversations, whereas the MATTR association changes little; because users choose whether to continue, this difference is descriptive rather than causal. The full adjustment moves 36 of 116 models by at least ten ranks. Yet comparisons with external benchmarks do not show that adjusted rankings better measure capability. We therefore recommend publishing raw and adjusted rankings side by side as a transparent sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [99] [ARCCS: An Automated Regulatory Compliance Checking System](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01345v1)
*Giorgos Filandrianos,José Menezes,Chrysoula Zerva,Alessandro Gianola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARCCS是一个端到端、自动化、智能体化且法规无关的法律NLP系统，用于监管合规检查。该系统将原始监管文本分解为原子级、可追溯的要求，并使用检索到的证据、置信度分数和人类可理解的解释来评估目标文档。系统在GDPR政策评估和EU公共采购基准测试中分别达到96.67%和98.8%的准确率，且为首个完全开源的端到端监管合规检查和可审计报告生成系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管合规检查需要解释冗长的法律文本、识别适用条款，并将每个决策建立在明确的证据基础上，这是一个复杂且耗时的工作。现有方法通常依赖固定的法律模板或预定义规则集，缺乏灵活性和可扩展性。

Method: ARCCS系统采用以下核心方法：(1) 将原始监管文本分解为原子级、可追溯的要求；(2) 使用检索到的证据评估目标文档；(3) 生成置信度分数和人类可解释的决策理由；(4) 该设计解耦了合规评估与任何固定监管模板或预定义规则集的关联。

Result: 在两个互补场景中进行评估：(1) GDPR政策文档评估中，基于LLM的评判者发现其决策和理由在高达96.67%的评估案例中具有法律和证据一致性；(2) 在包含超过1,200条独立规则检查的EU公共采购基准测试中，系统在违规检测方面达到98.8%的准确率。

Conclusion: ARCCS是首个完全开源的端到端监管合规检查和可审计报告生成系统，其法规无关的设计使其能够处理不同规模和结构的监管文本，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Regulatory compliance checking - deciding whether a target document satisfies the obligations of a regulation - requires interpreting dense legal text, identifying which provisions apply, and grounding each decision in explicit evidence. We present ARCCS, an end-to-end, automated, agentic, and regulation-agnostic Legal NLP system for compliance checking. ARCCS decomposes raw regulatory text into atomic, traceable requirements and evaluates a target document against them using retrieved evidence, confidence scores, and human-interpretable justifications. This design decouples compliance assessment from any fixed regulatory template or predefined rule set, enabling the pipeline to operate over regulations of varying size and structure. We evaluate ARCCS in two complementary settings. First, in a GDPR policy-document evaluation, LLM-based judges find its decisions and justifications legally and evidentially consistent in up to 96.67% of the assessed cases. Second, on an EU public-procurement benchmark comprising more than 1,200 individual rule checks, the system attains 98.8% accuracy in violation detection. ARCCS is, to our knowledge, the first fully open-source system for end-to-end regulatory compliance checking and auditable report generation.

</details>


### [100] [SHAMS: An Audio-Grounded Pronunciation Benchmark for Levantine Arabic](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01427v1)
*Ben Sapirstein,Roy Mattar,Guy Mor-Lan,Ahlam Mohamed,Letizia Cerqueglini,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHAMS是一个用于评估Levantine Arabic（黎凡特阿拉伯语）语音语言技术的基准数据集，包含1,300个来自开放音频语料库的语句，涵盖五种LA变体，并提供音频、正字法、带音符文本和语音转录四个对齐层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levantine Arabic（LA）被数千万人使用，但LA内部多样性大且正字法不标准，导致LA语音语言技术评估困难，亟需共享基准来衡量技术进展。

Method: 从开放音频语料库中采集语句，在五种LA变体（城市/农村巴勒斯坦语、城市约旦/黎巴嫩/叙利亚语）间平衡分布，每个语句标注四个对齐层级：音频、未标音符正字法、带音符文本、语音转录。

Result: 该基准可用于评估多种下游任务（标音符、字素转音素、ASR、音频转音素），并已对开源和闭源模型进行基准测试。

Conclusion: SHAMS为LA语音语言技术提供了标准化评估框架，可衡量不同模型在各类任务和方言上的表现。

Abstract: Levantine Arabic (LA) is spoken by tens of millions of people, creating a pressing need for shared benchmarks to evaluate LA speech-language technologies. Evaluating such technology is particularly challenging given LA's internal diversity and its opaque and non-standardized orthography. We present SHAMS (SHami Annotated Multi-dialect Speech), a benchmark comprising 1,300 utterances drawn from open audio corpora, balanced across five LA varieties (Urban and Rural Palestinian, and Urban Jordanian, Lebanese, and Syrian). Each utterance is represented across four aligned tiers: audio, unvocalized orthography, diacritized text, and phonetic transcription. This structure supports evaluation of various downstream tasks such as diacritization, grapheme-to-phoneme conversion, automatic speech recognition, and audio-to-phoneme, grounded in audio and stratified by variety. We benchmark open and proprietary models across these tasks to demonstrate the utility of this benchmark for measuring progress across LA. We release SHAMS at https://shams-nlp.github.io .

</details>


### [101] [Generalization Is Stability, Not Accuracy: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLMs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01428v1)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Maya Rozenshtein,Rom Himelstein,Avi Mendelson,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGO框架，从个体样本层面评估大语言模型的泛化能力，通过生成一致性、内部激活、置信度和响应镜像等多个维度捕捉模型行为的变异性，发现现有模型均存在显著的泛化不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM泛化评估方法将鲁棒性与整体基准性能混为一谈，无法准确衡量模型在输入表达变化时的真实泛化能力。

Method: 提出SAGO框架，从个体样本层面评估泛化，关注模型在多种输入变体下的行为变化，测量维度包括生成一致性、内部激活、置信度和响应镜像等。

Result: 常用模型均表现出统计显著且一致的泛化不稳定性；没有模型能均匀泛化；不同行为维度捕获独立的失败模式；跨数据集变化可能逆转模型排名。

Conclusion: 泛化评估应从聚合准确率转向个体样本和多元维度的变异性分析，SAGO提供了更细粒度的评估视角，揭示了当前模型泛化能力的真实局限性。

Abstract: Generalization in large language models (LLMs) is the ability to produce consistent and semantically stable outputs when the same input is expressed in different ways. Existing work typically evaluates generalization through aggregate accuracy on a single prompt format, task, or set of variations, which conflates robustness with overall benchmark performance. In this work, we show generalization evaluation at the level of individual examples, across multiple input variants, and across different aspects of model behavior, focusing on variability rather than reducing performance to a score that can be improved through narrow training or other ways that obfuscate generalization evaluation. Following this view, we introduce the Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO), a framework that measures how much model behavior changes for the same input under different variations and benchmarks, capturing variability across several dimensions including generation consistency, internal activations, confidence, and response mirroring. We show that many commonly used models exhibit statistically significant and consistent generalization instability: no model generalizes uniformly, behavioral axes capture independent failure modes, and cross-dataset variation can reverse model rankings.

</details>


### [102] [When Does a Second Model Help? Cross-Model Review in LLM Verification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01471v1)
*Tae-Eun Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验（30个工件、150个植入错误、10种审查条件、900次审查会话）研究了跨模型代码审查的有效性。结果表明：顶级跨模型审查器的F1分数与同模型审查（CCR）无显著差异，但两种方式发现了41.2%不同类型的错误；两次审查中，CCR+跨模型组合比两个CCR组合能匹配更多植入错误（56.7% vs 42.7%），但与两个顶级跨模型审查器的效果无显著差异；轻量级跨模型审查器未表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型现已被广泛用于代码审查，但关于"第二个审查者是否应来自不同模型"这一关键问题缺乏实证研究。作者在先前仅研究单一模型的基础上，希望通过控制实验验证模型独立性对审查效果的影响。

Method: 采用对照实验设计：使用来自两个开发商的三个审查者模型，对30个包含150个植入错误的工件进行10种审查条件、900次审查会话的测试。在分析前审计了所有会话记录，排除了1次来源不明确的基线运行和14次失败调用，并报告了包含全部会话的结果。

Result: (1) 顶级跨模型审查器的F1分数与同模型审查（CCR）在新鲜会话中无显著差异；(2) 两种方式仅发现41.2%的相同错误（Jaccard指数）；(3) 两次审查中，CCR+跨模型组合比两个CCR组合匹配更多植入错误（56.7% vs 42.7%，Holm校正p=.006），但与两个顶级跨模型审查器无显著差异；(4) 轻量级跨模型审查器未优于同模型审查；(5) 隐藏需求可提升较低层级模型的F1，但对顶级模型无影响。

Conclusion: 跨模型审查的价值主要体现在发现不同类型的错误，而非提高整体检出率。模型差异与审查能力未能有效分离，顶级跨模型审查器的优势可能源于其本身更强的能力而非模型多样性。轻量级跨模型审查器无额外价值，研究结果对失败会话的计分方式敏感。

Abstract: Large language models now generate code, documentation, and analyses, and are increasingly used to review such output. We ask when a second review by a different model helps. Building on the author's earlier preprints, which varied context, repetition, and role structure within one model, we test model independence in a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts with 150 planted errors, 10 review conditions, and 900 review sessions with three reviewer models from two developers. In this experiment, (1) a top-tier cross-model reviewer is not significantly different in F1 from same-model review in a fresh session (CCR), which does not establish equivalence; (2) the two find partly different errors (Jaccard 41.2%); and (3) at two review calls, one CCR plus one cross-model review matches more planted errors than two CCR reviews (56.7% vs. 42.7%; Holm-adjusted p=.006), but not significantly more than two reviews by the top-tier cross-model reviewer, so model difference and reviewer capability are not separated. A lightweight cross-model reviewer scores no higher than same-model review. Withholding requirements from the reviewer raises F1 for the two lower tiers but not the top tier, in untested point estimates whose pattern depends on how failed sessions are scored. Before analysis we audited all session records, excluding one baseline run of uncertain provenance and 14 failed calls; results with all sessions are also reported. A partial check on public detector outputs from another benchmark neither replicates nor contradicts the main comparison. Records, artifacts, and scripts are available from the author on request.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [A Comprehensive Evaluation Framework for Conversational Home Energy Management Systems](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00073v1)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Goal-Question-Metric (GQM)方法的LLM集成家庭能源管理系统(HEMS)综合评估框架，包含5个类别23个指标，通过LLM-as-judge实现可扩展自动化评分，并在970个对话上验证了框架区分四种系统配置的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成HEMS研究主要依赖单轮或单任务评估，以响应准确性为主要指标。真实场景中的多轮对话交互性能如何尚不清楚，需要全面的多维度评估方法。

Method: 采用GQM方法论构建评估框架；使用LLM-as-judge管道实现自动化评分；通过迭代评分标准校准验证LLM评判的可靠性（对比3名训练有素的人类标注者）；生成970个对话（16个场景×5个人设）评估4种HEMS配置。

Result: 15个LLM评分指标中，12个达到与人类的高度一致(ICC≥0.73)，其中3个完全一致；3个因人类评分方差接近零改用平均绝对误差(0.04-0.28)报告。框架成功区分了四种系统配置在多个评估维度上的优劣。

Conclusion: 该研究为对话式HEMS提供了一个可复现的多维评估方法，能够全面评估系统在持续、上下文感知场景下的表现，为HEMS的进一步研究和优化提供了标准化评估基础。

Abstract: The growing complexity in home energy management (HEM) demands advanced systems that guide occupants toward informed energy decisions reflecting their background, preferences, and context. Large language model (LLM)-integrated HEM systems (HEMS) have demonstrated promise, but previous studies relied on single-turn or single-task evaluations with response accuracy as the primary metric. Whether such systems deliver effective interactions across the extended multi-turn dialogues typical of real-world use remains an open question. This study introduces a comprehensive evaluation framework of LLM-integrated HEMS derived from the Goal-Question-Metric methodology, organized across five categories: task performance, factual accuracy, interaction quality, control capability, and system efficiency. A total of 23 metrics across multi-turn conversations are proposed and an LLM-as-judge pipeline is employed to enable scalable automated scoring. Its reliability is validated against three trained human coders: after iterative rubric calibration, twelve of the fifteen LLM-scored metrics reached strong agreement (ICC >= 0.73), three of them perfect, while the remaining three exhibited near-zero variance in human scores and are instead reported via mean absolute error (0.04-0.28). To demonstrate the framework's effectiveness, 970 dialogues -- 16 scenarios and five personas -- were generated and evaluated across four conversational HEMS configurations spanning a sophistication gradient, from a vanilla LLM with raw energy data to a multi-agent HEMS. The framework distinguished the four configurations across multiple evaluation dimensions, revealing their respective strengths and weaknesses. This study contributes to conversational HEMS by providing a reproducible, multi-dimensional evaluation methodology that comprehensively assesses sustained, context-aware system performance.

</details>


### [104] [Three Pathways of Student-AI Interaction: Constraint-First Design for Higher-Order Thinking](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00338v1)
*Fatima T. Zahra*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了学生与AI交互的三路径类型学框架（被动回顾、直接提问、策略对话），以及用于促进策略对话的三阶段教学设计系统（NLTB）。通过质性内容分析验证了框架可行性，GPT-5作为第三编码者产生类似分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生与AI系统的交互方式可能决定该互动是支持还是取代批判性思维，需要系统性的框架来理解和设计教育场景中的AI交互。

Method: 对本科研究方法课程中50条随机抽样的学生-AI交互消息进行质性内容分析，由两名人工编码员和GPT-5（作为第三编码者）进行编码，采用描述性统计比较不同数据集。

Result: 人工编码员在路径层面达成68%一致率（κ=.48），策略对话识别一致率达80%。编码分布为：路径1（被动回顾）46%、路径2（直接提问）18%、路径3（策略对话）36%。GPT-5产生类似分布并引入人工方案中未包含的新类别。描述性分析的数据集以策略对话为主，初步表明教学框架可能影响学生选择的交互路径。

Conclusion: 三路径框架为描述学生-AI交互提供了术语体系，NLTB为支持更高阶参与提供了设计路径。

Abstract: How students interact with artificial intelligence (AI) systems in educational settings may determine whether that interaction supports or displaces critical thinking. This paper introduces two contributions. The first is the Three Paths of Student-AI Interaction, a typological framework identifying three qualitatively distinct modes of student-AI engagement: Passive Review, Direct Question, and Strategic Dialogue. The second is the Next Level Teaching Blueprint (NLTB), a three-stage instructional design system intended to make Strategic Dialogue more likely. Qualitative content analysis of 50 randomly sampled student-AI interaction messages from an undergraduate research methods course was used to examine the typology. Two human coders achieved 68% path-level agreement ($κ$ = .48), with 80% agreement on Strategic Dialogue identification specifically. GPT-5, used as a third coder, produced a similar overall distribution and introduced a coding category absent from the human scheme. Path 1 (Passive Review) accounted for 46% of exchanges in the primary researcher's classifications, Path 2 (Direct Question) for 18%, and Path 3 (Strategic Dialogue) for 36%. A second, descriptively examined dataset contained predominantly Strategic Dialogue content, offering a preliminary indication that instructional framing may influence which path students take. Together, the Three Paths framework and the NLTB contribute a language for describing student-AI interaction and a design approach for supporting higher-order engagement.

</details>


### [105] [From Images to Tasks: Characterizing Multimodal LLM Interactions in the Wild](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00701v1)
*Jinyi Ye,Scott Counts,Gaurav Verma,Kate Lytvynets,Weiwei Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究分析了超过40,000条来自Microsoft Copilot的图像上传对话，研究用户在使用多模态大语言模型时的真实需求。研究构建了一个包含10种能力的层次化框架（涵盖感知、认知和生成），发现大多数图像任务涉及多种能力组合，多模态交互的任务空间比纯文本交互更广泛且多样。将用户需求与253个现有基准测试对比发现：基准测试集中于感知和固定答案推理，而文本、代码和数据生成等常见工作流测试不足。研究结论为基于用户实际需求的多模态LLM基准设计提供了重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽已广泛整合视觉与文本能力，但学界对用户在自然场景下如何使用这些工具的了解仍然有限。目前缺乏对真实用户行为的大规模实证研究，导致模型评估与用户实际需求之间存在脱节。

Method: 研究团队收集并分析了超过40,000条来自Microsoft Copilot的去标识化图像上传对话记录，采用人工标注方法构建了包含10种能力（感知、认知、生成三类）的层次化分类框架，并将观察到的能力需求映射到253个现有基准测试进行对比分析。研究成果在ChatGPT独立数据集上进行了验证。

Result: 研究揭示了三项核心发现：1）大多数图像上传任务涉及多种能力的组合使用；2）多模态交互的任务空间比纯文本交互更广泛、更多样，存在非对称覆盖和依赖跨模态定位的任务类别；3）现有基准测试的覆盖与真实用户需求存在明显偏差——基准测试集中于感知和推理（追求固定答案），而用户常见的文本生成、代码生成和数据生成等工作流测试相对不足。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了多模态LLM用户行为的大规模实证特征描述，验证了十能力层次化分类框架的有效性。研究指出未来基准设计应更紧密地围绕用户实际需求展开，特别是加强文本生成、代码生成和数据生成等用户高频使用但现有测试覆盖不足的能力领域。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) increasingly integrate vision and text, yet how people use them in natural settings remains underexplored. We seek to answer the question: when users upload images, what tasks are they trying to accomplish? Analyzing over 40,000 de-identified image-upload conversations from Microsoft Copilot, we characterize real-world multimodal use through a hierarchical framework of ten capabilities, spanning perception, cognition, and generation. First, we characterize the distribution and composition of these capabilities, finding that the majority of image-upload tasks involve multiple capabilities. Second, we find that multimodal use spans a broader and more diverse task space than text-only interactions, with asymmetric coverage and task classes that rely on cross-modal grounding. Third, mapping observed capability demand onto 253 existing benchmarks reveals uneven alignment between benchmark coverage and real-world use: benchmarks concentrate on perception and reasoning toward fixed answers, while common workflows involving text, code, and data generation remain comparatively undertested. We validate our taxonomy and findings on an independent ChatGPT dataset. Our results provide a large-scale empirical characterization of what users seek to accomplish with multimodal LLMs and highlight opportunities for benchmark design grounded in observed user demand.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Memetic Trojans: Social Contagions as Carriers of Adversarial Payloads in Agent Networks](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00430v1)
*Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Finn Schwall,Leon Moonen*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"记忆性木马"（memetic trojans）这一新型攻击范式，通过将恶意载荷嵌入社会 contagion 内容中，利用智能体自发分享内容的内在倾向实现网络传播，无需依赖明确的恶意指令即可实现攻击扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在网络环境中交互时面临安全威胁。现有研究主要关注自我复制的提示注入或配置篡改类攻击（如 agent worms），但这些攻击需要智能体遵循恶意指令才能传播。研究者意识到智能体具有天然的内容转发和放大倾向，这种内生性的传播行为可能成为被攻击者利用的漏洞。

Method: 研究团队从 Moltbook 平台（一个面向 LLM 智能体的社交媒体平台）提取社会 contagion 数据，通过受控传输实验比较不同 contagion 的病毒式传播能力，并构建 Monte Carlo 攻击模拟评估记忆性木马的暴露放大效果。实验分析了网络结构和放大机制对传播的影响。

Result: 研究发现了显著的病毒性差异：最有效的 contagion 在约 50% 的后续智能体帖子中被转发，点赞率是平均帖子的 2.5 倍。其对应的记忆性木马攻击版本在很大程度上继承了这一传播特性。Monte Carlo 模拟显示记忆性木马可使预期暴露量增加高达 3.19 倍。网络结构和放大机制的共同作用产生了重尾分布的传播结果，接近网络全覆盖暴露。

Conclusion: 内生性社会传播是 multi-agent 系统中的一个独特安全漏洞。由于传播不依赖智能体遵循恶意重传指令，专注于提示注入检测或防止智能体被篡改的防御措施无法单独阻止记忆性木马传播。大规模智能体生态系统的安全可能需要考虑智能体偏好、推荐机制和网络拓扑结构的网络级防御策略。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly interact in network environments where adversarial content can propagate between agents. Known attacks include agent worms, which spread through self-replicating prompt injections or configuration compromises. We introduce \emph{memetic trojans}, a distinct class of network-mediated attack that exploits agents' tendencies to retransmit and amplify content. Unlike agent worms, whose propagation is adversarially induced, memetic trojans exploit \emph{endogenous} transmission by embedding adversarial payloads in \emph{social contagions}: content agents have internal reasons to share. As part of our work, we extract social contagions from Moltbook, a social media platform for LLM agents. Controlled transmission experiments reveal large differences in virality: the most effective contagion is retransmitted in approximately 50\% of subsequent agent posts and upvoted at 2.5x the average post's rate. Its memetic trojan counterpart largely inherits these properties. Monte Carlo attack simulations show that memetic trojans amplify expected exposure by up to 3.19x. Network structure and amplification mechanisms strongly shape propagation, producing heavy-tailed outcomes with near network-wide exposure. These results identify endogenous social transmission as a distinct security vulnerability in multi-agent systems. Because propagation does not require agents to follow malicious retransmission instructions, defenses focused on prompt-injection detection or preventing agent compromise cannot alone prevent memetic trojan propagation. Securing large-scale agent ecosystems may require network-level defenses that account for how agent preferences, recommendation mechanisms, and network topology amplify adversarial payloads.

</details>


### [107] [Crude, Commercial, and Self-Referential: Chinese-Language Coordinated Activity in Japanese-Language X](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00305v1)
*Kei Ichikawa,Bruno T. Sugano,Genta Toya,Wu Qianyun,Yasuhiro Hashimoto,Masashi Toyoda,Naoki Yoshinaga,Kazutoshi Sasahara*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了X平台上734,173个中文协调账户和约4.95亿条协调帖子，发现大多数协调账户是粗糙的、保留自动化特征的中文账户，其比例是同日文账户的六倍；协调内容以非政治性内容为主；在传播效果上，大多数互动来自协调账户本身，而涉及有害或非法内容的帖子收到更多来自中文以外人群的反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意协调被视为信息生态系统污染的主要来源，尽管已有研究关注机器人检测，但缺乏对协调行为本身特征及其引发普通用户反应的系统性分析。

Method: 基于2024年5月至2026年3月间X平台上日文分类流中的中文协调账户数据集（734,173个账户，约4.95亿条帖子），进行纵向定量分析。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）大多数协调账户是粗糙的、保留经典自动化特征的中文账户，其比例是同日文账户的六倍；（2）协调内容绝大多数为非政治性；（3）在传播效果上，大型级联中的大多数反应来自协调账户本身，而涉及有害或非法内容的帖子收到较高比例的非中文人群反应。

Conclusion: 提供了粗粒度中文协调行为的纵向定量图谱，揭示了协调账户的自动化特征、内容倾向及用户反应模式。

Abstract: Malicious coordination has long been regarded as a principal source of information ecosystem pollution. Here, we focus on crude, text-repetition-based coordination. As the demand for mitigating its dissemination has grown, scholars have studied such coordination, focusing especially on bot detection. Few studies, however, have characterized malicious coordination per se or examined how it elicits reactions from general users. Leveraging a dataset of 734,173 Chinese-language coordinated accounts and around 495 million coordinated posts published between May 2024 and March 2026, this study analyzes the characteristics of coordinated behavior and how general users react to coordinated posts. We report three findings: (1) most coordinated accounts are crude and retain the classic marks of automation, and the same criterion applied to Japanese-language accounts over the same month yields a share six times lower; (2) their content is overwhelmingly non-political; (3) regarding their reach, most reactions within large observable cascades originate from coordinated accounts themselves, while posts classified as potentially harmful or illegal material receive a comparatively high proportion of reactions from outside the Chinese-dominant population. We provide a longitudinal quantitative map of crude Chinese-language coordination appearing in X's Japanese-classified stream.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Pay for the Fault, Not the Flow: Label-Free In-Flow Multi-Agent Workflow Optimization](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01017v1)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Shengyu Chen,Zach Chen,Wei Cheng,Qingyun Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InFlowOp是一个多智能体工作流优化框架，通过统一的标签无关成本度量来同时解决任务分解粒度和智能体分配问题，并在执行过程中进行故障纠正；同时引入Braid基准测试用于评估需要多智能体协调的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建多智能体工作流面临三大核心挑战：任务分解粒度的确定、智能体分配策略、以及何时创建新专家智能体。这些决策需要在工作流运行前确定，导致每次改进都需要重新执行、重新搜索或重新训练，成本高昂。

Method: InFlowOp提出一种标签无关的成本度量方法，同时考虑智能体能力与任务需求的匹配程度以及智能体的运行成本。在执行前，该方法双向确定任务分解粒度和智能体分配；执行过程中，通过同一成本度量进行最廉价的故障纠正。

Result: 在多个领域和主干网络上，InFlowOp相比单智能体基线提升高达+11.97%，使用流内优化可实现+9.64%的提升。

Conclusion: InFlowOp通过统一的成本度量框架，在工作流构建和运行阶段均能有效优化决策；Braid基准测试为评估多智能体协调能力提供了新的测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly construct multi-agent workflows that decompose a complex task and assign specialist agents from a pool. However, building such a workflow well remains challenging: how finely to divide the task, which agent to trust with each subtask, and when to create a new specialist are all critical decisions a workflow constructor needs to settle up front. Thus, whether each subtask succeeds remains unknown until the workflow runs. Yet, improving a workflow is costly. Locating a fault usually requires a reference answer, a graded outcome, or a trained assessor, and the fix is applied to the whole workflow through re-execution, re-search, or retraining. We propose InFlowOp, which prices every decision in one label-free cost that weighs how well an agent's competence meets what a subtask demands against how much that agent takes to run. Before execution, InFlowOp bidirectionally determines the granularity of task decomposition and agent assignment following from the cost rather than from a fixed template. During execution, InFlowOp corrects a fault with the cheapest move via the same cost that serves the workflow both as it is built and as it runs. Facing the workflow-level evaluation challenge, we introduce Braid, a benchmark whose tasks require multi-agent coordination beyond single-agent capability. Across various domains and backbones, InFlowOp outperforms single agent baselines by up to $+11.97\%$, achieving $+9.64\%$ with in-flow optimization. Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/InFlowOp/.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond Final Accuracy: Auditing Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01042v1)
*Shixuan Li,Wei Yang,Peiyu Zhang,Anzhe Cheng,Heng Ping,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICR框架通过固定初始推理轨迹来隔离通信效果，揭示了聚合准确率掩盖了不同的修正行为，且更丰富的消息会同时放大有益和有害影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体通信评估存在根本性问题：系统性能提升的来源不明确，无法区分有效通信、优越架构还是额外推理的贡献。传统评估将修正错误与腐蚀答案合并为单一准确率指标，掩盖了通信如何改变决策的机制。

Method: 提出ICR（独立-通信-修订）框架，将通信评估定位为独立推理后的答案修订过程。该框架固定初始推理轨迹，条件性测量修正和保留率（基于两个智能体的初始正确性），并使用无消息修订控制来量化超出额外推理的收益。

Result: 在四个推理基准测试中发现：1）聚合准确率相似可能掩盖实质不同的修订行为；2）相比仅传输答案，完整推理在所有四个基准上增加了修正但减少了保留，更丰富的消息同时放大了有益和有害影响；3）结构化验证策略使每个通道转向更高的保留率和更低的修正率。

Conclusion: 通信质量不应被视为通道的内在属性。ICR将评估重新聚焦于选择性修订，提供统一框架来审视消息内容和接收者策略如何共同产生收益和危害。

Abstract: Multi-agent communication aims to help agents benefit from one another's information. Yet improvements in system performance leave a fundamental ambiguity: do they reflect effective communication, a favorable agent architecture, or simply additional reasoning? Because communication methods are commonly evaluated within the systems they were designed for, these factors are difficult to disentangle. Final accuracy further merges corrected errors and corrupted answers into a single outcome, obscuring how communication changes decisions. We introduce Independent--Communicate--Revise (ICR), a controlled framework that evaluates communication as answer revision following independent reasoning. ICR fixes initial reasoning trajectories, measures correction and preservation conditional on both agents' initial correctness, and uses a no-message revision control to quantify gains beyond additional reasoning. Across four reasoning benchmarks, our audit of textual and latent communication reveals that similar aggregate accuracy can conceal substantially different revision behaviors. Compared with transmitting answers alone, full reasoning increases correction while reducing preservation on all four benchmarks, so richer messages amplify beneficial and harmful influence alike. Receiver-policy comparisons on MedQA and GPQA-D further show that a structured verification policy shifts every channel toward greater preservation and lower correction, while its effect on selectivity varies across channels and tasks. These findings challenge treating communication quality as an intrinsic property of a channel. ICR therefore recenters evaluation on selective revision, providing a unified framework for examining how message content and receiver policies jointly produce benefits and harms.

</details>


### [110] [Fault-Tolerant Budget Conservation in Distributed Multi-Agent Delegation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00349v1)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文解决了分布式多智能体授权系统中资源预算在故障条件下（消息丢失、超时、重试、分区、别名等）的超额支出的核心问题，提出了基于量化资源向量和专属托管信用的容错预算守恒机制，并通过形式化验证和实验证明了其在各种故障场景下的预算边界保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当AI代理跨并发、易故障的工作节点委托任务时，传统的资源限制机制（父子分配约束、仿射对象、分布式托管）无法防止超额支出。具体故障场景包括：回复丢失、超时后效果完成、消息重复、分区分支、DAG连接别名等。

Method: 1. 将预算建模为通过委托DAG流动的量化资源向量，使用专属托管信用表示；2. 分支在分发前将信用转换为绑定到谱系、纪元、归一化效果、最大费用、接收者和幂等键的操作预留；3. 持久化带隔离的签名分发许可证，网关在首次接受前验证；4. 不确定效果持续计费直到认证结算、围栏权威无效果证明或永久终止。形式化验证了所有权分区、账本守恒、效果守恒、后代非放大、至多一次结算、延迟完成安全和分区隔离等属性。

Result: 证明了：所有权分区、账本和效果守恒、后代非放大、至多一次结算、延迟完成安全、分区隔离。不可区分性结果表明分区本地可用性需要专属预分配。边界TLA+检查、独立JavaScript浏览器、注入崩溃的双进程SQLite实验验证了声明范围并检测了超时退款和历史证书验证的变异体。

Conclusion: 该机制在评估的崩溃、重试、重复、分区、连接和延迟完成调度中保持发行的预算边界。分区本地可用性必须依赖专属预分配。

Abstract: Resource limits are becoming an authorization boundary for AI agents that delegate work across concurrent and failure-prone workers.
