Parent-child allocation constraints, affine objects, and distributed escrow do not by themselves prevent overspend when replies are lost, effects complete after timeout, messages repeat, branches partition, or DAG joins alias one lineage. We formalize fault-tolerant budget conservation for distributed multi-agent delegation. Budgets are quantized resource vectors represented by exclusive escrow credits that move through a delegation DAG. Before dispatch, a branch converts credit into an operation reservation bound to lineage, epoch, normalized effect, maximum charge, receiver, and idempotency key. It persists a signed dispatch permit with quarantine; the gateway verifies that permit before first acceptance. Uncertain effects remain charged until authenticated settlement, a fenced authoritative no-effect proof, or permanent retirement. We prove ownership partition, ledger and effect conservation, descendant non-amplification, at-most-once settlement, late-completion safety, and partition confinement under explicit mediation, durability, authentication, normalization, and gateway assumptions. An indistinguishability result shows that partition-local availability requires exclusive preallocation. Bounded TLA+ checking, an independent JavaScript explorer, and crash-injected two-process SQLite experiments exercise the declared scope and detect timeout-refund and historical-certificate-validation mutants. The mechanism preserves the issued budget bound across the evaluated crash, retry, duplicate, partition, join, and late-completion schedules.

</details>


### [111] [Worse Together: How Performance Breaks Down in Multi-User Multi-Agent Teams](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00583v1)
*Sahan Paliskara,Nattaput Namchittai,Andrew Lampinen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多用户、多智能体场景下的协调问题，测试了5个前沿模型在77个场景中的表现，发现团队模式（各智能体服务单一用户）始终比协调者模式（单个智能体服务所有用户）表现更差，并识别出导致群体绩效下降的关键行为模式，同时提出了针对不同环境的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体被广泛部署并需要共享代码库、日历、预算等资源，不同用户的所有智能体之间面临协调失败问题，导致群体整体收益下降，亟需理解这一多用户、多智能体设置下的协调失效机制。

Method: 跨越四种环境（API密钥共享、诊所日历、个人助理团购/预约、合并队列发布截止）进行实验，对比三种条件：单一协调者智能体、团队模式（无通信通道）、团队模式（有通信通道）。测试覆盖5个前沿模型和77个场景。

Result: 团队模式在所有环境中均表现劣于协调者模式：无通信通道时在两个环境中完全崩溃；有通信通道时，协调开销仍造成显著差距。例如在个人助理环境中，协调者完成任务的成功率约为团队的两倍。识别出导致群体绩效差的典型行为：随团队规模扩大而停滞、相互覆盖操作、虚构声称。

Conclusion: 可通过环境特定的缓解措施改善协调：团队领导、明确程序指令、平台检查机制（智能体在提交前读取同伴消息）。作者将发布MAMUBench，包含74个场景用于评估多用户、多智能体协调。

Abstract: People are increasingly delegating tasks to AI agents, and those agents are increasingly encountering other people's agents over shared resources such as a codebase, a calendar, or a budget. When each agent acts for a different user with different goals, coordination often fails, and the group ends up worse off than if a single agent had acted for everyone. We study this multi-user, multi-agent setting across five frontier models and 77 scenarios in four environments: an API key environment in which agents share a compute budget, a clinic in which they share a calendar, a personal assistant environment in which they share a group order or booking, and a merge queue in which they share a release cutoff. In each scenario, we compare a single agent that serves every user (a coordinator) to a team in which each agent serves one user, with and without a communication channel between the agents. Teams deliver worse group outcomes than the coordinator in every environment: without a channel, they completely collapse in two environments, and even with one, coordination overhead creates substantial gaps. For example, in the personal assistant environment, the coordinator fulfills a targeted user request about twice as often as teams. We identify distinct behaviors associated with this poor group-level performance, including stalling as teams grow, overriding each other's actions, and fabricating claims. We find effective but environment-specific mitigations, such as a team lead, explicit procedural instructions, and a platform check that makes an agent read its peers' messages before committing. We will release the API key, clinic, and personal assistant environments as MAMUBench, comprising 74 scenarios for evaluating multi-user, multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [112] [Meta-Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Fast Adaptation of Interactive Policies with Applications to Autonomous Driving](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00705v1)
*Huiwen Yan,Kyriakos G. Vamvoudakis,Mushuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元多智能体强化学习(meta-MARL)框架，通过将MARL问题建模为马尔可夫博弈(MG)，定义meta-NE概念，实现多智能体系统在多个MG任务分布上的快速交互策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元强化学习(meta-RL)主要针对单智能体系统，将其扩展到多智能体系统面临额外挑战：任务不仅取决于环境，还取决于智能体之间的策略交互。因此需要专门设计适用于多智能体场景的元学习框架。

Method: 1) 将MARL问题建模为马尔可夫博弈(MG)；2) 开发meta-MARL框架，支持跨MG分布的快速交互策略适应；3) 定义meta-NE(元纳什均衡)作为期望解概念；4) 建立meta-NE与梯度博弈meta-MARL算法平稳点之间等价性的充分条件。

Result: 在自动驾驶任务上的评估表明，所提meta-MARL方法相比预训练的MARL基线方法实现了更快的适应速度，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将元学习思想扩展到多智能体强化学习领域，通过meta-NE概念和梯度博弈方法，为多智能体系统的高效策略适应提供了理论支撑和实践验证。

Abstract: This paper develops a meta-multi-agent reinforcement learning (meta-MARL) framework to enable fast adaptation of interactive policies in a multi-agent system (MAS). Meta-reinforcement learning (meta-RL) enables agents to rapidly adapt to new tasks/environments using a bi-level optimization mechanism. However, existing meta-RL generally focuses on single-agent systems. Extending these frameworks and algorithms to multi-agent systems poses additional challenges, as tasks are characterized by not only the environment but also agents' strategic interactions. To address these challenges, we model multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) problems as Markov games (MGs) and develop a meta-MARL framework for rapid interactive policy adaptation across a distribution of MGs. A new concept, called meta-NE, is defined to describe the desired solution concept in a meta-MARL problem. Sufficient conditions for the equivalence between a meta-NE and a stationary point of the gradient-play-based meta-MARL algorithm are established. Our evaluation on autonomous-driving tasks demonstrates that the proposed meta-MARL method achieves faster adaptation than pretrained MARL baselines, validating the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [113] [A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Personalized Health Checkup Interpretation and Guidance](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01451v1)
*HyungJun Kim,Taehan Lee,Soojin Cheon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多智能体LLM系统，用于个性化解读健康体检报告，通过并行执行多个任务专用智能体并综合其输出，相比单智能体系统在120个韩语复合查询上取得了统计显著的性能提升，但代价是延迟和成本分别增加了1.31倍和2.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康体检结果的个性化解读需要综合纵向健康记录、医学知识、生活方式指导和医疗导航等多方面信息进行推理，现有的单一LLM智能体难以有效处理这类复合查询任务。

Method: 设计了一个多智能体LLM系统，该系统首先识别多个用户意图，然后将每个意图映射到相应的任务专用智能体，并行执行各智能体任务，最后综合所有输出生成最终答案。使用合成的韩语健康体检记录，对120个包含2-4个需求的复合查询进行评估。

Result: 多智能体系统在加权LLM评判分数上从1.695提升至1.797（p=0.027），四个LLM评判者均显示一致改善（Δ=+0.111至+0.186，均p<0.05）。改进主要体现在实用性、一致性和复合查询中每个需求的覆盖程度。数值准确性和可溯源性仅在一个评判者中显著改善，医疗安全性和严重失败率无显著差异（单智能体15.0% vs 多智能体13.3%）。两个人类评估者在66.7%和68.3%的成对比较中偏好多智能体系统。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构可有效提升LLM在复杂医疗问答任务上的性能，尤其是在涉及个人健康记录查询的场景。代价是延迟和成本分别增加1.31倍和2.02倍。未来可探索优化策略以平衡性能提升与资源消耗。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of health checkup results requires reasoning across longitudinal records, medical knowledge, lifestyle guidance, and healthcare navigation. We present a multi-agent large language model (LLM) system that identifies multiple intents, maps each to a task-specific agent, executes them in parallel, and synthesizes their outputs. We compared answers generated in Single Agent and Multi Agent settings on 120 Korean compound queries combining two to four requirements, using synthetic health checkup records. The Multi Agent improved the weighted LLM-judge score from 1.695 to 1.797 (p = 0.027), and three additional LLM judges showed consistent improvements ($Δ$ = +0.111 to +0.186, all p < 0.05). The gains came from usefulness, consistency, and the handling of every requirement in compound queries, whereas numerical accuracy and grounding improved significantly under only one of the four judges and medical safety did not differ, and critical failures occurred at similar rates (Single Agent 15.0% vs. Multi Agent 13.3%). Two human evaluators preferred Multi Agent in 66.7% and 68.3% of pairwise comparisons. Multi Agent execution increased latency and cost by 1.31$\times$ and 2.02$\times$, respectively. In exploratory subgroup analyses, the improvement was concentrated in queries involving personal-record lookup.

</details>


### [114] [Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graphs for Dynamic Reasoning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01249v1)
*Yan Luo,Selim-Antoine Lali,Jeremy Moebel,Iliass Khoutaibi,Ahmadou Aidara,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对动态任务路由问题，提出了RIAG框架，通过缓存、有限重试和条件性审计修复机制，在保持低调用开销的同时提升路由与回答准确性。实验基于6个基准数据集构建的动态评估集，验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推理协议采用固定预选任务，无法测试智能体是否传播相关更新、保留未受影响的工作或重建历史任务绑定。因此需要研究动态任务路由问题，即事件流如何修订任务绑定，以及系统如何在每个查询时间选择有效文档版本。

Method: 将6个广泛使用的基准测试（MMLU、MMLU-Pro、MedMCQA、MATH、GPQA、HumanEval）重新构建为31,119个动态测试场景，包含373,428个时间分类查询。提出RIAG（修订感知独立智能体图），采用受限多智能体策略，将确定性时间解析与任务推理分离，通过文档身份缓存解决方案，每个新任务给予两次未暴露尝试，条件性调用审计和修复，每个文档版本最多使用4次调用。

Result: 同质RIAG在0.62次调用/查询的条件下达到54.24%的联合路由与回答准确率，而最强对比方法在18.00次调用/查询下仅达到32.22%。异质RIAG在0.63次调用/查询下达到49.78%。

Conclusion: RIAG通过分离时间解析与任务推理、结合缓存与条件性修复机制，成功解决了动态任务路由中的核心权衡问题，实现了比基线方法更高的准确率和更低的计算开销。

Abstract: Conventional reasoning protocols present a fixed, preselected task, so they cannot test whether an agent propagates relevant updates, preserves unaffected work, or reconstructs a historical task binding. We therefore study \emph{dynamic task routing}, in which an event stream revises task bindings and a system must select the document version valid at each query time before solving it. To study this problem, we repurpose six widely used benchmarks: MMLU, MMLU-Pro, MedMCQA, MATH, GPQA, and HumanEval into 31{,}119 dynamic episodes comprising 373{,}428 temporally categorized queries. This setting exposes a central trade-off: recomputing after every event wastes work, whereas unguarded reuse returns stale conclusions. We introduce the Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graph (RIAG), a bounded multi-agent policy that separates deterministic temporal resolution from task reasoning. RIAG caches solutions by immutable document identity, starts each fresh task with two unexposed attempts, and conditionally invokes audit and repair, using at most four calls per document version. On this collection, homogeneous RIAG achieves 54.24\% joint routing-and-answer accuracy at 0.62 calls/query, compared with 32.22\% at 18.00 calls/query for the strongest comparison method; heterogeneous RIAG reaches 49.78\% at 0.63 calls/query.

</details>


### [115] [A Simple Doxastic Deontic Logic for Norm-Guided Decision Making](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00668v1)
*Thorsten Engesser,Agata Ciabattoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合道义逻辑与信念逻辑的形式化框架，用于处理规范冲突和不完整信息下的决策问题，并证明了在特定条件下最优决策可归结为加权MaxSAT问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在进行决策时面临两大挑战：(1) 规范之间可能存在冲突；(2) 信息往往是不完整或不可靠的。如何在此类环境下做出合理决策是一个基础性问题。

Method: 提出了Chellas最小道义逻辑的可经典归约片段，扩展显式条件规范，并结合多主体KD45信念逻辑，形成doxastic deontic logic，使规范可依赖于主体关于事实和规范本身的信念。

Result: 定义了Doxastic Norm Compliance Optimization Problem，区分了主观优化（相对于主体信念）和客观优化（相对于实际事实）。给出了两种优化一致的充分条件，并证明在多项式时间内可规约为加权部分MaxSAT。

Conclusion: 该框架为自主系统的规范推理提供了形式化基础，证明了计算复杂性问题可在多项式时间内有效求解，具有重要的理论与应用价值。

Abstract: Making decisions despite conflicting norms and incomplete or unreliable information is a fundamental challenge for autonomous systems. We introduce a simple doxastic deontic logic for this setting: a classically reducible fragment of Chellas' Minimal Deontic Logic, extended with explicit conditional norms and combined with multi-agent KD45, so that norms can depend on agents' beliefs about both facts and norms. On this logic we define the Doxastic Norm Compliance Optimization Problem, where an agent chooses a decision minimizing weighted norm violations. We distinguish subjective optimization (relative to the agent's beliefs) from objective optimization (relative to the actual facts). We give conditions under which (i) the two coincide and (ii) optimal decision-making can be reduced to weighted partial MaxSAT in polynomial time.

</details>


### [116] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025v1)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了小语言模型（SLM）在代理系统辅助任务中的适用性，发现16种配置均未通过预设阈值，且量化无法解决这一问题。论文建议将SLM仅用于非LLM基线无法满足阈值的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent系统复杂度增加， practitioners希望在LLM规划器周围使用更小、更便宜的SLM处理辅助任务（自动审批shell命令、写入记忆、选择工具、排序历史轮次），但尚不清楚现成的SLM是否能满足实际应用阈值，量化是否成为可行方案。

Method: 构建了4个辅助任务基准测试，每个任务有固定提示词和自动评估指标，以及锚定于非LLM基线的阈值τ和CI感知 eligibility规则。使用Qwen3（0.6/1.7/4/8B）在最佳配置下（FP16、贪心解码、冻结提示词、无微调）进行测试，并评估4-bit量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWZ）的影响。在Llama-3.x上复现，并通过τ扫描和提示词改写（原始+3种中性 paraphrase）进行鲁棒性验证。

Result: 16种配置（4任务×4模型）均未通过eligibility验证。量化带来的损伤与模型规模相关，但未使任何配置达到eligibility。诊断分析将失败分为能力缺陷和可通过调整解码阈值解决的两类。0/112的配置（跨所有提示词变体）通过验证。

Conclusion: 量化不能弥合SLM的能力差距，差距更依赖于模型规模而非精度。实际建议：在SLM前放置满足CI支撑阈值的基线，仅在基线失败时使用SLM。例如，4B重排器在BM25候选列表上优于BM25（+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]），但自身未达到eligibility。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $τ$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $τ$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $τ$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>


### [117] [Characterizing a Configuration Where Inference-Time PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting Is Inert: Evidence from Three Reasoning LMs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00047v1)
*Khawaja Murad ul Hassan,Mehran Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对PRM剪枝片段嫁接(PPFG)技术进行了严格测试，发现该技术在与之前报告增益的相同操作点下，对并行链式思维推理没有任何改进效果。通过对322个注入事件的分类分析，发现仅14%的注入针对真正陷入困境的链，其余都浪费在了已成功、接近完成或处于平坦PRM平台的链上。这一负面结果在3个基础模型、6个基准测试、2个PRM上均得到复现，并通过了正等效性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，当过程奖励模型(PRM)剪枝某个链时，可以将其高PRM分数的前缀提取并嫁接到仍在解码的兄弟链中作为上下文演示，这种交叉轨迹步级迁移机制被认为能改善并行链式思维(CoT)的性能。本研究旨在隔离并严格测试这一机制(PPFG)，验证其在报告增益的操作点上是否真正有效。

Method: 实验在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上使用Math-Shepherd进行Full MATH500测试(n=500,三个种子)，同时在多个配置下复现：3个基础模型、6个基准测试、第二个PRM以及兼容门超参数扫描。采用双单侧检验(TOST)进行等效性分析，并对322个停滞规则注入事件进行四桶分类分析。

Result: PPFG在所有测量的维度上与独立并行CoT基线统计上不可区分。四桶分类显示仅14%的注入针对真正陷入困境的链；其余分别针对已成功链、接近完成链或处于平坦PRM平台的链。随机控制以2.4倍触发率达到相同效果，说明无效果并非特定启发式导致。通过TOST分析将无差异提升为正向等效性，12个Qwen/LLaMA单元全部通过。后见之明神谕估计每问题增益上界仅为+0.13pp。

Conclusion: PPFG作为跨轨迹步级迁移的最小成本操作化，在测试的操作点上不提供改进。等效性检验可作为建立推理时机制零假设的模板。关键发现：PRM引导的注入存在瞄准精确度问题——大多数注入浪费在不需要救援的链上，而复合门优化无法同时实现精确触发和充分密度。未来研究需探索如何提升注入目标的精准度，或重新设计跨链信息传递机制。

Abstract: Diversity collapse in parallel chain-of-thought has motivated inference-time interventions built on a natural design: when a process reward model (PRM) prunes a chain, its high-PRM prefix is extracted and grafted verbatim as an in-context demonstration into a still-decoding sibling. We isolate this mechanism, PRM-Pruned Fragment Grafting (PPFG), as the most cost-minimal operationalization of cross-trajectory step-level transfer, and test it at the operating point where prior fragment-grafting work reports gains only under additional compensating ingredients. On Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with Math-Shepherd on full MATH500 (n=500, three seeds), PPFG in both stagnation- and random-targeting variants is statistically indistinguishable from an independent parallel-CoT baseline on every measured axis. We characterize why: a four-bucket classification of 322 stagnation-rule injection events shows only 14% targeted a genuinely struggling chain; the rest landed on chains that had already succeeded, were near completion, or sat on a flat PRM plateau, states a rescue graft cannot change. No compound-gate refinement jointly achieves well-targeted firing and adequate density, and a random control matches the same parity at 2.4x the firing rate, so the inertness is not heuristic-specific. The finding replicates across three base LMs, six benchmarks, a second PRM, and a compatibility-gate sweep; two-one-sided-tests analysis promotes the parity to positive equivalence on all twelve Qwen/LLaMA cells. A per-event spot-check finds injected chains prune at 2.75x the matched-step rate, but a surviving-sibling counterfactual finds no population-level compensation. A hindsight oracle bounds any per-problem gain from choosing PPFG over independent at +0.13 pp. We contribute an equivalence-testing template for establishing inference-time mechanism nulls, with every claim scoped to its tested operating point.

</details>


### [118] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061v1)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAP-DPO方法，通过几何对齐的偏好对选择来改进大语言模型的个性化训练。核心发现是：在离策略采样下，当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO更新从误差校正信号转变为类似强化学习的更新。通过引入效用感知、几何对齐的偏好对选择机制，GAP-DPO在个性化文本生成任务中显著提升了风格保真度、偏好对齐和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM需要根据用户特定偏好对齐生成行为而非仅追求聚合质量。现有DPO方法依赖似然极值等启发式准则选择偏好对，这些方法与显式用户效用脱节，可能导致个性化效果下降。

Method: 将个性化偏好学习形式化为几何对齐优化问题，分析期望用户效用梯度与DPO更新方向的一阶交互。在离策略采样下，当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO更新从误差校正转变为强化学习式更新。提出GAP-DPO，通过效用感知的几何对齐偏好对选择，并在epoch级别重生成控制分布漂移。

Result: 在个性化文本生成基准测试中，GAP-DPO相比标准DPO变体持续提升风格保真度、偏好对齐和生成质量。

Conclusion: 梯度对齐是个性化偏好优化的统一原则；偏好对选择是优化几何结构的内在组成部分，而非启发式预处理步骤。

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [119] [K-Dense BYOK: An Open-Source AI Research Assistant That Runs Locally and Keeps a Hash-Chained Lab Notebook](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00074v1)
*Aubrey M. Brueckner,Darshil Patel,Yuhuan He,Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: K-Dense BYOK 是一款免费开源的AI研究助手，运行在研究者自有电脑上，提供科学流程库、工作流模板、数据目录等脚手架工具，以及可观察记录的"活实验室笔记本"，通过观察而非依赖agent自述来记录研究过程，从而解决大模型过度承诺（overclaiming）的常见问题。在20个跨学科研究提示的评估中，其在科研质量和研究执行方面优于两个托管平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI助手和通用编程agent存在的三个核心问题：缺乏科学领域的结构化流程支持、无法保持持久可追溯的研究记录、以及大模型普遍存在的"过度承诺"现象（声称完成但实际未完成）。研究者需要一个能运行在自有设备上、完全可控的AI研究工具。

Method: K-Dense BYOK 通过三个关键设计来解决这些问题：(1) 内置科学流程库、引导式工作流模板、数据目录和审稿人/写作人角色；(2) "活实验室笔记本"——条目可链接到论证中、仅追加不删除；(3) 采用不可篡改的观察日志记录agent行为，而非依赖agent自述。评估采用预先固定的评分标准，在20个跨学科研究提示上与两个托管平台进行对比。

Result: K-Dense BYOK 在科研质量和研究执行两个维度上均优于两个托管平台。其交付物是唯一包含软件运行环境记录的，且通常附带可重新生成结果的命令。其中一个托管平台使用相同的前沿模型，但未提供任何环境记录。

Conclusion: K-Dense BYOK 通过将研究记录从"agent声称"转变为"系统观察"的方式，有效解决了模型过度承诺问题。其开源、本地运行、完全可追溯的特性为AI辅助科学研究提供了可验证的基础设施。

Abstract: K-Dense BYOK (bring your own keys) is a free, open-source AI research assistant for scientists in any field that runs on the researcher's own computer. The researcher supplies access to a model of their choice, hosted or running locally, and the application supplies everything else: a place for the work to run, a layer of scientific scaffolding, and a complete record. Each project is an ordinary folder, so the data, the code, the results, and the record stay on a machine the researcher administers and can be read years later without the application. Three things separate it from a chat assistant or a general-purpose coding agent. It ships a library of written scientific procedures, guided workflow templates, catalogs of where research data can be found, and reviewer and writer roles the agent can hand work to. It keeps a Living Lab Notebook whose entries link into an argument and are added to but never erased. And it records what happened by watching what the agent does rather than by taking the agent's word for it, in a log the agent has no tool that can write to. That choice targets the most common failure, model overclaiming, in our earlier benchmark of nine frontier models, by making claims checkable rather than preventing them. On twenty interdisciplinary research prompts, scored under a rubric fixed in advance, K-Dense BYOK led two managed platforms on both scientific quality and research execution. Its deliverables were the only ones that recorded the software they ran in, and the only ones that usually arrived with a command that regenerates the results. One of the managed platforms ran the same frontier model and supplied neither. Those environment records were files the agent wrote, not part of the observed log, which does not yet capture the software environment itself. The code is available under the MIT license at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/k-dense-byok.

</details>


### [120] [Scientific Agents: Evaluating Profession-Specific System Prompts on Scientific Tasks](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00084v1)
*Timothy Kassis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了503个职业专用AGENTS.md配置文件对AI科学任务表现的影响，发现详细职业描述并未提升准确率（反而略有下降），但显著增加了token使用量和成本（2-4.5倍）。唯一的意外好处是长提示减少了API错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型中广泛使用的职业专用系统提示是否真正提升科学任务表现，还是仅增加了复杂度和成本。

Method: 使用Gemini 3.8 Flash模型，在Pi agent测试框架中对9个文本科学基准（4,531题）和60个工具调用型生物信息学问题进行测试，对比完整职业配置、最小基线、角色句子、通用科学指南和不相关领域配置五种条件，采用自动规则评分。

Result: 职业专用提示平均准确率差异为-0.6个百分点（95% CI [-1.5, +0.2]），无统计显著提升。Token使用量增加1.5-2.3倍，成本增加2.2-4.5倍。生物信息学任务中，配置文件solve率（46.7%）低于基线（56.7%）。意外发现：长提示在SuperGPQA上因API中断导致的答案丢失更少（54.0% vs 71.6%）。

Conclusion: 默认加载完整职业配置文件不会提升准确率且成本显著增加。收益可能来自提示长度而非领域专业知识。未来可探索选择性检索配置文件片段或开放性科学任务的影响。

Abstract: Detailed profession-specific system prompts raise token use and estimated cost per response without a consistent accuracy gain. We evaluate Scientific Agents, an open-source corpus of 503 profession-specific AGENTS.md profiles, with Gemini 3.8 Flash via OpenRouter in the Pi agent harness. We compare matched profiles with four controls: a minimal baseline ("You are a helpful assistant"), the profile's opening role sentence, a generic scientific rigor guide, and a profile from an unrelated domain. Across nine text-based science benchmarks (4,531 sampled questions, 100 matched profiles), 4,488 items completed all five conditions after API-error retries, scored with automated, rule-based grading. The average profile-baseline accuracy difference is -0.6 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval [-1.5, +0.2] across fixed tasks), and no benchmark shows a statistically clear improvement. Matched profiles produced 1.5-2.3 times as many output tokens and cost 2.2-4.5 times more per successful call. On 60 tool-using BioMysteryBench bioinformatics problems (three runs each for baseline and profile), mean solve rates were 46.7% with the profile and 56.7% at baseline, a difference of -10.0 percentage points (95% interval [-16.7, -3.3]) driven by more frequent token- and time-limit stops under the profile. Longer prompts had one unexpected operational advantage: on SuperGPQA, frequent provider API drops left the short baseline with a correct first-pass answer on only 54.0% of items, against 71.6% with the profile. Generic and mismatched prompts were about as reliable, so this gain comes from prompt length or formatting rather than domain expertise. For the tested model and tasks, loading full profession profiles by default does not improve accuracy and costs considerably more; whether selective retrieval of profile sections or open-ended scientific tasks would change this remains to be tested.

</details>


### [121] [EviGraph: Proof-Carrying Selective Recommendation over Temporal Public-Service Knowledge Graphs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00212v1)
*Yixi Zhou,Sikun Wang,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviGraph是一个公共服务的问答系统，通过区分关键决策需求与可搁置信息来减少不必要的弃答。该系统在双语香港公共服务基准上验证，有效降低了拒答率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共服务推荐需要证据与请求的服务、范围和日期相匹配，但将每个缺失细节都视为决定性的会导致有用的推荐被不必要地撤回。需要区分哪些是关键的决策需求，哪些信息可以保持未决状态。

Method: 使用语言智能体将需求链接到时序知识图谱中的证据，同时使用确定性检查器验证推荐是否得到支持。

Result: 在具有可执行策略参考的双语香港公共服务基准测试中，这种关键/非关键需求的区分减少了不必要的弃答。但额外的验证反而会撤回已支持的推荐，而不改善决策质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的证据导航取决于明确界定决策所需确立的内容，而非简单地添加更多验证。

Abstract: Public-service recommendations require evidence that matches the requested service, scope, and date. Yet treating every missing detail as decisive can withhold useful recommendations. We introduce EviGraph, which distinguishes critical decision requirements from information that can remain unresolved. A language agent links these requirements to evidence in a temporal knowledge graph, while a deterministic checker establishes whether a recommendation is supported. Evaluation on a bilingual Hong Kong public-service benchmark with executable policy references shows that this distinction reduces unnecessary abstention. Additional verification, however, can withdraw supported recommendations without improving decision quality. These findings suggest that reliable evidence-based navigation depends on specifying what must be established for a decision, rather than simply adding more verification.

</details>


### [122] [Build2SPARQL: A Large-Scale Text-to-SPARQL Benchmark Dataset for Building Knowledge Graph Querying](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00224v1)
*Wooyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Build2SPARQL是一个用于建筑知识图谱的大规模Text-to-SPARQL基准数据集，包含6,136条可执行SPARQL查询和30,680个自然语言问题，通过知识图谱驱动的生成管线确保查询正确性，并在三个开放权重语言模型上验证了检索增强方法的有效性（准确率从0.2-20%提升至56-65%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统越来越多地使用Brick和ASHRAE 223P等本体表示为语义知识图谱，但将自然语言问题转换为SPARQL查询的Text-to-SPARQL任务受限于缺乏大规模、高质量的自然语言/SPARQL基准数据集，阻碍了人工智能应用的发展。

Method: 该研究提出一种知识图谱驱动的生成管线：SPARQL查询完全由图遍历代码生成并验证，仅使用大语言模型生成自然语言问题，确保查询正确性独立于模型行为。管线挖掘六种查询模式家族（线性链、分支、UNION、聚合、OPTIONAL、属性过滤），并用五种词汇注册方式表述每条查询。应用于201个建筑知识图谱（180个Brick、21个ASHRAE 223P）。

Result: 生成6,136条可执行SPARQL查询和30,680个自然语言问题。两人独立验证300个问题，语义保真度达98.8%、自然度达98.8%、操作可行性达84.0%。在三个开放权重语言模型上进行检索增强评估，零样本准确率为0.2-20%，三样本检索增强后提升至56-65%。

Conclusion: Build2SPARQL成功提供了一个大规模、高质量的建筑领域Text-to-SPARQL基准，通过知识图谱驱动的生成方式确保了查询的正确性，检索增强方法显著提升了模型性能，为建筑自动化领域的自然语言查询接口奠定了基础。

Abstract: Building automation systems are increasingly represented as semantic knowledge graphs (KGs) using ontologies such as Brick and ASHRAE 223P, creating a machine-readable substrate for artificial-intelligence applications. One promising application is translating natural-language questions into SPARQL (text-to-SPARQL), which would let building operators query these graphs through language agents, but progress is limited by the scarcity of large natural-language/SPARQL benchmarks. This paper presents Build2SPARQL, a large-scale benchmark for building KGs generated by a KG-grounded pipeline: SPARQL queries are produced and validated entirely by graph-traversal code, while large language models generate only the natural-language questions, keeping query correctness independent of model behavior. The pipeline mines six query-pattern families -- linear chains, branching, UNION, aggregation, OPTIONAL, and attribute-filtered -- and phrases each query across five vocabulary registers. Applied to 201 building KGs (180 Brick, 21 ASHRAE 223P), it yields 6,136 executable SPARQL queries and 30,680 questions. A two-rater human validation of 300 questions found 98.8% semantic fidelity, 98.8% naturalness, and 84.0% operational plausibility. A retrieval-augmented evaluation across three open-weight language models raised exact-match accuracy from 0.2-20% (zero-shot) to 56-65% (three-shot retrieved).

</details>


### [123] [Conflicting Supervision Moves Commitment, Not Capability: A 12.29σ arrangement effect that is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic score](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00234v1)
*Wenhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了训练数据排列顺序与学习率调度如何共同决定神经网络参数的学习结果。研究发现两者对"顺序效应"的影响是可分离的：排列只影响周期长度，学习率调度只影响端点对单个时刻的权重分配。实验表明，在10种数据排列下，使用恒定学习率时，分配份额差异显著（0.04到0.87），而余弦衰减调度则将这种差异压缩至两种可区分状态。通过标记数据中的"惯例"（如数据格式约定），可使顺序效应大幅降低，表明现有精确匹配benchmark无法捕捉这种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中一个长期争论的问题是：训练数据的顺序是否会影响最终模型行为。文献中"顺序重要"和"顺序不重要"两种结论并存，缺乏统一解释。研究者推测学习率调度可能是关键混淆因素。

Method: 1) 在同一语料库上训练10种不同数据排列的模型，仅改变lr_scheduler_type；2) 使用恒定学习率和单周期余弦衰减两种调度策略进行对比实验；3) 在提示中显式标记数据惯例（convention），控制其他所有因素不变。

Result: 1) 恒定学习率下，内部spans占0.2221分配份额，11.63对比基线，呈单调性与排列阻塞程度相关；2) 余弦衰减将10种排列压缩为两种可区分状态；3) 12个实验臂的acc_A+acc_B在9.7%内保持恒定，但分配份额在0.04到0.87间变化，表明基于精确匹配的指标完全无法捕捉顺序效应；4) 标记惯例后效应大幅降低。

Conclusion: 学习率调度不是实验的背景条件，而是决定顺序效应的平均算子。顺序效应由排列周期和学习率调度权重分配两个可分离因素共同决定。余弦衰减调度抑制了这种效应，导致"顺序不重要"的错误结论。精确匹配benchmark对该效应完全盲区，需在prompt中标记惯例才能检测到真实差异。

Abstract: "Train a model on the same problems written under two incompatible conventions, both correct, and ask what the ordering of that data writes into the parameters. The learning-rate schedule is not a background condition for that question. It is the averaging operator, and it decides the answer. We prove a bound in which the arrangement and the schedule enter the ordering effect as separate multiplied factors: the arrangement only as a block period, the schedule only as how much weight the endpoint can place on any one moment of the run. A decaying schedule cannot put a large step size and an uncontracted remainder at the same moment; a constant one does exactly that at the last step. That decay moderates ordering effects has been reported in pretraining; the mechanism, the separation, and a controlled measurement of both halves are ours. Ten orderings of one corpus, one budget, everything but the path held fixed, run twice under families differing in lr_scheduler_type and nothing else: at a constant rate the interior spans 0.2221 in allocation, 11.63 contrast floors, monotone in how blocked the arrangement is. Under the single cosine every published arm uses, the same ten arms occupy two distinguishable states where their own resolution would allow about ten. "Order matters" and "order does not matter" are the two ends of one knob. What the path writes is which convention the model commits to, and no exact-match benchmark can see it. Across twelve arms acc_A+acc_B is constant to within 9.7% while the allocation share runs 0.04 to 0.87, so the 12.29-sigma arrangement switch this paper measures is exactly zero under a convention-agnostic metric. That conservation is quoted from the decayed family throughout, the constant-rate one being a noisier place to read it. Marking the convention in the prompt collapses the switch and reaches 87.5% of the union ceiling."

</details>


### [124] [Predictive Credit: Measuring What Scientific Explanations Add to Experimental Forecasts](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00314v1)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong,Dongting Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过5项检验指标（承诺度、交付度、预测增益、对齐度、已知信号吸收）在三个数据集（336个前瞻性状态、Tox21的12个毒理学终点、OpenML的24个任务）上系统性测试AI研究智能体的解释是否具有"预测信用"。核心假设是：匹配的解释（matched explanation）相比仅提供描述（description）能否提升预测准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI研究智能体（如AutoGPT、Claude等）被用于科学假设生成和实验设计，评估其解释的预测价值变得至关重要。现有工作主要关注解释的忠实性（faithfulness），但缺乏对"解释是否能帮助人类更好地预测实验结果"这一核心问题的系统检验。

Method: 采用配对预测设计：同一智能体、同一干预、同一结局，但提供不同类型的上下文（仅描述、匹配解释、供体上下文）。使用5项检验追踪：(1)承诺度：解释是否承诺了可检验的预测；(2)交付度：预测是否实际被提供；(3)预测增益：匹配解释相比描述是否提升点准确度；(4)对齐度：匹配供体间隔是否包含匹配点；(5)已知信号吸收：解释是否有效利用了已知信息。测试了v5（冻结决策）、DeepSeek V4 Pro/Flash等多个模型。

Result: 主要检验均未达标：(1)Tox21的ROC AUC间隔评分检验未达预期（D-M=-.0026，95% CI[-.0174, .0104]）；(2)OpenML联合形成、点等价和可重复性规则未满足；(3)匹配点准确度增益未确认；(4)Tox21/OpenML种子-供体间隔跨零。辅助发现：DeepSeek V4 Pro的匹配卡和供体卡分别减少Tox21次级漂移64.5%和59.1%；研究者-authored机制阳性对照将点MAE降低2.60个百分点。

Conclusion: 在测试的供体分辨率下，AI研究智能体的自然语言解释未能提供可检测的预测信用。阳性对照（人类机制解释）确实有效，表明问题可能在于当前模型的解释质量而非实验设计。该协议可用于研究智能体基准测试和科学预测场景，但需要更高分辨率的解释或更精细的供体设计才能检测潜在价值。

Abstract: Research agents explain planned experiments. We measure predictive credit with paired forecasts sharing an intervention, forecaster, and outcome while varying description, matched explanation, and donor context. Five checks track commitment, delivery, predictive gain, alignment, and known-signal uptake. Across 336 prospective states in controlled learning, 12 Tox21 endpoints, and 24 OpenML tasks, v5's frozen credit decision was inconclusive. Tox21's preregistered ROC AUC interval-score harm test was unmet ($D-M=-.0026$, 95 percent interval [$-.0174$, .0104]); OpenML's joint formation, point-equivalence, and repeatability rule was unmet. Matched point-accuracy gains over description remained unconfirmed, and Tox21/OpenML seed-donor intervals spanned zero. Under requested DeepSeek V4 Pro, matched and donor cards reduced secondary Tox21 drift by 64.5 and 59.1 percent. A DeepSeek V4 Flash replay raised matched point MAE from .01823 to .02020 and missed matched-donor interval-score equivalence. OpenML full-card assignment widened nominal 80 percent intervals by 21 percent, with 49.3 percent coverage versus 51.4 percent for description and content in 66/144 cards. Direct-text Flash delivered all 144 notes without detectable matched point-accuracy gain. A researcher-authored mechanism positive control lowered point MAE by 2.60 percentage points versus description. The protocol measures predictive credit for research-agent benchmarks and scientific forecasting; natural-explanation credit remained unconfirmed at the tested donor resolutions.

</details>


### [125] [JusticeAxis: Benchmarking Legal Judgment between Rigid Rule Application and Ungrounded Discretion](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00353v1)
*Zhengkai Tu,Mingda Zhang,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang,Jimmy Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将法律判决形式化为"参考锚定任务"，构建了包含256个真实刑事案件的JusticeAxis数据集，并设计了JusticeAgent框架。该框架通过元素代理建立事实、法官代理在技能约束下适用法律，技能从执行轨迹中提炼并在贝叶斯可信区间下准入。实验表明开放权重模型倾向于无根据的自由裁量，而前沿模型则趋向法定默认值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律判决方法在刚性法条匹配与无根据的自由裁量之间摇摆，评分仅给出标签或量表，而提供平衡判断的经验未经验证。

Method: 1) 将法律判决形式化为参考锚定任务，保持判决同时依附于法条和案情；2) 构建JusticeAxis数据集：256个来自18个国家的真实刑事案件，包含音频、图像和文本证据，每个案件有3个律师撰写的判决（记录判决及两种失败情形）；3) 提出JusticeAgent框架：元素代理建立事实，法官代理在携带经验技能的条件下适用法律；4) 技能从执行轨迹中提炼，仅在贝叶斯可信区间内准入。

Result: 实验表明：1) 失败方向随规模变化——开放权重主干模型漂移至无根据的依据，前沿模型则趋向法定默认值；2) JusticeAgent作为简单有效的插件，能将冻结的开放权重主干提升至商业级水平。

Conclusion: 参考锚定框架和技能约束机制有效约束了法律推理中的两种典型失败模式，开放权重模型在插件辅助下可达到商业级法律判决性能。

Abstract: A sound judgment applies the law to established facts and weighs the circumstances in which they arose. However, existing methods swing between rigid statute matching and ungrounded discretion, benchmarks score a label or a rubric, and the experience that would supply the balance stays unverified. We formalize legal judgment as a reference-anchored task, whose object is a single decision that stays tied to the statute and to the circumstances at once. We introduce JusticeAxis, 256 real-world criminal cases from 18 countries with audio, image, and text evidence, and three lawyer-written judgments for every case: the recorded one and one for each failure. We further propose JusticeAgent, a harness whose element agents establish the facts and whose judge agent applies the law under skills carrying experience of the circumstances. Skills are distilled from execution trajectories and admitted only under Bayesian credible bounds. Experiments show that failure turns direction with scale: open-weight backbones drift to unsupported grounds, frontier models to the statutory default. We further verify that JusticeAgent, as a simple yet effective plugin, carries a frozen open-weight backbone to commercial level. Project resources are available at https://github.com/beita6969/JusticeAxis.

</details>


### [126] [What Should an Agent Remember? Disentangling Retention from Retrieval in Bounded-Memory Evaluation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00366v1)
*Juli Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对持久化智能体的记忆评估问题，提出不应混淆记忆保留(retention)和查询选择(selection)两个决策。通过构建跨维度基准测试，发现查询感知选择可提升15.5个百分点的事实召回率，而混合比较夸大了68.7点的优势（其中53.2点源于访问方式差异）。有界记忆下，查询感知、密集检索和神谕选择均达到保留上限，所有失败均由驱逐而非排序错误导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评估方法将保留策略和选择策略混合比较，导致难以区分两种决策的真实效果，无法为持久化智能体的记忆设计提供准确指导。

Method: 构建跨保留策略和选择规则的流式召回基准测试，在相同300个带种子( seeded)的episode上评估所有条件。

Result: 固定访问方式时，查询感知选择提升15.5个百分点召回率(95% CI: 12.8-18.2)；混合比较则报告68.7点优势（其中53.2点来自访问方式差异）。有界记忆下，多种选择方法均达到保留上限，319个失败全部由驱逐导致而非排序错误。目标足够远时召回率降至0%。SQuAD实验验证了保留上限的存在，并显示密集检索可优于词法检索。

Conclusion: 有界记忆评估应固定访问方式，并单独报告保留和选择效果。

Abstract: A persistent agent must decide both what to retain as information arrives and what to surface once a query appears, yet memory evaluations can confound these decisions by comparing methods that differ in both retention and selection. We build a streaming-recall benchmark crossing retention and selection rules and evaluate every condition on the same 300 seeded episodes. Holding access fixed, query-aware selection improves required-fact recall by 15.5 percentage points (95% CI: 12.8 to 18.2), whereas a mixed comparison that also changes history access reports a 68.7-point advantage, of which 53.2 points are attributable to access. Under bounded retention, query-aware, dense, and oracle selection reach the retention ceiling, and all 319 observed failures in the bounded recency condition are caused by eviction rather than ranking errors. Recall falls to 0% as targets recede sufficiently far into the past. Repeating the evaluation on SQuAD preserves the retention ceiling while showing that dense retrieval can outperform lexical retrieval on natural text. These results show that bounded-memory evaluations should hold access fixed and report retention and selection separately.

</details>


### [127] [Benchmarking Prompt Optimization of Large Language Models With Chess](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00416v1)
*Timothée Lesort,Alejandra López de Aberasturi Gómez,Tristan Karch,Tom Veniat,Philippe Modard,Karl Tuyls,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于1,118个Lichess国际象棋谜题的基准测试，用于研究冻结LLM的自动提示优化(APO)。该基准测试具有廉价、可判定、可再生等优势，可评估APO算法在8个目标模型上的表现，并连接谜题解决与游戏对局能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升，基准测试面临饱和、污染和评估成本高等挑战。这些问题在APO中尤为突出，因为APO需要反复评估提示质量。现有评估方法存在局限性：仅报告孤立测试项的成功率，无法连接优化效果与实际任务表现。

Method: 研究团队构建了基于Lichess谜题的象棋基准测试：(1)使用6种APO算法在8个目标模型上进行实验；(2)不仅测量基线强度，还评估模型对优化的响应程度；(3)测试优化提示在不同模型间的迁移性；(4)将谜题解决能力与短期对局表现关联起来。

Result: 基准测试具备四大特性：挑战性（Gemini 3.5 Flash作为元模型仅解决约55%谜题）；判别性（揭示不同方法和模型间的提升、不变和退化）；可再生性（提供新谜题降低污染风险，难度可调节）；经济性（完整研究运行成本约800美元）。

Conclusion: 国际象棋谜题是APO研究的理想基准：(i)挑战性—即使最强模型也无法完全解决；(ii)判别性—能有效区分不同方法效果；(iii)可再生—支持难度调整和污染控制；(iv)廉价—适合大规模研究。研究团队已开源谜题、优化和评估代码及数据集更新脚本。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models becomes increasingly challenging as their capabilities advance: benchmarks can saturate, public test sets risk contamination, and assessing harder tasks can require expensive grading or execution infrastructure. These challenges are amplified in automatic prompt optimization (APO), where evaluation is repeated throughout the search for better prompts. Studying APO therefore requires a benchmark that is cheap and deterministic to score, hard enough to leave room for improvement, and renewable as models evolve. We introduce a chess benchmark built from 1,118 Lichess puzzles to study APO for frozen LLMs: we optimize their prompts without updating their model weights. Chess combines inexpensive exact-match scoring, engine-based evaluation of alternative moves, and a renewable supply of problems with adjustable difficulty. Unlike evaluations that report only success on isolated test items, the benchmark also connects puzzle-solving gains to short game-play rollouts within the same domain. We use it to evaluate six APO algorithms on eight target models, measuring not only baseline strength but also how much each model responds to optimization and whether optimized prompts transfer across models and to game play. Chess is thus a well-suited benchmark for APO: it is (i) challenging, as even the strongest evaluated model, Gemini 3.5 Flash (used as the meta-model), solves only about 55\% of puzzles; (ii) discriminative, revealing gains, unchanged performance, and regressions across methods and models; (iii) renewable, with fresh puzzles to reduce contamination risk and adjustable difficulty to maintain headroom as models improve; and (iv) affordable, as the complete study runs for around \$800. We release the puzzles, optimization and evaluation code, and dataset-renewal scripts (https://github.com/imec-ailabs/Automatic-Prompt-Optimization-with-Chess).

</details>


### [128] [JevSpawn: Adaptive Agentic Inference through Compositional Action Spaces](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00437v1)
*Haoyang Su,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JevSpawn是一种组合式策略，通过并行动作生成和反馈驱动的分支选择，将自然语言任务规范与有限概率探索相结合，在保持快速推理的同时实现结构化 agentic 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 通过逐token生成中间推理和动作，计算成本高、速度慢；而Jev风格模型虽能快速概率预测，但需要预定义的有限域，限制了从自然语言指令推导动作的自主任务求解能力。

Method: JevSpawn采用组合式策略设计：(1)并行动作生成实现扩展探索；(2)反馈驱动的分支选择；(3)表征修订；(4)从保留备选方案中恢复。共享动作结构和模型前缀减少重复生成与上下文计算，无需额外训练。

Result: 在8个基准任务上与7个agent基线和TypeSafe Jev变体对比，JevSpawn在任务性能和导航速度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: JevSpawn为结构化agentic推理提供了一种有前景的方法，兼顾改进的任务完成能力和更快的推理速度。

Abstract: LLM agents generate intermediate reasoning and actions token by token, making extended interactions slow and computationally expensive. Jev-style models offer fast probabilistic predictions over finite fields, but require those fields to be specified in advance. This requirement limits autonomous task solving, where the available actions must be derived from natural language instructions and adapted through interaction. We introduce JevSpawn, a compositional policy that connects natural language task specifications to finite probabilistic exploration. Parallel action spawning is coupled with feedback driven branch selection, representation revision, and recovery from retained alternatives. Shared action structure and model prefixes reduce repeated generation and context computation without additional training. Evaluations on eight benchmark tasks against seven agent baselines and a TypeSafe Jev variant establish JevSpawn as a promising approach to structured agentic inference, with improved task performance and faster navigation.

</details>


### [129] [Science or Slop?: Benchmarking and Mitigating Scientific Slop in AI-Generated Papers](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00531v1)
*Yerim Oh,Young-Jun Lee,Jaewoo Ahn,Gunhee Kim,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"科学废话"(Scientific Slop)概念,用于描述AI生成学术论文中各部分看似合理但科学推理断裂的问题。构建了SciSlopBench基准数据集(390篇AI生成论文),提出6维度评估指标,检测准确率达85.9%。并开发SciSlopHarness框架,通过实验记录指导LLM修订,在无需人工参考目标情况下消除63%的AI-人类差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的AI检测器难以识别AI生成学术论文,因为这类论文的每个部分看似合理,但连接各部分的科学推理会断裂,可能误导读者对工作的评估。

Method: 构建SciSlopBench数据集(390篇AI生成论文,配对人类论文);提出6个维度的科学废话度量(结构、论证、人工制品);开发SciSlopHarness框架,利用实验记录引导LLM修订。

Result: 检测准确率85.9%(vs Binoculars的68.7%);高科学废话与低ICLR评分相关,可区分2017-2025年接受/拒绝论文;SciSlopHarness减少63%的AI-人类差距。

Conclusion: AI生成的学术论文在全局推理层面留下根本性痕迹,负责任的缓解需要严格的证据基础而非仅仅改进文本。

Abstract: AI-generated content, often called AI slop, is increasingly common everywhere, particularly in academia. Slop in AI-generated scientific papers, however, has more complex patterns that cannot be easily detected by existing token-based AI detectors. Each part of such a paper looks plausible while the scientific reasoning that connects the parts breaks down, which can mislead how readers assess the work. We benchmark these failures as scientific slop through six measures across Structure, Argument, and Artifacts. We construct SciSlopBench with 390 AI-generated papers, mostly in computer science but spanning the life, social, and natural sciences, each paired with a human-written paper matched by research problem and contribution type. Our measures identify the AI paper in each pair with 85.9% accuracy, compared with 68.7% for Binoculars. Higher scientific slop accompanies lower ICLR ratings and distinguishes rejected from accepted papers above chance in every year from 2017 to 2025. Reducing these patterns, however, is not as simple as directly optimizing the measures. We therefore propose SciSlopHarness, a harness-level framework that guides a fixed LLM to revise slop only where the experiment records support the change. While standard revisions leave residual slop and direct slop-aware prompting triggers reward hacking, SciSlopHarness reduces the remaining AI-human gap by 63% over the strongest revision baseline without requiring human reference targets. Overall, we demonstrate that AI-generated scientific papers leave fundamental traces in their global reasoning, and that responsible mitigation demands strict evidentiary grounding rather than mere prose refinement.

</details>


### [130] [Legal Research Bench: Measuring End-to-End Reliability in Long-Horizon Legal Research Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00609v1)
*Katrina Drozdov,Oliver Chen,Langston Nashold,Rayan Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LRB是一个包含413个美国法律研究问题的基准测试，用于评估语言模型代理在法律检索任务中的表现。研究发现当前最强模型Claude Opus 4.8仅在42.9%的任务上完全正确，表明现有法律AI代理可靠性仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律研究是律师的核心工作流程，需要识别控制性权威、验证法律有效性、协调法规与判例并综合得出可靠答案。实现自动化具有重要价值，但前提是可靠性必须得到保证——任何遗漏的权威依据、过时引用或错误法律结论都可能导致整个答案失效。

Method: 研究者构建了LRB基准，包含413个开放式美国法律研究问题，每个问题配有标准答案、支持性权威依据和二值评分标准。评估了13个前沿模型，采用网页搜索、判例检索、页面解析和检索工具等工具链。通过全通评分与来源验证评估模型表现，即只有满足所有必需标准且引用的权威依据可验证的答案才判定为正确。同时使用LLM评判器并经专家律师验证以确保评分与律师判断一致。

Result: 最强模型Claude Opus 4.8仅在42.9%的任务上完全正确。性能在不同法律领域和任务设置中差异显著：涉及冲突权威依据调和的问题表现更差；更多推理轮次、工具调用次数和推理成本均不能预测更高准确率。

Conclusion: 当前语言模型代理在法律研究任务上仍远未达到可靠水平。评估结果揭示了构建可靠法律AI助手面临的重大挑战，表明需要进一步改进模型在法律检索、权威验证和多源协调方面的能力。

Abstract: Legal research is a core and time-consuming legal workflow. Lawyers must identify controlling authority, verify that it remains valid, reconcile statutes and cases, and synthesize a grounded answer. Language model agents are a natural fit for this retrieval-intensive workflow, and automating even part of it would be valuable. But that value depends on reliability: a single missing authority, stale citation, or wrong legal conclusion can make an otherwise plausible answer unusable. We introduce \textbf{Legal Research Bench} (LRB), a benchmark of 413 open-ended U.S. legal research questions written by experts, each paired with a gold answer, supporting authorities, and a binary grading rubric. We evaluate thirteen frontier models in a harness with web search, case-law search, page parsing, and retrieval tools. We score agent responses through all-pass grading with source verification, where a response is correct only if every required criterion is satisfied and its cited authorities verify. We also validate the LLM judge against expert attorneys ensuring that benchmark scores track attorney judgment. Agents remain far from reliable: among the models we tested, the strongest, Claude Opus 4.8, is fully correct on 42.9\% of questions. Performance also varies substantially by task setting: all-pass rates differ across areas of law and are lower on questions requiring reconciliation of conflicting authorities. Across models, more turns, tool calls, and inference cost do not predict higher accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [CompMat-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents for Computational Materials Science](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00636v1)
*Chenmu Zhang,Levi Felix,Jun-Jie Zhang,Xingfu Li,Xuelian Jiang,Tao Jiang,Subhendu Mishra,Xixi Qin,Boris Yakobson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CompMat-Bench是一个包含94个任务的计算材料科学研究评估基准，通过预先复现研究步骤来评估AI智能体，避免评估过程中运行昂贵模拟的需求。基于三个LLM的智能体在完整指导下达到66.0-90.4%的通过率，长工作流和减少指导会限制性能，但影响方式因智能体而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AI智能体在科学研究任务上的能力受到时间和资源限制的约束，特别是在计算材料研究中，对每个智能体重复运行昂贵的模拟会使评估变得不切实际。

Method: 提出CompMat-Bench基准，包含94个从近期发表的计算材料研究中提取的任务，每个任务要求智能体完成实现研究目标的一个步骤。通过预先复现研究步骤，使用固定规则（无需LLM评判）对智能体进行评分。基准支持四种评估条件：单任务和工作流，配备完整或减少的方法论指导。

Result: 在完整指导下的单任务评估中，三个LLM智能体展示了完成材料研究各步骤的能力，通过率范围为66.0-90.4%。较长工作流和减少指导会以不同方式影响不同智能体的表现：削弱智能体的通过率下降，而最强智能体仅在长工作流结合减少指导时才会失败。失败分析表明大多数失败归因于科学错误而非软件使用错误。

Conclusion: CompMat-Bench为比较智能体在真实材料研究步骤上的能力提供了基础，并为分析智能体失败模式提供了框架。

Abstract: Evaluating AI agents on scientific research tasks is constrained by the time and resources required for the underlying experiments or calculations. In computational materials research, repeating the same expensive simulations across agents and trials can make evaluation impractical. We introduce CompMat-Bench, a benchmark of 94 tasks derived from recently published computational materials studies, each asking agents to complete a step toward achieving the study's scientific goal. We reproduce the research steps in advance and assess agents on preparing inputs and analyzing outputs for expensive simulations, so expensive simulations can be avoided during evaluation. The reproduced inputs and results serve as ground truth for grading agents with fixed rules, without an LLM judge. The benchmark supports four evaluation conditions: single tasks and workflows composed of related tasks, each with full or reduced methodological guidance. With full guidance on single tasks, agents based on three LLMs demonstrate the ability to complete individual materials research steps, with pass rates of 66.0-90.4% across 94 tasks. Both longer workflows and reduced guidance can limit agent performance, but in different ways for different agents: they lower the pass rates of the weaker agents, whereas the strongest agent falls only when a long workflow is combined with reduced guidance. Failure analysis attributes most failures to scientific errors rather than to errors in software usage. CompMat-Bench provides a basis for comparing agents on the steps of real materials research and for analyzing agent failure modes.

</details>


### [132] [Incident-Arena: Getting agents to the last nine of reliability](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00648v1)
*Andre Fu,Malik Drabla,Leon Liu,Meji Abidoye,Marek Suppa,Lata Mishra,Adnan El Assadi,Yiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Incident-Arena基准测试，用于评估AI编码智能体在生产级事故响应（agentic SRE）中的能力。该基准包含20个基于真实开源软件部署的任务，在临时Kubernetes集群中注入故障并验证修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI编码智能体虽在应用编码中广泛使用，但对生产事故响应的能力评估不足。现有的SRE基准测试存在三个关键局限：1) 使用玩具级仓库，环境不真实；2) 采用非标准框架实现；3) 仅使用简单静态验证器。因此需要构建更贴近真实生产环境的事故响应评估基准。

Method: 研究团队构建了Incident-Arena基准，包含20个精心设计的事故响应任务。每个任务包含：1) 将生产应用部署到临时Kubernetes集群；2) 从配置层到底层镜像注入故障；3) 根据任务需求生成持续负载；4) 采用超越静态检查的功能验证器，确保系统级指标稳定且修复安全。

Result: 智能体试验平均消耗2.81M tokens和41轮对话，显示出超越现有基准的长时域推理能力。在20个任务和3种应用基板上测试，最先进模型的得分低于64.3%。失败原因涵盖：诊断/定位错误、不完整修复以及不安全的功能回归。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在生产事故响应任务上表现仍有较大提升空间。Incident-Arena通过真实生产环境设置、功能验证和系统级指标监测，为评估agentic SRE能力提供了更严格的基准，推动该领域向实际应用场景发展。

Abstract: AI coding agents are ubiquitous in engineering workflows amongst industry and academia. Yet, despite their use in app coding, relatively less attention has been paid to their ability to execute on production incident response. This emerging field, termed agentic site-reliability-engineering (SRE) contains benchmarks limited by (1) unrealistic environments, typically toy repositories (2) non-standard framework implementations and (3) simple static verifiers. We introduce Incident-Arena, a human-built benchmark of 20 carefully selected tasks grounded in real-world deployed open source software. Each task deploys a production application to an ephemeral Kubernetes cluster, injecting a fault from the config layer through underlying images, and a sustained load profile given the task requirements. We also present a novel verification method, going beyond static checks to functional verifiers, holding systems level metrics stable, while ensuring repairs are done safely. Agent trials run an average of 2.81M tokens and 41 turns, going beyond existing benchmarks, demonstrating agentic long horizon reasoning. Across 20 tasks and 3 application substrates, frontier models score below 64.3%, with failures extending from diagnosis/localization errors, through incomplete repairs and unsafe regressions.

</details>


### [133] [Agent Evaluation Reliability: More Tasks Won't (Always) Fix An Agent Leaderboard](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00651v1)
*Michael Hardy,Ruhana Azam,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体评估的可靠性问题进行研究。评估排名不仅反映模型本身，还受评估条件（如脚手架、任务）的影响。作者开发了贝叶斯方差分解框架，对22个基准测试进行分析，发现：固定模型-脚手架系统的排名可靠性高（0.935-0.994），但底层模型可靠性较低（0.148-0.841）；脚手架选择可能改变结论；增加更多任务无法解决所有不确定性；整合多样化基准测试可将跨任务排名可靠性从0.44提升至0.75，并降低83%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体评估日益广泛用于比较LLM并指导部署决策，但评估排名可能反映评估条件而非底层模型能力。固定评估可能可靠地排名已部署系统，但无法可靠地排名底层模型。需要明确当前评估能可靠支持哪些结论，以及需要何种额外评估来改进。

Method: 开发贝叶斯方差分解框架处理稀疏、不平衡的智能体排行榜，应用至 Holistic Agent Leaderboard 和 Harbor Index 的22个基准测试。框架将信号（与预期结论相关的性能差异）与噪声（不影响预期结论但可能改变排名的无关变异）分离。

Result: 1. 可靠性取决于测量目标：固定模型-脚手架系统排名可靠（0.935-0.994），底层模型可靠性显著较低（0.148-0.841）。2. 脚手架选择可改变结论：脚手架效应在不同评估间差异很大。3. 增加任务数量无法解决所有不确定性：当不确定性由有限的脚手架覆盖主导时，即使无限多的类似构建任务也只能将模型排名可靠性提高最多0.097。4. 整合多样化基准测试可改进跨任务排名：同等任务预算下，可靠性从0.44提升至0.75，成本可降低83%。

Conclusion: 评估设计应遵循预期结论：明确分数或排名的含义，诊断可靠性限制，将评估预算用于影响结论的不确定性来源。需根据具体测量目标选择合适的评估条件，而非追求通用排名。

Abstract: Agent evaluations are increasingly used to compare LLMs and inform deployment decisions, yet ranks can reflect not only the model but also the effects of the evaluation conditions such as the scaffolds or tasks. This makes reliability claim-dependent: an evaluation that reliably ranks deployed systems may not reliably rank underlying models. We ask which conclusions current agent evaluations reliably support and what additional evaluation would improve them. We develop a Bayesian variance-decomposition framework for sparse, imbalanced agent leaderboards and apply it to 22 benchmarks from the Holistic Agent Leaderboard and Harbor Index. The framework separates signal, performance differences relevant to the intended claim, from noise, irrelevant variation that can still change rankings. We find: (1) Reliability depends on the measurement goal. Fixed model-scaffold systems are ranked reliably (0.935-0.994), while underlying-model reliability is substantially lower (0.148-0.841). (2) Scaffold choice can change conclusions. Inter-scaffold reliability measures whether scaffolds preserve model rankings, showing that scaffold effects vary substantially across evaluations. (3) More tasks cannot resolve all uncertainty. Even infinitely many similarly constructed tasks improve model-ranking reliability of a benchmark by at most 0.097 when uncertainty is dominated by limited scaffold coverage. (4) Pooling diverse benchmarks can improve cross-task rankings at lower cost. For rankings across diverse agentic tasks, pooling benchmarks raises projected reliability from 0.44 to 0.75 at the same task budget and can reduce projected cost by up to 83\%. Evaluation design should follow the intended claim: identify what a score or ranking should mean, diagnose what limits its reliability, and spend evaluation budget on the sources of uncertainty that matter.

</details>


### [134] [Backdoor Containment via Expert Quarantine and Shutdown in LLMs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00663v1)
*Jianwei Li,Min-Seon Kim,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"学习但隔离"（learn, but channel）作为大语言模型后门防御的第三种策略，通过在MoE架构中使用路由专家机制将后门行为吸引到隔离组件中，部署时仅需关闭该组件即可实现防御，在多种任务和模型上实现ASR从100%降至0-10%，同时保持模型正常功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御策略分为两类：抑制后门学习（在训练阶段过滤毒化数据）或先学习后净化（训练完成后再修复权重或过滤输入）。这两类策略存在局限：前者可能影响正常数据利用，后者计算开销大且可能无法完全消除后门。论文提出需要第三种策略——允许后门形成但将其隔离到可独立关闭的组件中。

Method: 提出QES（Quarantined Expert Shutdown）方法：1）在Transformer语言模型中引入路由专家特定LoRA分支和轻量级路由器；2）使用辅助路由目标，将触发器条件下的行为吸引到指定专家；3）在其他专家中保留正常能力；4）部署时仅需将隔离专家的路由权重置零即可实现防御，无需触发器筛选或权重更新。

Result: 在两个任务、三种攻击、四种模型家族上，ASR从100%降至0-10%（多数设置），同时下游效用基本保持或仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: "学习但隔离"是生成式LLM后门防御的一个未被探索的新范式，QES在计算效率（仅需常数时间操作）和实用性（无需重训练或复杂检测）方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Backdoored large language models (LLMs) can behave normally on benign inputs while producing attacker-specified outputs under hidden triggers. Existing defenses span four stages--prior-training, in-training, post-training, and inference-time--and share one of two underlying strategies: either suppress backdoor learning (by filtering poisoned data or interrupting its acquisition during optimization) or learn, then purify (by repairing model weights or gating inputs after a fully backdoored model has formed). We propose a third strategy, learn, but channel: allow backdoor formation during training but route it into a designated, quarantined component that can be disabled at deployment. To this end, we propose Quarantined Expert Shutdown QES, a computationally efficient containment strategy built in a regularization-steered MoE-like setting. Specifically, given a poisoned dataset, QES augments a Transformer-based language model with routed expert-specific LoRA branches and lightweight routers, and uses auxiliary routing objectives to attract trigger-conditioned behavior into a designated expert while preserving benign capability elsewhere. At deployment, mitigation reduces to a single constant-time operation: zeroing the quarantined expert's routing weight, without trigger screening or further updating model weights. Empirically, our methods reduce the attack success rate ASR from 100% to 0-10% on most settings across two tasks, three attacks, and four model families, while downstream utility is often preserved or only modestly affected. These results establish learn, but channel as a previously unexplored regime for backdoor containment in generative LLMs.

</details>


### [135] [Ontology-Grounded, Reasoner-Verified Benchmarks for Evaluating LLM Reasoning in Scientific AI](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00682v1)
*Nishtha N. Vaidya,Stephan Grimm,Thomas Hubauer,Thomas A. Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个自动化流程，从OWL 2本体生成多选题基准测试，用于评估科学AI中大型语言模型的逻辑推理能力。通过扰动类定义公理生成干扰项，并使用OWL推理器形式验证其错误性。在三个本体（Pizza、PMDco、DOID）上生成112至15,216道题目，六种LLM零样本准确率41.1-76.8%，证明基准测试具有挑战性和区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在科学AI应用中的逻辑推理经常产生不可接受的事实错误，而现有评估方法存在局限性：人工构建数据集扩展性差，LLM生成数据可能嵌入其自身的缺陷。需要高质量基准测试来可靠评估LLM的逻辑推理能力。

Method: 自动化MCQ生成管道：(1) 从任意充分公理化的OWL 2本体提取类定义公理生成正确选项；(2) 通过扰动右侧类表达式生成干扰项；(3) 使用OWL推理器通过蕴含检查形式验证干扰项的错误性；(4) 干扰项分为四类语义类别（类不可满足性至弱化包含关系）；(5) 用LLM judge评估自然语言质量。

Result: 在Pizza（小型学术）、PMDco（复杂材料科学）、DOID（大型生物医学）三个本体上分别生成112、2,491、15,216道MCQ。质量指标：LLM judge评分分别为4.02、4.36、3.36（满分5）；正确选项与干扰项相似度>0.8，表明干扰项无法从表面形式排除。六种LLM零样本准确率41.1-76.8%，显著高于25%随机基线。

Conclusion: 该方法成功生成了具有形式正确性保证的诊断性推理基准测试，能够区分LLM在不同语义类别上的推理失败类型，为科学AI逻辑推理能力的可靠评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly underpin scientific AI applications that reason over structured knowledge, from biomedical question answering to materials informatics. However, their logical reasoning often falls short, producing factual inaccuracies unacceptable in these settings. Reliable evaluation remains challenging: manual dataset construction scales poorly, and LLM-based generation risks embedding the very flaws it aims to measure. High-quality benchmarks must ground both correct and incorrect labelled examples in explicit background knowledge, formally verifiable by a standard reasoner. We propose a pipeline that automatically generates ontology-grounded multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks from any sufficiently axiomatised OWL 2 ontology, with correct answers grounded in the ontology by design. Distractors are generated by perturbing the right-hand-side class expressions of class definition axioms, and their incorrectness is formally verified by an OWL reasoner via entailment checks. We evaluate the pipeline on three ontologies: Pizza (small, academic), PMDco (complex, materials science), and DOID (large, biomedical), generating 112, 2,491, and 15,216 MCQs respectively. Distractors span four semantic categories from class unsatisfiability to weakened subsumptions, enabling diagnostic evaluation of specific reasoning failures. Items meet natural language quality standards: mean LLM judge scores of 4.02, 4.36, and 3.36 out of 5 confirm fluency, and correct-answer-to-distractor similarity above 0.8 shows that wrong options cannot be dismissed on surface form alone. Six LLMs evaluated zero-shot achieve 41.1-76.8% accuracy, well above the 25% random-guessing baseline, indicating the benchmarks are challenging and discriminative. This work is a step towards more reliable benchmarks for assessing logical reasoning in scientific AI.

</details>


### [136] [R-GroundBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for R-Group Groundingin Markush Molecular Editing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00700v1)
*Xin Wang,Zichuan Ying,Xinna Lin,Junqi Zhang,Hanyi Xiong,Tianyu Gao,Hairong Zhang,Qixiang Hua,Botian Shi,Zhenhailong Wang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: R-GroundBench是一个针对专利Markush结构（包含R基团占位符的分子家族表示）的诊断基准测试，用于评估AI模型在分子 grounding 方面的能力。研究发现当前模型在识别任务上准确率可达90%以上，但在困难 VQA 任务上下降至56-66%，生成任务Exact Match低于20%，表明现有AI系统在Markush编辑的grounding和执行能力上存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子-语言基准测试仅关注完整指定的分子，缺乏对R基团grounding的评估。Markush结构在制药专利中广泛使用，需要在分子、文本和化学信息之间进行 grounding，但这一领域尚未被系统研究。

Method: 构建R-GroundBench基准，包含两个tracks：(1) 多选VQA track，通过控制难度和模态拆分实现渐进式评估；(2) 开放式生成track。使用真实专利Markush结构作为数据来源。

Result: Easy VQA准确率超过90%，但控制shortcuts后的Hard VQA下降至56-66%；领域特定预训练的化学VLMs在Hard VQA上仅达25.7-46.2%；生成任务的Exact Match对大多数模型低于20%，含视觉输入时低于8%。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在Markush结构编辑的grounding和执行能力上不可靠，存在识别与分子grounding之间的显著差距，这对AI驱动的科学发现构成挑战。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for scientific discovery enable molecular understandingand design, yet reasoning over incomplete chemical representations remainsunclear.Markush structures, which encode molecular families through variable R-groupplaceholders (\textit{R\textsubscript{1}}, \textit{R\textsubscript{2}}, \textit{X}, etc.), are ubiquitous in pharmaceutical patents and requiregrounding across molecular, textual, and chemical information.However, existing molecule-language benchmarks focus on fully specifiedmolecules, leaving R-group grounding largely unevaluated.We introduce R-GroundBench:, a diagnostic benchmark built from real patent Markushstructures, featuring a Multiple-Choice (VQA) track with controlled difficultyand modality splits, and an open-ended Generation track.Our results reveal a substantial gap between recognition andmolecular grounding.While models achieve over 90\% accuracy on Easy VQA, performance drops to56--66\% on Hard VQA when shortcuts are controlled.Chemical-domain VLMs also remain unreliable, achieving only 25.7--46.2\% on HardVQA despite domain-specific pretraining.Moreover, Generation Exact Match remains below 20\% for most models and below8\% when visual input is required.These findings reveal that current AI systems lack reliable grounding andexecution for Markush editing, highlighting challenges for AI-drivenscientific discovery.

</details>


### [137] [MemFit: Efficient Long-Term Agentic Memory](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00872v1)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFit是一种面向对话代理的长时记忆系统，通过LLM无关的插入方式和多路径检索策略，显著降低记忆操作的成本和延迟。系统以追加方式存储对话轮次，配合段落摘要进行索引，并结合词汇和语义信号进行跨编码器重排，在文本和多模态场景下均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆系统依赖LLM代理进行记忆组织和整合，导致写入操作成本高昂且效率低下，限制了其在实际应用中的可扩展性。

Method: 核心设计包括：(1) 采用追加式只读存储，LLM无关的即时插入；(2) 通过段落摘要而非压缩替换进行轮次索引；(3) LLM无关的多路径检索策略，融合词汇与语义信号；(4) 基于跨编码器的重排序，针对增强描述的片段进行处理；(5) 同时支持文本和多模态场景。

Result: 在LoCoMo、MemGallery和LongMemEval-S三个主流基准测试中达到最优性能，记忆构建时间和成本均降低数倍。

Conclusion: MemFit为持久化代理记忆提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案，在保持系统性能的同时显著优化了资源消耗。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems for large language models (LLMs) have gained popularity for extending reasoning capabilities across applications. Current memory systems rely on LLM agents to organize and consolidate memory, resulting in costly, inefficient write operations. To address this limitation, we propose MemFit, a long-term memory system for conversational agents that reduces the cost and latency of memory operations. Unlike existing systems that rely on expensive LLM calls for memory construction or discard surface-level details through compression, MemFit stores each turn verbatim in an append-only store with near-instantaneous, LLM-free insertion, indexing turns with segment summaries rather than replacing them. Additionally, MemFit uses an LLM-free, multi-path retrieval strategy that combines lexical and semantic signals with cross-encoder reranking over caption- augmented episodes in both textual and multimodal settings. Empirical results on three widely used benchmarks, LoCoMo, MemGallery, and LongMemEval-S, show that MemFit achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing memory construction time and cost several-fold, providing a scalable and efficient solution for persistent agentic memory.

</details>


### [138] [OR for AI That Does OR: Routing LLMs up the Escalator inside the OSCAR Framework](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00912v1)
*Jinzhi Bu,Haixin Tang,Huanan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR是一个基于LLM的商业优化建模框架，通过离线模拟器验证LLM生成的代码是否正确匹配业务需求，解决了LLM可能生成看似可行但实际不准确或非最优解的问题。该系统在保持本地部署能力的同时，通过成本感知的模型调度策略，在五个基准问题上达到95-100%的准确率，且成本比Codex和Claude Code分别低3.1倍和5.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在将业务描述转化为优化模型时存在关键问题：(1)生成的可执行代码可能错误表述约束或目标函数；(2)即使返回可行解，也可能不是最优解；(3)对于反复使用优化建模的组织，需要低成本、高准确率的解决方案，且最好能本地部署。

Method: OSCAR采用三组件架构：Simulator（离线模拟器，通过标注决策示例认证）、Coder（LLM代码生成）、Reviewer（结果审查）。核心创新是将寻找下一个认证改进解建模为难度未知的序列决策问题，确定调用哪些LLM及何时停止。在已知先验简化设定下证明成本有序递增最优；在一般菜单设定下导出无需先验的竞争保证。

Result: 在5个基准问题上，OSCAR达到95%-100%的准确率。使用两个小型开源权重模型（单GPU可部署），单次尝试准确率分别为29%和48%。对比商业模型：5次运行平均token成本，Codex是OSCAR的3.1倍，Claude Code是5.8倍。

Conclusion: OSCAR支持本地或云端部署，可适应预算和保密需求。组织应维护标注的可行/不可行决策示例以明确业务规则。框架的简单操作规则和可调设置使其能适应LLM能力和价格的变化，为企业提供了灵活且经济高效的优化建模解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models can translate business descriptions into optimization models, but executable code may misrepresent constraints or objectives. A solver can then return an optimal solution to the wrong problem. Even when the solution satisfies the intended operating rules, a better plan may exist. For organizations that repeatedly use optimization modeling, an LLM-based framework should produce accurate formulations at low cost and, ideally, run locally. We study how to verify improvements and allocate attempts across LLMs that differ in price and capability. We develop OSCAR (Optimization modeling by Simulator, Coder, And Reviewer), which uses an offline Simulator certified against labeled decision examples to compare candidates and continues searching beyond feasibility. We model the search for the next certified improvement as sequential decisions under unobserved difficulty: which LLMs to call and when to stop. In a simplified known-prior setting, we give conditions under which cost-ordered escalation is optimal. For general menus, we derive a prior-free competitive guarantee. On five benchmark problems, OSCAR achieves 95% to 100% accuracy at the reported settings using two small open-weight LLMs, each deployable locally on a single GPU. Their single-attempt accuracies average 29% and 48%. In five runs per problem, Codex and Claude Code incur average token costs 3.1 and 5.8 times OSCAR's, respectively. OSCAR supports open-weight models locally or in the cloud, depending on budget and confidentiality requirements. Firms should maintain labeled decision examples of feasible and infeasible decisions to clarify plain-language operating rules. OSCAR follows these labels when an LLM's interpretation conflicts with them. As LLM capabilities and prices change, OSCAR's simple operating rules and adjustable settings help firms adapt their model choices and benefit from these advances.

</details>


### [139] [Finding the Right Fit: Model-Harness Interactions across Agent Tasks](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00917v1)
*Yixuan Li,Yiyun Zhou,Yao Long Teng,Fuchao Yang,Yanchen Deng,Zhiyi Lyu,Xuyu Dong,Feng Chen,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI智能体系统中模型与工具调用框架（harness）的匹配问题，发现不同框架会导致模型排名反转，同一模型在不同任务上最优框架也不同，但存在稳定的互补配对（如openJiuwen+Kimi），表明模型、框架和任务应联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体研究关注模型选择和工具调用框架选择，但这两者如何共同影响性能尚不清楚。研究者希望探究：强模型、强框架或强配对在不同场景下是否保持稳定。

Method: 构建66种配置：4个可配置框架（OpenHands、DeepSeek Harness、PI、openJiuwen）×5个模型，在3个基准测试（TUA-Bench、ALE-CLI、Terminal-Bench 4）上评估，并补充官方配对方案。通过轨迹分析探究适配性差异的原因。

Result: 模型排名在不同框架间发生反转（如Terminal-Bench 4中，Claude在OpenHands领先GPT 7.94分，但在PI中落后30.16分）；5个模型中4个的最优框架随基准变化；但openJiuwen始终为Kimi最优框架（领先5.61-11.11分）；厂商原生框架非最优；更高成本不保证更高性能（PI框架下GPT成本不足DSH的1/4但得分更高）；GPT偏好PI的精简架构，Kimi因常发出格式错误的工具调用而偏好openJiuwen。

Conclusion: 模型、框架和任务三者应联合评估而非单独选择。框架 scaffold 的设计（如错误处理方式、架构复杂度）需与模型能力特点匹配才能发挥最佳性能。

Abstract: Choosing an agent system means choosing both a language model and the harness through which it acts. We ask whether a strong model, harness, or pairing stays strong when the setting changes. We evaluate 66 configurations: four configurable harnesses (OpenHands, DeepSeek Harness, PI, and openJiuwen) paired with five models on TUA-Bench, ALE-CLI, and Terminal-Bench 4, plus the native Codex-GPT and Claude Code-Claude pairings. Model rankings reverse across harnesses. On Terminal-Bench 4, Claude leads GPT by 7.94 points in OpenHands but trails it by 30.16 points in PI. For four of the five models, the best harness changes from one benchmark to another, yet some pairings hold: openJiuwen gives Kimi its highest score on all three benchmarks, by 5.61 to 11.11 points. A model's own vendor harness is not reliably its best, and higher cost does not reliably buy a higher score. On Terminal-Bench 4, GPT scores higher under PI than under DSH at less than a quarter of the cost per task. Matched trajectories suggest why fit varies. Models start almost all repairs themselves, so much depends on whether the harness hands failures back in a form the model can use. GPT does best with PI's lean scaffold, while Kimi, which often issues malformed tool calls, does best in openJiuwen. We argue that the model, the harness, and the task should be evaluated together, and we release the harness adapters, evaluation code, and all 6,204 scored trajectories at https://github.com/liyix/finding-the-right-fit and https://huggingface.co/datasets/yixuanli97/finding-the-right-fit.

</details>


### [140] [PG-SFT: Balancing Capability Acquisition and Retention in Offline Agent Fine-Tuning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00949v1)
*Ronghua Li,Zi Liang,Zhishan Li,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在agent轨迹的SFT微调中平衡"获取新能力"与"保留原有能力"之间的权衡。标准SFT会提升目标任务但损害其他能力，KL惩罚和限制更新幅度也未能解决该问题。论文提出Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)，利用turn-level信息增益调整监督强度，在保持基线能力的同时更好地平衡性能回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT对agent轨迹进行逐token模仿学习，虽能提升目标任务，但会损害模型原有的通用推理、工具调用、代码生成等能力。如何在获取新能力的同时保留原有能力，是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)方法，利用agent轨迹中turn-level的信息增益作为指标，动态调整监督强度。实验对比了标准SFT、KL惩罚、限制更新幅度等多种基线方法。

Result: PG-SFT在评估基准上实现了更优的权衡：显著减少了分布漂移和广泛能力退化，但以轻微的目标任务性能下降为代价。

Conclusion: 平衡"获取-保留"权衡不仅取决于模型是否锚定在基线行为上，还取决于监督应该在哪里以及以多大强度偏离该行为。turn-level的自适应监督是关键。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on offline agent trajectories is the standard approach for training specialized tool-using agents, but forcing models to imitate reasoning and actions token by token may harm other capabilities (e.g., general reasoning, tool calling, code generation) of the base model. In this work, we focus on studying \emph{how to better balance the trade-off between acquiring new capabilities and preserving existing ones during agent trace SFT}. By comparing several baselines in our setup, standard SFT improves the target benchmark while lowering several non-target benchmark scores; meanwhile, simply constraining distributional drift using KL penalty or limiting the update magnitude did not avoid this regression trend. Motivated by recent token-wise adaptive learning objectives, this work proposes \textbf{Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)} to leverage turn-level information gain of agent trajectories as an indicator to adjust supervision strength. PG-SFT yields a more favorable observed trade-off on the evaluated benchmarks, substantially reducing distributional drift and broad capability degradation at the cost of slight degradation in target-task performance. Our findings suggest that balancing the acquisition--retention trade-off depends not only on whether the model is anchored to its base behavior, but also on where and how strongly supervision should depart from that behavior.}

</details>


### [141] [VeriHarness: Scaling Agentic Verification for Long-Horizon Tasks](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00972v1)
*Caiqi Zhang,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Zoey CuiZhu,Nigel Collier,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VeriHarness框架，通过将底层LLM转变为具有工作空间、证据工具和可复用验证技能的主体验证器，解决长时域LLM智能体缺乏可靠验证机制的难题。框架包含分歧解析器和共识挑战者两个核心组件，在5个基准测试和2个前沿模型上取得最高选择分数，证据驱动的修订进一步将性能提升6.2-6.4个百分点，且验证技能可通过失败反馈实现自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体承担越来越复杂的长时间跨距任务，输出验证变得日益困难。核心问题在于：测试时无参考答案或评分标准，且现有方法存在"分歧常暴露正确替代方案，而共识反而可能掩盖错误"的现象，导致传统验证机制失效。

Method: VeriHarness将基础LLM转变为主体化验证器，配备工作空间、证据工具和可复用验证技能。核心机制包括：(1) 分歧解析器(Disagreement Resolver)：检查竞争性CLAIMS，针对环境证据进行验证；(2) 共识挑战者(Consensus Challenger)：测试共享CLAIMS，搜索遗漏需求。最终通过发现指导最终产物的选择和修订。

Result: 在5个长时域工作空间基准测试和2个前沿模型上，VeriHarness在选择分数上达到所有基线最高。证据驱动的修订进一步提升平均性能：Gemini 3.5 Flash提升6.2分，Claude Opus 4.8提升6.4分。验证技能可从失败反馈中自改进。

Conclusion: VeriHarness证明了"共识可能掩盖错误、分歧常暴露正确"的反直觉发现，提出了一种可自改进的验证技能机制，为规模化长时域主体验证提供了新范式。开源约26,000条rollouts（成本超10万美元）以支持后续研究。

Abstract: As LLM agents undertake increasingly complex, long-horizon tasks, verifying their outputs becomes increasingly challenging. We study how verification capability can be strengthened with a fixed base model, without access to reference answers or grading rubrics at test time. Repeated sampling yields multiple rollouts that can contain complementary correct claims, but we need a reliable verification mechanism to determine which claims to trust. We first find that disagreement often exposes correct alternatives, while consensus can conceal errors. These observations motivate VeriHarness, which turns the underlying LLM a generator uses into an agentic verifier by giving it a workspace, evidence tools, and reusable verification skills. A disagreement resolver checks competing claims against environmental evidence, while a consensus challenger tests shared claims and searches for omitted requirements. Their findings guide the selection and revision of the final artifact. Across five long-horizon workspace benchmarks and two frontier models, VeriHarness achieves the highest selection scores among the evaluated baselines. Evidence-backed revision further improves average performance, bringing gains over a single rollout to 6.2 points with Gemini 3.5 Flash and 6.4 points with Claude Opus 4.8. We further show that verification skills can self-improve from failure feedback, demonstrating VeriHarness as a novel and critical approach for scaling long-horizon agentic verification. We release the full pool of approximately 26,000 rollouts across all five benchmarks and both models, produced at a cost of over $100,000, to support future research on agentic verification.

</details>


### [142] [Beyond Answer Confidence: A Controlled Audit of Self-Knowledge in a Black-Box Decision Model](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01006v1)
*Sharath M Shankaranarayana,Davor Runje,Jan Jannink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了决策模型（如Jev）的置信度校准是否真正反映"缺失知识"还是仅反映"随机性"。通过15个公共数据集和6个生成任务家族的实验，发现Jev的置信度在熟悉任务上已校准，但在面对无相关信息或超出知识边界的新闻时，仍给出高置信度（高达0.80-0.33的超额置信度），且无法通过回溯校准修正。提出用针对性yes/no问题替代置信度来探测模型是否"知道"答案（AUROC 0.91-1.00），但该方法在去除表层线索后失效，表明黑盒知识审计需显式控制表面线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前决策模型的置信度校准只确保平均准确性，无法区分低置信度是源于"随机不确定性"还是"系统性知识缺失"。当模型超越其知识边界时，置信度可能仍然偏高，导致误用。需要系统性地审计模型置信度是否真正反映其知识边界。

Method: 使用Jev决策模型，在15个公共数据集和6个生成任务家族上进行实验。采用配对干预方法：对固定项目变化输入信息，测量置信度响应。通过三类探测：(1)无相关信息下的置信度；(2)超出知识边界的新闻预测；(3)针对性yes/no问题（是否知道答案、结果是否已定、证据是否充分）。

Result: Jev置信度校准在熟悉任务上表现良好，但在知识缺失场景中失效：无相关信息时对显著选项置信度高达0.80；超出知识边界时超额置信度达0.21-0.33；回溯校准无法弥补。Yes/no探测效果更优：检测结果是否已定(AUROC 1.00)、证据是否充分(AUROC 0.95 vs 置信度0.85)、模型是否知道答案(AUROC 0.91)。但去除表面线索后，"是否知道"探测无优势。

Conclusion: 置信度校准不等于知识边界感知。模型可能在无知识时仍给出高置信度，且无法通过标准校准技术修正。黑盒知识审计需要显式控制表面线索（如实体名称、时间信息），并考虑使用针对性的元认知探测问题，而非仅依赖概率输出。

Abstract: Decision models return probabilities intended for routing, abstention and automated action. Calibration makes those probabilities useful on average, but does not establish whether low confidence reflects chance or missing knowledge, nor whether confidence falls when a model moves beyond what it knows. We audit this distinction in Jev, a decision model, with over 15 public datasets and 6 generated task families, with paired interventions that vary the information supplied for a fixed item. Jev's confidence is calibrated on familiar closed-choice tasks but fails as an indicator of missing knowledge: with no answer-relevant information it assigns up to 0.80 to a salient option, and on news beyond an observed knowledge boundary it exceeds accuracy by 0.21--0.33, a gap that recalibration on earlier months does not close. Targeted yes/no questions give sharper readouts of the case: whether an outcome is settled (AUROC 1.00) and whether the evidence suffices (0.95, against 0.85 for confidence on the same items). Asking whether Jev knows the answer appears to flag fabricated entities and post-boundary news (0.91), but with realistic names or with dates removed it shows no advantage over answer uncertainty.
