Black-box knowledge audits therefore need explicit controls for surface cues. Code: https://github.com/Syntheme/beyond-answer-confidence.

</details>


### [143] [Improving Math Reasoning through Value-guided Informative Search](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01080v1)
*Shaohuai Liu,Yuning Wu,Haoran Liu,Enzo Jia,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APIVIS是一个训练时框架，通过将有限预算的Gumbel搜索适配到分块级数学推理，结合直接响应和搜索响应产生信息性相对奖励，并选择性监督搜索改进的token，以改进RLVR中搜索 rollout 策略优于当前策略的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在rollouts中引入搜索以增加轨迹多样性，但多样性本身不能保证搜索诱导的rollout策略优于当前策略。

Method: APIVIS结合直接响应和搜索响应于每个rollout组中，使搜索发现的改进产生信息性相对奖励；对搜索改进的token应用选择性监督，在均匀组奖励使GRPO失效时保留学习信号。

Result: 理论证明精确值引导选择可提升每个搜索状态处的期望验证器奖励，该保证可扩展到完整rollout策略，在有界值估计误差下有相应近似保证。实验在数学推理基准和不同规模模型上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: APIVIS在广泛认可的数学推理基准和不同模型规模上相比竞争性基于搜索的方法展现出实质性改进，验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models. Recent work introduces search into RLVR rollouts to increase trajectory diversity, but diversity alone does not ensure that the search-induced rollout policy improves upon the current policy. To address this gap, we propose APIVIS, a training-time framework that adapts finite-budget Gumbel search to chunk-level mathematical reasoning. APIVIS combines direct and searched responses within each rollout group, allowing improvements found by search to produce informative relative rewards. It further applies selective supervision to search-improved tokens, preserving a learning signal when uniform group rewards render GRPO ineffective. We show that exact value-guided selection improves the expected verifier reward at each searched state and that this guarantee extends to the complete rollout policy, with a corresponding approximate guarantee under bounded value-estimation error. Experiments on widely recognized mathematical reasoning benchmarks and different model scales demonstrate substantial improvements over competitive search-based methods, validating the effectiveness of APIVIS.

</details>


### [144] [CortexBridge: Cortical Alignment of EEG Montages for Foundation Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01124v1)
*Jiazhen Hong,Xiaotian Zhou,Zihao Ding,Kailong Wang,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CortexBridge是一个轻量级适配器，通过结合EEG特征与电极和脑图谱坐标，将任意电极排布映射到共享的皮层潜在空间，解决EEG基础模型在电极布局变化时迁移困难的问题。在MOABB的5个BCI数据集上测试3个冻结基础模型，13/15次评估中取得性能提升，SSVEP分类最大提升达13.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型通常使用固定通道词汇表或有限电极排布进行预训练，当电极布局发生变化时，模型迁移困难。这限制了EEG基础模型在实际应用中的通用性和灵活性。

Method: 提出CortexBridge，一种轻量级适配器。该方法结合EEG特征与电极坐标和脑图谱坐标，将任意电极排布映射到共享的皮层潜在空间。使用三个冻结的预训练基础模型（EEGPT、LaBraM、CBraMod）进行评估，在MOABB的5个BCI数据集上进行测试。

Result: CortexBridge在13/15次评估中提升了性能。平衡准确率平均提升：EEGPT 0.80%、LaBraM 0.70%、CBraMod 3.26%；最大提升达13.02%（12类SSVEP分类）。可视化显示SSVEP在Yeo视觉网络中表征更集中，而听觉P300分布不同。

Conclusion: 皮层对齐是一种可学习的、解剖学上合理的路由机制，能够将异构EEG电极排布适配到预训练基础模型，为EEG基础模型的跨数据集迁移提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models are often pretrained with a fixed channel vocabulary or a limited set of montages, making transfer difficult when electrode layouts change. We propose CortexBridge, a lightweight adapter that combines EEG features with electrode and atlas coordinates to map arbitrary montages into a shared cortical latent space. Evaluated with three frozen foundation models on five brain-computer interface (BCI) datasets from the Mother of All BCI Benchmarks (MOABB), CortexBridge improves performance in 13 of 15 evaluations. The gains in balanced accuracy average 0.80% for EEGPT, 0.70% for LaBraM, and 3.26% for CBraMod, with a maximum gain of 13.02% on 12-class steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) classification. Visualizations of the learned atlas representations reveal task-dependent spatial patterns, with SSVEP showing a more concentrated representation in the Yeo Visual network than auditory P300. These results establish cortical alignment as a learnable and anatomically grounded routing mechanism from heterogeneous EEG montages to pretrained foundation models.

</details>


### [145] [Reputation, Strategy, and Emotion Effects on Generative AI Cooperation: A Comparison Across Reasoning and Non-Reasoning Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01222v1)
*Celso de Melo,Zishan Feng,James Hale,Kazunori Terada,Giorgio Coricelli,Jonathan Gratch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用迭代囚徒困境测试前沿生成式AI模型的合作行为，发现推理模型比非推理模型表现出更复杂但仍具异质性的社会行为特征，揭示了模型特定的利用风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI系统在经济和社交关键场景中承担越来越自主的角色，理解其合作倾向及其影响因素变得至关重要。该研究旨在系统性地检验AI合作行为的特征及影响因素。

Method: 采用迭代囚徒困境范式，操控三个因素：对手机誉（正面/未知/负面）、策略类型（勒索型/慷慨型）、非语言情绪信号（竞争性/合作性面部表情）。研究一使用非推理模型（Claude 3.5、Gemini 2.0 Flash、GPT-4o），研究二使用推理模型（Claude 4.6、Gemini 3、GPT-5.2）。

Result: 非推理模型中合作行为系统性受三因素影响，与人类行为模式相似但权重各异；推理模型更集中依赖策略和声誉，近乎消除"波将金效应"（表面合作），情绪信号作用更条件化。推理模型表现出异质性的终局行为模式，从持续合作到最后一轮背叛不等。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型是日益复杂但仍具异质性的社会行为体；不同模型的特定利用风险对部署于谈判等多轮交互场景具有直接实践意义；亟需开发标准化合作基准以指导生成式AI在社交关键场景中的负责任部署。

Abstract: As generative AI (Gen AI) systems take on increasingly autonomous roles in economically and socially consequential interactions, understanding their propensity to cooperate -- and the signals that shape this propensity -- has become essential. We examine cooperative behavior in frontier Gen AI models using the iterated prisoner's dilemma, manipulating counterpart reputation (positive, unknown, negative), strategy (extortion vs. generosity), and non-verbal emotional signaling (facial expressions conveying competitive or cooperative appraisals). In a first study with non-reasoning models (Claude 3.5, Gemini 2.0 Flash, GPT-4o), cooperation was systematically shaped by all three factors, paralleling patterns long documented in human behavioral research, though models varied substantially in how heavily each factor was weighted. A second study with reasoning models (Claude 4.6, Gemini 3, GPT-5.2) revealed a more concentrated reliance on strategy and reputation, a near-elimination of the Potemkin effect observed in non-reasoning models (evidenced by near-uniform cooperation in a diagnostic harmony game), and a more conditional role for emotion consistent with a hierarchical cue-weighting strategy rather than a simple loss of social sensitivity. Reasoning models also showed heterogeneous end-game behavior, ranging from sustained cooperation to systematic last-round defection effect, revealing model-specific exploitability profiles with direct practical relevance for deployment in negotiation and other multi-round interactions. Together, these findings characterize Gen AI models as increasingly sophisticated, though heterogeneous, social actors, and underscore the practical value of developing standardized cooperation benchmarks to inform the responsible deployment of Gen AI in interactive, socially consequential settings.

</details>


### [146] [HHR: Hierarchical Hash Retrieval for Efficient LLM Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01230v1)
*Lianjun Liu,Tiantian Zheng,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出层级哈希检索（HHR）框架，通过几何感知键路由（GKR）和学习哈希投影（LHP）两阶段方法解决哈希检索中汉明距离与注意力相关性不匹配的问题，实现长上下文推理的高效加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理是LLM的计算瓶颈。现有哈希检索方法将查询和键编码为二进制代码使用汉明距离进行键选择，但存在关键不匹配问题：Query-Key logit取决于方向相似性和特征幅值的联合作用，而哈希二值化丢弃了幅值信息，导致低logit键的误检（假阳性）和高logit键的漏检（假阴性）。

Method: 提出两阶段粗到细框架：1）GKR（几何感知键路由）：学习头-wise正交变换重新分配特征幅值，导出更具判别性的页面级logit边界，实现低logit键的有效剪枝同时保留重要候选；2）LHP（学习哈希投影）：学习头-wise投影空间，将汉明距离与真实Query-Key相关性排序对齐，实现细粒度检索。通过GKR和LHP结合，抑制假阳性并恢复假阴性。

Result: 在多种LLM和基准测试上验证：LongBench上平均提升1.10分；Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在128K上下文长度下实现最高3.30倍解码加速和2.83倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: HHR通过GKR和LHP协同工作，有效解决了哈希检索中幅值信息丢失的核心问题，显著提高了基于哈希的稀疏注意力的保真度，在长上下文推理任务上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Efficient long-context inference is essential for large language models (LLMs), yet it poses a severe computational bottleneck. Hash-based retrieval offers an efficient alternative by encoding queries and keys into binary codes and using Hamming distance for key selection. However, this leads to a critical mismatch between Hamming distance and attention relevance. Query-Key logits depend jointly on directional similarity and feature magnitudes, whereas hash binarization discards magnitude information, causing both false-positive retrieval of low-logit keys and false-negative omission of high-logit keys. To address these failures, we propose Hierarchical Hash Retrieval (HHR), a coarse-to-fine framework that progressively improves retrieval accuracy through Geometry-Aware Key Routing (GKR) and Learned Hash Projection (LHP). GKR learns a head-wise orthogonal transformation to redistribute feature magnitudes and derive more discriminative page-level logit bounds, enabling effective pruning of low-logit keys while preserving important candidates. LHP then learns a head-wise projection space that aligns Hamming distance with the true Query-Key relevance ranking for fine-grained retrieval. By combining GKR and LHP, HHR suppresses false positives and recovers false negatives, substantially improving the fidelity of hash-based sparse attention. Extensive experiments across diverse LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that HHR achieves superior performance over existing methods. For example, on LongBench, HHR improves the average score by 1.10 points and, at a context length of 128K, achieves up to a 3.30x decoding speedup and a 2.83x end-to-end speedup for Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/lianjunl13-sudo/HHR.

</details>


### [147] [Questionnaire-Guided Disaggregation of Energy Appliance Use for Domestic Smart Meter Data](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01297v1)
*Achal Nanjundamurthy,Rupam Misra,Suzanne Little,Alan F. Smeaton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无标签的电力分解系统，将30分钟分辨率的智能电表数据分解为9类家电使用数据，结合事件检测与问卷引导估算，在4个数据集上实现最低整体分解误差，并提供节能建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰智能电表以30分钟间隔记录用电数据，虽适用于分时电价计费，但无法捕获具体家电使用情况。现有非侵入式负荷监测(NILM)方法在低分辨率数据上的准确性有限，需要新的分解方案。

Method: 混合方法：1) 对高功率负载采用事件检测；2) 结合问卷引导的估算方法。评估数据集包括：1个校准家庭(商业对比仪)、2个公开基准数据集(UK-DALE和REFIT，带分表计量)、1个爱尔兰智能电表数据集(2,968用户，累计4,800+设备年)。与两种独立开发的分解系统进行对比。

Result: 该混合方法在所有数据集的建筑上均获得最低的整体分解误差；在54个配对月份上，月度性能显著优于对比方法(p<0.001，Holm校正)。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效分析家庭用电模式，并为用户提供针对性的节能建议(如减少或转移特定家电用电以降低成本)，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Ireland's smart metering programme records electricity use at 30-minute resolution, with smart meters installed in over 80\% of households as of late 2025. While this is useful for billing of smart, time-of-use tariffs, it is too coarse to capture use of domestic appliances. We present a label-free disaggregation system that breaks usage data into 9 appliance categories by combining event detection for high-power loads with questionnaire-guided estimation. Our evaluation draws on four datasets: a calibration household with a commercial comparator, two public benchmarks (UK-DALE and REFIT) with per-appliance sub-metering, and a smart meter dataset of more than 4,800 years of use from 2,968 Irish consumers. Compared against two independently developed disaggregation systems our hybrid method combining analysis of usage data with questionnaire results, achieves the lowest whole-decomposition error on all buildings across the datasets, with better month-level performance over 54 paired months ($p<0.001$, Holm-corrected). Our method provides useful advice on a household's energy consumption patterns and advice on how to reduce or shift usage on some appliances in order to reduce costs.

</details>


### [148] [DAYJOB: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Professional Work](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01306v1)
*Stephanie Finley,Liudas Panavas,Thomas Mikkelson,Cam Hinton,Stacey Ganss,Bradley Monton,Emily Kendall,Michelle Spradlin,Lydia Bye,Michael O'Brien,Lauren Ylvisaker,Derek Ray,Suhaas Garre,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAYJOB是一个专业任务基准测试，包含130个由医疗和金融领域专业人士构建的真实任务，用于评估AI代理处理复杂专业工作的能力。评估显示当前最强模型Claude Opus 5.5仅能通过约24%的任务，中位数配置仅通过0.6%-2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工作通常以简短的请求开始，需要专业人士自行推断需求、确定相关文档、验证前提是否成立。现有的AI评估基准无法充分测试AI代理处理这种模糊、专业性强的任务能力。

Method: 构建包含医疗(50)和金融(80)领域的130个专业任务，每个任务预估需专业人员在13.6-16.6小时内完成。任务以容器化环境呈现，配有专家评估标准(二值判定，中位数47.5-57.5条)，由代理法官评判交付文件。测试了30种模型配置(来自13个开发者)。

Result: 最强模型Claude Opus 5.5在医疗任务通过率为24.7%，金融任务为23.9%；中位数配置分别仅为0.6%和2.5%。案例研究表明，AI代理会接受与记录内容相矛盾的前提，并在分析中沿用错误输入。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在处理需要专业判断、主动验证前提、处理模糊请求的专业任务方面能力仍然有限。团队发布了医疗全部任务、金融50个任务、评估框架和排行榜。

Abstract: Professional work often starts with a brief request that leaves the professional to work out what is needed, which documents matter, and whether the request's premise holds. We introduce DAYJOB, a benchmark of 130 tasks built by professionals in healthcare (50) and finance (80). The tasks are estimated to take a professional 13.6 hours on average in healthcare and 16.6 in finance. Each task is a containerized Harbor environment with an expert rubric of binary criteria (median 47.5 and 57.5 per task) that an agentic judge applies to the delivered files, and an attempt passes only if it meets every criterion. Across 30 model configurations from 13 developers, the strongest, Claude Opus 5.5, passes 24.7% of healthcare and 23.9% of finance attempts, and the median configuration passes 0.6% and 2.5%. In case studies, agents accept premises that the record contradicts and carry wrong inputs through otherwise consistent analyses. We release all healthcare tasks, 50 of the 80 finance tasks, the evaluation harness, and the leaderboard.

</details>


### [149] [ProtoFlow: Prototype-Guided Flow Matching for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01320v1)
*Shibo Feng,Wanjin Feng,Yang Qiu,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtoFlow是一个结合向量量化自编码器与原型先验流匹配的多变量时间序列预测框架，通过利用VQ码本学习到的潜在原型作为流匹配的结构化先验，避免了自回归方法的曝光偏差和训练-推理不匹配问题，实现了高效且优越的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VQ的时间序列预测方法依赖自回归(AR)token生成，存在曝光偏差和训练-推理不匹配问题；基于流匹配的非自回归方法虽高效，但通常使用通用高斯先验，缺乏语义信息。论文发现VQ码本已捕获代表性潜在原型，可作为更有效的流匹配先验。

Method: ProtoFlow框架包含三个核心步骤：(1)通过向量量化自编码器将多变量序列映射到离散潜在空间；(2)从学习到的码本构建结构化原型先验；(3)学习基于DiT的整流流模型，将样本从原型先验传输到未来潜在表示，条件为历史观测。核心创新是用学习到的原型先验替代通用噪声初始化。

Result: 实验表明ProtoFlow避免了AR token预测的展开不匹配问题，促进更快的训练收敛，在基准数据集上持续取得优越的预测性能，同时保持高效推理。

Conclusion: ProtoFlow成功将VQ码本的语义知识与流匹配的高效非自回归生成相结合，为高维多变量时间序列预测提供了一种高效、准确的新范式。

Abstract: Generative modeling has shown strong promise for multivariate time mseries (MTS) forecasting, especially scale to high-dimensional settings. Diffusion-based methods achieve competitive performance but typically require many sampling steps at inference. VAE-based non-iterative forecasting frameworks have therefore emerged as an efficient alternative. Within this line of work, vector quantization (VQ) enables controllable latent space modeling by mapping multivariate series into compact discrete representations. Existing VQ-based forecasting methods, however, typically rely on autoregressive (AR) token generation, which suffers from exposure bias and training-inference mismatch. Flow matching provides an efficient non-autoregressive alternative for latent forecasting, but existing formulations usually initialize transport from a generic Gaussian prior. We instead observe that the trained VQ codebook already captures representative latent prototypes and can thus serve as a more informative prior for flow matching. Based on this insight, we propose ProtoFlow, a forecasting framework that combines vector-quantized autoencoding with Prototype-prior Flow matching. Our method first maps multivariate sequences into a discrete latent space, then constructs a structured prior from the learned codebook, and finally learns a DiT-based rectified flow to transport samples from this prior to future latent representations conditioned on historical observations. By replacing generic noise initialization with a learned prototype prior, ProtoFlow avoids the rollout mismatch of AR token prediction and promotes faster training convergence. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that it consistently achieves superior forecasting performance with efficient inference.

</details>


### [150] [PRISM: A Category-Theoretic Framework for Measuring and Refining Multimodal Analogies](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01383v1)
*Mirella Zeisler,Ojas Shirekar,Mircea Licǎ,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM框架通过范畴论(Category Theory)将类比建模为显式关系映射，利用视觉-语言模型实现跨模态实例化，并通过pullback score量化关系对齐度。实验表明该方法在AnaloBench上达到82.5%的准确率，人类评估中57.65%的成对比较偏好精炼后的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI多模态类比生成方法缺乏可解释的度量来评估生成输出中是否真正理解和保持了关系结构。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Pullback Score：基于范畴论的图表示量化关系对齐度；2) 迭代精炼循环：利用pullback score作为上下文反馈信号，迭代修订生成图像以增强关系深度。采用视觉-语言模型(VLM)实现跨模态结构实例化。

Result: Pullback score在AnaloBench基准上选择正确类比的准确率达82.5%；VLM评估和人类评估均显示PRISM持续提升隐喻一致性和类比适切性，人类评估中57.65%的成对比较偏好精炼输出。

Conclusion: 局限性：定性分析表明精炼过程可能偏向视觉拥挤的构图而非真正更深层的关系对应。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning involves identifying and preserving relational structures across domains. However, existing approaches to AI-driven multimodal analogy generation lack an interpretable measure of whether this structure is understood and maintained in the generated output. We address this gap with Pullback Refinement via Interpretable Structural Mapping (PRISM), a modality-agnostic framework for measuring and improving relational alignment in multimodal analogies, evaluated on visual metaphor generation. PRISM represents analogies as explicit relational mappings grounded in category theory and uses VLMs to instantiate these structures across modalities. Its first component, the pullback score, quantifies relational alignment from the resulting graph representation. On the AnaloBench benchmark, selecting the correct analogy purely by pullback score achieves 82.5% accuracy, demonstrating that the score captures meaningful relational information. PRISM's second component is an iterative refinement loop that uses the pullback score as an in-context feedback signal to iteratively revise the generated image towards greater relational depth. VLM-as-a-judge and human evaluations show that PRISM consistently improves metaphor consistency and analogy appropriateness over zero- shot generation, with human participants preferring the refined output in 57.65% of pairwise comparisons. However, a qualitative analysis reveals that refinement can favour visually crowded compositions rather than genuinely deeper relational correspondences.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Memory: Harnessing Long-Horizon Agents with Explicit Belief States](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01415v1)
*Yu Luo,Jiamin Jiang,Yimin Zuo,Xidao Wen,Rongchen Gao,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Guiyang Liu,Cheng Zhang,Fang Situ,Qi Zhou,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoS是一个推理时框架，通过构建和维护显式信念状态来帮助LLM智能体进行长期任务执行，解决了当前智能体记忆管理无法保证连贯世界理解的问题，并能检测和恢复"信念陷阱"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体在执行复杂任务时，对交互历史的组织方式无法保证对当前世界状态的连贯理解，缺少显式的状态追踪机制。

Method: PoS在推理时构建并持续维护显式信念状态，每个信念结合当前世界状态估计和未解决的任务需求；框架包含一致性验证机制和任务进度监控，用于检测"信念陷阱"（智能体持续行动但未取得有意义进展）；恢复策略根据陷阱模式和未解决需求类型定制。

Result: 在四个基准测试（涵盖执行和诊断任务）上，使用三种LLM后端，PoS均取得最高整体性能。消融实验证明一致验证和恢复机制的重要性；上下文扩展实验显示对上下文增长的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 信念构建和持续维护可作为长期上下文管理的基础，值得超越单纯的历史记录和压缩方法进一步探索。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now undertake increasingly complex tasks, but the way they organize interaction history into memory does not ensure a coherent understanding of the current world. We introduce PoS, an inference-time framework that constructs and continually maintains explicit belief states as the agent's decision context. Each belief combines an estimate of the current world state with unresolved task requirements, making explicit what the agent still needs to learn and accomplish. To keep this belief reliable and actionable, PoS validates its consistency and monitors task progress to detect Belief Trapping, where the agent continues to act without making meaningful progress toward the goal. Recovery is then tailored to both the trapping pattern and the type of unresolved task requirement. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning execution and diagnosis show that PoS achieves the highest overall performance on every benchmark with all three LLM backbones. Ablations demonstrate the importance of consistency validation and recovery, while context-scaling experiments show resilience to context growth. Together, these results support belief construction and continual maintenance as a foundation for long-horizon context management beyond history retention and compression.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [152] [SW-KAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Stieltjes-Wigert q-Orthogonal Polynomials](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00050v1)
*Amirhosein Azarpour,Seyyed Moein Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SW-KAN利用Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式（定义在半无限域上）替代传统B样条或正交多项式，通过指数-tanh映射解决输入域不匹配问题，在O(N)复杂度下实现数值稳定的多项式展开，在图像分类和函数逼近任务中以更少参数达到更优精度，尤其适合资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体基于有界或半无限支撑的正交多项式，存在真实无界输入与多项式定义域之间的根本性失配问题，同时计算开销和梯度条件数问题尚未解决。

Method: 1) 引入Stieltjes-Wigert q-正交多项式（支撑域(0,∞)）；2) 设计光滑的指数-tanh映射实现域对齐并保持梯度良好条件数；3) 采用数值稳定的三项递推公式实现O(N)复杂度且无需特殊函数调用。

Result: SW-KAN在图像分类和连续函数逼近任务中均优于现有多项式KAN基线，以显著更少的参数实现复杂多元函数逼近，在降维和少样本条件下仍保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: SW-KAN通过log-normal权重结构和可学习q参数提供了独特的归纳偏置，在精度-效率权衡上具有竞争力，是资源受限场景下高效函数逼近和分类的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) represent a paradigmatic shift in deep learning by replacing fixed node activations with learnable univariate functions on edges, offering enhanced interpretability and parameter efficiency. While recent polynomial-based KAN variants have addressed the computational overhead of original B-spline implementations, they introduce a fundamental yet underexplored challenge: the domain mismatch between unbounded real-valued inputs and the bounded or semi-infinite support of orthogonal polynomial bases. To address this limitation, we propose the Stieltjes-Wigert Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (SW-KAN), a novel architecture that employs Stieltjes-Wigert q-orthogonal polynomials defined on the semi-infinite domain (0, infinity). We introduce a smooth exponential-of-tanh mapping that stably bridges the domain gap while preserving well-conditioned gradients, and leverage a numerically stable three-term recurrence that evaluates polynomial expansions in O(N) operations without special-function calls. Through comprehensive experiments spanning image classification and continuous function approximation, we demonstrate that SW-KAN achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across diverse tasks. The log-normal weight structure and learnable q-parameter of Stieltjes-Wigert polynomials provide a distinct inductive bias that enables robust performance under resource-constrained conditions, including reduced feature dimensionality and limited training data. The proposed architecture not only outperforms established polynomial KAN baselines on standard benchmarks but also exhibits strong representational capacity for approximating complex multivariate functions with remarkably few parameters, making it a compelling alternative for efficient function approximation and classification in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [153] ["very likely" Means "uncertain"? How LLMs Diverge from Humans in Linguistic Uncertainty Quantification](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00083v1)
*Jinhao Duan,Zicheng Liu,Zijie Liu,Kaidi Xu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM与人类在口头不确定性量化上的差异，提出METHODNAME算法通过优化方法直接从LLM输出学习最优的不确定性标记档案，解决了传统采样方法无法直接比较人类与LLM置信度语义的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过语言标记（如"possible"、"likely"）表达不确定性，但大多数LLM的不确定性量化方法依赖似然度或一致性信号，与人类的口头表达方式存在根本差异。从认知角度看，准确的口头不确定性反映元认知监测能力，代表知识边界的认知（"知道自己不知道"）。因此需要研究LLM与人类在口头不确定性表达上的分歧，以及语言标记是否能可靠地量化LLM的不确定性。

Method: 1) 从心理学和决策科学文献中整理人类不确定性标记语料库；2) 基于该语料库对LLM进行基准测试；3) 提出METHODNAME——一种基于优化的算法，通过将标记-不确定性映射拟合到经验正确性来学习最优不确定性档案，无需依赖重复采样估计不确定性。

Result: 观察到LLM编码的口头不确定性数值水平与人类存在显著差异；METHODNAME能够发现每个口头标记应传达的的概率质量，揭示了人类与LLM在口头表达上的系统性置信度偏差。

Conclusion: METHODNAME实现了人类与LLM置信度语义的直接、标记级别比较，解耦了两者之间的不匹配，揭示了口头表达中的系统性置信度差异，为改进LLM的不确定性通信提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Humans express uncertainty verbally via markers (e.g., "possible," "likely"), yet most LLM uncertainty quantification (UQ) relies on costing likelihood- or consistency-based signals. From a cognitive perspective, accurate verbal uncertainty reflects metacognitive monitoring, representing knowledge boundaries ("knowing that you don't know") to support regulation and information seeking. In this paper, we investigate how LLMs diverge from humans in verbal uncertainty quantification and whether verbal markers can reliably quantify LLM uncertainty. We curate a corpus of human uncertainty markers from psychology and decision-science literature and benchmark LLMs against it. We observe that LLMs encode verbal uncertainty with numerical levels that differ substantially from those of humans. We then introduce METHODNAME, a novel optimization-based algorithm that learns an optimal uncertainty profile over uncertainty markers directly from LLM outputs. By fitting a marker-uncertainty mapping to best explain empirical correctness, METHODNAME discovers how much probability mass each verbal marker should convey, rather than estimating uncertainty via repeated sampling. METHODNAME enables a direct, marker-level comparison of confidence semantics between humans and LLMs, disentangling mismatch and revealing systematic confidence disparities in verbal expressions.

</details>


### [154] [One Mastery Threshold Does Not Fit All Knowledge Tracing Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00095v1)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了知识追踪(KT)模型与掌握阈值之间的匹配问题，发现同一数值阈值在不同模型下产生截然不同的教学决策，建议阈值需根据模型和教学优先级重新校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导师系统使用掌握阈值决定学生何时可停止练习并进阶，但同一数值阈值在不同知识追踪模型下可能导致差异巨大的教学决策，现有研究缺乏跨模型阈值的系统比较。

Method: 在四个公共教育数据集上评估六种KT模型，测试12个阈值(0.50-0.99)，从进阶后表现、覆盖率、练习负担和先验表现群体差异四个维度进行综合分析，并在30种预定义教学设置下识别平衡性能与进阶的阈值。

Result: BKT模型对阈值变化不敏感，而神经模型随阈值升高变得极度选择性；最佳平衡阈值因模型和设置差异显著，约半数设置中神经模型与BKT阈值差异超过0.10；更严格的阈值并不能可靠地缩小表现差距，反而可能不成比例地限制进阶，先验较强的学生进阶频率最高为先验较弱学生的3.26倍。

Conclusion: 掌握阈值应根据KT模型或教学优先级变化进行重新校准，且应从表现、练习、进阶和公平性四个维度综合评估其效果。

Abstract: Tutoring systems use mastery thresholds to decide when students can stop practicing and advance, but the same numerical threshold can lead to very different decisions when the underlying knowledge tracing (KT) model changes. We examine six KT models across four public educational datasets and evaluate 12 thresholds from 0.50 to 0.99 using post-advancement performance, advancement coverage, practice burden, and disparities across prior-performance groups. We also identify thresholds that balance performance, extra practice, and advancement under 30 predefined instructional settings. Bayesian Knowledge Tracing (BKT) is relatively insensitive to threshold changes, while neural models become much more selective as thresholds increase. This partly reflects different model outputs: BKT estimates latent mastery probability, whereas neural models estimate the probability of a correct next response, so the same cutoff does not represent the same level of mastery. The best-balanced threshold varied substantially across models and settings. In half of the tested settings, neural models and BKT differed by more than 0.10 in their selected thresholds, although this gap became smaller when greater priority was placed on reducing extra practice and allowing more students to advance. Stricter thresholds also did not reliably reduce performance gaps and could disproportionately restrict advancement, with stronger-prior students advancing up to 3.26 times as often as weaker-prior students. These results show that mastery thresholds should be recalibrated when the KT model or instructional priorities change and evaluated by their effects on performance, practice, advancement, and access.

</details>


### [155] [The Hidden Costs of 99% Accuracy: A Trustworthiness Audit of the Telco Customer Churn Benchmark](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00118v1)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对IBM Telco Customer Churn基准数据集（n=7,043）中普遍报告的>95%准确率进行审计，揭示了四个被F1和准确率指标掩盖的信任度问题：预拆分SMOTE使F1虚高13.1个百分点（36%合成点来自测试集插值）；TotalCharges与tenure×MonthlyCharges高度冗余（R²=0.999）但SHAP特征重要性排名第九；等渗回归校准优于温度缩放；成本最优阈值比F1最优阈值低5-10倍，可节省约77,000美元/千客户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBM Telco Customer Churn数据集上测试准确率普遍>95%，最高达99.01%，但这些高性能可能源于方法论缺陷而非真实预测能力。现有研究以准确率和F1为核心指标，忽视了数据泄漏、特征冗余、校准和成本敏感性等信任度问题。

Method: 采用多维度审计方法：(1)跨10个分类器、15个随机种子的统计检验（Wilcoxon检验）；(2)最近邻插值机制的直接测量；(3)R²诊断检测特征冗余；(4)15个种子的校准审计比较等渗回归与温度缩放；(5)基于24个月CLV代理和50美元保留成本的成本敏感阈值优化。跨领域复制实验验证泛化性。

Result: 四大发现：①预拆分SMOTE使churn类F1虚高13.1个百分点（p<10^-4），约36%合成点为测试集实例的最近邻插值；②TotalCharges被tenure×MonthlyCharges决定（R²=0.999），移除后准确率变化<0.2%，但TreeSHAP将其排名第9；③等渗回归是最强默认校准方法，温度缩放在类加权树集成上对双峰预测概率分布失效；④成本最优阈值约为F1最优阈值的1/5至1/10，每千客户节省约77,000美元。F1和F2在同领域（伊朗电信）泛化，跨领域（银行）仅F1泛化。

Conclusion: 提出四组件报告检查清单（流程披露、冗余诊断、校准审计、成本敏感阈值）以提升 churn 预测研究的可信度，并发布可复现实现。强调在报告准确率/F1之外，需关注数据泄漏、特征工程、概率校准和业务成本结构。

Abstract: Customer churn prediction on the IBM Telco Customer Churn benchmark (n = 7,043) routinely reports test accuracies above 95%, with the most cited published study reporting 99.01%. We audit this benchmark for four trustworthiness failures invisible to the accuracy- and F1-centred reporting that dominates the literature. First, pre-split SMOTE inflates churn-class F1 by 13.1 percentage points across ten classifiers and fifteen seeds (Wilcoxon p < 10^-4 per classifier); the same leaky pipeline ordering paired with class weighting yields no inflation, isolating the effect to SMOTE's geometric construction. We measure the mechanism directly: approximately 36% of synthetic training points are nearest-neighbour interpolations of test-set instances. Second, the TotalCharges field is approximately determined by tenure multiplied by MonthlyCharges (R2 = 0.999); removing it changes accuracy by less than 0.2 percentage points, yet TreeSHAP ranks it ninth in mean absolute attribution - a pattern that materially corrupts SHAP-based interpretation. We propose an R2 > 0.95 pre-modelling diagnostic. Third, in a 15-seed calibration audit, isotonic regression is the strongest default; temperature scaling fails on class-weighted tree ensembles whose predicted-probability distribution is bimodal. Fourth, the cost-optimal decision threshold (under a 50 USD retention offer and 24-month CLV proxy) is approximately 5-10 times lower than the F1-optimal threshold, saving approximately 77,000 USD per 1,000 customers. We replicate F1 and F2 on Iranian Telecom Churn (within domain) and Bank Customer Churn (across domain): F1 generalises; F2 generalises only within telecom. We synthesise these findings into a four-component reporting checklist - pipeline disclosure, redundancy diagnostic, calibration audit, and cost-sensitive thresholds - and release a reproducible implementation.

</details>


### [156] [Generalized Biomedicine Discovery](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00120v1)
*Luyao Tang,Yingkai Yang,Hanqi Chen,Jiewei Zheng,Chaoqi Chen,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Generalized Biomedicine Discovery (GBD) 框架和 SCAN 方法，解决医学影像中的开放世界问题（长尾分布、细微病灶和层级分类）。SCAN 模拟专家诊断的认知过程，通过预测抑制过滤预期模式、惊讶触发显著性检测异常偏差、互补整合重构全局表征。实验表明 SCAN 能提升新概念发现能力同时保留已有临床知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界范式假设标签空间扁平且平衡，无法应对医学影像的三重挑战：长尾稀有疾病、被正常解剖结构掩盖的细微病灶、以及层级疾病分类体系。核心洞察是主流已知模式形成视觉流形，掩盖了细微的新颖性。

Method: 提出 SCAN 方法，模拟专家诊断的认知启发式感知进程：三阶段 pipeline——(1) 预测抑制：过滤预期正常模式；(2) 惊讶触发显著性：突出意外偏差；(3) 互补整合：将这些偏移整合入全局表征。SCAN 可插入现有架构。

Result: 大量实验表明 SCAN 改善了新概念发现性能，同时总体保留已有临床知识。能更好地平衡医学影像中的已知-未知权衡。

Conclusion: SCAN 提供了一种认知启发的解决方案，可有效处理医学影像的开放世界挑战。框架可扩展至现有架构，为生物医学发现提供实用工具。

Abstract: In real-world clinical practice, medical images face open-world shifts: (i) long-tailed rare diseases, (ii) subtle lesions dominated by normal anatomy, and (iii) hierarchical taxonomies. Yet most open-world paradigms assume flat, balanced label spaces, leaving these biomedical demands unresolved. We introduce Generalized Biomedicine Discovery (GBD) and a unified benchmark spanning long-tail, anomaly, and taxonomy-aware discovery. Our key insight is that dominant known patterns form a visual manifold that masks subtle novelty. Inspired by expert diagnosis, we propose SCAN (Surprise-evoked Complementary AccommodatioN), which follows a cognition-inspired perceptual progression: it applies predictive suppression to filter expected norms, triggers surprise-evoked salience to highlight unexpected deviations, and performs complementary accommodation to integrate these shifts into global representations. Extensive experiments show that SCAN improves novel concept discovery while generally preserving established clinical knowledge, and it plugs into existing architectures to better navigate the known-unknown trade-off in medical imaging. Code is available at https://github.com/lytang63/generalized-biomedicine-discovery.

</details>


### [157] [Four Ways to Grow a Classifier and Why One of Them Cannot Learn](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00180v1)
*Cagri Temel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在统一的实验协议下，对四种"建设性分类器"（constructive classifiers）的生长决策进行了诊断性评估，发现四种决策中仅有一种（最自然的软决策树深度增加方法）实际上无法学习，并给出了诊断与修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习和决策树领域广泛使用"边训练边增长"的结构化学习策略，但缺乏对每种增长决策实际效果的定量比较。之前的研究主要关注如何打破对称性或逃离鞍点，而本文首次系统性地诊断每种增长决策的代价与收益。

Method: 作者设计了一个统一的实验协议，在三种数据集（Iris、Wine、Digits）上，使用三个随机种子和五折交叉验证，分别测试四种增长决策：①将叶子扩展为双门的层级、②拟合新隐藏单元到残差后安装、③按最大期望误差分裂叶子、④仅在统计显著时进行更富表达力的分裂。采用精确的数学证明来诊断第一种决策的失效机制。

Result: ①对第一种方法给出了严格的数学证明：新门控的梯度恒为零，且当门控设为1/2时，两个子节点的梯度完全相同，导致新层级无法学习；添加随机扰动可修复此问题，只需一行代码断言新参数梯度非零。实验显示该构造方法在三个数据集上分别损失19.6、19.1和55.6个准确率点。②拟合新隐藏单元到残差：能获得更小的网络但不提高准确率，在Digits上反而显著降低准确率。③按最大期望误差分裂：能获得稀疏模型（Digits上仅用3.7个分裂点达到0.885准确率，而完整六层树需63个节点），但在更难的问题上损失4.3个准确率点。④要求统计显著性：既增大模型又降低准确率，徒劳无益。

Conclusion: 四种增长决策各有意图，但只有一种（深度增加）被证明存在根本性缺陷——梯度信息完全缺失而非薄弱。修复方法极简（随机扰动），实践准则明确：添加参数后应断言其梯度非零。统计显著性检验这一看似合理的约束实际上是有害的。不同决策适用于不同目标（压缩vs准确率），研究者应根据具体需求选择而非盲目使用。

Abstract: Constructive classifiers add structure while they train: a level to a tree, a unit to a hidden layer, a split at a leaf. This paper asks what each of four such growth decisions actually buys, measured under one fixed protocol in tree-structured and constructive models, and gives an exact diagnosis and a fix for the one that buys nothing.
  The diagnosis concerns the most natural way to deepen a soft decision tree: turn every leaf into a gate whose two children inherit the parent's class distribution, so that the function is unchanged. I prove that this leaves the gradient of every new gate identically zero and, with the gate at 1/2, gives the two children identical gradients, so the added level can never learn. Unlike the symmetry that Net2Net breaks with noise or the saddle point that splitting steepest descent escapes with second-order information, first-order information here is not weak but absent. Over three seeds of five-fold cross-validation the construction loses 19.6 accuracy points on Iris, 19.1 on Wine and 55.6 on Digits against the same depth trained from scratch. The fix is a small random perturbation of the children, whose size barely matters. The practical rule is one line in a test: after adding parameters, assert that their gradient is nonzero.
  The other three decisions each buy one thing. Fitting a new hidden unit to the residual error before installing it buys a smaller network on every dataset, though not a more accurate one, and on Digits it costs accuracy significantly. Splitting the leaf with the largest expected error buys sparsity, reaching 0.885 with 3.7 splits where a complete depth-six tree uses 63, but loses 4.3 points on a harder problem. Requiring statistical significance before a node receives a more expressive split buys nothing: the tree gets larger and less accurate.
  Every number in the paper is inserted from the measurement script.

</details>


### [158] [Uncertainty-Aware RL-Controlled Adaptive 3D Mapping](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00188v1)
*Alpay Ozkan,Tunc Ozan Aydin,Marc Pollefeys,Jelena Trisovic,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义熵、几何曲率和纹理丰富度的自适应体素细化框架，用于3D重建中的多分辨率TSDF融合，并通过强化学习智能体在用户指定内存预算下学习体素细分策略，实现了精度与内存的显式可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体素体积映射方法采用固定分辨率网格，在均匀区域浪费内存、在复杂区域丢失细节。现有自适应方法（如MAP-ADAPT）依赖启发式规则和专家调参，缺乏泛化能力，且无法显式控制内存使用。

Method: 1. 利用语义熵捕获标签不确定性，结合几何曲率和纹理丰富度作为场景复杂度线索，指导体素细化决策；2. 引入强化学习智能体学习体素细分策略，在用户指定的内存预算约束下优化，替代手工阈值；3. 构建多分辨率TSDF融合框架。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上，该方法相比MAP-ADAPT和固定分辨率基线实现了更高的几何精度、更好的语义一致性和更优的内存-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架无需依赖语义分类体系，通过语义熵等原则性指标实现自适应的分辨率分配，强化学习模块提供了直观且用户可控的内存-精度权衡调节机制。

Abstract: Voxel-based volumetric mapping is fundamental to 3D reconstruction, yet fixed-resolution grids remain inherently inefficient - wasting memory in uniform regions and losing detail in complex ones. Existing adaptive methods, such as MAP-ADAPT, partially address this by varying resolution based on geometry and user-defined semantic class lists, but these heuristics require expert tuning, lack generalization to unseen objects, and provide no explicit mechanism to control memory usage. We propose an adaptive framework that refines voxels based on semantic entropy, which captures label uncertainty, together with geometric curvature and texture richness as scene complexity cues, yielding principled resolution allocation without reliance on semantic taxonomies. To make the accuracy-memory trade-off explicit and user-controlled, we further introduce a reinforcement learning agent that learns voxel subdivision policies under a user-specified target memory budget, replacing hand-tuned thresholds with a single intuitive control parameter. The resulting multi-resolution TSDF achieves higher geometric accuracy, better semantic consistency, and improved memory-accuracy trade-offs compared to MAP-ADAPT and fixed-resolution baselines on both synthetic and real-world datasets. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/alpayozkan/UnRL.

</details>


### [159] [Energy Time-Series Imputation with Differentially Private Diffusion Models via Clipping-Aware Objective Conditioning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00209v1)
*Huizhen Huang,Yu Li,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）训练扩散模型用于能源时间序列填补时的梯度裁剪问题，提出了一种时间步感知的损失加权方法。通过v-prediction和动态权重衰减策略，在低信噪比的晚时间步减少梯度放大，从而降低裁剪比例并保留更多优化信号。在5个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种缺失场景下均优于ε-prediction基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在隐私敏感的能源时间序列填补中潜力巨大，但DP-SGD训练时存在梯度裁剪导致优化信号损失的问题，尤其在低信噪比的晚时间步更为严重。

Method: 提出时间步感知的损失加权策略：采用v-prediction缓解晚时间步梯度放大，采用静态损失加权作为均匀缩放控制，引入扩散调度感知的动态加权在裁剪前进行更强衰减。

Result: 在5个真实能源时间序列数据集上，针对随机点缺失、连续块缺失、持续停电及多种缺失严重程度，该方法在相同DP-SGD设置下持续优于ε-prediction基线。

Conclusion: 梯度诊断显示上尾预裁剪梯度范数更低、裁剪比例减少、晚时间步衰减更强，验证了裁剪感知目标优化对能源时间序列填补的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable recovery of missing measurements is important for monitoring and analysis in energy time-series systems, where fine-grained measurements may contain sensitive temporal information. Diffusion models trained with differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) provide a promising framework for privacy-sensitive energy time-series imputation. Under cosine diffusion schedules, late timesteps correspond to low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) conditions, where standard $\varepsilon$-prediction can induce large pre-clipping gradients. Such gradients are more likely to be clipped, reducing the retained optimization signal. The artificial intelligence (AI) contribution lies in formulating this objective--clipping interaction as an objective optimization problem under fixed-threshold DP-SGD and developing timestep-aware objective conditioning for diffusion-based energy time-series imputation. The method adopts $v$-prediction to mitigate late-timestep gradient amplification, uses static loss weighting as a uniform-scaling control, and introduces diffusion-schedule-aware dynamic weighting for stronger attenuation before clipping. For the engineering application, we evaluate the method on five real-world energy time-series datasets across random point missingness, contiguous block missingness, persistent outages, and multiple missing-data severities. Under matched DP-SGD settings, the proposed method consistently improves imputation utility over the $\varepsilon$-prediction baseline. Gradient diagnostics reveal lower upper-tail pre-clipping gradient norms, reduced clipping fractions, and stronger attenuation at late low-SNR timesteps, supporting the effectiveness of clipping-aware objective conditioning for energy time-series imputation.

</details>


### [160] [The Null Is the Hard Part: Exact Tests for Memorization in Generative Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00251v1)
*Sushovan Majhi,Pramita Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前生成模型记忆化审计方法存在根本性缺陷——缺乏零分布导致结论可能错误。研究者提出了两种零假设检验方法：整体模型层面使用排列检验，单图像层面使用匹配对照法。在24个生成器的测试中，基于最近邻偏好的统计仍能在7个模型中检测到记忆化，但限制在近重复尺度后则全部为阴性。研究建议使用IECP的小尺度质量作为尺度限制统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型（如Stable Diffusion）的记忆化审计方法存在严重问题：使用相似度阈值进行判断但缺乏零分布，导致结论可能错误。MemBench基准测试的缓解措施效果被高估——当使用伪发现率控制进行审计时，大部分"认证图像"在随机扰动、注意力重缩放或嵌入优化下无法被检测到。此外，领域内的数据复制测试在零假设下会错误地将10/24个未复制任何内容的生成器标记为阳性。

Method: 研究提出了两种零假设检验方法：（1）整体模型层面：在模型样本给定下，训练图像和留出图像是可交换的，通过重标记进行排列检验，当留出图像为随机分割时对任何统计量都精确；（2）单图像层面：使用匹配对照进行校准，将审计结果与随机图像的基准线对比。

Result: 研究发现：①使用MemBench规则，缓解措施将Stable Diffusion的记忆化"减半"，但使用伪发现率控制后，2/3的认证图像在随机提示扰动下无法检测到，5/6在注意力重缩放下无法检测到，全部在嵌入优化下无法检测到；②数据复制测试在自身零假设下错误标记了10/24个生成器；③最近邻偏好统计在排列检验下仍能检测到7/24个生成器，但限制在近重复尺度后的计数则全部为阴性；④使用匹配对照校准，61个MemBench图像中36个在5%伪发现率下被认证，留出对照在0.01%的校准分割中被认证。

Conclusion: 研究证明记忆化审计中零假设才是关键难题，并提供了两种有效的解决方案。推荐使用IECP（交叉欧拉特征分布）的小尺度质量作为尺度限制统计量，该方法可计数复制的不同图像并检验两个模型是否复制相同的图像。

Abstract: Memorization audits of generative models read similarity scores against thresholds, with no null distribution, and the conclusions they support can be wrong. By MemBench's rule, the benchmark's mitigations roughly halve Stable Diffusion's memorization; audited with false-discovery control, two thirds of the certified images are no longer detected under random prompt perturbations, five sixths under attention rescaling, and all of them under embedding optimization. The field's data-copying test, read against its own null, flags ten of twenty-four generators that reproduce nothing. We argue that for memorization the null is the hard part, and supply two. For a whole model, training and held-out images are exchangeable given its samples, and relabelling them is a permutation test, exact for any statistic when the held-out images are a random split; under it, a nearest-neighbour preference still fires on seven of those twenty-four, and a count restricted to the near-duplicate scale on none (McNemar p=0.016). For single images, the natural nulls fail twice, measurably: ranking an image among random images yields 596 false discoveries among 2,365 controls, and resampling independent generations makes the null three times too narrow. Calibrated against matched controls, the audit certifies 36 of 61 MemBench images at 5% false-discovery rate, held-out controls are certified in 0.01% of calibration splits, and on this benchmark two generations per image recover that count. A calibrated maximum, which reads occasional rather than typical copying, certifies 46. As the scale-restricted statistic we recommend the small-scale mass of the Intersection Euler Characteristic Profile, which also counts distinct images copied and tests whether two models copy the same ones.

</details>


### [161] [Partial AUC Maximization from Positive-unlabeled Data](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00284v1)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Taishi Nishiyama,Hiroshi Takahashi,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用正样本和未标注数据（PU数据）来最大化部分AUC（pAUC）的方法，无需负样本数据。在经验风险最小化框架下，证明了pAUC可以仅用正样本密度和边际密度表示，并据此推导出经验估计器。实验在10个真实数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中（如网络安全、医疗、广告等），获取标注的负样本数据通常困难且昂贵（涉及隐私问题或需要专家标注），但现有的最大化pAUC方法都依赖于同时拥有正负标注数据。这促使研究者探索仅利用正样本和未标注数据来训练分类器的方案。

Method: 在经验风险最小化（ERM）框架下，理论证明了pAUC（包括与FPR相关的阈值）可以仅用正样本密度p(x|y=1)和边际密度p(x)表示，而不直接依赖负样本密度。基于此推导，从PU数据构建了平滑的经验pAUC估计器，然后通过最大化该估计器来训练分类器。

Result: 在10个真实数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法能够有效利用PU数据学习到高pAUC的分类器，在无需负样本的情况下取得了良好的分类性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法首次实现了从PU数据中学习最大化pAUC的分类器，填补了该领域的研究空白，为标签稀缺场景下的高性能分类提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The partial area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (pAUC) is an important performance metric for binary classification that summarizes true positive rates within a specific range of false positive rates (FPRs). Classifiers that achieve high pAUC need to be obtained in many real-world applications such as cybersecurity, medical care, and advertising. Although many methods for maximizing the pAUC have been proposed, they typically require both labeled positive and negative data for training. However, in practice, labeled negative data are often difficult to collect due to privacy concerns or the need for high expertise to annotate them. In this paper, we propose a method for maximizing the pAUC from positive and unlabeled (PU) data without negative data. Within an empirical risk minimization framework, we show that the pAUC, including its FPR-dependent thresholds, can be represented using only the positive and marginal densities, and derive an empirical estimator from PU data. A classifier is then trained by maximizing the derived smoothed empirical pAUC estimator. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with ten real-world datasets.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Diagonal State Space Models: Exact Non-Abelian Group Tracking, Solvability Barriers, and Geometric Physical Manifolds](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00329v1)
*Zeyu Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非交换状态空间模型（NC-SSM），将传统SSM的状态转移提升至紧致李群（SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)），解决了传统模型在非阿贝尔群（如A_5）上的指数级电路模拟深度问题。通过闭式Euler-Rodrigues映射、有理Cayley变换和严格等距正交特性，实现了精确范数保持。在14个实验域中显著优于对角基线，尤其在非交换群（S_3、A_5等）、Dyck-2和AST任务上取得突破性进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选择性SSM（如Mamba、S4D、LRU）受限于转移矩阵可交换性（A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t）和可解仿射变换群（Aff_D），导致堆叠多层对角网络在非可解简单群（如A_5）上优化严重退化，需要指数级电路模拟深度来模拟非阿贝尔交换子。这构成了SSM表达能力的关键理论瓶颈。

Method: (1) 将状态转移提升至紧致李群SU(2)、SO(3)、SO(N)，利用群的非交换性增强表达能力；(2) 通过闭式Euler-Rodrigues映射实现精确等距（||U_t|| = 1）；(3) 提出纯Hopf-fibration Bloch投影读出消除符号歧义；(4) 设计真四元数并行前缀扫描（T=2048时9.06x加速）；(5) 引入恒等门控Lie SSM消除稀疏语法相位漂移。

Result: (1) NC-SSM在S_3、D_4、Q_8和A_5上达到100%跟踪，对角基线在A_5上崩溃至6.60%（p=8.81e-4）；(2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM打破Klein 1884年上限，在S_5上达50.92% vs 对角5.25%（p=0.0015，方差降低7.8x）；(3) SO(3)-SSM保持黎曼流形（300步漂移<3.12e-6，优于58万倍），实现0.04度航位推算误差；(4) NC-SSM在Dyck-2达74.36%，深度AST scope跟踪达30.26%（p=0.0081）；(5) 消融证实严格等距是无损长程联想记忆的数学必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文通过李群代数结构从根本上突破了传统SSM的可交换性瓶颈，证明了：(1) 紧致李群表示对非阿贝尔群结构建模的必要性；(2) 严格等距正交性是长程记忆保持的充要条件；(3) 所提方法在符号推理、形式语言和姿态估计等任务上具有通用有效性，为SSM的群论统一框架奠定了基础。

Abstract: Selective state space models (SSMs), such as Mamba, S4D, and LRU, are bounded by transition matrix commutativity (A_t A_t' = A_t' A_t) and solvable affine transformation groups (Aff_D of derived length <= 2). Consequently, stacked multi-layer diagonal networks face severe optimization degradation on non-solvable simple groups such as A_5 due to the exponential circuit emulation depth required to simulate non-abelian commutators. We propose Non-Commutative State Space Models (NC-SSM), their real-orthogonal counterpart SO(3)-SSM, and arbitrary-dimension Cayley-SSM, lifting state transitions to compact Lie groups SU(2), SO(3), and SO(N). Via closed-form Euler-Rodrigues maps and rational Cayley transforms, NC-SSM achieves exact norm-preserving isometry (||U_t|| = 1). We introduce pure Hopf-fibration Bloch projective readouts (S^3/{+-1} =~ S^2 =~ SO(3)) to eliminate sign ambiguity, true quaternion parallel prefix scans (9.06x speedup at T=2048), and Identity-Gated Lie SSMs to eliminate sparse syntax phase drift. Extensive benchmarks across 14 experimental regimes show: (1) NC-SSM achieves 100% tracking on S_3, D_4, Q_8 and simple group A_5, where a 3-layer deep diagonal baseline collapses to 6.60% (p = 8.81e-4); (2) Cayley-SO(5)-SSM breaks Klein's 1884 ceiling on symmetric group S_5 (50.92% vs diagonal 5.25%, p = 0.0015, delivering 7.8x variance reduction over SO(3)); (3) SO(3)-SSM preserves Riemannian manifolds across 300 steps (< 3.12e-6 drift, > 580,000x advantage), achieving 0.04 deg dead-reckoning error and active tangent denoising; (4) NC-SSM achieves 74.36% on Dyck-2 and 30.26% on deep AST scope tracking (p = 0.0081); and (5) ablation confirms strict isometry is mathematically necessary for lossless long-range associative memory.

</details>


### [163] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367v1)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoRA框架，通过可学习的路由器偏置和专家近似机制，对MoE模型进行结构化剪枝，有效减少内存占用的同时保持模型性能，在多个模型和基准测试上优于现有剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏激活实现了有限的per-token计算量，但仍需将完整专家池加载到内存中。现有的结构化剪枝方法存在两个问题：1）专家排名标准与模型性能对齐不佳；2）搜索有效专家子集计算成本高。此外，现有方法忽略了保留专家之间的路由行为冗余。

Method: MoRA框架包含两个核心组件：1）路由器偏置学习：为每个专家引入可学习的偏置项，通过最小化语言建模损失和路由多样性正则化器来优化这些偏置，使路由概率分布更尖锐，从而识别对模型性能关键重要的专家，同时鼓励选择具有不同路由偏好的专家；2）专家近似机制：作为剪枝后增强手段，利用保留专家通过仿射变换近似被剪枝专家的输出。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite和Moonlight-16B-A3B三个模型上评估，移除每个MoE层25%和50%的路由专家，在9个零样本基准测试上的实验表明MoRA优于现有最先进的剪枝算法。

Conclusion: MoRA通过路由器偏置学习和专家近似机制的结合，提供了一种有效的MoE模型结构化剪枝方案，在大幅减少内存占用的同时能够保持甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer. The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [164] [M$^2$Weather: A Benchmark for Joint Multi-Station and Multi-Variable Weather Forecasting](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00370v1)
*Rongwen Li,Xiao Wang,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M²Weather是一个联合多站点、多变量天气预测基准测试，通过收集2,809个高质量站点、5个物理耦合天气变量，覆盖法国、欧洲和全球三个空间尺度，并设计统一训练评估协议和轻量级即插即用适配器，系统评估了16个代表性模型，证明了联合建模站点和变量关系能显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天气预测研究将站点空间依赖性和变量物理耦合分开考虑，且使用不同数据集和实验设置，难以系统评估两者的个体和联合贡献。

Method: 构建多尺度基准测试数据集(法国/欧洲/全球)，制定统一训练评估协议，设计轻量级即插即用适配器以无需重训练方式引入缺失的站点或变量关系。

Result: 16个代表性模型的系统评估表明，联合建模站点和变量关系具有优势；在三个数据集上，为所有适配模型补全缺失关系均能降低MSE。

Conclusion: 站点间和变量间的互补信息是提升站点天气预测的重要资源，联合建模策略具有显著优势。

Abstract: Station weather forecasting is fundamentally shaped by both complex spatial dependencies across stations and strong physical coupling among weather variables. However, existing studies often consider these relationships separately and use different datasets and experimental settings, hindering systematic assessment of their individual and joint contributions. In this paper, we introduce $M^2$Weather, a benchmark for joint multi-station and multi-variable weather forecasting. Through multi-criteria quality control and station stratification, we collect 2,809 high-quality stations with 5 physically coupled weather variables across three spatial scales: France, Europe, and Global. This multi-scale design lets us examine whether conclusions persist from national to global station networks. We also introduce unified training and evaluation protocols to enable fair comparison of different station-variable modeling paradigms. To further examine the benefits of modeling station-variable relationships, we design a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter. With a trained weather forecasting model, this adapter can introduce missing station or variable relationships without retraining the model. This enables fair and efficient investigation of station-variable relationships. Systematic evaluation of 16 representative models shows the benefits of jointly modeling station and variable relationships. Completing missing relationships further reduces MSE for all adapted models on all three datasets. Together, these results identify the complementary information across stations and variables as an important resource for improving station weather forecasting. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/M2-Weather.

</details>


### [165] [A First Glance at Jev for Network Traffic Classification: Accuracy, Processing Time, and Cost](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00376v1)
*Shenghe Xu,Lifan Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次实证研究了通用决策模型Jev在网络流应用分类中的应用。实验表明，少量标注样本（40个）可显著提升Jev准确率（9.80%→28.42%），但仍低于训练好的树集成模型Random Forest（69.95%）和Extra Trees（66.80%）。在与GPT-5.6 Sol的对比中，Jev以更短响应时间（0.750s vs 6.036s）和更低成本取得相近准确率（29% vs 37%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流应用分类是网络管理的重要任务，但现有方法依赖专用模型。本文首次对通用决策模型（Jev）在应用分类任务上的表现进行实证研究，旨在探索大语言模型在网络分类领域的潜力。

Method: 在CESNET-QUICEXT-25数据集上，使用前10个数据包的尺寸、方向和包间隔时间作为特征，在52,000条记录（来自训练期后26周的采集数据）上进行评估。对比了Jev（不同上下文大小：40和150个标注样本）、Random Forest（8,000条训练记录）、Extra Trees（8,000条训练记录）以及GPT-5.6 Sol（通过Azure API调用，高推理成本）。

Result: 1) 40个标注样本使Jev准确率从9.80%提升至28.42%；2) Random Forest（69.95%）和Extra Trees（66.80%）在每周测试中均优于Jev；3) Jev在150个上下文示例下达到34.50%准确率；4) 在100条记录的配对测试中，Jev（40示例）准确率29%、中位响应时间0.750s；GPT-5.6 Sol准确率37%、响应时间6.036s；Jev成本更低。

Conclusion: 标注样本能显著提升Jev性能，但当前配置的Jev准确率仍低于训练好的树集成模型。与GPT-5.6 Sol相比，Jev响应更快、成本更低，但准确率无显著差异。由于监督预算和配置不同，无法将性能差距归因于单一原因。

Abstract: We evaluate Jev on ten dataset-defined application labels in CESNET-QUICEXT-25 using only the first ten packets' sizes, directions, and inter-packet times. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first empirical study of general-purpose decision models, represented here by Jev, for application classification of network flows. Across 52,000 records from 26 collection weeks following the training period, 40 fixed labeled examples raise Jev's accuracy from 9.80% to 28.42%. Random Forest and Extra Trees trained on 8,000 records achieve 69.95% and 66.80% and outperform Jev in every week. Increasing Jev's context to 150 examples yields 34.50% on the first test week. On a paired 100-record subset, Jev with 40 examples achieves 29% accuracy at a median request time of 0.750 s, versus 37% and 6.036 s for the generative language model OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol with high reasoning effort through Azure; Jev also incurs lower API charges. The paired subset does not establish an accuracy advantage for either service, and the timing reflects different service configurations. Thus, labeled examples substantially improve Jev, but the tested Jev configurations remain less accurate than trained tree ensembles; unequal supervision budgets and fixed configurations prevent attributing the gap to a single cause.

</details>


### [166] [STCFormer: Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Modeling with Dynamic Cluster Transformer for Station-based Weather Forecasting](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00377v1)
*Rongwen Li,Haixin Xie,Mingyang Wang,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Changjian Chen,Zhuo Tang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STCFormer提出了一种自适应时空Transformer，通过在每个时间窗口内根据站点局部演化动态分组，实现局部-全局注意力协同，在8个温度和风速预测任务上均达到最低24小时MSE，且在48项比较中47项排名前二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法在建模站点间空间依赖时存在局限：跨观测窗口的固定分组无法捕捉局部关系变化，聚类内交互可能遗漏全局上下文。此外，选择性交互相比全连接的理论优势尚不清晰。

Method: STCFormer核心包括：(1)局部自适应分组机制，根据站点在时间patch内的局部演化动态聚类；(2)聚类引导注意力块，结合簇内细粒度局部注意力与区域状态摘要的全局注意力，使站点能获取跨簇信息。理论证明：聚类条件局部注意力的Lipschitz上界不大于全连接注意力。

Result: 在3个真实气象数据集、8项温度和风速预测任务中，STCFormer在所有任务上实现最低24小时MSE，48项跨指标和预测时段的比较中47项排名第一或第二。消融实验和案例研究验证了局部自适应分组和互补局部-全局交互的优势。

Conclusion: 动态局部分组与局部-全局注意力协同机制能有效建模时空依赖，理论分析为选择性交互的鲁棒性优势提供了数学解释。

Abstract: Station-based weather forecasting supports daily life and economic activity, yet accurate forecasts require modeling complex spatial dependencies among stations. Recent clustering-based selective modeling offers a promising alternative to dense inter-station interactions. However, a grouping shared across an observation window may obscure local changes in station relationships, while intra-cluster interactions alone may miss important global context. The theoretical advantages of selective interactions over dense connectivity also remain insufficiently understood. We therefore propose STCFormer, an adaptive spatio-temporal Transformer that dynamically groups stations according to their local evolution within each temporal patch. Its Cluster-Guided Attention Block combines fine-grained local attention within clusters and global attention over regional state summaries, allowing each station to access information beyond its own cluster. We further show that a derived Lipschitz upper bound for cluster-conditioned local attention is no larger than its fully connected counterpart, explaining a potential robustness benefit and motivating the design of InfoLoss. Experiments on three real-world weather datasets spanning eight temperature and wind forecasting tasks show that STCFormer achieves the lowest 24-hour mean squared error on all eight tasks and ranks first or second in 47 of 48 comparisons across metrics and forecasting horizons. Ablations and case studies further confirm the benefits of locally adaptive grouping and complementary local-global interactions. Our code can be obtained at https://github.com/hnu-vis/STCFormer.

</details>


### [167] [Fusion techniques of time frequency-based images to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00380v1)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Mohammed Aledhari,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出使用融合技术（montage和blending）从脑电图（EEG）派生的时频图像中提取特征，并结合轻量级CNN模型预测重复经颅磁刺激（rTMS）治疗抑郁症的响应。研究发现，当使用分段级10折交叉验证时，准确率可达99.90%，但在严格的受试者级别交叉验证下，性能大幅下降，表明模型存在过拟合风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症是一种可导致自杀和自残的精神疾病，预测rTMS治疗结果对临床医生极具挑战性。现有方法难以处理高受试者间差异性和单域分析的有限特征。

Method: 提出两种融合技术（montage和blending）从EEG派生时频图像中提取更丰富的特征，采用轻量级自定义CNN进行训练。在15名患者的主要数据集和46名患者的次要数据集上进行了分段级10折交叉验证和严格的受试者级别交叉验证两类实验。

Result: 分段级交叉验证中，Montage CWT_ST融合在主数据集上达到99.90%准确率，次数据集达91.90%。但严格的受试者级别交叉验证中，除一个例外外，所有配置的AUC均在0.31-0.54之间，所有95%置信区间均包含0.5。最佳受试者级别结果为主数据集Montage CWT_ST，AUC 0.874±0.183，准确率82.7%。

Conclusion: 基于EEG时频图像的rTMS响应预测模型在分段级评估中表现优异，但严格的受试者级别验证揭示了显著的过拟合问题，表明现有方法难以泛化到新患者，临床应用中需谨慎。

Abstract: Depression is a mental condition that can lead to suicide and self-harm. Predicting the outcome of depression treatment is one of the most difficult tasks for clinicians. Among various treatment options, repetitive Transcranial Magnetic Stimulation (rTMS) is a widely used non-invasive method. Predicting rTMS response using Electroencephalogram (EEG) data is difficult because of high inter-subject variability and limited features from single-domain analysis. We introduce two fusion techniques, montage and blending, to overcome these limitations and extract richer features from EEG-derived Time-Frequency (TF) images. We then propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) trained on fused TF representations. \textcolor{black}{We use a primary dataset of 15 patients and a secondary dataset of 46 patients. We run two sets of experiments. The first set uses segment-level 10-fold cross-validation. In this setup segments from the same patient can appear in both training and testing. The Montage CWT\_ST fusion reaches 99.90\% accuracy on the primary dataset and 91.90\% on the secondary dataset. The second set uses strict subject-disjoint cross-validation. All segments of a patient stay in one fold and no patient appears in both training and testing. Performance collapses. We test four time-frequency methods, six fusion mechanisms, and fourteen model architectures. With one exception, every configuration on both cohorts falls between AUC 0.31 and 0.54 and every 95\% confidence interval contains 0.5. A patient-level permutation test on the best standalone method returns $p = 0.703$. The best subject-level result is Montage CWT\_ST on the primary cohort, which reaches AUC $0.874 \pm 0.183$ and 82.7\% accuracy.

</details>


### [168] [MatrixReward: Reward from Rubric Matrix for Open-Ended Generation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00389v1)
*Zihan Shen,Qi Liu,Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Chenglong Zhao,Xiaozhao Wang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MatrixReward是一种针对开放性问答生成任务的强化学习奖励机制，通过构建rollout-by-rubric赢率矩阵，利用列展开度和列相关性计算数据依赖的rubric权重，结合正负理想轮廓计算相对接近度奖励，在Qwen3-8B上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放性问答生成缺乏标准答案，现有逐点评分准则提供的信息有限，合并多个准则判断可能掩盖答案间差异。

Method: 构建rollout-by-rubric赢率矩阵，对每对采样响应进行逐准则比较；利用列展开度度量准则区分强度，列相关性揭示准则重复程度；结合先验权重计算数据依赖权重；归一化后以正负理想轮廓定义奖励边界，计算相对接近度作为质量奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上，四个开放性问答基准测试平均得分63.02，较最强基线提升约2.0%。

Conclusion: 基于相对比较构建的矩阵可用于更合理地为开放性生成强化学习构建奖励机制。

Abstract: Open-ended query generation lacks standard answers, thus necessitating an effective reward mechanism. Pointwise scoring rubrics provide limited information about the relative quality of sample answers under the same prompt; merging multiple rubric judgments into a single score may also mask the differences between these answers. We propose MatrixReward, which constructs rewards from a rollout-by-rubric win-rate matrix obtained by comparing every pair of sampled responses under each rubric. The spread of each matrix column captures how strongly that rubric distinguishes the current rollouts, while correlations between columns reveal rubric repetition; together, these statistics yield data-dependent rubric weights. We combine these weights with the prior weights of rubrics. After column normalization and weighting, the observed per-rubric maxima and minima define positive and negative ideal profiles. Each rollout's distances to these two ideals determine its relative-closeness quality reward. Evaluated using Qwen3-8B on four open-ended query-answering benchmarks, MatrixReward achieves an average score of 63.02, outperforming the strongest baseline by approximately 2.0%. These results support the idea that matrices derived from relative comparisons can be used to construct rewards more reasonably for open-ended generative reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [169] [Interpretable Synthetic Medical Tabular Data Generation for Clinical Decision Support Using Fuzzy Cognitive Maps](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00391v1)
*Michael Vasilakakis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用模糊认知图(FCMs)生成合成医学表格数据，在保持因果可解释性的同时保护隐私，在心脏病数据集上达到0.81准确率和0.90 AUROC，优于TVAE等深度生成模型，且仅需CPU运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率模型和深度生成模型在合成医学数据时缺乏可解释性，且无法保留临床上有意义的特征依赖关系，限制了其在安全关键医疗应用中的适用性。医学领域需要既能保护隐私、又具有因果可解释性的合成数据生成方法。

Method: 采用模糊认知图(FCM)框架：(1)使用语言可解释的模糊集描述临床特征；(2)通过模糊集交集计算FCM边权重编码特征间依赖关系；(3)通过在FCM中传播随机初始化的语言激活向量直至收敛生成合成患者记录；(4)解模糊化产生临床一致的数值。支持混合数据类型和健康记录的领域约束。

Result: 在UCI医学基准数据集上，采用"TSTR"(合成数据训练-真实数据测试)协议评估。心脏病数据集达到准确率0.81、AUROC 0.90，性能匹配或超过TVAE和高斯Copula基线，仅需CPU运行。保真度指标：KS补集达0.91、相关性相似度达0.95，隐私保护DCR评分持续优于TVAE。

Conclusion: 因果驱动、可解释的模糊建模为CBMSs中的可信合成数据生成提供了一种计算高效、透明且隐私保护良好的深度生成模型替代方案。

Abstract: Synthetic medical tabular data generation has become essential for developing and validating computer-based medical systems (CBMSs) when real clinical data is restricted due to privacy, ethical, or data availability limitations. Existing probabilistic and deep generative models often lack interpretability and fail to preserve clinically meaningful dependencies, limiting their suitability for safety-critical applications. This paper proposes a novel application of Fuzzy Cognitive Maps (FCMs) in a framework for synthetic medical tabular data generation with explicit causality and privacy preservation. Clinical features are described using linguistically interpretable fuzzy sets, and inter-feature dependencies are encoded as FCM edge weights computed from fuzzy set intersections. Synthetic patient records are generated by propagating randomly initialized linguistic activation vectors through the FCM until convergence, followed by defuzzification to produce clinically coherent numerical values. The approach natively handles mixed data types, and domain constraints common in health records. Experimental evaluation on UCI medical benchmark datasets demonstrates competitive performance under a Train-on-Synthetic-Test-on-Real (TSTR) protocol. The proposed method achieves accuracy of up to 0.81 and AUROC of up to 0.90 on the Heart Disease dataset, matching or exceeding TVAE and Gaussian Copula baselines while running exclusively on CPU. Fidelity metrics including KS Complement (up to 0.91) and Correlation Similarity (up to 0.95) confirm strong statistical coherence, and DCR Baseline Protection scores consistently exceed those of TVAE, confirming adequate privacy guarantees. These results demonstrate that causally grounded, interpretable fuzzy modeling offers a computationally efficient and transparent alternative to deep generative models for trustworthy synthetic data generation in CBMSs.

</details>


### [170] [NEUROTOKEN: Joint Source and Directional AAD with Envelope Decoding via Conditional Flow Matching](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00397v1)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NEUROTOKEN是一个统一的神经网络框架，通过条件流匹配方法(ATTUNEFLOW)实现听觉注意解码(AAD)，在5秒窗口下将源AAD性能提升9%-16%，并通过QUADTRACK和ENV-FLOW集成方案克服了传统方法的局限性。在严格trial-disjoint协议下，传统95-97%的方向-AAD性能下降17%-45%，表明需要基于似然的 formulation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决"鸡尾酒会问题"——在嘈杂环境中识别听者正在关注哪个说话者，这对下一代助听器和脑机接口至关重要。现有文献存在三个相互割裂的方向：方向-AAD只能分类左右侧但无法映射到具体语音流；基于回归的源-AAD使用皮尔逊相关排名候选流在1-5秒窗口下信噪比过低；包络重建方法缺乏原生AAD判断规则。

Method: 提出条件似然作为正确目标而非单一统计量。NEUROTOKEN包含三个共享EEG前端头的网络：ATTUNEFLOW头通过积分速度残差似然比(score candidates)评分候选流；推理时集成方案QUADTRACK(四种互补统计量)和ENV-FLOW(z标准化QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW)吸收各统计量的失效模式。

Result: 在KU Leuven、DTU、NJU三个数据集5秒窗口下，ATTUNEFLOW比最强非生成基线提升9%-16%，跨被试方差缩小约3倍；trial级别融合在三个数据集中两个超过93%。严格trial-disjoint协议下，经典95-97%方向-AAD性能下降17%-45%。

Conclusion: 传统方向-AAD的高性能存在过拟合风险；条件似然框架是实现鲁棒AAD的正确范式；集成多统计量方案是吸收失效模式的免费午餐。

Abstract: Identifying which speaker a listener is attending to in a noisy room -- the cocktail-party problem -- is the missing ingredient for next-generation hearing aids and brain-computer interfaces: it tells the device whose voice to amplify. Auditory attention decoding (AAD) reads this answer from EEG, but the literature splits into disconnected pieces: directional-AAD classifies side but does not map side to stream; regression-based source-AAD ranks candidate streams by a single Pearson correlation that is intrinsically noisy at the 1-5 s windows real devices need; and envelope reconstruction has no native AAD rule. We argue the right object is not any single statistic but the conditional likelihood of the attended envelope given EEG, and we make this practical with NEUROTOKEN: a single network whose three heads share one EEG front-end, with a conditional flow-matching head (ATTUNEFLOW) that scores candidates by an integrated velocity-residual likelihood ratio. Two inference-time ensembles -- QUADTRACK (four complementary statistics) and ENV-FLOW (z-normalised QUADTRACK+ATTUNEFLOW) -- absorb per-statistic failure modes for free. On KU Leuven, DTU, and NJU at 5 s, ATTUNEFLOW lifts per-segment source-AAD by 9%-16% over the strongest non-generative baseline and shrinks across-subject variance by ~3x; trial-level fusion exceeds 93% on two of three datasets. In parallel reproductions we show that canonical 95-97% direction-AAD numbers collapse by 17%-45% under a strict trial-disjoint protocol, clarifying both the true ceiling and why a likelihood-based formulation is needed.

</details>


### [171] [Representation Transitions Reveal Emerging Safety Risks in Multi-Turn LLM Agents](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00400v1)
*Haoyu Wang,Wei Zhao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART通过分析智能体内部表征的累积变化来检测多轮攻击，无需辅助模型，运行时开销低（0.14-0.56秒/步），在6个模型和2个基准上将攻击成功率从84%/97%大幅降低至25%/52%，优于现有SOTA防御ToolShield。去噪处理是关键，原始监控器仅能捕获7%-40%的攻击，去噪后可提升至60%-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮攻击将单独无害的动作组合成有害结果，现有防御仅在单步动作或状态层面评估，难以应对此类复合威胁。需要一种能检测跨轮累积效应、且计算成本低廉的防御方法。

Method: 1) 发现有害行为在内部表征中留下可检测的累积轨迹；2) 对安全方向进行去噪处理——将良性流量锚定为零并移除其主导变异方向；3) 提出DART框架：检测与归因表征偏移，并结合定向提醒进行干预。

Result: MT-AgentRisk上攻击成功率从84%降至25%（误报率均值12%），ASEval从97%降至52%（良性拒绝率0%）；全面超越ToolShield（MT-AgentRisk上仅达55%）；去噪至关重要（ASeval上未去噪仅7%-40%，去噪后达60%-85%）；单轮间接注入同样可检测；运行时开销仅0.14-0.56秒/步。

Conclusion: 内部表征的累积变化是检测多轮攻击的有效信号，去噪处理对分离有害信号与良性漂移至关重要。DART以极低计算开销实现了高效防御，为计算密集型 speculative 防御提供了轻量级补充方案。

Abstract: Multi-turn attacks on agentic systems can compose individually permissible actions into harmful outcomes, challenging defenses that assess actions or states in isolation. We show that such attacks leave a detectable signature in the agent's internal representations: harmful behavior emerges as an accumulated representation transition across context updates, whose triggering context can be identified from the same signal. We further find that naive aggregation is confounded by benign representation drift, as a contrastive safety direction need not assign zero to benign transitions. We address this by denoising the direction, anchoring benign traffic at zero and removing its leading variation directions, with no runtime cost.
  These findings motivate DART, a runtime framework that detects and attributes representation shifts and intervenes with targeted reminders. Across six models and two multi-turn benchmarks, DART reduces attack success from 84% to 25% on MT-AgentRisk, catching every attack at a mean false-alarm rate of 12%, and from 97% to 52% on ASEval, at costs in benign non-refusal of 8% and 0%, respectively. On MT-AgentRisk, it outperforms ToolShield, the state-of-the-art multi-turn defense, on all six models: under the same protocol, ToolShield reaches only 55%. Denoising is critical: on ASEval, the undenoised monitor catches only 7%-40% of attacks, while the denoised monitor catches 60%-85%. The same monitor covers single-turn indirect injection without modification and adds only 0.14-0.56 s overhead per monitored step without requiring an auxiliary model, making it a lightweight complement to computation-heavy speculative defenses.

</details>


### [172] [The Conflict Between Logic and Memory: Learning Higher-Order Interactions in Shallow MLPs](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00403v1)
*Gongyue Zhang,Honghai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究单隐藏层MLP在合成任务中拟合训练样本但无法恢复生成规则的现象，通过控制交互阶数和干扰输入，发现不同优化器（SGD、Adam、Muon）在此类任务上表现差异显著，干扰权重的学习会改变训练结果，且目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示之间存在关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络即使完美拟合训练数据，也可能无法学到生成标签的真实规则。这种"记忆 vs 泛化"的分离现象在单隐藏层MLP中尚未被系统研究，特别是交互阶数和干扰输入对这一现象的影响尚不清晰。

Method: 1) 使用合成任务控制交互阶数和干扰输入；2) 建立基准性质：纯奇偶校验任务无预测性低阶边缘分布、可得精确贝叶斯后验、可用宽度k的ReLU网络精确表示；3) 对比SGD、Adam、Muon三种优化器在不同阶数任务上的表现；4) 冻结干扰输入相关权重进行消融实验。

Result: 1) 2阶任务三种优化器均达100%峰值准确率；3阶时SGD 96.25%、Adam 50.87%、Muon 76.82%；4阶时Muon 99.21%；2) 冻结干扰输入的一阶权重，AdamW准确率从44.73%提升至95.07%；仅在测试时移除干扰输入则提升至48.38%；3) SGD在高阶(9-11阶)任务表现更优。

Conclusion: 优化器选择和干扰学习会约束浅层网络实现的高阶规则；干扰权重的学习对训练结果的影响超出其对预测的直接影响；目标对称性与浅层ReLU表示之间存在联系。

Abstract: A network can fit its training examples while failing to recover the rule that generated their labels. We examine this separation in single-hidden-layer multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), using synthetic tasks that control interaction order and the presence of nuisance inputs. We establish elementary benchmark properties: pure parity contains no predictive lower-order marginals, admits an exact Bayes posterior, and can be represented on clean latent inputs by a width-$k$ ReLU network. Experiments then identify distinct optimization outcomes. In a matched order-2--4 sweep, SGD, Adam, and Muon all reach 100\% peak test accuracy at order two; at order three they reach 96.25\%, 50.87\%, and 76.82\%, respectively, while Muon reaches 99.21\% at order four. In a separate mixed-order task, freezing only the first-layer weights connected to independent nuisance inputs raises AdamW's epoch-10 accuracy from 44.73\% to 95.07\%. Removing the same inputs only at test time raises it to 48.38\%. Thus, nuisance-weight learning changes the training outcome beyond its immediate effect on prediction. Bias interventions expose a connection between target symmetry and shallow ReLU representations. In a compact signal-only regime, both SGD and Muon learn orders five through eight, with higher SGD peak accuracy at orders nine through eleven. Together, the results show how optimization and nuisance learning constrain the higher-order rules realized by a shallow network.

</details>


### [173] [Do Better Scores Mean Better Physics? Physics-Grounded Explanations for Sim2Real Neural Operators](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00415v1)
*Somyajit Chakraborty,Xizhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 机器学习代理模型可加速物理模拟，但更低的预测误差不一定对应更低的物理相关流动统计误差。针对NACA4418翼型流动，通过配对的CFD模拟和PIV实验测量，发现不同神经算子的误差指标之间存在不一致性：某些模型在速度场误差上表现更好，但在涨落能量误差上表现更差。这提示我们需要在聚合预测分数之外报告互补的物理诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型能加速物理模拟，但预测误差的降低并不必然意味着物理相关流动统计误差的降低。这是一个关键问题：如何在评估代理模型时确保物理一致性。

Method: 使用配对的CFD模拟和实验PIV测量；以NACA4418翼型流动为研究对象；采用均值保持的输入干预方法从选定区域移除速度涨落；测试四种神经算子；进行输出衰减压力测试。

Result: 从能量最高10%的有效观测单元移除涨落比等面积随机移除对预测的影响更大；CNO在速度场误差上低于参考模型，但在双分量涨落能量误差上显著高于参考模型；输出衰减测试显示基准误差与区域积分涨落能量之间存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 单次基准测试结果支持在聚合预测分数之外报告互补的物理诊断指标；研究结果未确立反事实的物理正确性，仅表明不同误差度量之间可能存在权衡关系。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates accelerate physical simulation, but lower prediction error need not coincide with lower error in physically relevant flow statistics. We examine this question for flow around a NACA4418 airfoil using paired computational-fluid-dynamics simulations and experimental particle-image-velocimetry measurements. A mean-preserving input intervention removes velocity fluctuations from selected regions of observed flow histories. Across four neural operators, removing fluctuations from the most energetic 10% of valid observed cells changes forecasts more than equal-area random removal. Because the masks are not matched for removed fluctuation energy, this contrast measures sensitivity, not independent evidence of physical importance. Separately, a CNO has lower velocity-field error but substantially higher two-component fluctuation-energy error than the reference on both analysis subsets. An output attenuation stress test also demonstrates disagreement between benchmark errors and domain-summed fluctuation energy. These single-benchmark results motivate reporting complementary physical diagnostics alongside aggregate prediction scores; they do not establish counterfactual physical correctness.

</details>


### [174] [CommunityKV: Efficient Long-Context Decoding via Graph Partitioning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00418v1)
*Joe McKenna,Anastasios Alexandridis,Nathan Susanj,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommunityKV将稀疏注意力问题形式化为社区检测问题，通过构建token图并划分社区来实现语义连贯的token检索。在解码过程中使用局部更新规则进行常数时间社区分配，实现流式解码。实验表明在Qwen3和Llama-3.1模型上可实现最高1.71倍的生成吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer长上下文扩展受限于自注意力的二次方计算成本和KV缓存的线性增长。现有稀疏注意力方法要么需要大规模训练，要么依赖语义粗糙的启发式方法或难以高效更新的聚类方法，在解码过程中效率低下。

Method: CommunityKV从预填充阶段已计算的QK^T分数构建token图，对图进行社区划分以检索语义连贯的token组。新生成token通过局部更新规则以常数时间分配到社区，无需全局重新划分即可在流式解码中实现稀疏检索。

Result: 在Qwen3和Llama-3.1模型上，CommunityKV在三个长上下文基准测试中：每个查询头一个图可达1.25倍端到端生成吞吐量提升；查询组图聚合方法在精度相当的情况下可达1.71倍提升。

Conclusion: 社区检测为稀疏注意力提供了一种高效且语义连贯的解决方案，通过局部更新规则实现了常数时间的在线社区分配，在保持精度的同时显著提升了长上下文场景下的生成效率。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention and the linear growth of key-value cache memory transfer. Sparse attention mitigates this by retrieving only relevant tokens, but current approaches either require large-scale training or, within the training-free regime, rely on semantically coarse heuristics or expensive clustering that is difficult to update efficiently during decoding. We introduce CommunityKV, a framework that formulates sparse attention as a community detection problem. CommunityKV constructs a token graph from the $QK^T$ scores already computed during standard prefill, and partitions the graph into communities to enable retrieval of semantically coherent token groups. A local update rule assigns newly generated tokens to communities in constant time, enabling sparse retrieval throughout streaming decoding without global re-partitioning. We evaluate CommunityKV on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 models across three long-context benchmarks. With one graph per query head, CommunityKV delivers up to $1.25\times$ the end-to-end generation throughput of dense attention, while query-group graph aggregation yields up to $1.71\times$ with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [175] [Gumbel Straight Flow: Distilling Autoregressive Models into One-step Flow Maps](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00497v1)
*Yeongmin Kim,Arnaud Doucet,Andrew Campbell,Valentin De Bortoli,Thomas Mensink,David Ruhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gumbel Straight Flow (GSF)，一种连续流映射语言模型，利用预训练自回归模型的噪声-数据耦合机制，在Gumbel噪声与独热 token 序列之间构建线性路径，通过AR教师引导的流映射半群目标提升少步采样质量，在多个基准上超越现有少步生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的自回归生成面临推理效率与生成质量之间的权衡，少步生成（few-step generation）虽加速推理但易导致质量下降，亟需改进。

Method: 利用预训练自回归语言模型的噪声-数据耦合特性，证明Gumbel噪声与独热token序列之间可产生非交叉线性路径；采用流映射半群目标，引入AR教师直接指导切向量（速度）条件，实现高质量少步路径采样。

Result: 在预训练任务和下游任务的多项基准测试中，GSF均优于当前先进的少步语言生成基线方法。

Conclusion: GSF通过结合连续归一化流与预训练AR模型知识，有效提升了少步生成的保真度，为高效高质量语言生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present Gumbel Straight Flow (GSF), a continuous flow map language model that leverages the noise-data coupling of a pretrained autoregressive language (AR) model. We theoretically demonstrate that the coupling between Gumbel noise and one-hot token sequences induced by an autoregressive model yields non-intersecting linear paths connecting the noise to the sequence representations. To further enhance high-quality few-step path sampling, we use a flow map semigroup objective where the tangent (velocity) condition is guided directly by the AR teacher. Across various benchmarks, including pretraining and downstream tasks, GSF can outperform current few-step language generation baselines.

</details>


### [176] [SimplexUQ: An Evaluation Framework and Benchmark for Conformal Uncertainty on Simplex-Valued Predictions](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00523v1)
*Liang You,Hengyu Shi,Dongwen Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SimplexUQ是首个用于评估 simplex-valued（概率向量）预测中覆盖范围分配问题的基准测试协议，旨在揭示传统保边覆盖（marginal coverage）的共形预测方法如何在不同区域间不均匀分配覆盖率的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测虽然能保证边际覆盖率，但单一的校准阈值会导致覆盖在"简单"区域过度而在"困难"区域不足，造成覆盖分配不均的问题。

Method: 构建了 SimplexTasks-12 任务套件（6个合成+6个真实任务），在固定预测器、评分函数和分层映射的前提下，比较不同共形预测 wrapper 的表现，评估指标包括边际覆盖率、最差分层覆盖率、最大差异度等。

Result: 实验表明全局校准可能表面有效但实际表现差（如CIFAR-10边际覆盖0.900但最高熵分层仅0.542）；Mondrian校准可将最差分层提升至0.886并降低最大差异度至0.022；不同wrapper在不同任务和评估分组下各有优劣。
