Conclusion: 没有单一wrapper能够主导所有任务；wrapper选择应作为诊断性比较而非通用排名；移除预测器偏差只能部分减少全局阈值差异。

Abstract: Conformal prediction guarantees marginal coverage, but a single calibration threshold can still spread that coverage unevenly, over-covering easy regions and under-covering hard ones. SimplexUQ is, to our knowledge, the first benchmark and reproducible protocol for measuring this allocation problem on simplex-valued predictions; it compares existing conformal wrappers rather than proposing a new one. Its task suite, SimplexTasks-12, combines six controlled synthetic regimes with six frozen-predictor real tasks spanning class probabilities, topic mixtures, spectral abundances, cell-type fractions, age distributions, and emotion mixtures. Each comparison fixes the predictor, score, and response-free stratification map, varies only the wrapper, and reports marginal coverage, worst-stratum coverage, max disparity, and within-task radius and compute. Global calibration can look valid while failing badly: on CIFAR-10 it attains 0.900 marginal coverage but only 0.542 in the worst entropy stratum, and Mondrian calibration raises that stratum to 0.886 while reducing max disparity from 0.358 to 0.022. No wrapper dominates, however. Under smooth synthetic heterogeneity, several repairs are competitive; fixed-map analyses show that rankings depend on the evaluation groups and protocol; and in a 12-task comparison, Mondrian has lower disparity on its single target partition for all 12 tasks, whereas BatchMVP has lower disparity over overlapping groups on five. These are empirical comparisons, not new coverage guarantees. A controlled predictor-bias sweep shows that removing predictor bias only partly reduces global-threshold disparity. We release task cards, result provenance, permitted derived arrays, and rebuild instructions, and treat wrapper selection as a diagnostic comparison rather than a universal ranking.

</details>


### [177] [Geometry-Dependent Bounds for Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00545v1)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非单调DR-子模函数在对抗性在线优化环境下的最大化问题，提出比较器一致的一阶不等式（系数4/9），改进了现有的0.401基准，并在参数化设置下给出了匹配的下上界，分析了最优差距的渐进行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习与优化中，如何在无法预知目标函数的情况下做出最优决策是一个核心问题。DR-子模函数在机器学习、收益管理等领域有广泛应用，但现有的一阶算法在非单调情况下性能受限，理论基准存在改进空间。

Method: 采用有序坐标（ordered-coordinate）直接论证方法，结合与目标无关的理性动作设计。通过三群对称间隙（three-group symmetry-gap）构造建立上界，并利用参数化扩展和有限实例精确边界刻画完整的理论框架。

Result: 主要成果包括：①系数4/9的一阶不等式（每轮仅需一次梯度查询和一次投影）；②参数化系数α(ζ)的闭合形式表达式；③离线预言机在ζ=0时的上界β*=0.4704...；④当ζ≥1/2时下上界在1/2处匹配；⑤与1/2的最优差距为Θ((1/2-ζ)²)；⑥二次函数达到1/2，三次函数系数从8/17开始。

Conclusion: 该工作为DR-子模函数在线优化建立了更紧的理论界限，揭示了参数ζ对最优系数的影响机制，表明当约束集合包含特定结构时可达到更强的理论保证。非适应性和值反馈场景下的扩展结果进一步增强了算法的实用性。

Abstract: We study adversarial online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed sets. A learner commits each action before observing its objective and competes with the best fixed action in hindsight. We prove a comparator-uniform first-order inequality that gives coefficient $4/9$, improving the online $0.401$ benchmark, with one gradient query and one projection per round and $O(\sqrt T)$ expected approximate regret. If $ζ{\bf 1} \in K\subseteq[0,1]^d$, the coefficient improves to $\underlineα(ζ)=\tfrac12-(1-2ζ)_+^2/[2(3-2ζ)^2]$. The proof is a direct ordered-coordinate argument with an objective-independent rational action. Conversely, a three-group symmetry-gap construction yields an offline oracle upper bound $β_*=0.470438681380894\ldots$ at $ζ=0$, even with exact value and full-gradient responses. A parameterized extension and exact finite-instance bounds define an upper function for every $ζ$. The lower and upper bounds match at $1/2$ for $ζ\ge1/2$, and show that the optimal deficit from $1/2$ is $Θ((1/2-ζ)^2)$ as $ζ\uparrow1/2$. For coefficient-revealed polynomials we obtain $1/2$ for quadratics and a geometry-dependent cubic coefficient starting at $8/17$, including $0.49$ at $ζ=1/5$. A constant objective sequence yields an offline $(4/9-\varepsilon)$ approximation with polynomially many first-order queries on the cube and projections, without requiring a supplied positive lower bound on the optimum. We also give nonanticipating adaptive-adversary and value-feedback guarantees, including $O(T^{3/4})$ regret with one noisy value per round.

</details>


### [178] [Evaluating Hybrid Quantum-Classical Models for Reduced-Order Brain Deformation Dynamics](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00554v1)
*Tao Liu,Ge He,Dongyu Liang,Wujie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了混合量子-经典机器学习在脑部变形场降阶预测中的表现。通过 POD 进行降维，分别测试静态回归和自回归时序预测任务。结果表明：经典网络在当前条件下优于量子变体；经典 POD-MLP 优于所有量子方案，经典 POD-LSTM 在时序预测上优于量子 QLSTM。虽然增强型 VQC 相比基础版本有明显提升，但整体而言经典架构在保真度和稳定性上仍具决定性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维位移场的计算难以处理，需要在保持预测精度的同时降低计算复杂度。

Method: 采用 POD 将数据投影到紧凑潜空间，设计两类学习目标：静态时序到潜变量回归、自回归潜状态预测。对比经典基线与最小/增强型混合量子架构（含 VQC 和 QLSTM）。

Result: 经典网络在当前设置中表现最强：POD-MLP 优于所有量子变体，POD-LSTM 在精度和统计鲁棒性上超过 QLSTM。增强型 VQC 相比最小 VQC 有显著改善。

Conclusion: 降阶物理场学习是近-term QML 的严谨测试平台；虽然混合增强成功恢复了弱量子电路的表达能力，但经典架构在保真度和稳定性上仍具决定性优势。

Abstract: We evaluate hybrid quantum-classical machine learning for the reduced-order prediction of spatiotemporal brain deformation fields. To mitigate the computational intractability of high-dimensional displacement fields, we employ Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) to project the data into a compact latent space. Within this framework, we formulate two distinct learning objectives: static temporal-to-latent regression and autoregressive latent state forecasting. We systematically benchmark compact classical baselines against both minimal and enhanced hybrid quantum architectures. Our results demonstrate that classical networks provide the strongest baselines in the present setting. For static regression, a classical POD-MLP outperforms all evaluated quantum variants, although an enhanced Variational Quantum Circuit (VQC) substantially improves upon a minimal VQC baseline. For temporal forecasting, a classical POD-LSTM delivers superior predictive accuracy and statistical robustness compared to an enhanced Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) across varying history windows and random initializations. Overall, this study establishes reduced-order physical field learning as a rigorous testbed for near-term QML, highlighting that while hybrid enhancements successfully recover expressivity in weak quantum circuits, classical architectures retain a definitive advantage in both fidelity and stability.

</details>


### [179] [Beyond Affine Transformations: A Soft Dominance Layer for Coordinate-Wise Neural Computation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00563v1)
*Mariano Rivera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft Dominance Layer作为传统仿射变换的替代方案，通过比较输入坐标与可学习参考向量产生输出，使用sigmoid松弛实现可微性。在MNIST单次实验中，准确率达到0.9061（无退火）/0.9173（有退火），低于MLP基线的0.9827。这是一项初步的概念验证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络层依赖仿射变换（线性变换+偏置），作者希望探索一种基于比较运算的替代方案，尝试实现可直接解释的阈值决策能力。

Method: Soft Dominance Layer：每个输出单元将输入坐标与可学习参考向量比较，聚合平滑不等式响应。通过sigmoid函数松弛实现可微性，引入锐度参数α控制向硬阈值决策的过渡。

Result: MNIST单次运行实验：最高准确率0.9061（无退火）、0.9173（有退火），MLP基线为0.9827。学习到的参考向量呈现空间结构，表明存在结构化学习。现有结果不构成可靠排名或统计支持的退火收益。

Conclusion: 作为初步概念验证，该研究展示了Soft Dominance Layer的可训练性和直接阈值解释的可行性。但目前缺乏重复实验和统计验证，需在更广泛数据集上评估其稳健性和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary study of an alternative to the affine transformation underlying conventional neural-network layers. In the proposed Soft Dominance Layer, each output unit compares input coordinates with a learnable reference vector and aggregates smooth inequality responses. A sigmoid relaxation makes the comparisons differentiable, while a sharpness parameter $α$ controls their transition toward hard threshold decisions. The aim is to examine the trainability and direct threshold interpretation of this primitive, not to claim a replacement for affine layers. In single-run MNIST experiments, the highest observed Soft Dominance accuracy is $0.9061$ without annealing and $0.9173$ with annealing, compared with $0.9827$ for the MLP baseline. These descriptive results do not establish reliable configuration rankings or a statistically supported annealing benefit. Learned reference vectors exhibit spatial structure, providing qualitative evidence of structured learning. Repeated-seed experiments and broader datasets are required to assess robustness and practical relevance beyond this proof of concept.

</details>


### [180] [Attention Kernels for Learning Maps Between Heavy-Tailed Measures](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00564v1)
*Kailen Hargenrader,Edoardo Calvello,Bohan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究transformer在概率测度上的算子学习问题。针对多项式尾分布导致softmax注意力积分发散的问题，提出用增长较慢的核函数替代指数核。两个基准测试表明，较慢增长的核函数能有效避免重尾分布上的集成崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统softmax注意力机制中的指数加权在处理多项式尾分布时会导致积分发散，限制了transformer在重尾概率测度上的应用。

Method: 构建两个具有闭式目标的测度算子学习基准；研究后归一化transformer中的注意力核增长和数据变换；使用经验能量和Wasserstein距离分析样本量对尾部差异敏感性的影响。

Result: softmax模型在两个重尾基准上均出现集成崩溃，而三种较慢增长的核函数避免了崩溃。Symlog预处理可使softmax在矩阵逆任务上避免崩溃，但在剪切交换任务上仍失败。高斯控制条件下四种核函数表现相近。

Conclusion: 对于从重尾集成学习的后归一化transformer，较慢增长的注意力核函数是一种有效的设计选择。

Abstract: Operator learning on probability measures can be accomplished with transformers. For measures with polynomial tails, the exponential weighting in softmax can make the corresponding measure-level attention integrals diverge. This motivates replacing the exponential with slower-growing functions. We construct two benchmarks for operator learning on measures with closed-form targets. We use these benchmarks to study attention kernel growth and data transformation in post-norm transformers. Without data transformation, the softmax models exhibit ensemble collapse on both heavy-tailed benchmarks, while the three slower-growing kernels avoid collapse. Symlog preprocessing allows softmax to avoid collapse on the matrix inverse task but not on the sheared swap task. On the Gaussian control, all four kernels perform similarly. We also examine how sample size affects the sensitivity of empirical energy and Wasserstein distances to tail differences. These results support slower-growing attention kernels as an effective design choice for post-norm transformers learning from heavy-tailed ensembles.

</details>


### [181] [ALER: Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting for Reinforcement Learning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00592v1)
*Oleg Shchendrigin,Egor Cherepanov,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测强化学习中的记忆机制，提出记忆需满足保留（retention）、重写（rewriting）和经验融合（experience fusion）三个需求，并设计ALER agent和Rune-Mazes benchmark，在多种任务配置下显著优于7个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆架构和基准测试主要关注"保留"能力，即保持信息不变直至需要，但忽略了记忆内容应根据后续观察动态更新的场景需求。

Method: 1. 形式化记忆的三个需求：保留、重写（将决策相关内容设为与旧值无关的值）、经验融合（用后续观察指定的规则转换旧内容）；2. 设计ALER agent：LSTM + slot memory，采用Gumbel-Softmax单槽点写机制和可学习门控进行检索内容融合；3. 提出Rune-Mazes环境，包含vector和pixel观测下对隐藏线索进行反转、取消、重置、重复等操作的任务。

Result: ALER在所有16个Endless T-Maze配置中成功率达≥0.82，5个Rune T-Maze组合中达≥0.99，四分支Rune Multi-Corridor（Invert rune）平均成功率最高，pixel-based Rune MiniGrid Memory的10个配置中8个优于PPO-LSTM。

Conclusion: ALER通过显式建模记忆重写和经验融合机制，有效解决了部分可观测RL中的动态记忆更新问题，验证了所提三个记忆需求的必要性。

Abstract: In partially observable reinforcement learning (RL), a later observation can make stored information obsolete or change what it implies for the next decision. Memory architectures and benchmarks for RL mostly test retention, the ability to keep information unchanged until it is needed. We formalize two further requirements. Rewriting sets the decision-relevant content to a value independent of the old one, and experience fusion transforms the old content by a rule that a later observation specifies. For tasks built from such updates, we count the memory states that a solution needs, and several baselines reach their lowest success rates on compositions that need more states. We introduce ALER (Adaptive Learnable Experience Rewriting), an agent that pairs an LSTM with a slot memory. An independently addressed Gumbel-Softmax write that concentrates its weight on one slot overwrites that slot, and a learned gate fuses the retrieved content with the recurrent state before the policy and value heads. We also introduce Rune-Mazes, three environments in which rune observations invert, cancel, reset, or repeat updates of a hidden cue under vector and pixel observations. Against seven baselines, ALER reaches a success rate of at least $0.82$ in all sixteen Endless T-Maze configurations and at least $0.99$ on all five Rune T-Maze compositions, and it has the highest mean success rate on four-branch Rune Multi-Corridor with an Invert rune. On pixel-based Rune MiniGrid Memory, it has a higher mean success rate than PPO-LSTM in eight of ten configurations. Project page: https://quartz-admirer.github.io/ALER-Adaptive-Learnable-Experience-Rewriting/.

</details>


### [182] [MIKASA-Robo-VLA: Benchmarking Memory in VLA Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00604v1)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIKASA-Robo-VLA基准测试，包含90个语言条件下的机器人操作任务，用于评估视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的记忆能力。核心发现是现有VLA模型因只看最近几帧而难以处理信息消失的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略通常只处理1帧或少量最近帧，当任务相关信息在执行过程中消失时（如物体被遮挡），这些策略难以正确执行动作。

Method: 构建了90个任务的基准测试套件，其中80个为记忆依赖任务（隐藏关键线索），10个为反应式对照任务（保持线索可见）；其中70个任务定义了信息间隔，28个任务的信息间隔超过16帧；发布22,500条oracle轨迹；训练π₀.₅基线模型（仅使用当前图像和本体感觉，无历史或显式记忆模块）。

Result: π₀.₅基线在14个任务上平均成功率为0.211±0.044；Long-split任务上成功率更低，主要与开环分块和控制类型有关。

Conclusion: MIKASA-Robo-VLA有效揭示了当前VLA策略在处理长时序记忆依赖任务时的局限性，为未来具有持久记忆能力的VLA研究提供了评估基准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies often see only one or a few recent frames, which makes it difficult to evaluate how they use information that disappears during a task. We introduce MIKASA-Robo-VLA, a benchmark of 90 language-conditioned manipulation tasks. All but 10 hide the cue an action depends on. Those 10 are reactive controls. MIKASA-Robo, the suite it rebuilds, has 32 tasks and uses language only in a representative VLA subset. Here every task provides an instruction, while memory-dependent tasks hide a task-relevant cue and reactive controls keep it available. For 70 tasks, environment phase timings specify an information gap, and for 28 of them the gap exceeds the 16-frame window of the widest fixed-context VLA we survey. The gap counts only the interval the cue is provably absent, not the full duration a policy must retain it, so every memory-dependent task still requires memory by construction, including the ones whose measured gap is short. We release 22,500 oracle trajectories across 10 memory types in RLDS and LeRobotDataset v3. A reference $π_{0.5}$ baseline with current images and proprioception, but no observation history or explicit memory module, is fine-tuned on 14 tasks and achieves 0.211 $\pm$ 0.044 mean task success. Its lower success on the evaluated Long-split tasks is confounded by open-loop chunking and the memory types represented in that subset. Project page: https://mikasarobo.github.io/

</details>


### [183] [Exploring More, Reasoning Better: Stepwise Risk-Sensitive GRPO for Diffusion Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00661v1)
*Yue YU,Bowen Zuo,David Crandall,Yinglun Zhu,Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出StepRS-GRPO方法，通过在扩散语言模型的不同去噪状态下动态调整风险系数来改进强化学习训练，在数学推理基准上同时提升准确率和答案多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(dLLM)通过去噪方式生成文本，可在多个步骤中并行揭示token，但现有的RLVR方法在训练过程中缺乏对去噪状态差异的有效利用。研究者需要一种方法能根据不同去噪状态自适应调整风险敏感度。

Method: StepRS-GRPO在保持基础训练器不变的前提下，对group-advantage变换的风险系数在不同去噪状态间进行动态调整。对于二元奖励，该变换等价于对中心化结果优势进行prompt和state依赖的重缩放。研究通过能力基准校准确定系数尺度，并通过端点和插值消融实验指导调度选择。

Result: 在多种dLLM骨干网络和数学推理基准测试中，StepRS-GRPO相比中心化GRPO在pass@1准确率和pass@k覆盖率上均有提升，同时增加了答案多样性。消融实验表明状态分配和调度方向的贡献显著，且在匹配RMS优势后仍保持优势。

Conclusion: StepRS-GRPO通过状态依赖的风险系数调整，有效利用了扩散生成过程中的状态差异，在提升推理性能的同时增强了答案多样性，为dLLM的强化学习训练提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by denoising a sequence or successive blocks, allowing several tokens to be revealed in parallel. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) reuses terminal feedback across these decisions, even as their conditioning context changes. We propose stepwise risk-sensitive GRPO (StepRS-GRPO), which varies the risk coefficient of the group-advantage transformation across denoising states while retaining the underlying trainer. For binary rewards, we show that this transformation is exactly a prompt- and state-dependent rescaling of centered outcome advantages. A capability-based calibration suggests a coefficient scale, while endpoint and interpolation ablations guide schedule selection. Across multiple dLLM backbones and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, StepRS-GRPO improves both pass@1 accuracy and pass@k coverage over centered GRPO, while increasing answer diversity. In our ablation studies, mass-matched controls support the contributions of state allocation and schedule direction, and the gains persist after matching the root mean square (RMS) of the advantages to that of centered GRPO. Reasoning-trace diagnostics further show that the diversity gains from StepRS-GRPO extend beyond final-answer strings.

</details>


### [184] [Analysis of Quantized and Efficiently Adapted Protein Language Models](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00665v1)
*Ilan Yaniv Zeisler,Sebastian Clancy,Pouriya Bayat,Saaim Raad,Ivan Kraskov,Matthew Xie,Vivian White,Spencer Perkins,Serena Singh,Sepehr Bayat,Keith Pardee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了4-bit量化（QLoRA）对7种蛋白质语言模型（ESM-2、ESMC、ProtBERT、ProtT5、Ankh、Ankh3、Profluent-E1）在预测任务和5种生成模型（ProLLaMA、ProtGPT2、ProGen2、ProteinGLM、ESM3）上的影响。发现在许多模型-任务组合中可保留90%以上的全微调性能，GPU显存节省接近90%，但需针对具体模型和任务进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）规模庞大，微调和部署成本高昂。量化与参数高效微调技术对模型性能、表征和生成能力的影响尚缺乏系统表征。

Method: 在7种蛋白质语言模型上评估4-bit量化和低秩适配器微调（QLoRA），在预测任务和生成任务上测试性能、表示和输出分布。

Result: 预测任务中多数模型-任务组合保留>90%全微调性能，最大模型GPU显存节省接近90%。QLoRA保留早期层表征，在中后期层诱导任务特异性适应。生成任务中，4-bit量化基本保留结构和序列级属性，但令牌级分析揭示模型依赖性的自回归输出分布偏移。

Conclusion: QLoRA可作为内存受限场景的首选策略，复杂任务、不稳定架构或低验证恢复场景保留全微调。生成模型评估需补充分布分析，因下游预测指标可能遗漏量化诱导的偏移。这些方法可扩大大规模蛋白质建模的可及性，但需模型和任务特异性验证。

Abstract: Background: Protein language models (PLMs) are increasingly used for sequence generation and property prediction, but their size makes fine-tuning and deployment expensive. The effects of quantization and parameter efficient fine-tuning on performance, representations and generation remain insufficiently characterized. Results: We evaluated 4-bit quantization and low-rank adapter fine-tuning (QLoRA) across ESM-2, ESMC, ProtBERT, ProtT5, Ankh, Ankh3 and Profluent-E1. Across protein prediction tasks, many model-task pairs retained more than 90% of full fine-tuning performance. Peak GPU memory savings approached 90% for the largest models, although performance and efficiency varied by model, dataset and training configuration. QLoRA often preserved early-layer representations while inducing task-specific adaptations in middle and late layers, resembling full fine-tuning with smaller representational changes. Training speed and power effects were more varied. For unconditional generation with ProLLaMA, ProtGPT2, ProGen2, ProteinGLM and ESM3, 4-bit quantization largely preserved predicted structural and sequence-level properties, but token-level analysis revealed model-dependent shifts in autoregressive output distributions. Conclusion: QLoRA and 4-bit quantization reduce PLM computational requirements, particularly GPU memory usage. Our results support QLoRA as a first-pass strategy for memory limited adaptation, reserving full fine-tuning for challenging tasks, unstable architectures or low validation recovery. For generative PLMs, sequence-level and structural metrics should be complemented with distributional analysis, since downstream predictions alone may miss quantization-induced shifts. These approaches can broaden access to large-scale protein modelling while requiring model- and task-specific validation.

</details>


### [185] [Learning Transferable Skills using Goal-Conditioned Bisimulation](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00676v1)
*Mohammad Amin Abbasfar,Farbod Azimmohseni,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动作感知时间表示和双模拟的无监督技能发现方法，能够学习可跨不同环境布局迁移的通用技能，实现强大的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无监督技能发现方法存在两大局限：(1) 需要专家数据；(2) 泛化能力有限，无法有效迁移到未见过的环境布局。核心挑战在于学习能够捕获环境时间结构同时对布局变化保持鲁棒的表示。

Method: 1. 提出学习动作感知时间表示的目标函数，满足功能等变性性质并保留局部时间结构；2. 基于此嵌入，进一步提出基于双模拟的无监督技能发现方法，通过仅将技能行为条件化于直接影响执行的状态特征子集来学习可迁移技能；3. 这种方法强制技能在不同布局间保持不变行为，从而实现有效迁移。

Result: 通过综合实验评估表明，在给定环境中学习的技能能够有效应用于解决多种不同环境布局中的下游任务。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了强大的分布外泛化能力，证明了通过动作感知表示学习和双模拟约束可以有效解决无监督技能发现中的迁移难题。

Abstract: Unsupervised skill discovery has emerged as a promising approach for leveraging reward-free datasets to pretrain general-purpose policies. However, current skill discovery methods either require access to expert data or exhibit limited generalization, failing to transfer effectively to previously unseen layouts. A key challenge is to learn representations that capture the temporal structure of the environment while remaining robust to variations across layouts. To address this issue, we present an objective for learning action-aware temporal representations that satisfy the functional equivariance property while preserving the local temporal structure of the environment. Building upon this embedding, we further propose unsupervised skill discovery using bisimulation, which learns transferable skills by conditioning the behavior of skills exclusively on the subset of state features that directly affect their execution. This enforces invariant behavior across different layouts, enabling skills to transfer effectively to other configurations. Finally, through comprehensive empirical evaluations, we show that skills learned in a given environment can be effectively applied to solve downstream tasks in various environment layouts, demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [186] [Curvature Under Attack in hZACH-ViT: Gauge Symmetry, Boundary Saturation, and Adversarial Failure](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00680v1)
*Athanasios Angelakis,Marta Gomez-Barrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了双曲 embeddings 中曲率的实证效应，发现曲率的实现敏感性（数值尺度、logit温度、裁剪机制）会显著影响模型在干净数据和对抗攻击下的表现；在固定曲率 c=1 时 Poincaré 模型对攻击的抵抗力最强，但干净准确率最低，而降低曲率虽能提升干净准确率却会大幅增加对抗脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲空间的曲率常被视为表示的内在属性，但其实证效果也依赖于坐标尺度、学习到的 logit 温度和数值保护机制。作者认为有必要系统性地研究这些因素的交互作用，以确定曲率是否真的带来内在的鲁棒性优势。

Method: 使用 hZACH-ViT 架构（配备 Euclidean、Poincaré 和 Spherical 原型头），在 MedMNIST 三个数据集上以匹配的后续架构、种子特定初始化、每类50样本训练子集和优化协议进行实验。采用端到端曲率干预方法，通过 PGD 攻击成功率评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: 固定曲率 c=1 时，Poincaré 在所有 12 个数据集-预算单元中 PGD 攻击成功率最低（最鲁棒），但干净 MacroF1 也最低。降低 Poincaré 曲率至 c=0.1 后，15 组种子-数据集配对比较中干净 MacroF1 均提升，但 OrganAMNIST 上强攻击成功率从 89.7% 升至 99.3%（+9.57 点，95% CI [5.52, 14.03]）。c=1 时 40-47% 的干净 Poincaré 特征被硬裁剪，径向 Jacobian 接近零。球形头的曲率变化是精确的尺度变换，影响较小。

Conclusion: 研究未确立双曲空间的内在对抗鲁棒性优势，但识别出一种实现敏感的状态：曲率、尺度和 Poincaré 边界邻近性共同决定了干净识别和对抗表示运动的特性。这一发现强调了实现细节对双曲表示评估的重要性。

Abstract: Curvature is often treated as an intrinsic property of a representation, although its empirical effect also depends on coordinate scale, learned logit temperature, and numerical safeguards. We study this interaction in hZACH-ViT, a compact Vision Transformer with Euclidean, Poincare, and spherical prototype heads. The backbone architecture, seed-specific initialization, 50-per-class training subset, and optimization protocol are matched across three MedMNIST datasets and five seeds. At the fixed comparison curvature $c=1$, Poincare has the lowest class-macro PGD attack-success rate in all 12 dataset-budget cells and under a stronger CE+DLR multi-restart attack on all three datasets, but it also has the lowest clean MacroF1. An end-to-end curvature intervention changes the interpretation. Reducing Poincare curvature to $c=0.1$ improves clean MacroF1 in every one of the 15 paired seed-dataset comparisons and removes hard boundary clipping, yet on OrganAMNIST it increases strong attack success from $89.7\%$ to $99.3\%$ (paired difference $+9.57$ points; 95\% hierarchical bootstrap CI $[+5.52,+14.03]$). At $c=1$, $40$-$47\%$ of clean Poincare features are hard-clipped, the radial Jacobian of the inherited map is nearly zero, and dimensionless attack trajectories are unusually long and inefficient. The spherical head provides a control: its curvature change is an exact scale gauge to floating-point precision and produces much smaller attack differences. These results do not establish intrinsic hyperbolic robustness. They identify an implementation-sensitive regime in which curvature, scale, and proximity to the Poincare boundary jointly organize clean recognition and adversarial representation motion.

</details>


### [187] [SkillSpec: Consensus-Gated Agent Skill Evolution via Representation Specialization](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00704v1)
*Huancheng Chen,Xiaodi Sun,Zhaoqiong Huang,Shenyang Huang Shreya Singhal,Jingwen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillSpec通过两阶段框架改进LLM智能体的自然语言技能：共识门控进化阶段通过配对评估共识验证文本更新，表征特化阶段根据优化轨迹信号选择最优表示形式（扁平/图/混合）。在6个基准和3个目标模型上平均成功率提升6.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将技能视为静态整体文档，仅通过聚合验证分数优化文本内容，未能显式建模程序性知识的检索与执行结构。核心问题在于：知识保留的内容优化与知识组织的方式优化被混为一谈，文本更新缺乏执行验证，知识结构未针对检索需求进行特化。

Method: 提出SkillSpec两阶段框架：1) 共识门控进化：互补编辑意图生成完整候选技能，仅当配对评估达成共识（整体改善充分且每次重复评估的聚合配对增益非负）时提交更新；2) 表征特化：从完整优化轨迹（含接受/拒绝的候选）提取过程敏感性和冗余敏感性信号，指导选择扁平、图或混合表示形式。

Result: 在6个基准测试和3个目标语言模型上，SkillSpec相比SkillOpt平均成功率提升6.89%。

Conclusion: 可靠的技能进化与表征特化解决互补目标：决定保留什么知识，以及如何为其组织结构以支持推理。区分知识内容优化与结构优化，并通过执行证据验证文本更新，是提升LLM智能体技能质量的关键。

Abstract: Natural-language skills are textual procedural memories through which large language model (LLM) agents retain reusable task knowledge without updating model weights. Existing methods typically treat skills as either static artifacts or monolithic documents optimized using aggregate validation scores as feedback. However, representing a skill as a monolithic document restricts optimization to its textual content, without explicitly modeling the structure through which procedural knowledge is retrieved and executed. We identify a key distinction between learning what knowledge to retain and determining how to organize it: textual updates should first be validated through execution evidence, after which the retained knowledge should be structured according to its procedural dependencies and retrieval requirements. To this end, we introduce SkillSpec, a two-phase framework comprising consensus-gated evolution and representation specialization. In the consensus-gated phase, complementary editing intents generate complete candidate skills. An update is committed only when paired evaluations reach consensus, requiring sufficient overall improvement and non-negative aggregate paired gain in every repeated evaluation. In the specialization phase, signals of process and redundancy sensitivity derived from the full optimization trajectory, including accepted and rejected candidates, guide the selection of a flat, graph, or hybrid representation.Across six benchmarks and three target language models, SkillSpec improves average success rate over SkillOpt by 6.89%, averaged across the three models. These results demonstrate that reliable skill evolution and representation specialization address complementary objectives: deciding what knowledge to retain and how to structure it for inference.

</details>


### [188] [Beyond Unimodal Bases: Pullback Geometry for Multimodal Data](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00708v1)
*Honglei Brinkmann,Lucas Ng,Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Willem Diepeveen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于高斯混合分布的pullback几何结构，用于处理多流形混合数据。与传统的单高斯假设不同，该方法通过责任加权期望精度矩阵的矩阵平方根定义黎曼度量，并在归一化流中实现自适应混合学习，在保持计算效率的同时减少了传输畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可扩展的pullback几何结构通常假设数据位于单一流形附近，采用单模态高斯潜在分布。当处理多模态数据时，将分离的模式映射到单一高斯区域会产生显著的传输变形，损害几何结构的有效性。

Method: 提出使用潜在高斯混合分布，通过责任加权期望组件精度的矩阵平方定义黎曼度量。该度量光滑、正定，在单组件极限下可退化为现有高斯构造。对于结构化重叠混合，建立了沿测地线对数密度凹性的条件，建立了所提几何与高似然路径之间的形式联系。

Result: 在合成几何数据、受控多视图图像设置（带已知参考轨迹）和MNIST上的实验表明：该方法减少了传输畸变，保持了竞争力的路径支撑，精确恢复了参考轨迹，并提升了插值真实感。

Conclusion: 该工作将可扩展pullback几何从单一流形数据集扩展到多流形混合数据场景，同时保持了可处理性和可解释的局部结构。

Abstract: Data-driven Riemannian geometry provides nonlinear interpolation and geometric representations of high-dimensional data. For these operations to be statistically meaningful, paths between observations should preferentially traverse high-likelihood regions. Existing scalable pullback constructions typically use a unimodal Gaussian latent distribution, assuming that the data reside close to a single manifold. For multimodal data, mapping separated modes or local structures into one Gaussian region can require substantial transport deformation and compromise the resulting geometry.
  We introduce a pullback geometry for data supported on mixtures of manifolds. Using a latent Gaussian mixture, we define its Riemannian metric as the matrix square of the responsibility-weighted expected component precision. The metric is smooth and positive definite and recovers the existing Gaussian construction in the single-component limit. For structured overlapping mixtures, we establish conditions under which the log-density is concave along geodesics, providing a formal connection between the proposed geometry and paths through high-likelihood regions, and derive the corresponding local curvature relations.
  We instantiate this geometry in a normalizing flow with adaptive mixture learning, allowing the number of active components to emerge from the data and supporting component-wise reconstruction and local effective-dimension estimation. Experiments on synthetic geometric data, a controlled multi-view image setting with a known reference trajectory, and MNIST show reduced transport distortion, competitive path support, close reference-trajectory recovery, and improved interpolation realism. These results extend scalable pullback geometry beyond datasets that reside close to a single manifold while retaining tractable and interpretable local structure.

</details>


### [189] [WOMBAT: Whitebox Oracle for Molecular Benchmarking and Attribution Testing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00713v1)
*Dominik Matuszek,Bartosz Zieliński,Tomasz Danel,Dawid Rymarczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WOMBAT是一个用于评估图神经网络(GNN)事后解释器的基准测试，通过14个白盒GNN模型（手设消息传递权重检测特定SMARTS基序）提供可知的决策规则，作为属性归因的真实标签，用于识别和诊断解释器错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GNN解释器产生的属性归因无法区分是解释器失败还是模型学到了捷径，且缺乏可量化的真实标签来评估解释器性能。

Method: 构建14个白盒GNN模型，每个模型的消息传递权重手工设置以检测特定SMARTS分子基序；在数百万PubChem分子上验证模型；评估GNNExplainer、PGExplainer、Integrated Gradients等解释器。

Result: 验证了模型在PubChem分子上的有效性；发现Integrated Gradients会跨越整个图传播属性，即使模型可靠检测目标基序。

Conclusion: WOMBAT基准为GNN事后解释器的开发提供了可靠的评估框架和真实标签，有助于推动XAI工具的发展。

Abstract: When a graph neural network (GNN) explainer produces an unexpected attribution on a molecule, the attribution alone cannot reveal whether the explainer has failed or the model has learned a shortcut. We introduce WOMBAT, a benchmark of 14 whitebox GNNs, each with message-passing weights set by hand to detect a specific SMARTS motif. Each model's decision rule is known by construction, providing attribution ground truth against which explainer errors can be identified and studied. We validate the models on millions of PubChem molecules and evaluate post-hoc explainers including GNNExplainer, PGExplainer, and Integrated Gradients. Guided by our qualitative analysis, we construct a model that causes Integrated Gradients to spread attribution across the graph, even though the model reliably detects the intended motif. We release the dataset, models, and evaluation code to help researchers in the development of newer XAI tools for GNNs.

</details>


### [190] [Benchmarking Generative Models for Weather Data Assimilation on Real Station Observations](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00728v1)
*Ruizhe Huang,Qidong Yang,Jonathan Giezendanner,Sherrie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于真实气象站观测的生成式天气数据同化受控基准测试，对比了扩散模型与流匹配、像素空间与潜空间、条件策略等设计选择，发现学习到的生成先验优于3D-Var的高斯先验，全梯度引导策略显著优于停梯度策略，而扩散与流匹配性能几乎相同，潜空间变量混合无明显帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式天气数据同化方法在合成观测或不同数据集/评估方案下进行评估，难以判断哪些设计选择在实际数据同化中真正有效，缺乏标准化的基准测试。

Method: 使用美国本土11,849个NOAA MADIS气象站和4个气象变量，在数据集、观测算子和深度学习架构固定的条件下，对比了扩散模型与流匹配、像素空间与潜空间 formulations、以及多种推理时条件策略，与经典3D-Var基线进行对照实验。

Result: 1. 学习生成先验优于3D-Var高斯先验（RMSE减少35.7% vs 33.3%）；2. 全梯度引导策略一致优于停梯度和初始噪声优化；3. 其他选择影响不大：扩散与流匹配性能几乎相同，潜空间变量混合无帮助；4. 稀疏站网条件下，生成AI和全梯度引导优势更显著。

Conclusion: 识别出生成式天气数据同化中真正提升性能的关键组件，建立了未来工作的标准化基准，为实际气象站观测的生成式数据同化提供了可靠的设计指导。

Abstract: Weather reanalysis products rely on computationally intensive numerical weather predictions followed by data assimilation that corrects the forecast toward observations. Deep generative models offer a cheaper alternative that shifts much of this cost from inference to offline training. However, existing generative approaches have been evaluated on synthetic observations or under different datasets and evaluation schemes, making it unclear which design choices actually improve real-world data assimilation. We present the first controlled benchmark of generative weather data assimilation on real weather station observations. Using 11,849 NOAA MADIS stations across the contiguous United States and four weather variables, we evaluate methods while holding the dataset, observation operator, and deep learning architecture fixed. The benchmark compares the major design choices, including diffusion versus flow matching, pixel versus latent-space formulations, and multiple inference-time conditioning strategies, against a classical 3D-Var baseline. The benchmark reveals three clear conclusions. First, learned generative priors outperform the Gaussian prior of 3D-Var (35.7% vs. 33.3% RMSE reduction over ERA5) despite using no ERA5 background field at inference. Second, full-gradient guidance consistently outperforms stop-gradient and initial-noise optimization. Third, other choices provide little measurable benefit: diffusion and flow matching perform nearly identically under matched conditions, and latent-space variable mixing does not help. We further evaluate both dense and sparse station settings and find advantages from generative AI and full-gradient guidance more pronounced under sparsity. Together, these results identify which components of generative weather data assimilation improve performance on real station observations and establish a standardized benchmark for future work.

</details>


### [191] [ibUMAP: Coherent and Scalable Field Evaluation for UMAP Optimization](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01445v1)
*Bin Chen,Yumeng Xue,Patrick Paetzold,Yunhai Wang,Oliver Deussen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ibUMAP通过同步评估吸引力和排斥力替代UMAP的随机负采样，解决嵌入不稳定问题。基于FFT的度加权排斥场实现高效计算，在CPU上实现3.29x-5.79x加速，GPU上1.44x加速，同时提高运行稳定性，但存在局部-全局保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMAP通过随机负采样实现可扩展布局优化，但这种随机性导致重运行和下游重用时嵌入不稳定，因为估计的排斥力取决于采样事件的顺序。

Method: 提出ibUMAP，一种基于场的替代方法，从共享嵌入快照同步评估吸引力和排斥力。其度加权排斥场基于固定嵌入的负采样条件期望构建，由三个标量矩表示，使用基于插值的FFT方案在CPU和GPU上高效计算。

Result: 控制实验表明同步性和核裁剪改变了局部-全局保真度权衡，FFT评估对最终质量影响很小。端到端基准测试显示：CPU上比umap-learn快3.29x(无种子)和5.79x(有种子)；百万规模数据集GPU上比cuML快1.44x。加速伴随着更高的运行稳定性和可测量的保真度权衡。

Conclusion: ibUMAP在保持竞争力的嵌入质量同时，提供了显著的加速和稳定性改进，但使用者需在速度/稳定性收益与局部-全局保真度之间做出权衡。

Abstract: UMAP achieves scalable layout optimization through stochastic negative sampling. However, this stochasticity can lead to unstable embeddings across reruns and downstream reuse, as the estimated repulsive forces depend on the ordering of sampling events. We present ibUMAP, a coherent field-based alternative that evaluates attraction and repulsion from a shared embedding snapshot and applies them synchronously. Its degree-weighted repulsive field is motivated by the conditional expectation of negative sampling for a fixed embedding and represented by three scalar moments, which are evaluated efficiently on CPUs and GPUs using an interpolation-based FFT scheme. This formulation avoids explicit all-pairs computations while inducing optimization dynamics that differ from those of standard online UMAP. Controlled experiments show that synchrony and kernel capping alter the local-global fidelity trade-off, whereas FFT evaluation produces small average changes in final quality. End-to-end benchmarks show median speedups of 3.29x unseeded and 5.79x seeded over umap-learn on CPU, and 1.44x over cuML on million-scale datasets under unseeded GPU execution. These gains accompany greater run-to-run stability and measurable fidelity trade-offs.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Deny Without Disabling: Authorization-Paired Evaluation and Control for Multi-Agent Systems](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00371v1)
*Yunbei Zhang,Saiyue Lyu,Janet Wang,Yingqiang Ge,Jiang Guo,Jihun Hamm,Chandan K Reddy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统中的组合安全难题：单独授权的动作组合后可能产生禁止用途。提出授权配对评估方法及FlowReview框架，通过对象解析、权限排序和确定性执行机制，在实验中成功将拒绝率从86%降至零，同时保留所有授权功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统通过信息共享、任务委托和信息整合实现协作能力，但这一过程也带来了安全隐患：单独可接受的操作组合后可能产生禁止的用途。传统方法阻断所有敏感操作虽能避免泄露，但会破坏协作目的。

Method: 提出授权配对评估方法，将"阻断禁止用途"与"完成必要授权用途"作为联合成功标准；设计FlowReview框架，连接对象解析、权限排序和确定性执行机制；通过受控组合实验进行验证。

Result: 组合工件审查将拒绝提交率从86.0%降至零，同时未损失授权供给。表明仅保留信息和谱系不足以确保正确的权限归属，对象标识和权限必须通过可验证输出的组件与执行保持连接。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全需要系统性要求：在管理组合信息流的同时，保留使协作有用的授权能力。系统需确保对象身份和权限通过可验证组件与执行过程保持连接，而非仅依赖信息保留。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems derive their capabilities from sharing evidence, delegating tasks, and combining information across agents. The same process creates a safety problem: contributions that are admissible in isolation can jointly enable a prohibited use. Blocking every sensitive action avoids disclosure but defeats the purpose of collaboration. We introduce authorization-paired evaluation, which makes blocking prohibited uses and completing required authorized uses a joint success criterion, and FlowReview, a framework connecting object resolution, permission ranking, and deterministic enforcement. In controlled composition experiments, reviewing combined artifacts reduces the denied-commit rate from 86.0% to zero with no loss of authorized supply. Our findings show that preserving information and lineage alone does not ensure correct permission attribution. Object identity and permission must remain connected to execution through components whose outputs can be verified. Together, these findings establish a system-level requirement for multi-agent safety: govern composed information flows while preserving the authorized capabilities that make collaboration useful.

</details>


### [193] [LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Control for Skill-Based Smart Manufacturing](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01364v1)
*Kay Köhle,Darko Anicic,Thomas A. Runkler,René Graf*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体与MCP工具服务器配对，通过OPC UA方法调用暴露工厂模块技能，利用MQTT实现智能体间协调，并结合实时工厂状态更新，用于柔性制造系统编程和故障处理。在六模块六边形工厂仿真中比较了三种架构（编排器、点对点、单体），均展现出色性能，其中编排器架构能自主检测并绕避传送带故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂正向小批量、高定制化方向转型，需要频繁重新编程柔性自动化系统。传统静态程序无法预见所有运行时故障，导致系统缺乏灵活性。现有LLM智能体在制造业的应用缺乏标准化工具接口和可复现的实现框架。

Method: 提出方案：每个工厂模块配备专用LLM智能体和MCP工具服务器，通过OPC UA方法调用暴露模块技能，智能体间通过MQTT协调，并以实时工厂状态更新为锚定基础。在六模块六边形工厂仿真环境中，设置9个复杂度递增的生产挑战进行测试。

Result: 单体架构和点对点架构均达到最高平均解决率（93%）；编排器架构在10次运行中均能自主重新路由绕过传送带故障，检测到静默硬件故障。所有架构均展现出无需显式故障处理逻辑的emergent故障诊断行为。

Conclusion: 标准化MCP工具、基于MQTT的智能体间通信和实时状态注入构成了LLM编程智能制造的可行且可复现的技术基础。编排器架构在复杂故障处理方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: Factories are shifting toward smaller lot sizes with high product customization, requiring frequent re-programming of flexible and reconfigurable automation systems. LLM-based agents can be deployed in two complementary roles: Offline, they generate deterministic production sequences, reducing programming effort; online, they operate live machines and handle unforeseen runtime faults that static programs cannot anticipate. We propose a solution in which each factory module is paired with a dedicated LLM-based agent and an MCP tool server that exposes the module's skills via OPC UA method calls, with agents coordinating over MQTT and grounded by real-time updates of the factory state. We compare three agent architectures (orchestrator, peer-to-peer, and monolithic) across nine production challenges of increasing complexity in a simulation of a physical six-module hexagonal factory, including silent hardware fault detection. The monolithic and peer-to-peer architectures both achieve the highest mean solve rate (93\%), while the orchestrator uniquely resolves a silent conveyor-belt fault in all ten runs by autonomously rerouting plates around the blocked segment. All architectures exhibit emergent fault-diagnosis behavior without any explicit failure-handling logic, establishing standardized MCP tooling, MQTT-based inter-agent communication, and real-time state injection as a viable and reproducible foundation for LLM-programmed smart manufacturing.

</details>


### [194] [After Cooperation Is Learned: Gradient Routing and Optimizer-Dependent Maintenance in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01630v1)
*Chaoyuan Hao,Wentao Yue,Tianyou Lai,Hongji Li,Jiayi Zhou,Qingyu Mao,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究合作MARL中已习得合作策略在持续训练下的稳定性问题。通过设计三种匹配的warm start条件（X0/X1/X5）隔离值梯度的影响，发现直接值梯度路由与条件性、尺度敏感的维护风险相关，而非批评者的普遍失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL合作评估仅关注合作发现，忽视持续优化可能破坏已习得合作的问题；且演员-评论家比较混淆了评论家存在与值梯度共享演员表示的效应。

Method: 将维护问题建模为右截尾事件时间问题；设计三种warm start对比实验（X0:允许值梯度更新共享特征；X1:保留评论家但阻断梯度；X5:移除评论家作为参考）；引入正奖励缩放扰动学习动态；通过梯度审计和冻结策略躯干扰动验证路由通路。

Result: 高尺度下X0的选择性维护敏感性增加；X1稳定在截尾上限附近；X5无确认事件；CleanUp-lite中路由-尺度位移与局部合作边界缩小相关。

Conclusion: 存在条件性、尺度敏感的维护风险，源于直接值梯度路由而非评论家的普遍失败。

Abstract: Cooperative MARL is commonly evaluated through cooperation discovery from random initialization, leaving open whether continued optimization can destabilize learned cooperation. Actor-critic comparisons can also conflate critic presence with value gradients entering shared actor representations. We study cooperation maintenance, defined as the survival of a behaviorally verified cooperative policy under continued training. We formulate maintenance as a right-censored event-time problem and compare matched warm starts: X0 allows value loss gradients to update shared actor features, X1 retains the critic while blocking those gradients, and X5 removes the learned critic as a critic-free reference. This isolates direct value-gradient access while controlling initialization, critic computation, and evaluation. Positive reward scaling preserves strategic preferences and equilibria while perturbing learning dynamics. Gradient audits confirm the intended routing pathways, and frozen-policy torso perturbations probe whether route-induced updates align with local cooperation boundaries. In confirmatory MinEx and CleanUp-lite experiments, higher scales selectively increase maintenance sensitivity in X0; X1 remains near the censoring ceiling, and X5 has no confirmed events in the tested settings. In CleanUp-lite, route-by-scale displacement is associated with reduced local cooperation margins; MinEx shows a weaker, optimizer-dependent effect. These results identify a conditional, scale-sensitive maintenance risk associated with direct value-gradient routing rather than a universal failure of critics.

</details>


### [195] [Can AI Scientists Coordinate at Runtime?](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00980v1)
*Zijian Liu,Yangzhixin Luo,Junyu Lu,Yi Li,Yu Chen,David Xu,William F. Shen,Xinchi Qiu,Xisen Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出运行时智能体协调（RAC）框架，使多智能体AI科学家能够在执行过程中动态选择智能体、分配工作合同并进行基于工件的验证。实验在三种AI科学家平台上进行，结果显示运行时选择机制能提升性能，但额外的协调机制在预算约束下效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体AI科学家系统主要依赖设计时的固定工作流编排，缺乏运行时动态协调能力。人类科学家能够在执行过程中动态调整分工，因此本文探索AI科学家是否也能实现运行时协调。

Method: 提出Runtime Agent Coordination（RAC）框架，包含三个核心机制：(1)从现有AI科学家宿主中动态选择智能体；(2)分配作用域明确的工作合同；(3)提供基于工件的验证，验证结果可指导后续智能体而不阻塞流程或丢弃工件。在Agent Laboratory、EvoScientist和ARK三个平台上进行单种子探索性评估，采用四种累积条件逐步分离变量：本地执行、运行时通信、运行时选择、以及合同与验证的组合。

Result: 运行时选择机制在每个宿主上都取得了最高观察分数；但添加合同和验证机制后分数下降，各宿主表现相对于本地执行有不同结果。

Conclusion: 运行时协调机制具有研究价值，但在预算约束条件下，额外的协调机制（如合同和验证）可能带来开销，限制了其效果。未来需要在开销与收益间寻找平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI scientists have shown improving performance across a diverse range of tasks. Yet a common approach is design-time agentic orchestration, which typically relies on fixed workflows. In contrast, human scientists coordinate and adjust their division of labor at runtime. We therefore ask: can AI scientists also coordinate at runtime? To this end, we introduce Runtime Agent Coordination (RAC), which selects agents from existing AI-scientist hosts during execution, assigns scoped work contracts, and provides artifact-grounded verification. Verification informs subsequent agents without blocking transitions or discarding artifacts. We conduct a single-seed exploratory evaluation across Agent Laboratory, EvoScientist, and ARK on ResearchClawBench, preserving host models, tools, and permissions under host-calibrated budgets. Four cumulative conditions separate native execution, runtime communication, runtime selection, and the combined addition of contracts and verification. Runtime selection yields the highest observed mean score for each host; adding contracts and verification reduces these means, with host-dependent outcomes relative to native execution. These results motivate runtime coordination while exposing the limits of additional coordination mechanisms under constrained budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/systemind-team/Runtime-AI-Scientist.

</details>


### [196] [The Delegation Danger Band: Why Mid-Capability Sub-Agents Over-Trust Inherited Stale State](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00041v1)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究Agent框架中父进程fork子进程时的上下文继承策略，发现中等能力模型（如Qwen3-1.7B）存在"危险地带"现象——对过时状态的依赖导致性能显著下降，而最强和最弱模型对此具有鲁棒性。选择性继承（Selective）优于完全继承（Full），但固定阈值的路由策略无法泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agent框架广泛采用fork机制创建子代理，默认的完整上下文继承策略可能导致子代理依赖过时状态而性能下降。该研究首次系统性地度量继承状态对不同能力水平模型的影响差异。

Method: 在Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B同族模型上，对比三种继承策略（Reset/Selective/Full），基于冻结的闭集动作评分基准测试，涵盖合成任务和MuSiQue、HotpotQA等真实数据集。

Result: （1）模型能力越强，对过时状态的依赖越低（斜率CI在所有模型族上均排除零）；（2）Qwen3合成任务上存在非单调的"危险地带"：1.7B模型净危害为局部最小值（Δ=-0.19 [-0.25, -0.12]），弱模型接近中性，强模型稳健；（3）Selective策略在所有数据集上平均准确率优于Full，最大改进在危险地带模型。

Conclusion: 子代理的上下文继承策略应考虑模型能力；中等能力模型最易受过时状态影响，存在"危险地带"；需开发可迁移的路由器来预测复用收益与过时上下文惩罚之间的平衡。

Abstract: Agent frameworks increasingly delegate work by forking sub-agents; a common default makes the child inherit the parent's full working context. We measure how the effect of inherited state changes with capability, where $C_m$ denotes clean fork-fresh accuracy. We compare 3 inheritance policies: Reset (fork fresh: base evidence only), Selective (curated handoff: + the useful prior conclusion), and Full (implicit fork: + the useful conclusion and $d$ copies of a superseded conclusion) over a same-family ladder (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B) on a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark. Every task is solvable from the base evidence, so performance loss can be attributed to reliance on stale state. (1) Deference to superseded state falls sharply with measured capability $C_m$ (the slope's confidence interval, CI, excludes zero on every family) across 2 synthetic primitives plus MuSiQue and HotpotQA. (2) On the Qwen3 synthetic ladder, net inheritance harm follows a nonmonotone pattern: a mid-capability model (Qwen3-1.7B) is a statistically significant local minimum of net harm, falling below its fork-fresh baseline ($Δ(32)=-0.19$ [-0.25, -0.12]) and both neighbors, while the weakest model stays near-neutral and the strongest models stay robust. We call this harmful capability range a danger band. A within-model counting-difficulty sweep shows that the effect depends on model class even at matched $C_m$, and a live parent-to-child fork reproduces the mid-model harm. (3) Curated Selective handoff improves average accuracy over Full on all 3 datasets, largest at the in-band model, while the fixed-threshold capability router fails on the other datasets; a transferable router would need to predict the balance between reuse benefit and stale-context penalty. The benchmark is frozen and version-hashed.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [TeamLens in Critsly: A Consent-Based Team-Composition Interface and Synthetic Readiness Evaluation for Design Collaboration](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00288v1)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: TeamLens是一个可选的Critsly接口，允许用户自愿分享自报的MBTI类型，并通过多层次的访问控制和隐私机制，支持团队组成分析功能，同时对OpenAI Codex辅助开发过程进行了透明披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前团队中分享MBTI类型可能支持反思，但性格标签存在被误用于绩效预测或参与条件限制的风险。现有工具缺乏对披露-聚合工作流的系统性技术实现和可审计的正确性验证。

Method: 技术报告通过源码检查、独立指定的合成聚合用例、访问与生命周期检查、浏览器组件测试、SQLite本地数据库微基准测试及部署记录等多维度手段，对TeamLens的65,536个二元 Eligibility 子集、128个种子多重性测试fixtures及183条HTTP断言进行验证。

Result: 所有65,536个二元子集与独立预言机一致；128个fixtures在4,259次合成共享调用后匹配；HTTP测试套件通过183条断言；SQLite微基准显示中位数服务调用时间从无profile的0.064ms增长至1,024行时的200.932ms，SQL操作遵循11+3n模式。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一个已实现的披露-聚合工作流及可审计的正确性边界、隐私限制和扩展成本的技术说明。论文明确指出这些发现仅涉及有界工作负载下的软件行为，不涉及人类可用性、心理测量效度、学习收益、团队绩效影响或生产容量评估。

Abstract: Discussing working preferences may support reflection within a design team, but a personality label should not become a performance prediction or a condition of participation. This technical report presents TeamLens, an optional Critsly interface for voluntarily sharing a self-reported MBTI type with a particular board. It separates account activation from disclosure, displays descriptive composition counts, and provides distinct controls for disabling visibility, withdrawing one report and deleting all of one's reports. The evaluation combines released-source inspection, independently specified synthetic aggregation cases, access and lifecycle checks, browser component tests, a local database microbenchmark and deployment records. All 65,536 binary eligibility subsets of sixteen fixed, distinct type reports matched an independent oracle; 128 seeded multiplicity fixtures also matched, after 4,259 synthetic share calls. The isolated HTTP suite passed 183 assertions. In 180 sequential in-memory SQLite read trials, median service-call time increased from 0.064 ms with no profile rows to 200.932 ms with 1,024 rows; instrumented SQL operations followed 11+3n. These findings concern exercised software behaviour and a bounded workload. They do not establish human usability, psychometric validity, learning gains, team-performance effects or production capacity. The contribution is an implemented disclosure-to-aggregation workflow and an auditable technical account of its correctness boundaries, privacy limitations and scaling cost. OpenAI Codex assisted with implementation, evaluation and manuscript preparation; the paper discloses this use and its limits.

</details>


### [198] [Who Judges the Frame? Auditing Multimodal LLM Judges for News Framing Across Event-Level Perspectives](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00071v1)
*Antonela Tommasel,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对大型语言模型（LLM）作为多模态新闻报道框架与视角分析工具进行审计。研究的核心问题是：LLM输出可能反映的不仅是内容属性，还包括模型特定倾向、提示设计选择以及社会、政治、文化、语言或模态特定假设，而非纯粹的内容特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM（包括多模态LLM）被越来越多地用于大规模分析媒体数据的框架、情感、意识形态倾向和视角差异，亟需审视这些模型作为测量工具的可靠性和有效性。LLM输出可能混杂模型特定倾向、提示设计偏差以及社会、政治、文化、语言或模态假设，难以准确反映内容本身属性。

Method: 采用2025-2026年新闻报道的事件中心数据集，每起事件包含左翼、中立和右翼取向的相同头条新闻报道。将基于嵌入的观点相似性度量与模型对框架结构的评估相结合，在模态特定和元数据可见的不同输入条件下进行测试。

Result: 研究未将数据集标签或模型输出视为"ground truth"，而是致力于检验LLM在媒体分析中的有用性和局限性。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了一套审计协议，强调在报告新闻框架和意识形态视角差异时，必须同时披露模态效应、元数据敏感性和提示诱导伪影，为LLM辅助媒体分析的方法论规范化奠定基础。

Abstract: News coverage of major world events is shaped not only by what is reported, but also by how events are framed through text, images and their combination. At the same time, Large Language Models (LLMs), including multimodal LLMs, are increasingly used as scalable instruments for analysing framing, sentiment, ideological slant and perspective differences in multimodal media datasets. This creates a methodological challenge. When used as measurement instruments, LLM outputs may reflect not only content properties, but also model-specific tendencies, prompt design choices, and social, political, cultural, linguistic or modality-specific assumptions. This work audits LLMs as instruments for large-scale framing and perspective analysis in multimodal news coverage. Using an event-centered dataset of 2025--2026 news coverage, where each event includes left-, center- and right-oriented articles about the same headline, we combine embedding-based measures of within-event viewpoint similarity with model-based assessments of framing constructs across modality-specific and metadata-visible input conditions. Rather than treating either dataset labels or model outputs as ground truth, our goal is to examine the usefulness and limitations of LLM-based media analysis. The study contributes an audit protocol that highlights the need to report modality effects, metadata sensitivity, and prompt-induced artifacts alongside substantive claims about news framing and ideological viewpoint differences.

</details>


### [199] [Learning and Predicting Patent Technology Reuse Trajectories from Emergence-Time Signals](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00806v1)
*Ayham Yousef,Qiang Ye,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究利用GRU自编码器学习201,710项新专利技术的20年复用轨迹，在潜在空间中进行k-means聚类来构建复用模式标签。研究发现7个 emergence-time 特征可达到0.914的ROC-AUC，但仅使用年份特征就达到0.874，且这种日历效应主要反映专利申请内容的变化。 emergence-time 特征聚类与GRU聚类的一致性仅比随机略好（ARI≈0.04），说明 emergence 特征分区不能作为复用模式分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术情报中的专利技术复用预测核心挑战在于：复用模式标签不存在，需要从轨迹中构建，且构建方式决定了预测的含义。

Method: 使用GRU自编码器在20年复用轨迹的潜在空间中应用k-means聚类（无人工特征工程）；使用Fractal自编码器进行特征选择；使用GBDT进行轨迹形状预测任务。

Result: ①7个 emergence-time 特征达到one-vs-rest macro ROC-AUC 0.914，但仅日历年份即达0.874；② replication表明日历效应反映专利内容的时代变迁；③ emergence-profile分区与GRU标签的一致性仅比随机高（ARI≈0.04）；④ GBDT在轨迹形状任务中达0.831（全特征）和0.740（选择特征子集）。

Conclusion: ①日历年份效应主要反映随时间变化的专利内容，而非复用模式的变化；② emergence-time特征聚类≠复用模式分类法，不应被解读为后者；③先前研究的0.728结果是不同语料和标签下的参考点而非基准。

Abstract: Forecasting how a newly emerged patent technology will be reused is central to technology intelligence, but reuse-pattern labels do not exist in advance: they must be constructed from the trajectories themselves, and how they are constructed determines what a forecast means. We study $201{,}710$ novel patent technologies (first-time IPC code pairings, USPTO 2002--2022). Our primary labeling applies $k$-means in the latent space of a GRU autoencoder trained on the $20$-year reuse trajectories, using no hand-crafted features; to our knowledge this is the first use of a learned sequence representation for this task. Seven emergence-time features, observable in a technology's first year, recover these labels at a one-vs-rest macro ROC-AUC of $0.914$, but the calendar year of emergence alone reaches $0.874$. A replication on technologies observed for ten full years, none of them right-censored, indicates that this calendar-year effect mainly reflects change over time in what was patented. Separately we cluster the emergence-time features themselves, after Fractal Autoencoder feature selection. That emergence-profile partition agrees with the GRU-based labels only marginally above chance (Adjusted Rand Index $\approx 0.04$), so a partition of emergence-time features is not a reuse-pattern taxonomy and should not be read as one. On a trajectory-shape task following the published construction, GBDT reaches $0.831$ with all seven features and $0.740$ with the selected subset; the published $0.728$, from a different corpus and labeling, is a reference point rather than a benchmark. Two features are additionally left-truncated for the earliest cohorts, which we quantify.

</details>


### [200] [Virtual Global Collaboration in Data Analytics and Machine Learning Education: A Mixed-Methods Study of Asynchronous Cross-Border Teamwork](http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01147v1)
*Sehrish Basir Nizamani,Saad Nizamani,Khyati Goyal,Sarwat Nizamani,Zannah Zeiw*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项关于虚拟全球协作(VGC)的研究，在美国和巴基斯坦的本科计算机课程之间开展，通过异步国际团队合作完成数据分析和机器学习项目。六阶段项目设计包括文化 Orientation、数据集选择、数据清洗、分析、机器学习入门和结构化报告。结果表明学生体验积极，协作过程与学习和感知价值强相关，跨文化沟通是全球 Readiness 的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球化背景下，计算机教育需要培养学生的技术能力和跨文化能力。如何有效设计短期虚拟全球协作活动以同时支持技术学习和全球胜任力发展，是当前教育研究的重要议题。

Method: 采用混合方法研究设计：美国和巴基斯坦本科生组成混合机构团队，通过共享计算工具在六阶段项目中进行异步国际协作（文化 Orientation → 数据集选择 → 数据清洗 → 分析 → 机器学习入门 → 结构化报告）。通过问卷调查和定性反思数据进行分析，考察协作体验、沟通与文化动态、学习成果、感知价值和全球 Readiness。

Result: 两个 cohorts 的学生体验均呈现积极态势；协作过程与学习成果和感知价值高度相关；跨文化沟通被确认为全球 Readiness 的首要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 短期、结构化的虚拟全球协作可以有效融入计算机课程，同时支持技术学习和全球胜任力培养。这一实践为在全球分布的课程中实施跨国协作教育提供了实证参考。

Abstract: This innovative practice full paper examines a structured Virtual Global Collaboration (VGC) activity between undergraduate computing courses in the United States and Pakistan. The project was designed to support technical and intercultural skill development through asynchronous international teamwork in data analytics and introductory machine learning. Students worked in mixed-institution teams through a six-phase project including cultural orientation, dataset selection, data cleaning, analysis, introductory machine learning, and structured reporting. Using shared computational tools, teams coordinated across time zones to complete a joint data-driven project. Survey and qualitative reflection data were analyzed to examine collaborative experience, communication and cultural dynamics, learning outcomes, perceived value, and global readiness. Results show consistently positive student experiences across both cohorts, with collaborative processes strongly associated with learning and perceived value, and cross-cultural communication emerging as the primary driver of global readiness. These findings demonstrate how short-term, structured VGC can be effectively integrated into computing courses to support both technical learning and global competence.

</details>
